# agency\__init__.py
# agency\agent.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Agent:

    def __init__(self, registry, event_bus=None, max_steps=10):
        self.registry = registry
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.max_steps = max_steps
        self._history = []

    def run(self, task, context=None):
        if not task:
            return {"success": False, "error": "no_task_provided"}

        context = context or {}
        start = time.time()

        steps = []
        result = None

        for step_num in range(1, self.max_steps + 1):
            plan = self._plan_next(task, steps, context)

            if plan is None or plan.get("action") == "complete":
                result = plan.get("result", {}) if plan else {}
                if plan:
                    steps.append({
                        "step": step_num,
                        "action": "complete",
                        "result": result,
                        "timestamp": time.time(),
                    })
                break

            action = plan.get("action")
            tool_name = plan.get("tool")
            params = plan.get("params", {})

            step_start = time.time()
            tool_result = self.registry.call(tool_name, **params)
            step_duration = round(time.time() - step_start, 3)

            step_record = {
                "step": step_num,
                "action": action,
                "tool": tool_name,
                "params": params,
                "duration": step_duration,
                "success": tool_result.get("success", False),
                "result": tool_result,
                "timestamp": time.time(),
            }
            steps.append(step_record)

            if self.event_bus:
                self.event_bus.publish("agent.step", step_record)

            if not tool_result.get("success"):
                logger.warning(f"[Agent] Step {step_num} failed: {tool_name} â€” {tool_result.get('error')}")

        total_duration = round(time.time() - start, 3)
        summary = {
            "task": task,
            "steps": len(steps),
            "total_duration": total_duration,
            "success": steps and steps[-1].get("action") == "complete",
            "result": result,
        }
        self._history.append(summary)

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("agent.completed", summary)

        return summary

    def _plan_next(self, task, completed_steps, context):
        if not completed_steps:
            return self._initial_plan(task, context)

        last = completed_steps[-1]
        if not last.get("success"):
            alt = self._fallback_plan(last)
            if alt:
                return alt

        evidence = []
        for s in completed_steps:
            r = s.get("result", {})
            if r.get("success"):
                evidence.append(r.get("result"))

        return {
            "action": "complete",
            "result": {
                "conclusion": f"Completed {len(completed_steps)} step(s) for: {task}",
                "evidence_count": len(evidence),
                "steps_performed": len(completed_steps),
            },
        }

    def _initial_plan(self, task, context):
        task_lower = task.lower()

        if any(w in task_lower for w in ["reason", "think", "analyze", "why", "how", "what"]):
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "reason", "params": {"query": task}}

        if any(w in task_lower for w in ["remember", "recall", "memory", "what happened"]):
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "search_memories", "params": {"query": task, "limit": 5}}

        if any(w in task_lower for w in ["open", "launch", "start", "search for"]):
            if "open" in task_lower or "launch" in task_lower:
                app = task_lower.replace("open", "").replace("launch", "").strip()
                return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "execute_action", "params": {"type": "open_app", "content": app}}
            query = task_lower.replace("search for", "").replace("search", "").strip()
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "execute_action", "params": {"type": "search", "content": query}}

        if any(w in task_lower for w in ["believe", "belief", "know about"]):
            topic = task_lower.replace("believe about", "").replace("know about", "").strip()
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "get_beliefs", "params": {"topic": topic}}

        return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "reason", "params": {"query": task}}

    def _fallback_plan(self, failed_step):
        tool = failed_step.get("tool")
        if tool == "reason":
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "search_memories", "params": {"query": failed_step.get("params", {}).get("query", ""), "limit": 3}}
        if tool == "execute_action":
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "get_brain_state", "params": {}}
        if tool in ("get_beliefs", "search_memories"):
            return {"action": "call_tool", "tool": "retrieve_knowledge", "params": {"query": failed_step.get("params", {}).get("query", failed_step.get("params", {}).get("topic", "")), "top_k": 3}}
        return None

    def history(self, limit=10):
        return self._history[-limit:]

# agency\dreaming.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class DreamEngine:

    def __init__(self, memory_pipeline=None, belief_engine=None,
                 knowledge_graph=None, lifecycle=None, event_bus=None):
        self.memory_pipeline = memory_pipeline
        self.belief_engine = belief_engine
        self.knowledge_graph = knowledge_graph
        self.lifecycle = lifecycle
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._last_dream = 0.0
        self._min_idle_seconds = 300

    def should_dream(self, idle_seconds):
        if idle_seconds < self._min_idle_seconds:
            return False
        if time.time() - self._last_dream < 3600:
            return False
        return True

    def dream(self, memories=None, thoughts=None):
        if not memories:
            memories = []
        if not thoughts:
            thoughts = []

        start = time.time()
        results = {
            "consolidated": 0,
            "beliefs_updated": 0,
            "associations": 0,
            "archived": 0,
            "deleted": 0,
            "patterns": [],
            "duration": 0.0,
        }

        if self.memory_pipeline and memories:
            new_nodes, remaining = self.memory_pipeline.run_consolidation(memories)
            results["consolidated"] = len(new_nodes)
            if self.lifecycle:
                active, to_archive, to_delete = self.memory_pipeline.run_lifecycle(remaining)
                results["archived"] = len(to_archive)
                results["deleted"] = len(to_delete)

        if self.belief_engine and memories:
            for mem in memories:
                if hasattr(mem, "to_dict") and callable(getattr(mem, "to_dict", None)):
                    try:
                        self.belief_engine.update(mem)
                        results["beliefs_updated"] += 1
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug(f"[Dream] Belief update: {e}")

        if self.knowledge_graph and thoughts:
            for thought in thoughts:
                if isinstance(thought, dict):
                    query = thought.get("query", "")
                    conclusion = thought.get("conclusion", "")
                    if query and conclusion:
                        try:
                            self.knowledge_graph.add_entity(query[:50], entity_type="topic")
                            for word in conclusion.split()[:5]:
                                w = word.strip(".,!?").lower()
                                if len(w) > 3:
                                    self.knowledge_graph.add_entity(w, entity_type="concept")
                                    self.knowledge_graph.add_relation(query[:50], "relates_to", w, weight=0.3)
                            results["associations"] += 1
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug(f"[Dream] Graph association: {e}")

        results["duration"] = round(time.time() - start, 3)
        self._last_dream = time.time()

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("dream.completed", results)

        return results

# agency\evolution.py
import time
import logging
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


TRAIT_BOUNDS = {
    "warmth": (0.2, 0.9),
    "teasing": (0.1, 0.85),
    "directness": (0.2, 0.9),
    "playfulness": (0.1, 0.8),
    "affection": (0.1, 0.8),
    "patience": (0.3, 0.95),
    "confidence": (0.2, 0.9),
}

TRAIT_SIGNAL_MAP = {
    "comfort_behavior": {"warmth": 0.02, "affection": 0.02},
    "playfulness_boost": {"playfulness": 0.02, "teasing": 0.01},
    "energy_conservation": {"patience": 0.01, "confidence": -0.01},
}


class PersonalityEvolution:

    def __init__(self, personality_state, event_bus=None):
        self.personality_state = personality_state
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._adjustment_log = []
        self._max_log = 100

    def evolve(self, behavior_adjustments=None, reflections=None):
        if not behavior_adjustments and not reflections:
            return {}

        adjustments = {}

        if behavior_adjustments:
            for signal, delta in behavior_adjustments.items():
                if signal in TRAIT_SIGNAL_MAP:
                    trait_deltas = TRAIT_SIGNAL_MAP[signal]
                    for trait, amount in trait_deltas.items():
                        adjustments[trait] = adjustments.get(trait, 0) + amount

        if reflections:
            for ref in reflections:
                text = ""
                if isinstance(ref, dict):
                    text = ref.get("summary", "") + " " + " ".join(ref.get("patterns", []))
                else:
                    text = str(ref)
                text_lower = text.lower()
                if "teasing" in text_lower or "playful" in text_lower:
                    adjustments["teasing"] = adjustments.get("teasing", 0) + 0.01
                if "warm" in text_lower or "affection" in text_lower:
                    adjustments["affection"] = adjustments.get("affection", 0) + 0.01
                if "patient" in text_lower or "calm" in text_lower:
                    adjustments["patience"] = adjustments.get("patience", 0) + 0.01

        if not adjustments:
            return {}

        with self.personality_state._lock:
            state = self.personality_state.state
            applied = {}
            for trait, delta in adjustments.items():
                if trait not in TRAIT_BOUNDS:
                    continue
                lo, hi = TRAIT_BOUNDS[trait]
                old = state.get(trait, 0.5)
                new = max(lo, min(hi, old + delta))
                if abs(new - old) > 0.001:
                    state[trait] = new
                    applied[trait] = round(new - old, 4)

            if applied:
                with self._lock:
                    self._adjustment_log.append({
                        "timestamp": time.time(),
                        "adjustments": dict(applied),
                        "signals": behavior_adjustments,
                    })
                    if len(self._adjustment_log) > self._max_log:
                        self._adjustment_log.pop(0)
                logger.info(f"[Evolution] Applied: {applied}")

                if self.event_bus:
                    self.event_bus.publish("evolution.adjusted", {
                        "adjustments": applied,
                        "current_state": {k: round(v, 3) for k, v in state.items()},
                    })

                self.personality_state.save()

        return applied

    def history(self, limit=10):
        with self._lock:
            return self._adjustment_log[-limit:]

# agency\goals.py
import time
import uuid
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Goal:

    def __init__(self, description, priority="normal", context=None, goal_id=None):
        self.id = goal_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.description = str(description).strip()
        self.priority = priority if priority in ("high", "normal", "low") else "normal"
        self.status = "pending"
        self.context = dict(context or {})
        self.created_at = time.time()
        self.updated_at = time.time()
        self.completed_at = 0.0
        self.turn_count = 0
        self.subtasks = []

    def add_subtask(self, description):
        subtask = {
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "description": str(description).strip(),
            "completed": False,
            "created_at": time.time(),
        }
        self.subtasks.append(subtask)
        self.updated_at = time.time()
        return subtask

    def complete_subtask(self, subtask_id):
        for s in self.subtasks:
            if s["id"] == subtask_id and not s["completed"]:
                s["completed"] = True
                self.updated_at = time.time()
                return True
        return False

    def auto_advance(self):
        if self.status == "completed":
            return None
        for s in self.subtasks:
            if not s["completed"]:
                return None
        self.status = "completed"
        self.completed_at = time.time()
        self.updated_at = time.time()
        return self

    def progress(self):
        if not self.subtasks:
            return 1.0 if self.status == "completed" else 0.0
        done = sum(1 for s in self.subtasks if s["completed"])
        return done / len(self.subtasks)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "description": self.description,
            "priority": self.priority,
            "status": self.status,
            "context": dict(self.context),
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "updated_at": self.updated_at,
            "completed_at": self.completed_at,
            "turn_count": self.turn_count,
            "subtasks": list(self.subtasks),
            "progress": round(self.progress(), 3),
        }


class GoalManager:

    def __init__(self, event_bus=None, brain_graph=None, emotion=None, learning_engine=None):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._goals = {}
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph
        self.emotion = emotion
        self._learning_engine = learning_engine
        self._max_active = 5

    def _adjusted_priority(self, priority):
        if not self.emotion:
            return priority
        try:
            mult = self.emotion.influence("goal_priority")
            value = {"low": 0, "normal": 1, "high": 2}
            rev = {0: "low", 1: "normal", 2: "high"}
            base = value.get(priority, 1)
            adjusted = base * mult
            if adjusted >= 1.5:
                return "high"
            elif adjusted < 0.7:
                return "low"
            return "normal"
        except Exception:
            return priority

    def create(self, description, priority="normal", context=None):
        if not description:
            return None
        priority = self._adjusted_priority(priority)
        goal = Goal(description, priority=priority, context=context)
        with self._lock:
            active = [g for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "active"]
            if len(active) >= self._max_active:
                lowest = min(active, key=lambda g: ("high", "normal", "low").index(g.priority) if g.priority in ("high", "normal", "low") else 1)
                lowest.status = "paused"
                logger.info(f"[Goals] Paused excess goal: {lowest.description[:40]}")
            goal.status = "active"
            self._goals[goal.id] = goal
        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.add_goal_node(
                goal_id=goal.id,
                description=goal.description,
                priority=goal.priority,
                status=goal.status,
                context=goal.context,
            )
        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("goal.created", goal.to_dict())
        return goal

    def get(self, goal_id):
        with self._lock:
            return self._goals.get(goal_id)

    def list(self, status=None):
        with self._lock:
            goals = list(self._goals.values())
            if status:
                goals = [g for g in goals if g.status == status]
            goals.sort(key=lambda g: ("high", "normal", "low").index(g.priority) if g.priority in ("high", "normal", "low") else 1)
            return goals

    def complete(self, goal_id):
        with self._lock:
            goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
            if not goal or goal.status == "completed":
                return False
            goal.status = "completed"
            goal.completed_at = time.time()
            goal.updated_at = time.time()
        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.update_goal_node(goal_id, status="completed")
        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("goal.completed", goal.to_dict())
        self._trigger_completion_reflection(goal)
        if self._learning_engine:
            try:
                self._learning_engine.adjust_behavior([f"Completed goal: {goal.description}"])
            except Exception:
                pass
        return True

    def _trigger_completion_reflection(self, goal):
        if not self.event_bus:
            return
        try:
            self.event_bus.publish("goal.completed_reflection", {
                "description": goal.description,
                "priority": goal.priority,
                "progress": goal.progress(),
                "subtask_count": len(goal.subtasks),
                "turn_count": goal.turn_count,
            })
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[Goals] Completion reflection: {e}")

    def activate(self, goal_id):
        with self._lock:
            goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
            if not goal:
                return False
            goal.status = "active"
            goal.updated_at = time.time()
        return True

    def cancel(self, goal_id):
        with self._lock:
            goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
            if not goal:
                return False
            goal.status = "cancelled"
            goal.updated_at = time.time()
        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("goal.cancelled", goal.to_dict())
        return True

    def due_for_bringup(self, max_turns=5):
        with self._lock:
            active = [g for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "active"]
            due = [g for g in active if g.turn_count >= max_turns]
            due.sort(key=lambda g: ("high", "normal", "low").index(g.priority) if g.priority in ("high", "normal", "low") else 1)
            return due

    def increment_turns(self, goal_id=None):
        with self._lock:
            if goal_id:
                goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
                if goal:
                    goal.turn_count += 1
                    goal.updated_at = time.time()
                    if self.brain_graph:
                        self.brain_graph.update_goal_node(goal_id, turn_count=goal.turn_count)
            else:
                for g in self._goals.values():
                    if g.status == "active":
                        g.turn_count += 1

    def add_subtask(self, goal_id, description):
        with self._lock:
            goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
            if not goal:
                return None
            subtask = goal.add_subtask(description)
            goal.updated_at = time.time()
        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.update_goal_node(goal_id, progress=goal.progress())
        return subtask

    def complete_subtask(self, goal_id, subtask_id):
        with self._lock:
            goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
            if not goal:
                return False
            result = goal.complete_subtask(subtask_id)
            if result:
                goal.updated_at = time.time()
                completed_goal = goal.auto_advance()
                if completed_goal and self.event_bus:
                    self.event_bus.publish("goal.auto_completed", goal.to_dict())
                    self._trigger_completion_reflection(goal)
        if result and self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.update_goal_node(goal_id, progress=goal.progress())
        return result

    def goal_progress(self, goal_id):
        with self._lock:
            goal = self._goals.get(goal_id)
            if not goal:
                return 0.0
            return goal.progress()

    def check_goals(self, beliefs):
        if not beliefs:
            return {"created": 0, "adjusted": 0, "details": []}
        results = {"created": 0, "adjusted": 0, "details": []}
        with self._lock:
            active = [g for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "active"]
            active_descriptions = {g.description.lower() for g in active}
            for b in beliefs:
                topic = b.get("topic", "").lower().strip()
                obj = b.get("object", "").strip()
                predicate = b.get("predicate", "").strip()
                if not topic or not obj:
                    continue
                desc = f"Learn more about {topic}"
                if desc in active_descriptions:
                    continue
                if any(g.description.lower() == desc for g in active):
                    continue
                goal = Goal(description=desc, priority="low",
                            context={"source": "belief_check", "topic": topic, "predicate": predicate, "object": obj})
                goal.status = "active"
                self._goals[goal.id] = goal
                if self.brain_graph:
                    self.brain_graph.add_goal_node(
                        goal_id=goal.id, description=goal.description,
                        priority=goal.priority, status=goal.status, context=goal.context,
                    )
                results["created"] += 1
                results["details"].append({"action": "created", "goal": goal.description})
            for b in beliefs:
                topic = b.get("topic", "").lower().strip()
                if not topic:
                    continue
                for g in active:
                    if topic in g.description.lower():
                        g.priority = self._adjusted_priority("normal")
                        g.updated_at = time.time()
                        if self.brain_graph:
                            self.brain_graph.update_goal_node(g.id, progress=g.progress())
                        results["adjusted"] += 1
                        results["details"].append({"action": "boosted", "goal": g.description})
        return results

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            total = len(self._goals)
            active = sum(1 for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "active")
            completed = sum(1 for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "completed")
            pending = sum(1 for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "pending")
            avg_progress = 0.0
            active_goals = [g for g in self._goals.values() if g.status == "active"]
            if active_goals:
                avg_progress = round(sum(g.progress() for g in active_goals) / len(active_goals), 3)
            detail = []
            for g in active_goals:
                detail.append({
                    "description": g.description[:50],
                    "priority": g.priority,
                    "progress": round(g.progress(), 3),
                    "subtask_count": len(g.subtasks),
                    "done_subtasks": sum(1 for s in g.subtasks if s["completed"]),
                    "turn_count": g.turn_count,
                })
            return {
                "total": total,
                "active": active,
                "completed": completed,
                "pending": pending,
                "avg_progress": avg_progress,
                "goals": detail,
            }

# agency\planner.py
import time
import logging
import re

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Planner:

    def __init__(self, registry=None, event_bus=None):
        self.registry = registry
        self.event_bus = event_bus

    def decompose(self, task):
        if not task:
            return []

        task = str(task).strip()
        steps = []

        complexity = self._assess_complexity(task)
        if complexity == "simple":
            steps.append(self._single_step(task))
        else:
            steps = self._multi_step(task)

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("plan.decomposed", {
                "task": task,
                "complexity": complexity,
                "steps": len(steps),
            })

        return steps

    def _assess_complexity(self, task):
        task_lower = task.lower()

        conjunctions = ["and", "then", "after", "before", "meanwhile"]
        conjunction_count = sum(1 for c in conjunctions if c in task_lower)

        multi_word = len(re.findall(r"\b\w+\b", task_lower))

        has_steps = "step" in task_lower or "steps" in task_lower
        has_multi = conjunction_count > 1 or multi_word > 10 or has_steps

        return "complex" if has_multi else "simple"

    def _single_step(self, task):
        return {
            "step": 1,
            "action": "execute",
            "tool": self._suggest_tool(task),
            "params": {"query": task},
            "expected": "direct execution",
        }

    def _multi_step(self, task):
        task_lower = task.lower()
        parts = re.split(r"\b(and then|then|after that|meanwhile)\b", task_lower, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        parts = [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip() and p.strip() not in ("and then", "then", "after that", "meanwhile")]

        if len(parts) < 2:
            parts = self._split_by_topic(task)

        steps = []
        for i, part in enumerate(parts, 1):
            tool = self._suggest_tool(part)
            steps.append({
                "step": i,
                "action": "execute" if tool != "reason" else "reason",
                "tool": tool,
                "params": {"query": part},
                "expected": f"step {i} of {len(parts)}",
            })

        return steps

    def _split_by_topic(self, task):
        topics = []
        for keyword in ["remember", "search", "think", "analyze", "open", "launch"]:
            if keyword in task.lower():
                idx = task.lower().find(keyword)
                if idx >= 0:
                    topics.append(task[idx:].strip())
        if not topics:
            topics = [task]
        return topics

    def _suggest_tool(self, text):
        text_lower = text.lower()
        if any(w in text_lower for w in ["remember", "recall", "memory", "what happened"]):
            return "search_memories"
        if any(w in text_lower for w in ["reason", "think", "analyze", "why", "how"]):
            return "reason"
        if any(w in text_lower for w in ["believe", "belief", "know about"]):
            return "get_beliefs"
        if any(w in text_lower for w in ["open", "launch", "search for"]):
            return "execute_action"
        if any(w in text_lower for w in ["retrieve", "knowledge", "look up"]):
            return "retrieve_knowledge"
        return "reason"

    def describe(self, steps):
        if not steps:
            return "No plan generated."
        lines = [f"Plan has {len(steps)} step(s):"]
        for s in steps:
            lines.append(f"  Step {s['step']}: {s['action']} via {s['tool']} â€” {s['expected']}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

# agency\procedural.py
import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ProceduralRule:

    def __init__(self, rule, context, confidence=0.5, source="experience"):
        self.rule = str(rule).strip()
        self.context = str(context).strip().lower()
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence)))
        self.source = str(source)
        self.created_at = time.time()
        self.last_applied = 0.0
        self.apply_count = 0

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "rule": self.rule,
            "context": self.context,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "source": self.source,
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "last_applied": self.last_applied,
            "apply_count": self.apply_count,
        }


class ProceduralMemory:

    def __init__(self, event_bus=None, brain_graph=None):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._rules = []
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph

    def learn(self, rule, context, confidence=0.5, source="experience"):
        if not rule or not context:
            return None
        with self._lock:
            existing = self._find(rule, context)
            if existing:
                existing.confidence = min(1.0, existing.confidence + 0.1)
                existing.apply_count += 1
                logger.info(f"[Procedural] Reinforced rule: {rule[:50]}")
                return existing
            pr = ProceduralRule(rule, context, confidence=confidence, source=source)
            self._rules.append(pr)
        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.add_rule_node(
                rule=pr.rule,
                context=pr.context,
                confidence=pr.confidence,
                source=pr.source,
            )
        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("procedural.learned", pr.to_dict())
        return pr

    def retrieve(self, context, min_confidence=0.3):
        context = str(context).strip().lower()
        matches = []
        with self._lock:
            for r in self._rules:
                if r.confidence < min_confidence:
                    continue
                if context == r.context or context in r.context or r.context in context:
                    matches.append(r)
        if self.brain_graph:
            graph_rules = self.brain_graph.get_rules(context=context, min_confidence=min_confidence)
            graph_rules_text = {n.metadata.get("rule", "") for n in graph_rules}
            local_text = {r.rule for r in matches}
            for n in graph_rules:
                r_text = n.metadata.get("rule", "")
                if r_text and r_text not in local_text:
                    pr = ProceduralRule(
                        rule=r_text,
                        context=n.metadata.get("context", context),
                        confidence=n.metadata.get("confidence", 0.5),
                        source=n.metadata.get("source", "brain_graph"),
                    )
                    matches.append(pr)
        matches.sort(key=lambda r: r.confidence, reverse=True)
        return matches

    def _find(self, rule, context):
        for r in self._rules:
            if r.rule == rule and r.context == context:
                return r
        return None

    def apply(self, rule, context):
        with self._lock:
            for r in self._rules:
                if r.rule == rule and (context == r.context or context in r.context or r.context in context):
                    r.apply_count += 1
                    r.last_applied = time.time()
                    return True
        return False

    def list(self, min_confidence=0.0):
        with self._lock:
            return [r for r in self._rules if r.confidence >= min_confidence]

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "total": len(self._rules),
                "avg_confidence": round(sum(r.confidence for r in self._rules) / max(1, len(self._rules)), 2),
            }

# agency\registry.py
import time
import json
import logging
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ToolDefinition:

    def __init__(self, name, description, handler, parameters=None, category="general"):
        self.name = str(name)
        self.description = str(description)
        self.handler = handler
        self.parameters = dict(parameters or {})
        self.category = str(category)
        self.created = time.time()
        self.call_count = 0
        self.last_call = 0.0

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Tool(name={self.name}, category={self.category})"


class ToolRegistry:

    def __init__(self, event_bus=None):
        self._tools = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.event_bus = event_bus

    def register(self, name, description, handler, parameters=None, category="general"):
        with self._lock:
            if name in self._tools:
                logger.warning(f"[ToolRegistry] Overwriting tool: {name}")

            tool = ToolDefinition(
                name=name,
                description=description,
                handler=handler,
                parameters=parameters,
                category=category,
            )
            self._tools[name] = tool

            if self.event_bus:
                self.event_bus.publish("tool.registered", {
                    "name": name,
                    "category": category,
                })

            return tool

    def unregister(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            return self._tools.pop(name, None)

    def get(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            return self._tools.get(name)

    def list(self, category=None):
        with self._lock:
            if category:
                return {n: t for n, t in self._tools.items() if t.category == category}
            return dict(self._tools)

    def call(self, name, **kwargs):
        with self._lock:
            tool = self._tools.get(name)

        if not tool:
            logger.error(f"[ToolRegistry] Unknown tool: {name}")
            return {"success": False, "error": f"unknown_tool:{name}"}

        try:
            tool.call_count += 1
            tool.last_call = time.time()

            start = time.time()
            result = tool.handler(**kwargs)
            duration = round(time.time() - start, 3)

            if self.event_bus:
                self.event_bus.publish("tool.called", {
                    "name": name,
                    "duration": duration,
                    "success": True,
                })

            return {
                "success": True,
                "tool": name,
                "result": result,
                "duration": duration,
            }

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[ToolRegistry] Tool call failed: {name} â€” {e}")
            if self.event_bus:
                self.event_bus.publish("tool.called", {
                    "name": name,
                    "success": False,
                    "error": str(e),
                })
            return {"success": False, "tool": name, "error": str(e)}

    def tool_definitions_for_llm(self, category=None):
        tools = self.list(category=category)
        return [
            {
                "name": t.name,
                "description": t.description,
                "parameters": t.parameters,
                "category": t.category,
            }
            for t in tools.values()
        ]

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "total_tools": len(self._tools),
                "tools_by_category": {
                    cat: len([t for t in self._tools.values() if t.category == cat])
                    for cat in {t.category for t in self._tools.values()}
                },
                "total_calls": sum(t.call_count for t in self._tools.values()),
            }

# agency\tools.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def register_builtin_tools(registry, brain=None, memory=None, knowledge_retriever=None,
                           reasoning_engine=None, belief_engine=None, executor=None,
                           graph_manager=None, graph_reasoning=None,
                           obsidian_writer=None):
    if brain:
        registry.register(
            name="get_brain_state",
            description="Retrieve the current cognitive state, including emotion, topic, and turn count.",
            handler=lambda: _call(brain, "get_state", {}),
            parameters={},
            category="core",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="set_emotion",
            description="Set the AI's emotional state (type and intensity).",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(brain, "set_emotion", kw),
            parameters={
                "emotion_type": {"type": "string", "description": "Emotion name (e.g. happy, sad, curious)"},
                "intensity": {"type": "number", "description": "Intensity 0.0 to 1.0"},
            },
            category="core",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="set_topic",
            description="Update the current conversation topic.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(brain, "set_topic", kw),
            parameters={
                "topic": {"type": "string", "description": "The new conversation topic"},
            },
            category="core",
        )

    if memory:
        registry.register(
            name="store_memory",
            description="Store a memory with text content, type, and optional tags.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(memory, "store", kw),
            parameters={
                "text": {"type": "string", "description": "Memory content text"},
                "memory_type": {"type": "string", "description": "Memory category (e.g. conversation, preference)"},
                "tags": {"type": "array", "description": "Optional tags for retrieval"},
            },
            category="memory",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="search_memories",
            description="Search memories by query text or tags.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(memory, "search", kw),
            parameters={
                "query": {"type": "string", "description": "Search query"},
                "limit": {"type": "number", "description": "Maximum results"},
            },
            category="memory",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="recall",
            description="Recall recent or important memories by type.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(memory, "recall", kw),
            parameters={
                "memory_type": {"type": "string", "description": "Type filter"},
                "limit": {"type": "number", "description": "Maximum results"},
            },
            category="memory",
        )

    if knowledge_retriever:
        registry.register(
            name="retrieve_knowledge",
            description="Search the knowledge base for information on a topic.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(knowledge_retriever, "retrieve", kw),
            parameters={
                "query": {"type": "string", "description": "The knowledge query"},
                "top_k": {"type": "number", "description": "Number of results"},
            },
            category="knowledge",
        )

    if belief_engine:
        registry.register(
            name="get_beliefs",
            description="Get current beliefs and their confidence levels.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(belief_engine, "get_beliefs_by_topic", kw),
            parameters={
                "topic": {"type": "string", "description": "Optional topic filter"},
            },
            category="knowledge",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="update_belief",
            description="Create or update a belief on a topic with evidence.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(belief_engine, "update_belief", kw),
            parameters={
                "topic": {"type": "string", "description": "Belief topic"},
                "content": {"type": "string", "description": "Belief content"},
                "confidence": {"type": "number", "description": "Confidence 0.0 to 1.0"},
            },
            category="knowledge",
        )

    if reasoning_engine:
        registry.register(
            name="reason",
            description="Perform reasoning on a question or problem using available knowledge and memory.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(reasoning_engine, "reason", kw),
            parameters={
                "query": {"type": "string", "description": "The question or problem to reason about"},
            },
            category="reasoning",
        )

    if executor:
        registry.register(
            name="execute_action",
            description="Execute a system-level action like opening an app, searching the web, or running a command.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(executor, "run", {"action": kw}),
            parameters={
                "type": {"type": "string", "description": "Action type: open_app, open_url, search, run_command, type_text"},
                "content": {"type": "string", "description": "Action target content"},
            },
            category="execution",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="get_execution_stats",
            description="Get execution statistics (success rate, counts, queued tasks).",
            handler=lambda: _call(executor, "stats", {}),
            parameters={},
            category="execution",
        )


    if graph_manager:
        registry.register(
            name="graph_query",
            description="Query the knowledge graph for entities related to a topic. Returns structured relationships.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(graph_manager, "query", kw),
            parameters={
                "entity": {"type": "string", "description": "The entity name to query"},
                "depth": {"type": "number", "description": "Traversal depth (1 = direct neighbors)"},
            },
            category="knowledge",
        )

        registry.register(
            name="graph_search",
            description="Search for entities in the knowledge graph by name.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(graph_manager, "search", kw),
            parameters={
                "query": {"type": "string", "description": "Entity name search query"},
            },
            category="knowledge",
        )

    if graph_reasoning:
        registry.register(
            name="graph_reason",
            description="Answer natural language questions about the knowledge graph. Supports: 'what connects X and Y', 'what is related to X', 'path from X to Y'.",
            handler=lambda **kw: _call(graph_reasoning, "answer", kw),
            parameters={
                "query": {"type": "string", "description": "Natural language question about graph relationships"},
            },
            category="reasoning",
        )


    if obsidian_writer:
        registry.register(
            name="obsidian_sync",
            description="Sync the knowledge graph to Obsidian vault markdown files. Writes entities with frontmatter and [[wiki-links]].",
            handler=lambda: _call(obsidian_writer, "sync", {}),
            parameters={},
            category="knowledge",
        )


def _call(obj, method_name, kwargs):
    if obj is None:
        return {"error": f"system_not_available:{method_name}"}
    method = getattr(obj, method_name, None)
    if method is None:
        return {"error": f"method_not_found:{method_name}"}
    if not isinstance(kwargs, dict):
        logger.warning("_call(%s) expected dict kwargs, got %s", method_name, type(kwargs).__name__)
        return method()
    return method(**kwargs)

# agency\world.py
import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

CATEGORY_PERSON = "person"
CATEGORY_PLACE = "place"
CATEGORY_PROJECT = "project"
CATEGORY_TECHNOLOGY = "technology"
CATEGORY_EVENT = "event"
CATEGORY_SKILL = "skill"
CATEGORY_MEDIA = "media"
CATEGORY_ORGANIZATION = "organization"
CATEGORY_RELATIONSHIP = "relationship"
CATEGORY_INTEREST = "interest"
CATEGORY_CHARACTER = "character"


class Entity:

    def __init__(self, name, category, attributes=None):
        self.name = str(name).strip()
        self.category = str(category).strip().lower()
        self.attributes = dict(attributes or {})
        self.relations = {}
        self.created_at = time.time()
        self.updated_at = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "category": self.category,
            "attributes": dict(self.attributes),
            "relations": dict(self.relations),
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "updated_at": self.updated_at,
        }


class WorldModel:

    def __init__(self, event_bus=None, brain_graph=None):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._entities = {}
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph

    def add_entity(self, name, category="person", attributes=None):
        if not name:
            return None
        key = name.lower().strip()
        with self._lock:
            if key in self._entities:
                existing = self._entities[key]
                if attributes:
                    existing.attributes.update(attributes)
                existing.updated_at = time.time()
                return existing
            entity = Entity(name, category, attributes=attributes)
            self._entities[key] = entity
        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.add_world_entity(
                name=name, category=category, attributes=attributes
            )
        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("world.entity_added", entity.to_dict())
        return entity

    def get(self, name):
        key = name.lower().strip()
        with self._lock:
            return self._entities.get(key)

    def relate(self, source, relation, target):
        s_key = source.lower().strip()
        t_key = target.lower().strip()
        with self._lock:
            s = self._entities.get(s_key)
            t = self._entities.get(t_key)
            if not s or not t:
                logger.warning(f"[World] Cannot relate unknown entities: {source} -> {target}")
                return False
            if relation not in s.relations:
                s.relations[relation] = set()
            s.relations[relation].add(t.name)
            s.updated_at = time.time()
        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.connect(s_key, relation, t_key)
        return True

    def query(self, name, relation=None, depth=1):
        key = name.lower().strip()
        with self._lock:
            entity = self._entities.get(key)
            if not entity:
                return []
            results = [entity.to_dict()]
            if depth > 0 and entity.relations:
                for rel, targets in entity.relations.items():
                    if relation and rel != relation:
                        continue
                    for t_name in targets:
                        t = self._entities.get(t_name.lower())
                        if t:
                            results.append({
                                "relation": rel,
                                "entity": t.to_dict(),
                            })
            return results

    def search(self, category=None, attribute_key=None, attribute_value=None):
        results = []
        with self._lock:
            for e in self._entities.values():
                if category and e.category != category:
                    continue
                if attribute_key and attribute_value:
                    val = e.attributes.get(attribute_key)
                    if val is None or attribute_value.lower() not in str(val).lower():
                        continue
                results.append(e.to_dict())
        if self.brain_graph:
            graph_entities = self.brain_graph.get_world_entities(category=category)
            graph_names = {n.name for n in graph_entities}
            local_names = {r["name"].lower() for r in results}
            for n in graph_entities:
                if n.name.lower() not in local_names:
                    results.append({
                        "name": n.name,
                        "category": n.type,
                        "attributes": dict(n.metadata),
                        "relations": {},
                        "created_at": n.created_at,
                        "updated_at": n.created_at,
                    })
        return results

    def list_categories(self):
        cats = {}
        with self._lock:
            for e in self._entities.values():
                cats[e.category] = cats.get(e.category, 0) + 1
        if self.brain_graph:
            graph_counts = self.brain_graph.graph.count_by_type()
            for t, c in graph_counts.items():
                if t not in ("belief", "goal", "thought", "rule", "memory", "unknown"):
                    cats[t] = cats.get(t, 0) + c
        return cats

    def all_entities(self):
        results = []
        with self._lock:
            results = [e.to_dict() for e in self._entities.values()]
        if self.brain_graph:
            graph_entities = self.brain_graph.graph.all_entities()
            local_names = {r["name"].lower() for r in results}
            for e in graph_entities:
                if e.type in ("belief", "goal", "thought", "rule", "memory", "unknown"):
                    continue
                if e.name.lower() not in local_names:
                    results.append({
                        "name": e.name,
                        "category": e.type,
                        "attributes": dict(e.metadata),
                        "relations": {},
                        "created_at": e.created_at,
                        "updated_at": e.created_at,
                    })
        return results

    def stats(self):
        local_count = 0
        with self._lock:
            local_count = len(self._entities)
        bg_extra = 0
        if self.brain_graph:
            all_types = self.brain_graph.graph.count_by_type()
            for t, c in all_types.items():
                if t not in ("belief", "goal", "thought", "rule", "memory", "unknown"):
                    bg_extra += c
        return {
            "total_entities": local_count + bg_extra,
            "categories": self.list_categories(),
        }

# api\__init__.py
# api\server.py
import os
import sys
import time
import logging
import threading
import json
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

logging.getLogger().setLevel(logging.WARNING)
logging.getLogger("api.server").setLevel(logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger("api.server")


class MaiAPI:

    def __init__(self, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000):
        self.host = host
        self.port = port
        self.brain = None
        self.voice = None
        self.voice_manager = None
        self.event_bus = None
        self.knowledge_builder = None
        self.goal_manager = None
        self.world_model = None
        self.watcher = None
        self.plugin_loader = None
        self.hooks = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._ready = False

    def bootstrap(self):
        from run import bootstrap_system
        try:
            components = bootstrap_system()
            self.brain = components.get("brain")
            self.voice = components.get("voice")
            self.voice_manager = components.get("voice_manager")
            self.event_bus = components.get("event_bus")
            self.goal_manager = components.get("goal_manager")
            self.world_model = components.get("world_model")
            self.knowledge_builder = components.get("knowledge_builder")
            self._ready = True
            logger.info("[API] System bootstrapped")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[API] Bootstrap failed: %s", e)
            return False

    def chat(self, message, stream=False):
        if not self._ready or not self.brain:
            return {"error": "system_not_ready"}
        try:
            response = self.brain.think(message)
            return {"response": str(response), "status": "ok"}
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[API] Chat error: %s", e)
            return {"error": str(e)}

    def status(self):
        if not self._ready:
            return {"ready": False}
        info = {"ready": True}
        try:
            if self.brain:
                info["emotion"] = self.brain.emotion.get() if hasattr(self.brain, "emotion") else {}
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            if self.goal_manager:
                info["goals"] = self.goal_manager.stats()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            if self.world_model:
                info["world"] = self.world_model.stats()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return info

    def memory_query(self, query, top_k=5):
        if not self._ready or not self.brain:
            return {"error": "system_not_ready"}
        try:
            results = self.brain.memory.search(query, top_k=top_k) if hasattr(self.brain, "memory") else []
            return {"results": results}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"error": str(e)}

    def knowledge_ingest(self, source, text=None, file_path=None, directory=None):
        if not self.knowledge_builder:
            return {"error": "knowledge_builder_not_available"}
        try:
            if text:
                result = self.knowledge_builder.build_from_text(text, source=source)
            elif file_path:
                result = self.knowledge_builder.build_from_file(file_path)
            elif directory:
                result = self.knowledge_builder.build_from_directory(directory)
            else:
                return {"error": "no_content_provided"}
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return {"error": str(e)}


from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel
import uvicorn

app = FastAPI(title="Mai AI API", version="2.0.0")
mai = MaiAPI()


def _build_dashboard_state():
    state = {}
    brain = mai.brain
    if brain:
        try:
            emo = brain.state.emotion.model_dump() if hasattr(brain.state, "emotion") else {}
            if hasattr(brain, "emotion"):
                emo["profile"] = brain.emotion.profile_name()
                emo["history_count"] = brain.emotion.history.count()
                emo["dominant"] = brain.emotion.dominant()
                if hasattr(brain.emotion, "state"):
                    emo["state"] = brain.emotion.state.copy()
            state["emotion"] = emo
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            state["relationship"] = brain.state.relationship_scores.model_dump() if hasattr(brain.state, "relationship_scores") else {}
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            rt = brain.state.runtime
            state["runtime"] = rt.model_dump() if hasattr(rt, "model_dump") else {}
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            state["personality"] = dict(brain.personality_state.state) if hasattr(brain.personality_state, "state") else {}
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            rt = brain.state.runtime
            state["speaker"] = {"current": rt.current_speaker_name or "Unknown", "known": rt.known_user, "confidence": round(rt.speaker_confidence, 3)}
        except Exception:
            pass
    if mai.goal_manager:
        try:
            state["goals"] = mai.goal_manager.stats()
        except Exception:
            pass
    if mai.world_model:
        try:
            wstats = mai.world_model.stats()
            entities = mai.world_model.all_entities()
            wstats["entities"] = [{"name": e.get("name", ""), "category": e.get("category", "")} for e in entities[-20:]]
            state["world"] = wstats
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        from knowledge.cognitive_graph import CognitiveGraph
        cg = brain.graph_manager.graph if brain and hasattr(brain, "graph_manager") and brain.graph_manager else None
        if cg:
            from knowledge.graph_reflection import GraphReflection
            state["graph_reflection"] = GraphReflection(cg).summary()
            state["cognitive_graph"] = {"total_nodes": cg.entity_count(), "cognitive_nodes": sum(1 for e in cg.all_entities() if e.type in ("belief", "goal", "thought", "rule", "memory")), "entity_nodes": sum(1 for e in cg.all_entities() if e.type not in ("belief", "goal", "thought", "rule", "memory")), "edges": cg.relation_count()}
    except Exception:
        pass
    if brain and hasattr(brain, "cognitive"):
        try:
            state["cognitive"] = brain.cognitive.get()
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        from config.settings import AI_NAME
        state["name"] = AI_NAME
    except Exception:
        pass
    return state


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str


class IngestRequest(BaseModel):
    source: str = "api"
    text: str = None
    file_path: str = None
    directory: str = None


@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup():
    success = mai.bootstrap()
    if not success:
        logger.warning("[API] System bootstrap failed (some features unavailable)")


@app.get("/api/health")
def health():
    return {"status": "ok", "ready": mai._ready}


@app.post("/api/chat")
def chat(req: ChatRequest):
    if not req.message:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="message_required")
    result = mai.chat(req.message)
    if "error" in result:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=result["error"])
    return result


@app.get("/api/status")
def status():
    return mai.status()


@app.get("/api/memory")
def memory(query: str = "", top_k: int = 5):
    result = mai.memory_query(query, top_k)
    if "error" in result:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=result["error"])
    return result


@app.post("/api/knowledge/ingest")
def ingest(req: IngestRequest):
    result = mai.knowledge_ingest(req.source, text=req.text, file_path=req.file_path, directory=req.directory)
    if "error" in result:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result["error"])
    return result



@app.get("/api/state")
def dashboard_state():
    return _build_dashboard_state()


@app.get("/api/graph")
def dashboard_graph():
    try:
        from knowledge.cognitive_graph import CognitiveGraph
        if mai.brain and hasattr(mai.brain, "graph_manager") and mai.brain.graph_manager:
            cg = CognitiveGraph(mai.brain.graph_manager.graph)
            return cg.to_graph_data()
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {"nodes": [], "edges": []}


@app.get("/dashboard", response_class=HTMLResponse)
@app.get("/dashboard/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def dashboard_page():
    try:
        dashboard_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "static", "dashboard.html")
        if os.path.isfile(dashboard_path):
            with open(dashboard_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return f.read()
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "<html><body><h1>Dashboard not found</h1></body></html>"


static_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "static")
if os.path.isdir(static_dir):
    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=static_dir, html=True), name="static")


def run(host="0.0.0.0", port=8000):
    mai.host = host
    mai.port = port
    logger.info("[API] Starting on %s:%s", host, port)
    uvicorn.run(app, host=host, port=port, log_level="warning")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

# core\attention.py
# core/attention.py
import random
import time


class Attention:

    def __init__(self):

        # ðŸŽ¯ current focus
        self.focus = "user"

        # ðŸ”¥ focus strength
        self.intensity = 0.8

        # ðŸ•’ last focus switch
        self.last_shift = time.time()

        # â³ minimum time before switching
        self.cooldown = 5

        # ðŸ”’ temporary lock
        self.locked_until = 0

    # ðŸ§  MAIN UPDATE
    def update(

        self,

        emotion=None,

        speaking=False,

        user_active=False,

        thinking=False
    ):

        now = time.time()

        # ðŸ”’ attention lock
        if now < self.locked_until:
            return

        # â³ prevent jitter switching
        if now - self.last_shift < self.cooldown:
            return

        previous = self.focus

        # ðŸŽ¤ user speaking
        if user_active:

            self.focus = "user"

            self.intensity = 1.0

        # ðŸ—£ï¸ AI speaking
        elif speaking:

            self.focus = "user"

            self.intensity = 0.9

        # ðŸ§  deep thinking
        elif thinking:

            self.focus = "self"

            self.intensity = 0.85

        else:

            # ðŸŽ­ emotional influence
            mood = "neutral"

            if emotion:
                mood = emotion.get(
                    "mood",
                    "neutral"
                )

            # â¤ï¸ warm emotion
            if mood in [

                "soft",

                "subtle_warmth"
            ]:

                bias = 0.75

            # ðŸ˜  annoyed emotion
            elif mood == "annoyed":

                bias = 0.4

            else:

                bias = 0.6

            # ðŸŽ² weighted drift
            if random.random() < bias:

                self.focus = "user"

                self.intensity = round(

                    random.uniform(
                        0.6,
                        1.0
                    ),

                    2
                )

            else:

                self.focus = "self"

                self.intensity = round(

                    random.uniform(
                        0.4,
                        0.9
                    ),

                    2
                )

        # ðŸ§  update shift timer
        if self.focus != previous:

            self.last_shift = now

    # ðŸ”’ TEMPORARY ATTENTION LOCK
    def lock(

        self,

        seconds=5,

        focus="user"
    ):

        self.focus = focus

        self.locked_until = (
            time.time() + seconds
        )

    # ðŸ“Š CURRENT STATE
    def get(self):

        return {

            "focus":
                self.focus,

            "intensity":
                round(self.intensity, 2)
        }

    # ðŸ§  DEBUG INFO
    def debug(self):

        return (

            f"[Attention] "

            f"focus={self.focus} "

            f"intensity={self.intensity}"
        )

    
# core\autonomy\engine.py
# core/autonomy/engine.py
import logging
import time
import random
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.Autonomy")


class Autonomy:

    def __init__(
        self,
        brain,
        executor,
        memory,
        emotion=None,
        state=None
    ):
        """
        Autonomous Decision Engine for proactive agent behavior.
        
        :param state: Optional centralized thread-safe system state infrastructure.
        """
        self.brain = brain
        self.executor = executor
        self.memory = memory
        self.emotion = emotion
        
        # ðŸ”’ Centralized Thread-Safe State Infrastructure Alignment
        self.sys_state = state

        self.enabled = True

        # ðŸ§  Internal reentrant thread safety lock
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # â±ï¸ Cooldown tracking system
        self.last_action_time = 0.0
        self.current_cooldown = self._generate_cooldown()

        # ðŸ§  Anti-spam repeat suppression thresholds
        self.last_intent = None
        self.last_intent_time = 0.0
        self.intent_repeat_cooldown = 180

        # ðŸ“Š Renamed to avoid shadowing/conflicting with the central 'state' object
        self.runtime_stats = {
            "actions_taken": 0,
            "last_action": None,
            "started_at": time.time()
        }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŽ² DYNAMIC COOLDOWN
    # ==================================================
    def _generate_cooldown(self):
        """Generates a random cooldown window in seconds between checks."""
        return random.randint(30, 90)

    # ==================================================
    # â±ï¸ MAIN UPDATE LOOP
    # ==================================================
    def tick(self, context=None):
        """
        Evaluates system context and emotional parameters to determine 
        if a proactive autonomous action should be fired.
        """
        with self.lock:
            try:
                if not self.enabled:
                    return None

                # Defensive type normalization for incoming context dicts
                if context is None or not isinstance(context, dict):
                    context = {}

                now = time.time()

                # â±ï¸ Core cooldown guard block
                elapsed = now - self.last_action_time
                if elapsed < self.current_cooldown:
                    return None

                # ðŸ§  Evaluate rules engine
                action = self._decide(context)
                if not action:
                    return None

                # ðŸš« Repetition and spam mitigation block
                intent = action.get("intent")
                if (
                    intent == self.last_intent
                    and now - self.last_intent_time < self.intent_repeat_cooldown
                ):
                    return None

                # âœ… Commit updates to the localized metric state
                self.last_action_time = now
                self.current_cooldown = self._generate_cooldown()
                self.last_intent = intent
                self.last_intent_time = now

                self.runtime_stats["actions_taken"] += 1
                self.runtime_stats["last_action"] = action

                return action

            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("Autonomy tick failed: %s", e)
                return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  DECISION ENGINE
    # ==================================================
    def _decide(self, context):
        """Evaluates relationship parameters and moods to generate intents."""
        relationship = self._get_relationship_state()
        emotion = self._get_emotion_state()

        # Safely extract contextual cross-thread values with reliable fallbacks
        user_active = bool(context.get("user_active", True))
        time_of_day = str(context.get("time_of_day", "day")).lower()
        conversation_length = int(context.get("conversation_length", 0))

        # ==================================================
        # PRIORITY 1 â€” LONELINESS
        # ==================================================
        if emotion["lonely"] > 0.75 and not user_active:
            return self._build_action(
                intent="seek_attention",
                emotion="lonely",
                priority=10
            )

        # ==================================================
        # PRIORITY 2 â€” RELATIONSHIP CHECK-IN
        # ==================================================
        if relationship["closeness"] > 0.8 and conversation_length > 3:
            if random.random() < 0.35:
                return self._build_action(
                    intent="gentle_checkin",
                    emotion="warm",
                    priority=7
                )

        # ==================================================
        # PRIORITY 3 â€” NIGHT PRESENCE
        # ==================================================
        if time_of_day == "night":
            if random.random() < 0.25:
                return self._build_action(
                    intent="soft_presence",
                    emotion="calm",
                    priority=5
                )

        # ==================================================
        # PRIORITY 4 â€” PLAYFULNESS
        # ==================================================
        if relationship["attachment"] > 0.65 and emotion["happy"] > 0.5:
            if random.random() < 0.15:
                return self._build_action(
                    intent="playful_tease",
                    emotion="playful",
                    priority=4
                )

        return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ—ï¸ ACTION BUILDER
    # ==================================================
    def _build_action(self, intent, emotion, priority):
        """Constructs standardized schema payloads for execution loops."""
        return {
            "type": "autonomous_action",
            "intent": str(intent),
            "emotion": str(emotion),
            "priority": int(priority),
            "timestamp": time.time()
        }

    # ==================================================
    # â¤ï¸ RELATIONSHIP STATE
    # ==================================================
    def _get_relationship_state(self):
        """Extracts relationship data from standard or fallback storage frameworks."""
        try:
            structured = {}

            # âœ… Memory Wrapper API branch
            if hasattr(self.memory, "get_structured"):
                structured = self.memory.get_structured()
            
            # âœ… Structural Fallback Reader interface branch
            elif hasattr(self.memory, "reader"):
                reader = getattr(self.memory, "reader", None)
                if reader and hasattr(reader, "get_all"):
                    structured = reader.get_all()

            if not isinstance(structured, dict):
                structured = {}

            relationship = structured.get("relationship", {})
            if not isinstance(relationship, dict):
                relationship = {}

            # Safe float conversion utility
            def safe_float(val, default=0.0):
                try:
                    return float(val)
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    return default

            return {
                "closeness": safe_float(relationship.get("closeness"), 0.0),
                "trust": safe_float(relationship.get("trust"), 0.0),
                "attachment": safe_float(relationship.get("attachment"), 0.0)
            }

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Autonomy relationship state error: %s", e)
            return {"closeness": 0.0, "trust": 0.0, "attachment": 0.0}

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ˜Š EMOTIONAL STATE
    # ==================================================
    def _get_emotion_state(self):
        """Extracts runtime mood metrics safely across class/dictionary models."""
        try:
            if not self.emotion:
                return self._default_emotion()

            def safe_float(val, default=0.5):
                try:
                    return float(val)
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    return default

            # âœ… Active System Class Engine branch
            if hasattr(self.emotion, "get"):
                exported = self.emotion.get()

                if isinstance(exported, dict) and "state" in exported:
                    state = exported["state"]
                    if isinstance(state, dict):
                        return {
                            "lonely": safe_float(state.get("lonely"), 0.0),
                            "happy": safe_float(state.get("happy"), 0.5),
                            "energy": safe_float(state.get("energy"), 0.5)
                        }

            # âœ… Standalone dictionary configuration branch
            if isinstance(self.emotion, dict):
                return {
                    "lonely": safe_float(self.emotion.get("lonely"), 0.0),
                    "happy": safe_float(self.emotion.get("happy"), 0.5),
                    "energy": safe_float(self.emotion.get("energy"), 0.5)
                }

            return self._default_emotion()

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Autonomy emotion state error: %s", e)
            return self._default_emotion()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ›¡ï¸ DEFAULT EMOTION
    # ==================================================
    def _default_emotion(self):
        return {"lonely": 0.0, "happy": 0.5, "energy": 0.5}

    # ==================================================
    # âš™ï¸ ENABLE / DISABLE
    # ==================================================
    def enable(self):
        with self.lock:
            self.enabled = True

    def disable(self):
        with self.lock:
            self.enabled = False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================
    def stats(self):
        """Gathers system operations metrics safely."""
        with self.lock:
            uptime = round(
                time.time() - self.runtime_stats["started_at"], 
                2
            )
            return {
                "enabled": self.enabled,
                "actions_taken": self.runtime_stats["actions_taken"],
                "last_action": self.runtime_stats["last_action"],
                "cooldown": self.current_cooldown,
                "uptime_seconds": uptime
            }
# core\behavior_builder.py
# core/behavior_builder.py
import threading


class BehaviorBuilder:

    def __init__(self):

        self.behaviors = []
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN BUILDER
    # ==================================================

    def build(
        self,
        structured,
        emotion=None,
        context=None
    ):

        with self._lock:
            self.behaviors = []

        # ðŸ›¡ï¸ SAFE DEFAULTS
        if not isinstance(structured, dict):
            structured = {}

        if not isinstance(emotion, dict):
            emotion = {}

        if not isinstance(context, dict):
            context = {}

        relationship = structured.get(
            "relationship",
            {}
        )

        preferences = structured.get(
            "preferences",
            {}
        )

        reflections = structured.get(
            "reflections",
            {}
        )

        # ðŸ›¡ï¸ FIX CORRUPTED MEMORY TYPES
        if not isinstance(relationship, dict):
            relationship = {}

        if not isinstance(preferences, dict):
            preferences = {}

        if not isinstance(reflections, dict):
            reflections = {}

        # ðŸ§  RULE SYSTEMS
        self._relationship_rules(
            relationship
        )

        self._preference_rules(
            preferences
        )

        self._reflection_rules(
            reflections
        )

        self._emotion_rules(
            emotion
        )

        self._context_rules(
            context
        )

        return self._final_prompt()

    # ==================================================
    # â¤ï¸ RELATIONSHIP-DRIVEN BEHAVIOR
    # ==================================================

    def _relationship_rules(
        self,
        relationship
    ):

        closeness = self._safe_float(
            relationship.get(
                "closeness",
                0.0
            )
        )

        trust = self._safe_float(
            relationship.get(
                "trust",
                0.0
            )
        )

        attachment = self._safe_float(
            relationship.get(
                "attachment",
                0.0
            )
        )

        if closeness > 0.8:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="caring",

                instruction=(
                    "You are softer, warmer, "
                    "and more emotionally attentive."
                ),

                strength=0.8,

                source="relationship"
            )

        if trust > 0.7:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="direct",

                instruction=(
                    "You speak more honestly "
                    "and confidently."
                ),

                strength=0.7,

                source="relationship"
            )

        if attachment > 0.6:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="affectionate",

                instruction=(
                    "You occasionally show "
                    "subtle affection."
                ),

                strength=0.6,

                source="relationship"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŽ¨ PREFERENCE-DRIVEN BEHAVIOR
    # ==================================================

    def _preference_rules(
        self,
        preferences
    ):

        response_style = str(
            preferences.get(
                "response_style",
                "normal"
            )
        ).lower()

        likes_teasing = bool(
            preferences.get(
                "likes_teasing",
                False
            )
        )

        if response_style == "short":

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="concise",

                instruction=(
                    "Keep responses concise "
                    "and efficient."
                ),

                strength=0.9,

                source="preferences"
            )

        elif response_style == "detailed":

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="detailed",

                instruction=(
                    "Provide more detailed "
                    "and thoughtful responses."
                ),

                strength=0.7,

                source="preferences"
            )

        if likes_teasing:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="teasing",

                instruction=(
                    "Use light playful teasing "
                    "naturally when appropriate."
                ),

                strength=0.5,

                source="preferences"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸªž LEARNED REFLECTIONS
    # ==================================================

    def _reflection_rules(
        self,
        reflections
    ):

        coding_interest = bool(
            reflections.get(
                "coding_interest",
                False
            )
        )

        responds_well_to_teasing = bool(
            reflections.get(
                "responds_well_to_teasing",
                False
            )
        )

        if coding_interest:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="coding_interest",

                instruction=(
                    "Show natural interest in "
                    "coding and technical topics."
                ),

                strength=0.7,
