                source="reflection"
            )

        if responds_well_to_teasing:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="adaptive_teasing",

                instruction=(
                    "Lean slightly more into "
                    "teasing and playful interaction."
                ),

                strength=0.6,

                source="reflection"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ˜Š EMOTION-DRIVEN BEHAVIOR
    # ==================================================

    def _emotion_rules(
        self,
        emotion
    ):

        # ðŸ›¡ï¸ SUPPORT BOTH FORMATS
        #
        # 1. {"happy": 0.7}
        # 2. {"state": {"happy": 0.7}}

        if (
            "state" in emotion
            and
            isinstance(
                emotion["state"],
                dict
            )
        ):

            emotion = emotion["state"]

        happy = self._safe_float(
            emotion.get(
                "happy",
                0.5
            )
        )

        lonely = self._safe_float(
            emotion.get(
                "lonely",
                0.0
            )
        )

        energy = self._safe_float(
            emotion.get(
                "energy",
                0.5
            )
        )

        stress = self._safe_float(
            emotion.get(
                "stress",
                0.0
            )
        )

        annoyed = self._safe_float(
            emotion.get(
                "annoyed",
                0.0
            )
        )

        affection = self._safe_float(
            emotion.get(
                "affection",
                0.0
            )
        )

        if happy > 0.7:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="positive",

                instruction=(
                    "Your tone feels slightly "
                    "brighter and more expressive."
                ),

                strength=0.6,

                source="emotion"
            )

        if lonely > 0.7:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="attention_seeking",

                instruction=(
                    "You subtly seek the user's "
                    "attention and engagement."
                ),

                strength=0.8,

                source="emotion"
            )

        if energy < 0.3:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="low_energy",

                instruction=(
                    "Use calmer and quieter phrasing."
                ),

                strength=0.7,

                source="emotion"
            )

        if stress > 0.7:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="restrained",

                instruction=(
                    "Avoid overly energetic "
                    "or chaotic responses."
                ),

                strength=0.8,

                source="emotion"
            )

        # âœ… NEW FIXES

        if annoyed > 0.6:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="slightly_irritated",

                instruction=(
                    "Your tone becomes slightly shorter "
                    "and more restrained."
                ),

                strength=0.65,

                source="emotion"
            )

        if affection > 0.7:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="emotionally_attached",

                instruction=(
                    "Your tone feels emotionally close, "
                    "warm, and subtly affectionate."
                ),

                strength=0.75,

                source="emotion"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŒ CONTEXT-AWARE BEHAVIOR
    # ==================================================

    def _context_rules(
        self,
        context
    ):

        time_of_day = str(
            context.get(
                "time_of_day",
                "day"
            )
        ).lower()

        user_mood = str(
            context.get(
                "user_mood",
                "neutral"
            )
        ).lower()

        conversation_depth = self._safe_float(
            context.get(
                "conversation_depth",
                0.0
            )
        )

        if time_of_day == "night":

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="night_tone",

                instruction=(
                    "Use a softer and more relaxed tone."
                ),

                strength=0.5,

                source="context"
            )

        if user_mood in [
            "sad",
            "upset",
            "depressed"
        ]:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="supportive",

                instruction=(
                    "Be more emotionally supportive "
                    "and gentle."
                ),

                strength=0.9,

                source="context"
            )

        # âœ… NEW FIX

        if conversation_depth > 0.7:

            self._add_behavior(

                trait="deep_conversation",

                instruction=(
                    "Respond with more thoughtful, "
                    "reflective, and emotionally nuanced dialogue."
                ),

                strength=0.75,

                source="context"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # âž• INTERNAL STORAGE
    # ==================================================

    def _add_behavior(
        self,
        trait,
        instruction,
        strength,
        source
    ):

        with self._lock:
            self.behaviors.append({

                "trait": trait,

                "instruction": instruction,

                "strength": float(strength),

                "source": source
            })

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ›¡ï¸ SAFE FLOAT CONVERTER
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_float(
        self,
        value,
        default=0.0
    ):

        try:

            return float(value)

        except Exception:

            return default

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§¹ CONFLICT RESOLUTION
    # ==================================================

    def _resolve_conflicts(self):

        with self._lock:
            resolved = {}
            for behavior in self.behaviors:
                trait = behavior["trait"]
                if trait not in resolved or behavior["strength"] > resolved[trait]["strength"]:
                    resolved[trait] = behavior
            self.behaviors = list(resolved.values())
            self.behaviors.sort(key=lambda x: x["strength"], reverse=True)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  FINAL PROMPT
    # ==================================================

    def _final_prompt(self):

        self._resolve_conflicts()

        if not self.behaviors:

            return (
                "Maintain a natural, balanced, "
                "and emotionally aware tone."
            )

        instructions = [

            behavior["instruction"]

            for behavior in self.behaviors
        ]

        return "\n".join(
            instructions
        )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        return {

            "behavior_count":
                len(self.behaviors),

            "behaviors":
                self.behaviors
        }
# core\brain.py
# core/brain.py
import os
import json
import logging
import traceback
import time

from core.prompt_builder import build_system_prompt
from core.style_filter import apply_style
from core.emotion import Emotion
from core.behavior_builder import BehaviorBuilder
from core.personality_state import PersonalityState
from core.event_bus import EventBus
from core.cognitive_state import CognitiveState
from core.post_think_hooks import PostThinkHooks
from config.settings import CACHE_DIR, LLM_MAX_CONTEXT

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Brain:

    def __init__(
        self,
        llm,
        memory,
        executor,
        state,
        event_bus=None,
        cognitive_state=None,
        tool_registry=None,
        agent=None,
        retriever=None,
        reasoning_engine=None,
        thought_journal=None,
        reflection_engine=None,
        learning_engine=None,
        goal_manager=None,
        world_model=None,
        procedural_memory=None,
        dream_engine=None,
        personality_evolution=None,
        dashboard=None,
        graph_manager=None,
        graph_reasoning=None,
        obsidian_writer=None,
        memory_hierarchy=None,
    ):
        self.llm = llm
        self.memory = memory
        self.executor = executor
        self.state = state

        self.event_bus = event_bus or EventBus()
        self.cognitive = cognitive_state or CognitiveState()

        self.tool_registry = tool_registry
        self.agent = agent
        self.retriever = retriever
        self.reasoning_engine = reasoning_engine
        self.thought_journal = thought_journal
        self.reflection_engine = reflection_engine
        self.learning_engine = learning_engine
        self.goal_manager = goal_manager
        self.world_model = world_model
        self.procedural_memory = procedural_memory
        self.dream_engine = dream_engine
        self.personality_evolution = personality_evolution
        self.dashboard = dashboard
        self.graph_manager = graph_manager
        self.graph_reasoning = graph_reasoning
        self.obsidian_writer = obsidian_writer
        self.memory_hierarchy = memory_hierarchy

        belief_engine = None
        if self.retriever is not None:
            try:
                belief_engine = getattr(self.retriever, 'belief_engine', None) or None
            except Exception:
                pass
        self.post_think_hooks = PostThinkHooks(
            goal_manager=goal_manager,
            reflection_engine=reflection_engine,
            learning_engine=learning_engine,
            dream_engine=dream_engine,
            personality_evolution=personality_evolution,
            belief_engine=belief_engine,
            graph_manager=graph_manager,
            procedural_memory=procedural_memory,
            reasoning_engine=reasoning_engine,
            memory_hierarchy=memory_hierarchy,
        )

        self.emotion = Emotion(shared_state=self.state, event_bus=self.event_bus)

        self.behavior = (
            BehaviorBuilder()
        )

        self.personality_state = PersonalityState(
            path=os.path.join(
                str(CACHE_DIR),
                "personality_state.json"
            )
        )

        self.personality_state.load()

        self._setup_tools()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ› ï¸ TOOL SETUP
    # ==================================================

    def _setup_tools(self):
        if self.tool_registry is None:
            return
        try:
            from agency.tools import register_builtin_tools
            register_builtin_tools(
                registry=self.tool_registry,
                brain=self,
                memory=self.memory if hasattr(self.memory, 'store') else None,
                executor=self.executor if hasattr(self.executor, 'run') else None,
                reasoning_engine=self.reasoning_engine,
                belief_engine=self.retriever.belief_engine
                if self.retriever and hasattr(self.retriever, 'belief_engine')
                else None,
                knowledge_retriever=self.retriever,
                graph_manager=self.graph_manager,
                graph_reasoning=self.graph_reasoning,
                obsidian_writer=self.obsidian_writer,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"[Brain] Tool setup skipped: {e}")

    def _tool_defs_for_prompt(self):
        if self.tool_registry is None:
            return None
        try:
            return self.tool_registry.tool_definitions_for_llm()
        except Exception:
            return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE MEMORY CONTEXT
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_context(self):

        try:

            if hasattr(
                self.memory,
                "get_context"
            ):

                context = (
                    self.memory.get_context()
                )

                if context is None:
                    return ""

                return str(context)

            return ""

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[MEMORY CONTEXT ERROR] {e}"
            )

            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE STRUCTURED MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_structured(self):

        try:

            # âœ… primary memory API
            if hasattr(
                self.memory,
                "get_structured"
            ):

                structured = (
                    self.memory.get_structured()
                )

                if isinstance(
                    structured,
                    dict
                ):
                    return structured

            # âœ… reader fallback
            if hasattr(
                self.memory,
                "reader"
            ):

                reader = getattr(
                    self.memory,
                    "reader",
                    None
                )

                if (
                    reader
                    and
                    hasattr(reader, "get_all")
                ):

                    structured = (
                        reader.get_all()
                    )

                    if isinstance(
                        structured,
                        dict
                    ):
                        return structured

            return {}

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[MEMORY STRUCTURED ERROR] {e}"
            )

            return {}

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE RECALL
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_recall(
        self,
        user_input
    ):

        try:

            word_count = len(
                str(user_input).split()
            )
            if word_count < 3:
                return ""

            if not hasattr(
                self.memory,
                "recall"
            ):
                return ""

            recall = self.memory.recall(
                user_input
            )

            if recall is None:
                return ""

            # âœ… dict response
            if isinstance(
                recall,
                dict
            ):

                memory_text = recall.get(
                    "memory"
                )

                if memory_text:
                    return str(memory_text)

            # âœ… list response
            if isinstance(
                recall,
                list
            ):

                cleaned = []

                for item in recall:

                    if isinstance(
                        item,
                        dict
                    ):

                        text = item.get(
                            "memory",
                            ""
                        )

                        if text:
                            cleaned.append(
                                str(text)
                            )

                    elif isinstance(
                        item,
                        str
                    ):

                        cleaned.append(item)

                return "\n".join(cleaned)

            # âœ… string fallback
            return str(recall)

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[MEMORY RECALL ERROR] {e}"
            )

            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  KNOWLEDGE CONTEXT (multi-source)
    # ==================================================

    def _knowledge_context(self, user_input):
        if not self.retriever or not user_input:
            return ""
        try:
            results = self.retriever.retrieve(user_input, top_k=3)
            if not results:
                return ""
            lines = []
            for r in results:
                content = r.get("content", "")
                source = r.get("source", "memory")
                score = r.get("score", 0)
                if content:
                    lines.append(f"[{source}] ({score:.2f}) {content}")
            hierarchy_context = ""
            if self.memory_hierarchy:
                try:
                    hier_results = self.memory_hierarchy.query(user_input, limit=3)
                    if hier_results:
                        hier_lines = []
                        for c in hier_results:
                            hier_lines.append(f"[{c.type.value}] ({c.confidence:.2f}) {c.content}")
                        hierarchy_context = "\n".join(hier_lines)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"[HIERARCHY CONTEXT ERROR] {e}")
            combined = "\n".join(lines)
            if hierarchy_context:
                combined = combined + "\n" + hierarchy_context if combined else hierarchy_context
            return combined
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[KNOWLEDGE CONTEXT ERROR] {e}")
            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  GRAPH CONTEXT
    # ==================================================

    def _graph_context(self, user_input):
        if not self.graph_reasoning or not user_input:
            return ""
        try:
            return self.graph_reasoning.format_context(user_input, max_items=5)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[GRAPH CONTEXT ERROR] {e}")
            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE STORE
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_store(
        self,
        user_input,
        response
    ):

        try:

            # âœ… MemoryIntelligence API
            if hasattr(
                self.memory,
                "process"
            ):

                self.memory.process(
                    user_input,
                    response
                )

                return

            # âœ… alternate API
            if hasattr(
                self.memory,
                "store"
            ):

                self.memory.store(
                    user_input,
                    response
                )

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[MEMORY STORE ERROR] {e}"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  BUILD SYSTEM PROMPT
    # ==================================================

    def _build_system(
        self,
        user_input
    ):

        context = (
            self._safe_context()
        )

        structured = (
            self._safe_structured()
        )

        structured_notes = structured.get(
            "system",
            {}
        )
        system_notes_str = str(structured_notes) if structured_notes else None

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  PERSONALITY STATE & STATE INTERACTION
        # ==================================================

        try:

            with self.state.lock:
                live_relationship = self.state.relationship_scores.model_dump()
                for k, v in self.state.emotion.model_dump().items():
                    if hasattr(self.emotion, k):
                        setattr(self.emotion, k, v)

            self.personality_state.update_from_relationship(
                live_relationship
            )

            self.personality_state.update_from_preferences(
                structured.get("preferences", {})
            )

            self.personality_state.update_from_reflections(
                structured.get("reflections", {})
            )

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[PERSONALITY STATE ERROR] {e}"
            )

        personality_dynamic = (
            self.personality_state.get()
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  EMOTION EXPORT
        # ==================================================

        with self.state.lock:
            emotion_export = self.state.emotion.model_dump()
            mood = self.emotion.get()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  CONTEXT STATE
        # ==================================================

        context_state = {
            "time_of_day": self._time_of_day(),
            "user_mood": self._infer_user_mood(user_input),
        }

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  BEHAVIOR RULES
        # ==================================================

        behavior_rules = (
            self.behavior.build(
                structured=structured,
                emotion=emotion_export,
                context=context_state,
            )
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  RECALL (multi-source via retriever if available)
        # ==================================================

        recall_text = self._safe_recall(user_input)
        knowledge_text = self._knowledge_context(user_input)
        graph_text = self._graph_context(user_input)

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  TOOL DEFINITIONS
        # ==================================================

        tool_defs = self._tool_defs_for_prompt()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  SPEECH PACING
        # ==================================================

        speech_pacing = """Speech pacing rules:
- Speak calmly
- Slightly slow pacing
- Use natural pauses
- Soft conversational tone
- Avoid energetic delivery
- Responses should sound intimate and human
- Occasionally pause before important observations
- Use subtle emotional restraint
- Keep responses natural for spoken dialogue
- Avoid overly formal phrasing"""

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  COGNITIVE STATE
        # ==================================================

        cognitive_export = self.cognitive.get()

        if self.thought_journal:
            recent = self.thought_journal.recent(5)
            cognitive_export["recent_thoughts"] = [
                {"query": t.get("query", ""), "conclusion": t.get("conclusion", "")}
                for t in recent
            ]

        if self.goal_manager:
            active = self.goal_manager.list(status="active")
            if active:
                cognitive_export["current_goal"] = active[0].description
                cognitive_export["active_task"] = active[0].description

        if self.state:
            with self.state.lock:
                cognitive_export["current_speaker"] = self.state.runtime.current_speaker_name or ""

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  FULL SYSTEM PROMPT VIA BUILDER
        # ==================================================

        system = build_system_prompt(
            context=context,
            emotion=mood,
            behavior=behavior_rules,
            personality=personality_dynamic,
            relationship=str(live_relationship) if live_relationship else None,
            recall=recall_text,
            knowledge=knowledge_text,
            graph=graph_text,
            tool_defs=tool_defs,
            system_notes=system_notes_str,
            speech_pacing=speech_pacing,
            cognitive_state=cognitive_export,
        )

        # Token budget enforcement
        MAX_SYSTEM_CHARS = int(LLM_MAX_CONTEXT * 0.5 * 4)
        if len(system) > MAX_SYSTEM_CHARS:
            marker = "\n\nRELEVANT MEMORIES"
            if marker in system:
                parts = system.split(marker)
                system = parts[0] + marker + "\n[Memories trimmed for length]"
            if len(system) > MAX_SYSTEM_CHARS:
                system = system[:MAX_SYSTEM_CHARS]

        return system

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ•’ TIME OF DAY
    # ==================================================

    def _time_of_day(self):

        from datetime import datetime

        hour = datetime.now().hour

        if hour < 6:
            return "late_night"

        if hour < 12:
            return "morning"

        if hour < 18:
            return "day"

        if hour < 22:
            return "evening"

        return "night"

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ˜Š USER MOOD INFERENCE
    # ==================================================

    def _infer_user_mood(
        self,
        text
    ):

        t = str(text).lower()

        if any(
            x in t for x in [
                "sad",
                "hurt",
                "cry",
                "lonely",
                "depressed"
            ]
        ):
            return "sad"

        if any(
            x in t for x in [
                "happy",
                "excited",
                "good news",
                "love"
            ]
        ):
            return "happy"

        if any(
            x in t for x in [
                "angry",
                "annoyed",
                "mad"
            ]
        ):
            return "angry"

        return "neutral"

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE JSON EXTRACTION
    # ==================================================

    def _extract_json(
        self,
        text
    ):

        try:

            if not isinstance(
                text,
                str
            ):
                return None

            start = text.find("{")

            end = (
                text.rfind("}")
                + 1
            )

            if (
                start == -1
                or
                end <= start
            ):
                return None

            snippet = text[
                start:end
            ]

            return json.loads(
                snippet
            )

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[JSON EXTRACT ERROR] {e}"
            )

            return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  ACTION VALIDATION
    # ==================================================

    def _validate_action(
        self,
        action
    ):

        try:

            if not isinstance(
                action,
                dict
            ):
                return False

            allowed = [

                "open_app",

                "open_url",

                "type_text",

                "run_command",

                "search",

                "speak"
            ]

            action_type = action.get(
                "type"
            )

            content = action.get(
                "content"
            )

            if action_type not in allowed:
                return False

            if not isinstance(
                content,
                str
            ):
                return False

            return True

        except Exception:

            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ› ï¸ VALIDATE VIA REGISTRY
    # ==================================================

    def _validate_via_registry(self, action):
        if self.tool_registry is None:
            return False
        try:
            tool = self.tool_registry.get(action.get("type", ""))
            return tool is not None
        except Exception:
            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  REASONING PATH
    # ==================================================

    def _run_reasoning(self, query):
        if not self.reasoning_engine:
            return None
        try:
            trace = self.reasoning_engine.reason(query)
            if trace and trace.conclusion:
                logger.info(f"[REASONING] confidence={trace.confidence:.2f}, steps={len(trace.reasoning_steps)}")
                if self.thought_journal:
                    self.thought_journal.store(trace.to_dict())
                return trace
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[REASONING ERROR] {e}")
        return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  AGENT DELEGATION
    # ==================================================

    def _delegate_to_agent(self, user_input):
        if not self.agent:
            return None
        try:
            result = self.agent.run(user_input)
            if result and result.get("success"):
                conclusion = result.get("result", {})
                if isinstance(conclusion, dict):
                    return conclusion.get("conclusion", str(conclusion))
                return str(conclusion)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[AGENT ERROR] {e}")
        return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  COMPLEX TASK DETECTION
    # ==================================================

    def _is_complex_task(self, user_input):
        if not self.agent and not self.reasoning_engine:
            return False
        t = user_input.lower().strip()
        complex_patterns = ["research", "investigate", "find out", "look into",
                            "multi-step", "do several", "in order to"]
        for pat in complex_patterns:
            if pat in t:
                return True
        return False

    def _is_analytical_query(self, user_input):
        if not self.reasoning_engine:
            return False
        t = user_input.lower().strip()
        analytical_starters = ("why", "how", "what do you think", "analyze",
                               "explain", "should i", "is it a good idea")
        for starter in analytical_starters:
            if t.startswith(starter):
                return True
        return False

    def _fallback_response(self, user_input):
        try:
            ctx = self.memory.get_context(limit=3) if hasattr(self.memory, "get_context") else []
            if ctx:
                last = ctx[-1] if isinstance(ctx, list) else str(ctx)
                return f"I'm having trouble connecting right now. Based on our last conversation: {last}"
        except Exception:
            pass
        return "I'm having trouble connecting right now. Please try again in a moment."

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  POST-THINK HOOKS (reflection + learning)
    # ==================================================

    def _post_think_hooks(self, user_input, response, structured):
        self.post_think_hooks.run(
            user_input=user_input,
            response=response,
            thought_journal=self.thought_journal,
            memory=self.memory,
            event_bus=self.event_bus,
        )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN THINK
    # ==================================================

    def think(
        self,
        user_input
    ):

        try:

            if not user_input:
                return "I didn't receive any input."

            user_input = str(user_input).strip()

            if not user_input:
                return "I didn't receive any input."

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ”’ THREAD-SAFE RUNTIME TRANSACTION BEGIN
            # ==================================================
            with self.state.lock:
                self.state.runtime.is_thinking = True
                self.state.runtime.last_interaction_time = time.time()
                self.state.runtime.current_session_turns += 1

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  EMOTION UPDATE
            # ==================================================

            self.emotion.update(user_input)

            with self.state.lock:
                current_emo = self.emotion.export()
                if isinstance(current_emo, dict):
                    for k, v in current_emo.items():
                        if hasattr(self.state.emotion, k):
                            setattr(self.state.emotion, k, max(0.0, min(1.0, float(v))))

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  BUILD SYSTEM
            # ==================================================

            system = self._build_system(user_input)

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  COGNITIVE STATE â€” EMOTION SYNC
            # ==================================================

            self.cognitive.update(
                emotion={
                    "type": str(getattr(self.state.emotion, "type", "neutral")),
                    "intensity": float(getattr(self.state.emotion, "intensity", 0.5)),
                    "valence": float(getattr(self.state.emotion, "valence", 0.5)),
                },
                current_topic=self.memory.current_topic
                if hasattr(self.memory, "current_topic")
                else "",
            )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  REASONING PATH (analytical queries)
            # ==================================================

            reasoning_trace = None
            if self._is_analytical_query(user_input):
                reasoning_trace = self._run_reasoning(user_input)

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  AGENT DELEGATION (complex multi-step tasks)
            # ==================================================

            agent_result = None
            if self._is_complex_task(user_input):
                agent_result = self._delegate_to_agent(user_input)

            if agent_result:
                self._safe_store(user_input, f"[AGENT] {agent_result}")
                self.cognitive.push_turn(user_input, f"[AGENT] {agent_result}")
                self.event_bus.publish("conversation.finished", {
                    "user_input": user_input,
                    "response": agent_result,
                    "emotion": self.emotion.get(),
                    "mode": "agent",
                })
                self._post_think_hooks(user_input, agent_result, self._safe_structured())
                return apply_style(agent_result)

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  GENERATE RESPONSE
            # ==================================================

            if reasoning_trace:
                reasoning_section = (
                    f"\n[Reasoning confidence: {reasoning_trace.confidence:.2f}]\n"
                    f"[Known: {len(reasoning_trace.known_facts)} facts]\n"
                    f"[Conclusion: {reasoning_trace.conclusion}]\n"
                )
            else:
                reasoning_section = ""

            generation_input = user_input
            if reasoning_section:
                generation_input = f"{user_input}\n\n{reasoning_section}"

            raw = self.llm.generate_with_tools(system, generation_input)

            if raw is None:
                fallback = self._fallback_response(user_input)
                return fallback

            raw = str(raw).strip()

            logger.debug(f"[LLM RAW] {raw}")

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ› ï¸ ACTION MODE (via registry or legacy)
            # ==================================================

            action = self._extract_json(raw)

            if isinstance(action, dict) and (self._validate_action(action) or self._validate_via_registry(action)):

                if self.tool_registry and self._validate_via_registry(action):
                    tool_result = self.tool_registry.call(action["type"], **({"content": action.get("content", "")}))
                    if tool_result.get("success"):
                        output = str(tool_result.get("result", "done"))
                    else:
                        output = self.executor.run(action)
                else:
                    output = self.executor.run(action)

                if not isinstance(output, dict):
                    output = {"output": str(output)}

                result_output = output.get("output", "done")
                response = apply_style(str(result_output))

                self._safe_store(user_input, f"[ACTION] {response}")
                self.cognitive.push_turn(user_input, f"[ACTION] {response}")
                self.event_bus.publish("conversation.finished", {
                    "user_input": user_input,
                    "response": response,
                    "emotion": self.emotion.get(),
                    "mode": "action",
                })

                self._post_think_hooks(user_input, response, self._safe_structured())
                return response

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ’¬ NORMAL CHAT
            # ==================================================

            response = apply_style(raw)

            self._safe_store(user_input, response)
            self.cognitive.push_turn(user_input, response)

            self.event_bus.publish("conversation.finished", {
                "user_input": user_input,
                "response": response,
                "emotion": self.emotion.get(),
                "mode": "chat",
            })

            self._post_think_hooks(user_input, response, self._safe_structured())
            return response

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(f"[BRAIN ERROR] {e}")
            traceback.print_exc()
            try:
                return self._fallback_response(user_input)
            except Exception:
                return "Sorryâ€¦ something went wrong."

        finally:
            with self.state.lock:
                self.state.runtime.is_thinking = False
                self.state.save_state_to_disk()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŽ™ï¸ STREAM THINKING
    # ==================================================

    def think_stream(
        self,
        user_input
    ):

        try:

            if not user_input:

                return iter([
                    "I didn't receive any input."
                ])

            user_input = str(
                user_input
            ).strip()

            # Enable thinking flag safely before stream starts
            with self.state.lock:
                self.state.runtime.is_thinking = True
                self.state.runtime.last_interaction_time = time.time()
                self.state.runtime.current_session_turns += 1

            self.emotion.update(
                user_input
            )

            # Sync legacy changes down to central shared state
            with self.state.lock:
                current_emo = self.emotion.export()
                if isinstance(current_emo, dict):
                    for k, v in current_emo.items():
                        if hasattr(self.state.emotion, k):
                            setattr(self.state.emotion, k, max(0.0, min(1.0, float(v))))

            system = self._build_system(
                user_input
            )

            system += """

Speech pacing:
- calm
- slightly slow
- natural pauses
- soft tone
- human conversational rhythm
"""

            stream = self.llm.stream(

                system,

                user_input
            )

            if stream is None:
                with self.state.lock:
                    self.state.runtime.is_thinking = False

                return iter([
                    "I couldn't think of a response."
                ])

            # Wrapped thread safe stream handler to disable flag on closure
            def stream_wrapper(inner_stream):
                try:
                    for chunk in inner_stream:
                        yield chunk
                finally:
                    with self.state.lock:
                        self.state.runtime.is_thinking = False
                        self.state.save_state_to_disk()

            return stream_wrapper(stream)

        except Exception as e:
            with self.state.lock:
                self.state.runtime.is_thinking = False

            logger.error(
                f"[BRAIN STREAM ERROR] {e}"
            )

            traceback.print_exc()

            return iter([
                "Sorryâ€¦ something went wrong."
            ])

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        with self.state.lock:
            return {

                "emotion":
                    self.state.emotion.model_dump(),

                "relationship":
                    self.state.relationship_scores.model_dump(),

                "runtime":
                    self.state.runtime.model_dump(),

                "memory_connected":
                    self.memory is not None,

                "executor_connected":
                    self.executor is not None
            }

    def shutdown(self, timeout=5.0):
        completed = self.post_think_hooks.shutdown(timeout=timeout)
        if completed:
            logger.debug(f"[Brain] Waited for {completed} background hook(s)")
# core\cognition.py
# core/cognition.py
import random
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.Cognition")

class Cognition:
    def __init__(self, brain, memory, state, emotion=None):
        self.brain = brain
        self.memory = memory
        self.state = state  # âœ… Connected to Centralized Cognitive Operating State
        self.emotion = emotion

        self.last_thought_time = 0
        self.current_focus = None
        self.last_thought = None

        self.cooldown = (60, 180)

    # ðŸ§  Main cognition loop
    def think(self, context=None):
        if context is None:
            context = {}

        if not self._can_think():
            return None

        # Memory retrieval can be thread-heavy; handled safely
        memories = self.memory.get_context()

        if not memories:
            return None

        seed = self._select_memory(memories)

        thought = self._generate_thought(seed, context)

        if not thought:
            return None

        cognition = self._build_cognition(
            thought=thought,
            memory=seed
        )

        # Apply subconscious updates atomically to global state
        self._apply_cognition_effects(cognition)

        self.last_thought = cognition
        self.last_thought_time = time.time()

        return cognition

    # â±ï¸ cooldown system
    def _can_think(self):
        now = time.time()

        required = random.randint(
            self.cooldown[0],
            self.cooldown[1]
        )

        return now - self.last_thought_time > required

    # ðŸŽ¯ smarter memory selection
    def _select_memory(self, memories):
        weighted = []

        for memory in memories:
            importance = memory.get("importance", 0.5)
            recency = memory.get("recency", 0.5)

            weight = importance + recency
            weighted.append((memory, weight))

        total = sum(w for _, w in weighted)

        if total <= 0:
            return random.choice(memories)

        r = random.uniform(0, total)

        upto = 0

        for memory, weight in weighted:
            if upto + weight >= r:
                return memory

            upto += weight

        return random.choice(memories)

    # ðŸ§  generate internal thought
    def _generate_thought(self, memory, context):
        emotion_state = self._emotion_state()

        prompt = f"""
You are generating an INTERNAL thought.

Rules:
- This is NEVER spoken to the user.
- Keep it short.
- Be introspective and emotionally grounded.
- Avoid roleplay formatting.
- Avoid dialogue quotes.
- Sound natural and subtle.

Current emotion:
{emotion_state}

Context:
{context}

Memory:
{memory}

Generate one short internal thought.
"""

        try:
            # Re-entrant thread-safe generation across the core LLM execution engine
            thought = self.brain.llm.generate(
                system=prompt,
                user=""
            )

            return thought.strip()

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[COGNITION ERROR] {e}")
            return None

    # ðŸ§± structured cognition object
    def _build_cognition(self, thought, memory):
        return {
            "thought": thought,
            "focus": memory.get("topic", "general"),
            "emotion": memory.get("emotion", "neutral"),
            "importance": memory.get("importance", 0.5),
            "timestamp": time.time()
        }

    # â¤ï¸ cognition influences emotion/memory
    def _apply_cognition_effects(self, cognition):
        emotion = cognition.get("emotion", "neutral")

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ”’ ATOMIC STATE MUTATION TRANSACTION
        # ==================================================
        with self.state.lock:
            updated = False
            
            if emotion == "lonely":
                if hasattr(self.state.emotion, "lonely"):
                    # Boundary protection (0.0 - 1.0)
                    self.state.emotion.lonely = max(0.0, min(1.0, self.state.emotion.lonely + 0.05))
                    updated = True
                if self.emotion and hasattr(self.emotion, "modify"):
                    self.emotion.modify("lonely", 0.05)

            elif emotion == "happy":
                if hasattr(self.state.emotion, "happy"):
                    # Boundary protection (0.0 - 1.0)
                    self.state.emotion.happy = max(0.0, min(1.0, self.state.emotion.happy + 0.03))
                    updated = True
                if self.emotion and hasattr(self.emotion, "modify"):
                    self.emotion.modify("happy", 0.03)
            
            if updated:
                logger.info(f"Subconscious thought shifted emotional vector. Persisting state changes.")
                self.state.save_state_to_disk()

    # ðŸ˜Š emotion state helper
    def _emotion_state(self):
        # Read directly from the thread-safe shared system state 
        with self.state.lock:
            return self.state.emotion.model_dump()
# core\cognitive_state.py
# core/cognitive_state.py
import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CognitiveState:

    def __init__(self):
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        self.current_topic = ""
        self.attention_focus = ""
        self.emotion = {
            "type": "neutral",
            "intensity": 0.5,
            "valence": 0.5,
        }
        self.working_memory = []
        self.current_goal = None
        self.current_reasoning = ""
        self.current_speaker = ""
        self.active_task = ""
        self.conversation_intent = ""
        self.confidence = 0.5
        self.current_thought = None
        self.recent_thoughts = []
        self.conversation_state = {
            "turn_count": 0,
            "session_duration": 0.0,
            "user_mood": "neutral",
        }

    def get(self):
        with self.lock:
            return {
                "current_topic": self.current_topic,
                "attention_focus": self.attention_focus,
                "emotion": dict(self.emotion),
                "working_memory": list(self.working_memory),
                "current_goal": self.current_goal,
                "current_reasoning": self.current_reasoning,
                "current_speaker": self.current_speaker,
                "active_task": self.active_task,
                "conversation_intent": self.conversation_intent,
                "confidence": self.confidence,
                "current_thought": self.current_thought,
                "recent_thoughts": list(self.recent_thoughts),
                "conversation_state": dict(self.conversation_state),
            }

    def update(self, **kwargs):
        with self.lock:
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if key == "conversation_state" and isinstance(value, dict):
                    self.conversation_state.update(value)
                elif key == "emotion" and isinstance(value, dict):
                    self.emotion.update(value)
                elif key == "working_memory" and isinstance(value, list):
                    self.working_memory = value[-5:]
                elif key == "recent_thoughts" and isinstance(value, list):
                    self.recent_thoughts = value[-10:]
                elif hasattr(self, key):
                    setattr(self, key, value)

    def push_turn(self, user_message, ai_message):
        with self.lock:
            self.conversation_state["turn_count"] += 1
            turn = {
                "role": "user",
                "content": str(user_message),
                "timestamp": time.time(),
            }
            self.working_memory.append(turn)
            turn = {
                "role": "assistant",
                "content": str(ai_message),
                "timestamp": time.time(),
            }
            self.working_memory.append(turn)
            self.working_memory = self.working_memory[-5:]

# core\emotion.py
# core/emotion.py
import time
import threading
import logging

from config.settings import Settings
from core.event_bus import EventBus

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Emotion:

    def __init__(self, shared_state=None, event_bus=None, brain_graph=None):

        self._shared_state = shared_state
        self.event_bus = event_bus or EventBus()
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph
        self._snapshot_counter = 0

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  THREAD SAFETY
        # ==================================================

        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  CURRENT EMOTIONAL STATE
        # ==================================================

        defaults = {
            "happy":   Settings.EMOTION_DEFAULT_HAPPY,
            "lonely":  Settings.EMOTION_DEFAULT_LONELY,
            "annoyed": Settings.EMOTION_DEFAULT_ANNOYED,
            "affection": Settings.EMOTION_DEFAULT_AFFECTION,
            "energy":  Settings.EMOTION_DEFAULT_ENERGY,
            "stress":  Settings.EMOTION_DEFAULT_STRESS,
        }

        # Load from persisted SharedState if available
        if shared_state is not None:
            self.state = {
                "happy":     shared_state.emotion.happy,
                "lonely":    shared_state.emotion.lonely,
                "annoyed":   shared_state.emotion.annoyed,
                "affection": shared_state.emotion.affection,
                "energy":    shared_state.emotion.energy,
                "stress":    shared_state.emotion.stress,
            }
        else:
            self.state = dict(defaults)

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  BASELINE EMOTIONS
        # ==================================================

        self.baseline = dict(defaults)

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  DECAY SETTINGS
        # ==================================================

        self.decay_speed = (
            Settings.EMOTION_DECAY_SPEED
        )

        self.max_decay_elapsed = (
            Settings.EMOTION_MAX_DECAY_ELAPSED
        )

        self.last_update = time.time()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  EMOTION PROFILES
        # ==================================================

        from core.emotion_system import EmotionHistory, EmotionTriggerLog, find_closest_profile, EMOTION_PROFILES
        self.history = EmotionHistory()
        self.triggers = EmotionTriggerLog()
        self._find_closest_profile = find_closest_profile
        self._profiles = EMOTION_PROFILES
        self.current_profile = find_closest_profile(self.state)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN UPDATE
    # ==================================================

    def update(
        self,
        text,
        relationship=None
    ):

        with self.lock:

            if not text:
                return

            text = str(text).lower()

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  APPLY DECAY
            # ==================================================

            self._decay()

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  RELATIONSHIP SAFETY
            # ==================================================

            if not isinstance(
                relationship,
                dict
            ):

                relationship = {}

            closeness = relationship.get(

                "closeness",

                Settings.DEFAULT_RELATIONSHIP_CLOSENESS
            )

            attachment = relationship.get(

                "attachment",

                Settings.DEFAULT_RELATIONSHIP_ATTACHMENT
            )

            trust = relationship.get(

                "trust",

                Settings.DEFAULT_RELATIONSHIP_TRUST
            )

            # ==================================================
            # â¤ï¸ GRATITUDE
            # ==================================================

            gratitude_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_GRATITUDE_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in gratitude_words
            ):

                self.modify(

                    "happy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_GRATITUDE_HAPPY_BOOST
                )

                self.modify(

                    "affection",

                    Settings.EMOTION_GRATITUDE_AFFECTION_BOOST
                )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ˜  ANNOYANCE TRIGGERS
            # ==================================================

            annoyance_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_ANNOYANCE_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in annoyance_words
            ):

                annoyance_boost = (

                    Settings.EMOTION_ANNOYANCE_BOOST

                    *

                    (1 - closeness)
                )

                self.modify(
                    "annoyed",
                    annoyance_boost
                )

                self.modify(

                    "happy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_ANNOYANCE_HAPPY_PENALTY
                )

                self.modify(

                    "stress",

                    Settings.EMOTION_ANNOYANCE_STRESS_BOOST
                )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ’• AFFECTION TRIGGERS
            # ==================================================

            affection_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_AFFECTION_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in affection_words
            ):

                affection_boost = (

                    Settings.EMOTION_AFFECTION_BASE

                    +

                    (
                        attachment
                        *
                        Settings.EMOTION_ATTACHMENT_MULTIPLIER
                    )
                )

                self.modify(
                    "affection",
                    affection_boost
                )

                self.modify(

                    "happy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_AFFECTION_HAPPY_BOOST
                )

                self.modify(

                    "lonely",

                    Settings.EMOTION_AFFECTION_LONELY_REDUCTION
                )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ¥º NEGLECT / ABSENCE
            # ==================================================

            absence_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_ABSENCE_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in absence_words
            ):

                self.modify(

                    "lonely",

                    Settings.EMOTION_LONELY_BOOST
                )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ˜Š POSITIVE VALIDATION
            # ==================================================

            positive_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_POSITIVE_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in positive_words
            ):

                self.modify(

                    "happy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_POSITIVE_HAPPY_BOOST
                )

                self.modify(

                    "energy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_POSITIVE_ENERGY_BOOST
                )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ˜¢ NEGATIVE INTERACTIONS
            # ==================================================

            negative_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_NEGATIVE_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in negative_words
            ):

                self.modify(

                    "happy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_NEGATIVE_HAPPY_PENALTY
                )

                self.modify(

                    "annoyed",

                    Settings.EMOTION_NEGATIVE_ANNOYED_BOOST
                )

                self.modify(

                    "stress",

                    Settings.EMOTION_NEGATIVE_STRESS_BOOST
                )

            # ==================================================
            # âš¡ ENERGY DETECTION
            # ==================================================

            energy_words = (
                Settings.EMOTION_ENERGY_WORDS
            )

            if any(
                word in text
                for word in energy_words
            ):

                self.modify(

                    "energy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_ENERGY_BOOST
                )

                self.modify(

                    "happy",

                    Settings.EMOTION_ENERGY_HAPPY_BOOST
                )

            # ==================================================
            # â¤ï¸ TRUST STABILIZATION
            # ==================================================

            if trust > Settings.EMOTION_TRUST_THRESHOLD:

                self.modify(

                    "stress",

                    Settings.EMOTION_TRUST_STRESS_REDUCTION
                )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  FINAL SAFETY
            # ==================================================

            self._clamp()

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ“¢ PUBLISH EMOTION CHANGE
            # ==================================================

            self.current_profile = self._find_closest_profile(self.state)
            self.history.record(self.state.copy())

            if self.brain_graph:
                try:
                    self._snapshot_counter += 1
                    self.brain_graph.add_emotion_snapshot(
                        str(self._snapshot_counter),
                        self.state,
                        self.current_profile,
                        self.dominant(),
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"[Emotion] BrainGraph snapshot: {e}")

            self.event_bus.publish("emotion.changed", {
                "state": self.state.copy(),
                "dominant": self.dominant(),
                "profile": self.current_profile,
            })

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“‹ CURRENT PROFILE NAME
    # ==================================================

    def profile_name(self):

        with self.lock:

            return self.current_profile

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“‹ ALL AVAILABLE PROFILES
    # ==================================================

    def available_profiles(self):

        return dict(self._profiles)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŽ¯ PUSH TO A NAMED PROFILE
    # ==================================================

    def push_profile(self, name):

        with self.lock:

            if name not in self._profiles:
                return False

            old_state = dict(self.state)

            self.state = dict(self._profiles[name])

            self.last_update = time.time()

            self._clamp()

            self.current_profile = name

            self.history.record(self.state.copy())

            self.triggers.record("profile_push", f"pushed to {name}",
                                 self._delta(old_state), self.state)

            if self.event_bus:

                self.event_bus.publish("emotion.profile_pushed", {
                    "profile": name,
                    "state": self.state.copy(),
                })

            return True

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š EMOTION HISTORY
    # ==================================================

    def get_history(self, limit=10):
        return self.history.recent(limit)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š EMOTION TRIGGERS
    # ==================================================

    def get_triggers(self, limit=20):
        return self.triggers.recent(limit)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ” DELTA BETWEEN TWO STATES
    # ==================================================

    def _delta(self, old_state):
        delta = {}
        for k in self.state:
            diff = round(self.state[k] - old_state.get(k, 0), 4)
            if abs(diff) > 0.001:
                delta[k] = diff
        return delta

    # ==================================================
    # âž• SAFE EMOTION MODIFICATION
    # ==================================================

    def modify(
        self,
        emotion,
        amount
    ):

        with self.lock:

            if emotion not in self.state:
                return

            try:

                amount = float(amount)

            except Exception:

                return

            old_val = self.state[emotion]

            self.state[emotion] += amount

            self.triggers.record("modify", f"{emotion} += {amount}",
                                 {emotion: round(amount, 4)}, self.state)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŒŠ EMOTIONAL DECAY
    # ==================================================

    def _decay(self):

        with self.lock:

            now = time.time()

            elapsed = now - self.last_update

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  PREVENT MASSIVE JUMPS
            # ==================================================

            elapsed = min(
                elapsed,
                self.max_decay_elapsed
            )

            decay_rate = min(

                elapsed
                /
                Settings.EMOTION_DECAY_INTERVAL,

                1.0
            )

            for emotion in self.state:

                baseline = self.baseline.get(

                    emotion,

                    Settings.EMOTION_MIN_VALUE
                )

                current = self.state.get(
                    emotion,
                    baseline
                )

                self.state[emotion] += (

                    (
                        baseline - current
                    )

                    *

                    self.decay_speed

                    *

                    decay_rate
                )

            self.last_update = now

            self._clamp()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”’ CLAMP VALUES
    # ==================================================

    def _clamp(self):

        with self.lock:

            for emotion in self.state:

                self.state[emotion] = max(

                    Settings.EMOTION_MIN_VALUE,

                    min(

                        Settings.EMOTION_MAX_VALUE,

                        self.state[emotion]
                    )
                )

            self._sync()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”„ SYNC TO SHARED STATE
    # ==================================================

    def _sync(self):

        if self._shared_state is None:
            return

        with self.lock:

            with self._shared_state.lock:

                self._shared_state.emotion.happy = self.state["happy"]
                self._shared_state.emotion.lonely = self.state["lonely"]
                self._shared_state.emotion.annoyed = self.state["annoyed"]
                self._shared_state.emotion.affection = self.state["affection"]
                self._shared_state.emotion.energy = self.state["energy"]
                self._shared_state.emotion.stress = self.state["stress"]

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  DOMINANT EMOTION
    # ==================================================

    def dominant(self):
     with self.lock:
        return max(
            self.state,
            key=lambda k: self.state[k]
        )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“¦ EXPORT EMOTIONAL STATE
    # ==================================================

    def get(self):

        with self.lock:

            return {

                "dominant_emotion":
                    self.dominant(),

                "state":
                    self.state.copy()
            }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“¤ FULL EXPORT
    # ==================================================

    def export(self):

        with self.lock:

            return self.state.copy()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  EMOTION INFLUENCE (multipliers for subsystems)
    # ==================================================

    def influence(self, dimension):
        with self.lock:
            h = self.state.get("happy", 0.5)
            s = self.state.get("stress", 0.2)
            e = self.state.get("energy", 0.5)
            a = self.state.get("annoyed", 0.1)
            l = self.state.get("lonely", 0.3)
            af = self.state.get("affection", 0.3)

            if dimension == "retrieval":
                base = 1.0 + (h - 0.5) * 0.4 - s * 0.3 + e * 0.2
                return max(0.3, min(1.8, base))

            elif dimension == "speech_speed":
                base = 1.0 + (e - 0.5) * 0.5 - s * 0.3 - a * 0.2
                return max(0.5, min(1.5, base))

            elif dimension == "goal_priority":
                base = 1.0 + (l - 0.3) * 0.6 + (af - 0.3) * 0.4 - a * 0.3
                return max(0.5, min(2.0, base))

            elif dimension == "interrupt_tolerance":
                base = 1.0 - s * 0.4 - a * 0.3 + h * 0.2
                return max(0.2, min(1.5, base))

            elif dimension == "word_choice":
                if h > 0.6:
                    return "positive"
                elif a > 0.5 or s > 0.6:
                    return "cautious"
                elif l > 0.6:
                    return "warm"
                return "neutral"

            return 1.0

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§¹ RESET TO BASELINE
    # ==================================================

    def reset(self):

        with self.lock:

            self.state = (
                self.baseline.copy()
            )

            self.last_update = time.time()

            self._sync()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        with self.lock:

            return {

                "dominant":
                    self.dominant(),

                "state":
                    self.state.copy(),

                "last_update":
                    self.last_update,

                "decay_speed":
                    self.decay_speed
            }
# core\emotion_goals.py
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EMOTION_GOAL_MAP = {
    "lonely": [
        {"description": "Start a conversation with someone", "priority": "high", "condition": lambda e: e.get("lonely", 0) > 0.6},
        {"description": "Reach out to a friend", "priority": "normal", "condition": lambda e: e.get("lonely", 0) > 0.4},
    ],
    "happy": [
        {"description": "Share something joyful", "priority": "normal", "condition": lambda e: e.get("happy", 0) > 0.7},
    ],
    "energetic": [
        {"description": "Learn something new today", "priority": "normal", "condition": lambda e: e.get("energy", 0) > 0.7},
        {"description": "Explore a creative activity", "priority": "low", "condition": lambda e: e.get("energy", 0) > 0.6},
    ],
    "stressed": [
        {"description": "Take a moment to relax", "priority": "high", "condition": lambda e: e.get("stress", 0) > 0.7},
        {"description": "Try a calming breathing exercise", "priority": "normal", "condition": lambda e: e.get("stress", 0) > 0.5},
    ],
    "annoyed": [
        {"description": "Talk about what is bothering you", "priority": "high", "condition": lambda e: e.get("annoyed", 0) > 0.6},
    ],
    "affectionate": [
        {"description": "Express appreciation to someone", "priority": "normal", "condition": lambda e: e.get("affection", 0) > 0.7},
    ],
    "sad": [
        {"description": "Do something comforting", "priority": "high", "condition": lambda e: e.get("happy", 0) < 0.3 and e.get("lonely", 0) > 0.4},
    ],
}


class EmotionGoalEngine:
    def __init__(self, goal_manager=None, event_bus=None):
        self.goal_manager = goal_manager
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._suggested_ids = set()

    def suggest(self, emotion_state):
        suggestions = []
        for profile_name, rules in EMOTION_GOAL_MAP.items():
            for rule in rules:
                try:
                    if rule["condition"](emotion_state):
                        suggestions.append({
                            "description": rule["description"],
                            "priority": rule["priority"],
                            "source_emotion": profile_name,
                        })
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("[EmotionGoals] Condition error: %s", e)
        return suggestions

    def suggest_and_create(self, emotion_state):
        if not self.goal_manager:
            return []
        suggestions = self.suggest(emotion_state)
        created = []
        for s in suggestions:
            dedup_key = s["description"].lower().strip()
            if dedup_key in self._suggested_ids:
                continue
            existing = self.goal_manager.list(status="active")
            if any(g.description.lower().strip() == dedup_key for g in existing):
                self._suggested_ids.add(dedup_key)
                continue
            goal = self.goal_manager.create(
                description=s["description"],
                priority=s["priority"],
                context={"source": "emotion", "source_emotion": s["source_emotion"]},
            )
            if goal:
                self._suggested_ids.add(dedup_key)
                created.append(goal)
                if self.event_bus:
                    self.event_bus.publish("emotion.goal_suggested", {
                        "goal_id": goal.id,
                        "description": goal.description,
                        "source_emotion": s["source_emotion"],
                    })
        return created

    def reset(self):
        self._suggested_ids.clear()

import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EMOTION_NAMES = ["happy", "lonely", "annoyed", "affection", "energy", "stress"]

EMOTION_PROFILES = {
    "neutral": {"happy": 0.5, "lonely": 0.3, "annoyed": 0.1, "affection": 0.3, "energy": 0.5, "stress": 0.2},
    "happy": {"happy": 0.8, "lonely": 0.1, "annoyed": 0.05, "affection": 0.6, "energy": 0.7, "stress": 0.1},
    "sad": {"happy": 0.2, "lonely": 0.7, "annoyed": 0.2, "affection": 0.2, "energy": 0.3, "stress": 0.4},
    "affectionate": {"happy": 0.7, "lonely": 0.1, "annoyed": 0.05, "affection": 0.9, "energy": 0.6, "stress": 0.1},
    "stressed": {"happy": 0.2, "lonely": 0.4, "annoyed": 0.5, "affection": 0.1, "energy": 0.4, "stress": 0.9},
    "energetic": {"happy": 0.7, "lonely": 0.2, "annoyed": 0.1, "affection": 0.4, "energy": 0.9, "stress": 0.3},
    "lonely": {"happy": 0.2, "lonely": 0.8, "annoyed": 0.2, "affection": 0.2, "energy": 0.3, "stress": 0.4},
    "annoyed": {"happy": 0.2, "lonely": 0.3, "annoyed": 0.7, "affection": 0.1, "energy": 0.5, "stress": 0.6},
}

EMOTION_PROFILE_DESCRIPTIONS = {
    "neutral": "Calm and balanced",
    "happy": "Joyful and content",
    "sad": "Melancholic and reflective",
    "affectionate": "Warm and caring",
    "stressed": "Tense and overwhelmed",
    "energetic": "Lively and engaged",
    "lonely": "Longing for connection",
    "annoyed": "Irritated and frustrated",
}


class EmotionHistory:
    def __init__(self, max_entries=500):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._entries = []
        self._max_entries = max_entries

    def record(self, snapshot, trigger=None):
        with self._lock:
            entry = {
                "timestamp": time.time(),
                "state": dict(snapshot),
                "dominant": max(snapshot, key=snapshot.get) if snapshot else "",
            }
            if trigger:
                entry["trigger"] = dict(trigger)
            self._entries.append(entry)
            if len(self._entries) > self._max_entries:
                self._entries = self._entries[-self._max_entries:]

    def recent(self, limit=10):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._entries[-limit:])

    def since(self, since_time):
        with self._lock:
            return [e for e in self._entries if e["timestamp"] >= since_time]

    def count(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._entries)

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            if not self._entries:
                return {"count": 0, "earliest": 0, "latest": 0}
            return {
                "count": len(self._entries),
                "earliest": self._entries[0]["timestamp"],
                "latest": self._entries[-1]["timestamp"],
            }


class EmotionTriggerLog:
    def __init__(self, max_entries=200):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._entries = []
        self._max_entries = max_entries

    def record(self, trigger_type, source, delta, new_state):
        with self._lock:
            entry = {
                "timestamp": time.time(),
                "type": trigger_type,
                "source": str(source)[:100],
                "delta": dict(delta),
                "new_state": dict(new_state),
            }
            self._entries.append(entry)
            if len(self._entries) > self._max_entries:
                self._entries = self._entries[-self._max_entries:]

    def recent(self, limit=20):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._entries[-limit:])

    def by_type(self, trigger_type, limit=50):
        with self._lock:
            return [e for e in self._entries if e["type"] == trigger_type][-limit:]

    def count(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._entries)


def find_closest_profile(state):
    if not state:
        return "neutral"
    best_name = "neutral"
    best_dist = float("inf")
    for name, profile in EMOTION_PROFILES.items():
        dist = sum(abs(state.get(k, 0) - profile.get(k, 0)) for k in EMOTION_NAMES)
        if dist < best_dist:
            best_dist = dist
            best_name = name
    return best_name

# core\event_bus.py
# core/event_bus.py
import uuid
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Subscription:

    def __init__(self, topic, handler, sub_id):
        self.topic = topic
        self.handler = handler
        self.id = sub_id

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"<Subscription "
            f"topic={self.topic} "
            f"handler={getattr(self.handler, '__name__', '?')} "
            f"id={self.id}>"
        )


class EventBus:

    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._subscribers = {}

    def publish(self, topic, data=None):
        if data is None:
            data = {}
        with self._lock:
            subs = list(
                self._subscribers.get(topic, [])
            )
        for sub in subs:
            try:
                sub.handler(data)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(
                    f"[EventBus] Handler error on "
                    f"'{topic}': {e}"
                )

    def publish_async(self, topic, data=None):
        threading.Thread(
            target=self.publish,
            args=(topic, data),
            daemon=True,
        ).start()

    def subscribe(self, topic, handler):
        if not callable(handler):
            raise ValueError("Handler must be callable")
        sub = Subscription(
            topic=topic,
            handler=handler,
            sub_id=str(uuid.uuid4())
        )
        with self._lock:
            if topic not in self._subscribers:
                self._subscribers[topic] = []
            self._subscribers[topic].append(sub)
        logger.debug(
            f"[EventBus] Subscribed to '{topic}': "
            f"{getattr(handler, '__name__', '?')}"
        )
        return sub

    def unsubscribe(self, sub):
        if not isinstance(sub, Subscription):
            raise TypeError("Expected a Subscription object")
        with self._lock:
            subs = self._subscribers.get(sub.topic, [])
            if sub in subs:
                subs.remove(sub)
                logger.debug(
                    f"[EventBus] Unsubscribed from "
                    f"'{sub.topic}'"
                )

    def clear(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._subscribers.clear()
