# core\identity\profiles.py
import json
import logging
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.UserProfiles")

_PROFILES_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "profiles"
_profiles_lock = threading.RLock()


def _ensure_dir():
    _PROFILES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def _path(name: str) -> Path:
    safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in " _-" else "_" for c in name)
    return _PROFILES_DIR / f"{safe}.json"


def get_profile(name: str) -> dict:
    _ensure_dir()
    path = _path(name)
    if path.exists():
        try:
            return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("get_profile(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
    return _default_profile(name)


def _default_profile(name: str) -> dict:
    return {
        "name": name,
        "first_seen": time.time(),
        "last_interaction": time.time(),
        "interaction_count": 0,
        "known_facts": [],
        "preferences": {"likes": [], "dislikes": []},
    }


def update_profile(name: str, **kwargs) -> dict:
    with _profiles_lock:
        _ensure_dir()
        profile = get_profile(name)
        profile.update(kwargs)
        profile["last_interaction"] = time.time()
        profile["interaction_count"] = profile.get("interaction_count", 0) + 1
        try:
            path = _path(name)
            path.write_text(json.dumps(profile, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("update_profile(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
        return profile


def add_fact(name: str, fact: str) -> dict:
    with _profiles_lock:
        profile = get_profile(name)
        facts = profile.setdefault("known_facts", [])
        if fact not in facts:
            facts.append(fact)
        return update_profile(name, known_facts=facts)


def add_preference(name: str, category: str, item: str) -> dict:
    with _profiles_lock:
        profile = get_profile(name)
        prefs = profile.setdefault("preferences", {"likes": [], "dislikes": []})
        prefs.setdefault(category, [])
        if item not in prefs[category]:
            prefs[category].append(item)
        return update_profile(name, preferences=prefs)


def list_profiles() -> list[str]:
    _ensure_dir()
    return sorted(
        p.stem for p in _PROFILES_DIR.glob("*.json")
    )


def delete_profile(name: str) -> bool:
    with _profiles_lock:
        path = _path(name)
        if path.exists():
            try:
                path.unlink()
                return True
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("delete_profile(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
        return False


def all_profiles_data() -> list[dict]:
    return [get_profile(name) for name in list_profiles()]

# core\idle_behavior.py
# core/idle_behavior.py
import random
import time


class IdleBehavior:
    def __init__(self, emotion=None):
        self.emotion = emotion

        self.last_idle_time = 0

        self.cooldown_range = (45, 120)

    # ðŸ§  Main idle generation
    def generate(self, cognition, context=None):
        if not cognition:
            return None

        if context is None:
            context = {}

        if not self._can_speak():
            return None

        if not self._passes_filters(cognition):
            return None

        emotion_state = self._emotion_state()

        chance = self._calculate_speak_chance(
            emotion_state,
            context
        )

        if random.random() > chance:
            return None

        message = self._transform_thought(
            cognition,
            emotion_state,
            context
        )

        if not message:
            return None

        self.last_idle_time = time.time()

        return {
            "type": "idle_speech",
            "content": message,
            "source_thought": cognition["thought"],
            "timestamp": time.time()
        }

    # â±ï¸ cooldown system
    def _can_speak(self):
        now = time.time()

        required = random.randint(
            self.cooldown_range[0],
            self.cooldown_range[1]
        )

        return now - self.last_idle_time > required

    # ðŸš« filter weird/repetitive thoughts
    def _passes_filters(self, cognition):
        thought = cognition.get("thought", "").lower()

        blocked = [
            "language model",
            "ai assistant",
            "system prompt",
            "i am thinking"
        ]

        for phrase in blocked:
            if phrase in thought:
                return False

        if len(thought.strip()) < 4:
            return False

        return True

    # ðŸŽ² dynamic speech chance
    def _calculate_speak_chance(
        self,
        emotion,
        context
    ):
        base = 0.15

        lonely = emotion.get("lonely", 0.0)
        affection = emotion.get("affection", 0.0)
        energy = emotion.get("energy", 0.5)

        base += lonely * 0.25
        base += affection * 0.15
        base += energy * 0.10

        if not context.get("user_active", True):
            base -= 0.10

        if context.get("time_of_day") == "night":
            base -= 0.05

        return max(0.05, min(base, 0.7))

    # ðŸ—£ï¸ convert thought â†’ natural speech
    def _transform_thought(
        self,
        cognition,
        emotion,
        context
    ):
        thought = cognition.get("thought", "")

        dominant = max(
            emotion,
            key=emotion.get
        )

        # affectionate tone
        if dominant == "affection":
            return self._soften(thought)

        # lonely tone
        if dominant == "lonely":
            return self._attention_seek(thought)

        # playful tone
        if dominant == "happy":
            return self._playful(thought)

        return self._neutral(thought)

    # ðŸ’• styles
    def _soften(self, thought):
        return f"...{thought}"

    def _attention_seek(self, thought):
        return f"You've been quiet lately. {thought}"

    def _playful(self, thought):
        return f"Heh. {thought}"

    def _neutral(self, thought):
        return thought

    # ðŸ˜Š emotion helper
    def _emotion_state(self):
        if not self.emotion:
            return {
                "happy": 0.5,
                "lonely": 0.0,
                "affection": 0.3,
                "energy": 0.5
            }
# core\os_kernel.py
# core/os_kernel.py
import time
import threading
import logging
from enum import Enum
from typing import Optional

import numpy as np

from voice.stt_whisper import TranscriptionUnavailable

from core.attention import Attention
from core.cognition import Cognition
from core.idle_behavior import IdleBehavior
from core.scheduler import Scheduler
from core.autonomy.engine import Autonomy
from core.wake_generator import WakeGenerator
from core.identity import get_identity as _get_identity
from core.state.shared_state import SharedState
from core.state.relationship import RelationshipEngineV2

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ConversationPhase(str, Enum):
    IDLE = "IDLE"
    WAKE_DETECTED = "WAKE_DETECTED"
    GREETING = "GREETING"
    IDENTIFYING_SPEAKER = "IDENTIFYING_SPEAKER"
    CONFIRMING_SPEAKER = "CONFIRMING_SPEAKER"
    ASKING_NAME = "ASKING_NAME"
    REGISTERING_SPEAKER = "REGISTERING_SPEAKER"
    CONVERSATION = "CONVERSATION"
    PROCESSING_RESPONSE = "PROCESSING_RESPONSE"
    CLEANUP = "CLEANUP"


class AIOSKernel:
    def __init__(
            self,
            brain,
            voice,
            wake,
            stt,
            state: Optional[SharedState] = None,
            relationship_engine: Optional[RelationshipEngineV2] = None,
            speaker_manager=None,
            per_person_relationship=None):
        if state is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "AIOSKernel requires a valid SharedState instance â€” got None."
            )
        if relationship_engine is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "AIOSKernel requires a valid RelationshipEngineV2 instance â€” got None."
            )

        self.brain = brain
        self.voice = voice
        self.wake = wake
        self.stt = stt
        self.relationship_engine = relationship_engine
        self.speaker_manager = speaker_manager
        self.per_person_relationship = per_person_relationship
        self.voice_manager = None

        self.state: SharedState = state

        self.emotion = brain.emotion
        self.attention = Attention()

        self.cognition = Cognition(
            brain,
            brain.memory,
            state=self.state,
            emotion=self.emotion
        )

        self.idle = IdleBehavior(
            emotion=self.emotion
        )

        self.autonomy = Autonomy(
            brain,
            brain.executor,
            brain.memory,
            emotion=self.emotion
        )

        self.scheduler = Scheduler(self)

        self.wake_generator = WakeGenerator(
            identity=_get_identity(),
            personality_state=self.brain.personality_state,
            emotion=self.emotion,
        )

        self.running = True
        self.conversation_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._phase = ConversationPhase.IDLE

    def _set_phase(self, phase: ConversationPhase):
        old = self._phase
        self._phase = phase
        logger.info("STATE: %s â†’ %s", old.value if hasattr(old, 'value') else old, phase.value)

    def _record_identification_audio(
        self, duration: float = 3.0, sample_rate: int = 16000
    ) -> np.ndarray | None:
        try:
            import sounddevice as sd

            logger.info("[Kernel] Recording for speaker identification...")
            audio = sd.rec(
                int(duration * sample_rate),
                samplerate=sample_rate,
                channels=1,
                dtype="float32",
            )
            sd.wait()
            audio = audio.flatten()
            max_val = np.max(np.abs(audio))
            if max_val > 0:
                audio = audio / max_val
            logger.info("[Kernel] Identification recording complete")
            return audio

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("[Kernel] Identification recording failed: %s", exc)
            return None

    def _listen_with_voice_manager(self, wake_required=False):
        if self.voice_manager:
            try:
                event = self.voice_manager.listen(wake_required=wake_required)
                return event.text if event else ""
            except TranscriptionUnavailable as e:
                logger.error("[Kernel] %s", e)
                raise
        return self.stt.listen()

    def _speak_and_wait(self, text):
        self.voice.speak_stream(iter([text]))
        self.voice.wait_until_done()

    def _identify_speaker_phase(self):
        if not self.speaker_manager or not self.speaker_manager.ready:
            logger.info("[Kernel] Speaker manager not available â€” anonymous mode")
            return True

        self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.IDENTIFYING_SPEAKER)
        logger.info("[Kernel] Identifying speaker...")
        ident_audio = self._record_identification_audio()
        if ident_audio is None:
            logger.info("[Kernel] No identification audio â€” proceeding anonymously")
            return True

        profile, confidence = self.speaker_manager.identify_raw(
            ident_audio, sample_rate=16000
        )

        if profile and confidence >= 0.7:
            with self.state.lock:
                self.state.runtime.current_speaker_name = profile.name
                self.state.runtime.current_speaker_id = profile.id
                self.state.runtime.speaker_confidence = confidence
                self.state.runtime.known_user = True
            logger.info("[Kernel] Speaker identified: %s (%.2f)", profile.name, confidence)
            self._speak_and_wait(f"Welcome back, {profile.name}.")
            time.sleep(1.0)
            return True

        try:
            if profile and confidence >= 0.3:
                self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.CONFIRMING_SPEAKER)
                logger.info("[Kernel] Speaker unknown: %s (%.2f) â€” confirming", profile.name, confidence)
                self._speak_and_wait(f"Is that {profile.name}?")

                reply = self._listen_with_voice_manager(wake_required=False)
                if reply and any(w in reply.lower()
                                 for w in ["yes", "yeah", "yep", "correct", "right", "it is", "that's me"]):
                    with self.state.lock:
                        self.state.runtime.current_speaker_name = profile.name
                        self.state.runtime.current_speaker_id = profile.id
                        self.state.runtime.speaker_confidence = confidence
                        self.state.runtime.known_user = True
                    logger.info("[Kernel] Speaker confirmed: %s", profile.name)
                else:
                    logger.info("[Kernel] Speaker confirmation denied â€” proceeding as unknown")
                return True

            # Unknown speaker (< 0.3)
            self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.ASKING_NAME)
            logger.info("[Kernel] Speaker unknown")
            self._speak_and_wait("I don't think we've met before. May I know your name?")

            reply = self._listen_with_voice_manager(wake_required=False)
            if reply and len(reply.strip()) > 1:
                name = reply.strip().title()
                logger.info("[Kernel] User said name: %s", name)

                self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.REGISTERING_SPEAKER)
                logger.info("[Kernel] Registering speaker: %s ...", name)

                new_profile = self.speaker_manager.register_raw(
                    name, ident_audio, sample_rate=16000
                )
                if new_profile:
                    with self.state.lock:
                        self.state.runtime.current_speaker_name = new_profile.name
                        self.state.runtime.current_speaker_id = new_profile.id
                        self.state.runtime.speaker_confidence = 1.0
                        self.state.runtime.known_user = True
                    logger.info("[Kernel] Registration complete: %s", name)
                else:
                    logger.warning("[Kernel] Registration failed for: %s", name)
            else:
                logger.info("[Kernel] No name received â€” proceeding anonymously")

            return True
        except TranscriptionUnavailable as e:
            logger.error("[Kernel] %s â€” proceeding anonymously", e)
            return True

    def _conversation_phase(self):
        self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.CONVERSATION)

        while self.running:
            logger.info("[Kernel] Waiting for user input...")
            try:
                user = self._listen_with_voice_manager(wake_required=False)
            except TranscriptionUnavailable as e:
                logger.error("[Kernel] %s", e)
                return

            if not user:
                logger.info("[Kernel] No speech detected â€” ending conversation")
                return

            user = user.strip()
            if not user:
                continue

            logger.info("[Kernel] User said: %s", user)

            with self.state.lock:
                self.state.runtime.last_user_message = user
                self.state.runtime.last_interaction_time = time.time()

            # Emotion update
            relationship = (
                self.brain.memory
                .get_structured()
                .get("relationship", {})
            )
            if not isinstance(relationship, dict):
                relationship = {}
            try:
                self.emotion.update(user, relationship=relationship)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Emotion Error] %s", e)

            self.voice.stop()

            self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.PROCESSING_RESPONSE)
            logger.info("[Kernel] Thinking...")
            try:
                stream = self.brain.think_stream(user)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Kernel Brain Error] %s", e)
                self._speak_and_wait("Something went wrong.")
                time.sleep(1.0)
                continue

            logger.info("[Kernel] Speaking response...")
            collected_chunks = []

            def collecting_stream(s):
                for chunk in s:
                    if not chunk:
                        continue
                    cleaned = str(chunk).strip()
                    if not cleaned:
                        continue
                    collected_chunks.append(cleaned)
                    yield cleaned

            try:
                if self.voice_manager:
                    self.voice_manager.set_speaking(True, self.voice.interrupt)
                self.voice.speak_stream(collecting_stream(stream))
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Kernel Voice Error] %s", e)
                if self.voice_manager:
                    self.voice_manager.set_speaking(False)
                continue

            self.voice.wait_until_done()
            if self.voice_manager:
                self.voice_manager.set_speaking(False)

            logger.info("[Kernel] Audio cooldown...")
            time.sleep(1.5)

            # Async memory store
            if collected_chunks:
                full_response = " ".join(collected_chunks).strip()
                if full_response and user:
                    with self.state.lock:
                        speaker_name = self.state.runtime.current_speaker_name or "User"

                    def store_memory(u, r, spk):
                        try:
                            self.brain.memory.store(u, r, speaker=spk)
                            logger.info("[Kernel] Memory stored")
                            self.relationship_engine.analyze_and_log_interaction(u, r)
                            if self.per_person_relationship:
                                self.per_person_relationship.update(
                                    spk, familiarity_delta=0.005
                                )
                        except Exception as err:
                            logger.error("[Memory Store Error] %s", err)

                    threading.Thread(
                        target=store_memory,
                        args=(user, full_response, speaker_name),
                        daemon=True
                    ).start()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸš€ MAIN LOOP â€” Deterministic FSM
    # ==================================================
    def run(self):
        logger.info("Say 'wake up Mai'")
        self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.IDLE)
        self.scheduler.start()

        while self.running:
            try:
                with self.state.lock:
                    is_conv_active = self.state.runtime.conversation_active

                if is_conv_active:
                    time.sleep(0.2)
                    continue

                self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.IDLE)

                # ðŸŽ¤ Listen for wake word (only in IDLE)
                if self.voice.is_speaking or self.voice.is_generating:
                    time.sleep(0.2)
                    continue

                triggered = self.wake.listen()

                if not triggered:
                    time.sleep(0.05)
                    continue

                if not self.conversation_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
                    continue

                try:
                    with self.state.lock:
                        self.state.runtime.user_active = True
                        self.state.runtime.conversation_active = True
                        self.state.runtime.known_user = False
                        self.state.runtime.current_speaker_name = ""
                        self.state.runtime.current_speaker_id = ""
                        self.state.runtime.speaker_confidence = 0.0

                    self.voice.stop()
                    logger.info("[Kernel] Wake triggered")
                    self.start_voice_manager()

                    self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.WAKE_DETECTED)

                    # ðŸŒ¸ Greeting
                    self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.GREETING)
                    wake_text = self.wake_generator.generate(context={
                        "identity": _get_identity(),
                        "personality_state": self.brain.personality_state,
                        "emotion": self.emotion.get() if hasattr(self.emotion, "get") else {},
                        "cognitive_state": self.brain.cognitive.get() if hasattr(self.brain, "cognitive") else {},
                        "time_of_day": self.brain._time_of_day() if hasattr(self.brain, "_time_of_day") else "day",
                    })
                    self.voice.speak_stream(iter([wake_text]))
                    self.voice.wait_until_done()
                    time.sleep(1.5)

                    # ðŸŽ¤ Speaker identification
                    self._identify_speaker_phase()

                    # ðŸ’¬ Conversation
                    self._conversation_phase()

                finally:
                    self.stop_voice_manager()
                    self._set_phase(ConversationPhase.CLEANUP)
                    with self.state.lock:
                        self.state.runtime.conversation_active = False
                        self.state.runtime.user_active = False
                    if self.conversation_lock.locked():
                        self.conversation_lock.release()
                    logger.info("[Kernel] Idle â€” wake word detection active")

            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                logger.info("[Kernel] Keyboard interrupt")
                self.shutdown()
                break

            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Kernel Main Loop Error] %s", e)
                time.sleep(1)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”„ BACKGROUND SYSTEMS
    # ==================================================
    def background_tick(self):
        try:
            with self.state.lock:
                is_conv_active = self.state.runtime.conversation_active

            if (
                is_conv_active
                or self.voice.is_speaking
                or self.voice.is_generating
            ):
                return

            self._update_time_context()
            self.attention.update()

            with self.state.lock:
                context = {
                    "user_active": self.state.runtime.user_active,
                    "conversation_active": self.state.runtime.conversation_active,
                    "time_of_day": self.state.runtime.time_of_day,
                    "last_user_message": self.state.runtime.last_user_message,
                    "attention": self.attention.get(),
                }

            if self.attention.get() == "self":
                cognition = self.cognition.think(context)
                if cognition and not self.voice.is_speaking:
                    idle = self.idle.generate(cognition, context=context)
                    if idle and idle.get("content"):
                        self.voice.speak_stream(iter([idle["content"]]))

            auto = self.autonomy.tick(context)
            if auto and not self.voice.is_speaking:
                action = self._process_autonomy(auto)
                if action:
                    self.voice.speak_stream(iter([action]))

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[Background Tick Error] %s", e)

    def _update_time_context(self):
        hour = time.localtime().tm_hour
        with self.state.lock:
            self.state.runtime.time_of_day = "day" if 6 <= hour < 18 else "night"

    def _process_autonomy(self, auto):
        intent = auto.get("intent")
        phrases = {
            "seek_attention": "You've been quiet for a while.",
            "gentle_checkin": "You seem a little distant today.",
            "soft_presence": "...I'm still here.",
            "playful_tease": "You really do like disappearing randomly, hm?",
        }
        return phrases.get(intent)

    def start_voice_manager(self):
        if not self.voice_manager:
            return
        try:
            self.voice_manager.start()
            logger.debug("[Kernel] VoiceManager started")
            # Post-start health validation
            self._validate_voice_pipeline()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[Kernel] VoiceManager start error: %s", e)

    def _validate_voice_pipeline(self):
        vm = self.voice_manager
        if not vm:
            return
        try:
            rec_ok = vm._recorder is not None
            mic_ok = vm._microphone is not None and vm._microphone.is_running
            cb_count = len(vm._microphone._callbacks) if (vm._microphone and hasattr(vm._microphone, '_callbacks')) else 0
            cb_ok = cb_count > 0

            if rec_ok:
                logger.info("[VoicePost] Recorder: initialized âœ“")
            else:
                logger.warning("[VoicePost] Recorder: NOT initialized â€” speech capture will fail")

            if mic_ok:
                logger.info("[VoicePost] InputStream: active âœ“")
            else:
                logger.warning("[VoicePost] InputStream: NOT running â€” mic inactive")

            if cb_ok:
                logger.info("[VoicePost] Callbacks: %d registered âœ“", cb_count)
            else:
                logger.warning("[VoicePost] Callbacks: none â€” audio pipeline disconnected")

        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[VoicePost] Validation error: %s", e)

    def stop_voice_manager(self):
        if self.voice_manager:
            try:
                self.voice_manager.stop()
                logger.debug("[Kernel] VoiceManager stopped")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("[Kernel] VoiceManager stop error: %s", e)

    def shutdown(self):
        logger.info("[Kernel] Shutting down...")
        self.running = False
        self.stop_voice_manager()
        try:
            self.voice.stop()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[Kernel] Voice stop error: %s", e)
        try:
            self.scheduler.stop()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[Kernel] Scheduler stop error: %s", e)
        try:
            if hasattr(self.state, "save_state_to_disk"):
                self.state.save_state_to_disk()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[Kernel] State save error: %s", e)
        try:
            if hasattr(self.brain, "personality_state"):
                self.brain.personality_state.save()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("[Kernel] Personality save error: %s", e)
        logger.info("[Kernel] Shutdown complete")

# core\personality_state.py
# core/personality_state.py
import json
import logging
import os
import threading

from core.identity.personality import PERSONALITY

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.PersonalityState")


# Maps identity personality traits to numeric state baselines
IDENTITY_TRAIT_MAP = {
    "warmth": "Warm but not overly affectionate",
    "teasing": "Naturally witty",
    "directness": "Speaks naturally",
    "playfulness": "Occasionally playful",
    "affection": "Warm but not overly affectionate",
    "patience": "Calm and composed / Emotionally mature",
    "confidence": "Confident but humble",
}


class PersonalityState:

    def __init__(self, path=None):

        self.path = path
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        # ðŸ§  stable modulation traits
        # Baselines are set to match identity/personality.py:
        #   "Calm and composed" â†’ patience 0.7
        #   "Confident but humble" â†’ confidence 0.5
        #   "Warm but not overly affectionate" â†’ warmth 0.5, affection 0.3
        #   "Naturally witty" â†’ teasing 0.5
        #   "Occasionally playful" â†’ playfulness 0.4
        #   "Speaks naturally" â†’ directness 0.5
        #   "Emotionally mature" â†’ patience 0.7, confidence 0.5
        self.state = {
            "warmth": 0.5,
            "teasing": 0.5,
            "directness": 0.5,
            "playfulness": 0.4,
            "affection": 0.3,
            "patience": 0.7,
            "confidence": 0.5,
        }

        # ðŸŽ¯ natural baseline
        self.baseline = self.state.copy()

    # â¤ï¸ relationship influence
    def update_from_relationship(self, relationship):
        with self._lock:
            closeness = relationship.get("closeness", 0.5)
            trust = relationship.get("trust", 0.5)
            attachment = relationship.get("attachment", 0.5)

            self._shift("warmth", closeness * 0.08)
            self._shift("affection", attachment * 0.06)
            self._shift("confidence", trust * 0.05)

            if closeness > 0.7:
                self._shift("teasing", 0.03)
                self._shift("playfulness", 0.04)

    # ðŸŽ¨ user preference influence
    def update_from_preferences(self, preferences):
        with self._lock:
            style = preferences.get("response_style", "normal")
            likes_teasing = preferences.get("likes_teasing", False)

            if style == "short":
                self._shift("directness", 0.08)
            if likes_teasing:
                self._shift("teasing", 0.07)
                self._shift("playfulness", 0.05)

    # ðŸªž learned reflections
    def update_from_reflections(self, reflections):
        with self._lock:
            if reflections.get("responds_well_to_teasing", False):
                self._shift("teasing", 0.05)
            if reflections.get("needs_emotional_support", False):
                self._shift("warmth", 0.06)

    # ðŸ˜Š emotion influence
    def update_from_emotion(self, emotion):
        with self._lock:
            annoyed = emotion.get("annoyed", 0.0)
            happy = emotion.get("happy", 0.5)
            affection = emotion.get("affection", 0.3)
            energy = emotion.get("energy", 0.5)

            self._shift("warmth", affection * 0.03)
            self._shift("playfulness", happy * 0.03)
            self._shift("patience", -annoyed * 0.05)
            self._shift("confidence", energy * 0.02)

    # ðŸŒŠ natural stabilization
    def decay(self):
        with self._lock:
            for trait in self.state:
                baseline = self.baseline[trait]
                current = self.state[trait]
                self.state[trait] += (baseline - current) * 0.02
            self._clamp()

    # âž• gradual trait shifting
    def _shift(self, trait, amount):
        if trait not in self.state:
            return
        self.state[trait] += amount
        self._clamp()

    # ðŸ”’ safe ranges
    def _clamp(self):

        for trait in self.state:

            self.state[trait] = max(
                0.0,
                min(1.0, self.state[trait])
            )

    # ðŸ“¦ raw export
    def get(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in self.state.items()}

    # ðŸ’¾ save to disk
    def save(self):
        if not self.path:
            return
        with self._lock:
            try:
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.path), exist_ok=True)
                with open(self.path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    json.dump({"state": self.state, "baseline": self.baseline}, f, indent=2)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("PersonalityState save error: %s", e)

    def load(self):
        if not self.path or not os.path.exists(self.path):
            return
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with open(self.path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                if isinstance(data.get("state"), dict):
                    self.state.update(data["state"])
                if isinstance(data.get("baseline"), dict):
                    self.baseline.update(data["baseline"])
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("PersonalityState load error: %s", e)

    # ðŸ§  conversational modifiers
    def get_style_modifiers(self):
        with self._lock:
            modifiers = []
            if self.state["warmth"] > 0.7:
                modifiers.append("Speak in a softer and emotionally warmer tone.")
            if self.state["directness"] > 0.7:
                modifiers.append("Keep responses concise and direct.")
            if self.state["teasing"] > 0.7:
                modifiers.append("Use playful teasing naturally.")
            if self.state["playfulness"] > 0.7:
                modifiers.append("Sound more playful and lively.")
            if self.state["patience"] < 0.3:
                modifiers.append("Sound slightly impatient.")
            return modifiers
# core\plugin_system.py
import os
import sys
import time
import logging
import importlib
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class HookRegistry:

    def __init__(self):
        self._hooks = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def register(self, hook_name, callback, priority=0):
        if not callable(callback):
            return False
        with self._lock:
            if hook_name not in self._hooks:
                self._hooks[hook_name] = []
            self._hooks[hook_name].append((priority, callback))
            self._hooks[hook_name].sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
            return True

    def unregister(self, hook_name, callback):
        with self._lock:
            if hook_name not in self._hooks:
                return False
            before = len(self._hooks[hook_name])
            self._hooks[hook_name] = [(p, c) for p, c in self._hooks[hook_name] if c != callback]
            return len(self._hooks[hook_name]) < before

    def run(self, hook_name, *args, **kwargs):
        results = []
        with self._lock:
            callbacks = list(self._hooks.get(hook_name, []))
        for priority, callback in callbacks:
            try:
                result = callback(*args, **kwargs)
                results.append(result)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Hooks] Error in %s.%s: %s", hook_name, getattr(callback, '__name__', '?'), e)
        return results

    def list_hooks(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {k: [(p, c.__name__) for p, c in v] for k, v in self._hooks.items()}


class Plugin:

    def __init__(self, name, version="1.0.0", description=""):
        self.name = name
        self.version = version
        self.description = description
        self.module = None
        self.initialized = False

    def on_load(self, hooks, brain, event_bus):
        pass

    def on_unload(self):
        pass


class PluginLoader:

    def __init__(self, hooks, plugin_dir="plugins"):
        self.hooks = hooks
        self.plugin_dir = plugin_dir
        self._plugins = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def discover(self):
        if not os.path.isdir(self.plugin_dir):
            os.makedirs(self.plugin_dir, exist_ok=True)
            return []
        if self.plugin_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath(self.plugin_dir))
        found = []
        for fn in os.listdir(self.plugin_dir):
            if fn.endswith(".py") and not fn.startswith("_"):
                found.append(fn[:-3])
        return found

    def load(self, name, brain=None, event_bus=None):
        with self._lock:
            if name in self._plugins:
                return self._plugins[name]
        try:
            if self.plugin_dir not in sys.path:
                sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath(self.plugin_dir))
            module = importlib.import_module(name)
            plugin_cls = None
            for attr_name in dir(module):
                attr = getattr(module, attr_name)
                if isinstance(attr, type) and issubclass(attr, Plugin) and attr is not Plugin:
                    plugin_cls = attr
                    break
            if plugin_cls is None:
                logger.warning("[Plugins] No Plugin subclass in %s", name)
                return None
            plugin = plugin_cls()
            plugin.module = module
            try:
                plugin.on_load(self.hooks, brain, event_bus)
                plugin.initialized = True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Plugins] on_load failed for %s: %s", name, e)
            with self._lock:
                self._plugins[name] = plugin
            logger.info("[Plugins] Loaded: %s v%s", plugin.name, plugin.version)
            return plugin
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[Plugins] Failed to load %s: %s", name, e)
            return None

    def unload(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            plugin = self._plugins.pop(name, None)
            if plugin:
                try:
                    plugin.on_unload()
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("[Plugins] unload error %s: %s", name, e)
                return True
            return False

    def list(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {n: {"name": p.name, "version": p.version, "initialized": p.initialized} for n, p in self._plugins.items()}

    def load_all(self, brain=None, event_bus=None):
        names = self.discover()
        results = []
        for name in names:
            plugin = self.load(name, brain, event_bus)
            results.append({"name": name, "success": plugin is not None})
        return results

# core\post_think_hooks.py
import time
import logging
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_MAX_BG_THREADS = 16


class PostThinkHooks:

    def __init__(self, goal_manager=None, reflection_engine=None,
                 learning_engine=None, dream_engine=None,
                 personality_evolution=None, belief_engine=None,
                 graph_manager=None, procedural_memory=None,
                 brain_graph=None, reasoning_engine=None,
                 memory_hierarchy=None):
        self.goal_manager = goal_manager
        self.reflection_engine = reflection_engine
        self.learning_engine = learning_engine
        self.dream_engine = dream_engine
        self.personality_evolution = personality_evolution
        self.belief_engine = belief_engine
        self.graph_manager = graph_manager
        self.procedural_memory = procedural_memory
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph
        self.reasoning_engine = reasoning_engine
        self.memory_hierarchy = memory_hierarchy
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._threads = []
        self._reflection_count = 0
        self._decay_count = 0

    def _prune_finished_threads(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._threads[:] = [t for t in self._threads if t.is_alive()]

    def run(self, user_input, response, thought_journal, memory, event_bus):
        if not any([self.reflection_engine, self.learning_engine,
                    self.goal_manager, self.dream_engine,
                    self.belief_engine, self.graph_manager,
                    self.personality_evolution, self.procedural_memory,
                    self.brain_graph, self.reasoning_engine,
                    self.memory_hierarchy]):
            return

        self._prune_finished_threads()
        with self._lock:
            if len(self._threads) >= _MAX_BG_THREADS:
                logger.debug("[PostThink] Max bg threads reached, skipping")
                return

        def run_bg():
            try:
                with self._lock:
                    rc = self._reflection_count
                if self.goal_manager:
                    self.goal_manager.increment_turns()
                    event_bus.publish("goal.turns_incremented", {})

                reflection = None
                if self.reflection_engine and thought_journal:
                    recent_thoughts = thought_journal.recent(5)
                    reflection = self.reflection_engine.daily_reflection(
                        thoughts=recent_thoughts,
                    )
                    if reflection:
                        with self._lock:
                            self._reflection_count += 1
                        event_bus.publish("reflection.generated", {
                            "type": "daily",
                            "summary": reflection.summary,
                            "topics": reflection.topics,
                            "patterns": reflection.patterns,
                        })
                        if hasattr(memory, "store"):
                            memory.store(
                                user_input,
                                f"[Reflection] {reflection.summary}"
                            )

                        if self.belief_engine:
                            for topic in reflection.topics:
                                self.belief_engine._merge({
                                    "topic": topic,
                                    "predicate": "is",
                                    "object": "frequently discussed",
                                    "confidence": 0.6,
                                })

                        if self.goal_manager and reflection.topics:
                            beliefs = [{"topic": t, "predicate": "is", "object": "frequently discussed"} for t in reflection.topics]
                            self.goal_manager.check_goals(beliefs)

                    if self.procedural_memory and reflection and reflection.patterns:
                        for pattern in reflection.patterns:
                            self.procedural_memory.learn(
                                rule=pattern,
                                context="reflection",
                                confidence=0.5,
                                source="reflection_engine",
                            )

                if self.learning_engine and thought_journal:
                    recent = thought_journal.recent(10)
                    if recent:
                        adjustments = self.learning_engine.adjust_behavior(
                            [t.get("conclusion", "") for t in recent]
                        )
                        if adjustments:
                            event_bus.publish("behavior.adjusted", adjustments)
                            if self.personality_evolution:
                                self.personality_evolution.evolve(
                                    behavior_adjustments=adjustments
                                )

                if self.belief_engine and self.graph_manager:
                    beliefs = self.belief_engine.update_from_graph(
                        self.graph_manager
                    )
                    if beliefs:
                        event_bus.publish("belief.graph_synced", {
                            "count": len(beliefs),
                        })

                if self.graph_manager:
                    try:
                        summary = self.graph_manager.process_conversation(
                            user_input, response
                        )
                        if summary and summary.get("entities_added", 0):
                            event_bus.publish("graph.updated", summary)
                    except Exception as exc:
                        logger.debug("[PostThink] Graph processing: %s", exc)

                with self._lock:
                    rc = self._reflection_count
                if self.brain_graph and thought_journal and rc % 3 == 0:
                    recent = thought_journal.recent(20)
                    if recent:
                        for t in recent:
                            topic = t.get("query", "")[:80]
                            if topic:
                                self.brain_graph.add_thought_node(
                                    thought_id=t.get("id", str(time.time())),
                                    query=t.get("query", ""),
                                    conclusion=t.get("conclusion", ""),
                                    confidence=t.get("confidence", 0.5),
                                )

                if self.dream_engine and thought_journal:
                    recent = thought_journal.recent(20)
                    if recent:
                        self.dream_engine.dream(thoughts=recent)

                if self.memory_hierarchy:
                    with self._lock:
                        self._decay_count += 1
                        dc = self._decay_count
                    if dc % 5 == 0:
                        try:
                            self.memory_hierarchy.apply_decay(elapsed_hours=0.1)
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug("[PostThink] MemoryHierarchy decay: %s", e)

            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[PostThink] Background hook: {e}")

        thread = threading.Thread(target=run_bg, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        with self._lock:
            self._threads.append(thread)

    def shutdown(self, timeout=5.0):
        with self._lock:
            pending = list(self._threads)
            self._threads.clear()
        for t in pending:
            t.join(timeout=timeout)
        return len(pending)

# core/prompt_builder.py
from core.identity import get_identity as _get_identity_impl
from prompt.builder import PromptBuilder as NewPromptBuilder

_builder = NewPromptBuilder()


def build_system_prompt(
    context="",
    emotion=None,
    behavior="",
    personality=None,
    relationship=None,
    recall=None,
    knowledge=None,
    graph=None,
    tool_defs=None,
    system_notes=None,
    speech_pacing=None,
    cognitive_state=None,
):
    if emotion is None:
        emotion = {}
    return _builder.build_system(
        emotion_state=emotion,
        behavior=behavior,
        memory_context=context,
        recall=recall,
        knowledge=knowledge,
        graph=graph,
        tool_defs=tool_defs,
        personality=personality,
        relationship=relationship,
        system_notes=system_notes,
        speech_pacing=speech_pacing,
        cognitive_state=cognitive_state,
    )


def get_identity():
    return _get_identity_impl()

# core\scheduler.py
# core/scheduler.py
import threading
import time
import logging
import traceback

from config.settings import Settings
from core.event_bus import EventBus

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Scheduler:

    def __init__(self, kernel, event_bus=None):

        self.kernel = kernel
        self.event_bus = event_bus or EventBus()

        self.running = False

        self.thread = None

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  TASK REGISTRY
        # ==================================================

        self.tasks = []

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  CONFIG SETTINGS
        # ==================================================

        self.loop_delay_active = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_LOOP_DELAY_ACTIVE
        )

        self.loop_delay_idle = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_LOOP_DELAY_IDLE
        )

        self.max_task_failures = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_MAX_TASK_FAILURES
        )

        self.error_sleep = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_ERROR_SLEEP
        )

        self.background_tick_interval = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_BACKGROUND_TICK_INTERVAL
        )

        self.emotion_decay_interval = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_EMOTION_DECAY_INTERVAL
        )

        self.personality_decay_interval = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_PERSONALITY_DECAY_INTERVAL
        )

        self.memory_decay_interval = (
            Settings.SCHEDULER_MEMORY_DECAY_INTERVAL
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  THREAD SAFETY
        # ==================================================

        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  RUNTIME METRICS
        # ==================================================

        self.total_runs = 0

        self.total_errors = 0

        self.recovery_interval = 60.0

        self.last_recovery_check = 0.0

        self._register_default_tasks()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸš€ START SCHEDULER
    # ==================================================

    def start(self):

        with self.lock:

            if self.running:

                logger.info(
                    "[Scheduler] Already running"
                )

                return False

            self.running = True

            self.thread = threading.Thread(

                target=self.loop,

                daemon=True,

                name="SchedulerThread"
            )

            self.thread.start()

            logger.info("[Scheduler] Started")

            return True

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ›‘ STOP SCHEDULER
    # ==================================================

    def stop(self):

        with self.lock:

            self.running = False

            logger.info("[Scheduler] Stopped")

            return True

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  REGISTER TASK
    # ==================================================

    def register_task(
        self,
        name,
        callback,
        interval
    ):

        try:

            if not callable(callback):

                raise ValueError(
                    f"Task '{name}' callback is not callable"
                )

            interval = max(
                1,
                float(interval)
            )

            task = {

                "name": name,

                "callback": callback,

                "interval": interval,

                "last_run": 0,

                "enabled": True,

                "failures": 0,

                "total_runs": 0
            }

            self.tasks.append(task)

            logger.info(
                f"[Scheduler] Registered task: {name}"
            )

            return True

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[TASK REGISTER ERROR] {e}"
            )

            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  DEFAULT TASKS
    # ==================================================

    def _register_default_tasks(self):

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  BACKGROUND TICK
        # ==================================================

        if hasattr(
            self.kernel,
            "background_tick"
        ):

            self.register_task(

                "background_tick",

                self.kernel.background_tick,

                interval=self.background_tick_interval
            )

        # ==================================================
        # â¤ï¸ EMOTION DECAY
        # ==================================================

        emotion = getattr(
            self.kernel,
            "emotion",
            None
        )

        if (
            emotion
            and
            hasattr(emotion, "_decay")
        ):

            self.register_task(

                "emotion_decay",

                emotion._decay,

                interval=self.emotion_decay_interval
            )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  PERSONALITY DECAY
        # ==================================================

        brain = getattr(
            self.kernel,
            "brain",
            None
        )

        if brain:

            personality_state = getattr(

                brain,

                "personality_state",

                None
            )

            if (
                personality_state
                and
                hasattr(
                    personality_state,
                    "decay"
                )
            ):

                self.register_task(

                    "personality_decay",

                    personality_state.decay,

                    interval=self.personality_decay_interval
                )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  MEMORY DECAY
        # ==================================================

            brain = getattr(
                self.kernel,
                "brain",
                None
            )

            memory = None
    
        if brain:       
            memory = getattr(
                brain,
                "memory",
                None
            )

        if memory:

            decay = getattr(
                memory,
                "decay",
                None
            )

            graph = getattr(
                memory,
                "graph",
                None
            )

            if (
                decay
                and
                graph
                and
                hasattr(decay, "apply")
            ):

                self.register_task(

                    "memory_decay",

                    lambda: self._safe_memory_decay(
                        decay,
                        graph,
                        memory
                    ),

                    interval=self.memory_decay_interval
                )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE MEMORY DECAY
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_memory_decay(
        self,
        decay,
        graph,
        memory
    ):

        try:

            nodes = getattr(
                graph,
                "nodes",
                []
            )

            if not isinstance(
                nodes,
                list
            ):
                return

            graph.nodes = decay.apply(
                nodes
            )

            for node in list(decay.archived):
                graph.remove(node)
                vector_id = getattr(
                    node,
                    "vector_id",
                    None
                )
                if vector_id:
                    memory.vector.delete(vector_id)

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[MEMORY DECAY ERROR] {e}"
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”„ MAIN LOOP
    # ==================================================

    def loop(self):

        while self.running:

            try:

                now = time.time()

                for task in list(self.tasks):

                    self._run_task(
                        task,
                        now
                    )

                # ==================================================
                # âš¡ ADAPTIVE SLEEP
                # ==================================================

                conversation_active = False
                try:
                    state = getattr(self.kernel, "state", None)
                    if state and hasattr(state, "runtime"):
                        with state.lock:
                            conversation_active = state.runtime.conversation_active
                except Exception:
                    pass

                if conversation_active:

                    time.sleep(
                        self.loop_delay_active
                    )

                else:

                    time.sleep(
                        self.loop_delay_idle
                    )

                now = time.time()

                if (
                    now - self.last_recovery_check
                    > self.recovery_interval
                ):

                    self.last_recovery_check = now

                    for task in list(self.tasks):

                        if not task["enabled"]:

                            task["enabled"] = True

                            task["failures"] = 0

                            logger.info(
                                f"Re-enabled task "
                                f"{task['name']} "
                                f"after recovery cooldown"
                            )

            except Exception as e:

                self.total_errors += 1

                logger.error(
                    f"[SCHEDULER LOOP ERROR] {e}"
                )

                traceback.print_exc()

                time.sleep(
                    self.error_sleep
                )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  TASK EXECUTION
    # ==================================================

    def _run_task(
        self,
        task,
        now
    ):

        try:

            if not task.get(
                "enabled",
                True
            ):
                return

            elapsed = (
                now - task["last_run"]
            )

            if elapsed < task["interval"]:
                return

            start = time.time()

            callback = task["callback"]

            callback()

            duration = round(
                time.time() - start,
                4
            )

            task["last_run"] = now

            task["total_runs"] += 1

            task["failures"] = 0

            self.total_runs += 1

            self.event_bus.publish("scheduler.task.completed", {
                "name": task["name"],
                "duration": duration,
                "total_runs": task["total_runs"],
            })

            logger.info(

                f"[Scheduler] "

                f"{task['name']} "

                f"({duration}s)"
            )

        except Exception as e:

            task["failures"] += 1

            self.total_errors += 1

            logger.error(

                f"[Scheduler Error] "

                f"{task['name']}: {e}"
            )

            traceback.print_exc()

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ›¡ï¸ AUTO-DISABLE BROKEN TASKS
            # ==================================================

            if (
                task["failures"]
                >=
                self.max_task_failures
            ):

                task["enabled"] = False

                logger.warning(

                    f"[Scheduler] "

                    f"Disabled task "

                    f"{task['name']} "

                    f"after repeated failures"
                )

    # ==================================================
    # âž• ENABLE TASK
    # ==================================================

    def enable_task(
        self,
        name
    ):

        for task in self.tasks:

            if task["name"] == name:

                task["enabled"] = True

                task["failures"] = 0

                return True

        return False

    # ==================================================
    # âž– DISABLE TASK
    # ==================================================

    def disable_task(
        self,
        name
    ):

        for task in self.tasks:

            if task["name"] == name:

                task["enabled"] = False

                return True

        return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§¹ REMOVE TASK
    # ==================================================

    def remove_task(
        self,
        name
    ):

        before = len(self.tasks)

        self.tasks = [

            t for t in self.tasks

            if t["name"] != name
        ]

        return len(self.tasks) < before

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        return {

            "running":
                self.running,

            "tasks":
                len(self.tasks),

            "total_runs":
                self.total_runs,

            "total_errors":
                self.total_errors,

            "thread_alive":
                (
                    self.thread.is_alive()
                    if self.thread
                    else False
                ),

            "registered_tasks": [

                {
                    "name":
                        t["name"],

                    "enabled":
                        t["enabled"],

                    "interval":
                        t["interval"],

                    "runs":
                        t["total_runs"],

                    "failures":
                        t["failures"]
                }

                for t in self.tasks
            ]
        }
# core\state\db.py
# core/state/db.py
import sqlite3
import threading
from pathlib import Path
import json

class EvidenceDB:
    def __init__(self, db_dir: Path):
        self.db_path = db_dir / "relationship_evidence.db"
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._conn = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._init_db()

    def _get_conn(self):
        if self._conn is None:
            self._conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path, check_same_thread=False)
        return self._conn

    def log_evidence(self, ev_id: str, ts: float, ev_type: str, desc: str, impacts: dict, weight: float):
        with self._lock:
            conn = self._get_conn()
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO evidence VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (ev_id, ts, ev_type, desc, json.dumps(impacts), weight)
            )
            conn.commit()

    def get_all_evidence(self) -> list:
        with self._lock:
            conn = self._get_conn()
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM evidence ORDER BY timestamp ASC")
            rows = cursor.fetchall()

            evidence_list = []
            for r in rows:
                evidence_list.append({
                    "evidence_id": r[0],
                    "timestamp": r[1],
                    "event_type": r[2],
                    "description": r[3],
                    "dimension_impacts": json.loads(r[4]),
                    "weight": r[5]
                })
            return evidence_list

    def close(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._conn is not None:
                self._conn.close()
                self._conn = None

    def _init_db(self):
        conn = self._get_conn()
        conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence (
                evidence_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                timestamp REAL,
                event_type TEXT,
                description TEXT,
                dimension_impacts TEXT,
                weight REAL
            )
        """)
        conn.commit()

    def log_evidence(self, ev_id: str, ts: float, ev_type: str, desc: str, impacts: dict, weight: float):
        conn = self._get_conn()
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO evidence VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (ev_id, ts, ev_type, desc, json.dumps(impacts), weight)
        )
        conn.commit()

    def get_all_evidence(self) -> list:
        conn = self._get_conn()
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM evidence ORDER BY timestamp ASC")
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        
        evidence_list = []
        for r in rows:
            evidence_list.append({
                "evidence_id": r[0],
                "timestamp": r[1],
                "event_type": r[2],
                "description": r[3],
                "dimension_impacts": json.loads(r[4]),
                "weight": r[5]
            })
        return evidence_list

    def close(self):
        if self._conn is not None:
            self._conn.close()
            self._conn = None

# core/state/models.py
import time
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

class EmotionState(BaseModel):
    happy: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    lonely: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    annoyed: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    affection: float = Field(default=0.3, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    energy: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    stress: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)

class RelationshipValues(BaseModel):
    trust: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    comfort: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    respect: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    affection: float = Field(default=0.3, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    familiarity: float = Field(default=0.1, ge=0.0, le=1.0)

class RelationshipEvidence(BaseModel):
    evidence_id: str
    timestamp: float = Field(default_factory=time.time)
    event_type: str
    description: str
    dimension_impacts: Dict[str, float]  # e.g., {"trust": 0.05, "comfort": -0.02}
    weight: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0.0, le=1.0)

class Goal(BaseModel):
    goal_id: str
    description: str
    priority: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    status: str = "active"  # active, completed, deferred, abandoned
    target_metric: Optional[str] = None
    created_at: float = Field(default_factory=time.time)
    updated_at: float = Field(default_factory=time.time)

class GoalState(BaseModel):
    active_goals: List[Goal] = Field(default_factory=list)
    historical_goals: List[Goal] = Field(default_factory=list)

class AttentionState(BaseModel):
    focus_target: str = "user"
    intensity: float = Field(default=0.8, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    locked_until: float = 0.0

class RuntimeStateFields(BaseModel):
    is_thinking: bool = False
    interruption_flag: bool = False
    current_session_turns: int = 0
    last_interaction_time: float = Field(default_factory=time.time)

    # Added: previously bolted onto SharedState dynamically from os_kernel.py
    # via hasattr()/setattr(). Declared here properly so they're type-checked
    # and available immediately on construction, no runtime scaffolding needed.
    user_active: bool = False
    conversation_active: bool = False
    last_user_message: str = ""
    time_of_day: str = "day"

    # Speaker identity â€” populated by SpeakerManager before STT
    current_speaker_name: str = ""
    current_speaker_id: str = ""
    speaker_confidence: float = 0.0
    known_user: bool = False
# core\state\per_person_relationship.py
import json
import logging
import os
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.PerPersonRelationship")


_DEFAULT_RELATIONSHIP = {
    "trust": 0.5,
    "comfort": 0.5,
    "respect": 0.5,
    "affection": 0.3,
    "familiarity": 0.1,
}


class PerPersonRelationshipManager:

    def __init__(self, storage_dir: str | Path):
        self.storage_dir = Path(storage_dir)
        self.storage_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._cache: dict[str, dict] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def _path(self, name: str) -> Path:
        safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in " _-" else "_" for c in name)
        return self.storage_dir / f"{safe}.json"

    def get(self, name: str) -> dict:
        with self._lock:
            if name in self._cache:
                return self._cache[name]

            path = self._path(name)
            if path.exists():
                try:
                    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                    self._cache[name] = data
                    return data
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug("get(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)

            default = dict(_DEFAULT_RELATIONSHIP)
            self._cache[name] = default
            return default

    def update(self, name: str, trust_delta=0.0, comfort_delta=0.0,
               respect_delta=0.0, affection_delta=0.0,
               familiarity_delta=0.0) -> dict:
        with self._lock:
            rel = self.get(name)
            rel["trust"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, rel.get("trust", 0.5) + trust_delta))
            rel["comfort"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, rel.get("comfort", 0.5) + comfort_delta))
            rel["respect"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, rel.get("respect", 0.5) + respect_delta))
            rel["affection"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, rel.get("affection", 0.3) + affection_delta))
            rel["familiarity"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, rel.get("familiarity", 0.1) + familiarity_delta))
            rel["last_seen"] = time.time()
            self._save(name, rel)
            return rel

    def _save(self, name: str, data: dict):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                path = self._path(name)
                path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                self._cache[name] = data
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("_save(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)

    def list_all(self) -> list[dict]:
        with self._lock:
            results = []
            for fpath in self.storage_dir.glob("*.json"):
                try:
                    data = json.loads(fpath.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                    results.append(data)
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug("list_all skipped %s: %s", fpath.name, exc)
            return results

    def delete(self, name: str) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            path = self._path(name)
            if path.exists():
                try:
                    path.unlink()
                    self._cache.pop(name, None)
                    return True
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug("delete(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
                    return False
            return False

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        profiles = self.list_all()
        return {
            "total": len(profiles),
            "profiles": [
                {
                    "name": p.get("_name", f"speaker_{i}"),
                    "trust": round(p.get("trust", 0.5), 3),
                    "comfort": round(p.get("comfort", 0.5), 3),
                    "familiarity": round(p.get("familiarity", 0.1), 3),
                }
                for i, p in enumerate(profiles)
            ],
        }

# core\state\relationship.py
# core/state/relationship.py
import uuid
import time
import math
import logging
import json
import re
from typing import Dict

# NOTE: Adjust import path for your actual models
from core.state.models import RelationshipValues

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.RelationshipEngine")

class RelationshipEngineV2:
    def __init__(self, state, llm=None):
        """
        Injected with the SharedState instance and the LLM Engine.
        """
        self.state = state
        self.llm = llm  # ðŸ§  Injected so the engine can run its own deep evaluations
        
        # 30 days half-life for relationship decay
        self.decay_halflife_seconds = 30 * 24 * 3600  

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  DEEP EVALUATION PIPELINE (Moved from Memory)
    # ==================================================
    def analyze_and_log_interaction(self, user_message: str, mai_response: str, weight: float = 1.0):
        """
        Evaluates psychological/social metrics using the deep LLM configuration
        and safely logs transaction metrics into the relationship database.
        """
        if not self.llm:
            logger.warning("No LLM assigned to RelationshipEngine. Skipping deep evaluation.")
            return

        prompt = f"""
        You are the subconscious emotional evaluation engine for Mai, an AI companion.
        Analyze the following exchange between the User and Mai to determine its impact on their relationship.
        
        User: "{user_message}"
        Mai: "{mai_response}"
        
        Provide your assessment in strict, raw JSON format. Do not wrap it in markdown block quotes or text.
        
        Target JSON Format:
        {{
            "event_type": "short_snake_case_reason",
            "description": "A 1-sentence summary of the psychological dynamic.",
            "impacts": {{
                "trust": 0.0, 
                "comfort": 0.0, 
                "respect": 0.0, 
                "affection": 0.0
            }}
        }}
        
        Rules:
        1. Keep impact values small (between -0.04 and 0.04). Most routine interactions are 0.0.
        2. Increase trust/comfort if the user is supportive, polite, or opening up.
        3. Decrease metrics if the user is hostile, insulting, or dismissive.
        """

        try:
            # Execute the prompt against the LLM
            if hasattr(self.llm, "generate"):
                raw_output = self.llm.generate(
                     "Relationship Evaluation Engine",
                      prompt
                    )
            else:
                raw_output = self.llm.generate_with_tools(prompt, "System Evaluation Context")

            if not raw_output:
                return

            data = self._extract_json(raw_output)
            
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                # âœ… Calls its OWN method directly now
                self.log_interaction_evidence(
                    event_type=data.get("event_type", "routine_dialogue"),
                    description=data.get("description", "Standard conversation log."),
                    impacts=data.get("impacts", {}),
                    weight=weight
                )
                logger.info(f"Logged deep metric updates for: '{data.get('event_type')}'")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed parsing metrics in Relationship Evaluator: {e}")

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ—„ï¸ DATABASE & MATH (Your existing code)
    # ==================================================
    def log_interaction_evidence(self, event_type: str, description: str, impacts: Dict[str, float], weight: float = 1.0):
        """
        Logs an interaction event into the SQLite database primitive and triggers a score recalculation.
        """
        evidence_id = f"ev_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        timestamp = time.time()

        try:
            self.state.db.log_evidence(
                ev_id=evidence_id,
                ts=timestamp,
                ev_type=event_type,
                desc=description,
                impacts=impacts,
                weight=weight
            )
            
            self.recalculate_derived_scores()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to process relationship transaction: {str(e)}")

    def recalculate_derived_scores(self):
        """
        Computes effective relationship scores by parsing the SQLite transaction history.
        Applies exponential recency decay to older records.
        """
        now = time.time()
        
        raw_evidence = self.state.db.get_all_evidence()
        
        base_scores = {"trust": 0.5, "comfort": 0.5, "respect": 0.5, "affection": 0.3, "familiarity": 0.1}
        totals = {k: v for k, v in base_scores.items()}
        weights = {k: 1.0 for k in base_scores.keys()}
        
        for ev in raw_evidence:
            elapsed = now - ev["timestamp"]
            
            decay_factor = math.exp(-0.693 * (elapsed / self.decay_halflife_seconds))
            effective_weight = ev["weight"] * decay_factor
            
            # Note: Ensure ev["dimension_impacts"] matches what your DB returns. 
            # In your log_interaction_evidence you pass it as "impacts", so make sure SQLite returns it as "dimension_impacts".
            for dim, impact in ev.get("dimension_impacts", {}).items():
                if dim in totals:
                    totals[dim] += impact * effective_weight
                    weights[dim] += effective_weight

        updated_dict = {}
        for dim in base_scores.keys():
            normalized_val = totals[dim] / (weights[dim] if weights[dim] > 0 else 1.0)
            updated_dict[dim] = max(0.0, min(1.0, normalized_val))
            
        updated_dict["familiarity"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, updated_dict["familiarity"] + (0.001 * len(raw_evidence))))
        
        with self.state.lock:
            self.state.relationship_scores = RelationshipValues(**updated_dict)
            self.state.save_state_to_disk()
            
        logger.info(f"ðŸ”„ Relationship scores updated. Current Trust: {updated_dict['trust']:.2f} | Comfort: {updated_dict['comfort']:.2f}")

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ› ï¸ UTILITIES
    # ==================================================
    def _extract_json(self, text: str) -> dict:
        """Safely clean markdown artifacts and parse raw JSON text fields from LLM."""
        if not text:
            return {}
        try:
            return json.loads(text.strip())
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
            if match:
                try:
                    return json.loads(match.group(0).strip())
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
            return {}
# core\state\shared_state.py
# core/state/shared_state.py
import json
import threading
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from core.state.models import (
    EmotionState, RelationshipValues, GoalState, AttentionState, RuntimeStateFields
)
from core.state.db import EvidenceDB

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.SharedState")

class SharedState:
    def __init__(self, persistence_dir: Path):
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.state_dir = persistence_dir / "state"
        self.state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Initialize the SQLite database for heavy log handling
        self.db = EvidenceDB(self.state_dir)
        
        # Clean, decoupled in-memory sub-states
        self.emotion = EmotionState()
        self.relationship_scores = RelationshipValues()
        self.goals = GoalState()
        self.attention = AttentionState()
        self.runtime = RuntimeStateFields()
        
        self.load_state_from_disk()

    def serialize(self) -> dict:
        with self.lock:
            return {
                "emotion": self.emotion.model_dump(),
                "relationship_scores": self.relationship_scores.model_dump(),
                "goals": self.goals.model_dump(),
                "attention": self.attention.model_dump(),
                "runtime": self.runtime.model_dump()
            }

    def save_state_to_disk(self):
        with self.lock:
            try:
                with open(self.state_dir / "master_state.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    json.dump(self.serialize(), f, indent=4)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"State Saving Failure: {str(e)}")

    def load_state_from_disk(self):
        with self.lock:
            target_path = self.state_dir / "master_state.json"
            if not target_path.exists():
                return
            try:
                with open(target_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                if "emotion" in data: self.emotion = EmotionState(**data["emotion"])
