                if "relationship_scores" in data: self.relationship_scores = RelationshipValues(**data["relationship_scores"])
                if "goals" in data: self.goals = GoalState(**data["goals"])
                if "attention" in data: self.attention = AttentionState(**data["attention"])
                if "runtime" in data: self.runtime = RuntimeStateFields(**data["runtime"])
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to recover state: {str(e)}")

# core\style_filter.py
# core/style_filter.py
import random
import re
import threading

from core.identity.speech import SPEECH


class StyleFilter:
    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.recent_suffixes = []
        self.speech_rules = SPEECH["style"]

    # ðŸŽ­ MAIN STYLE PASS
    def apply_style(
        self,
        text,
        personality=None,
        emotion=None,
        context=None
    ):

        if not text:
            return text

        if personality is None:
            personality = {}

        if emotion is None:
            emotion = {}

        if context is None:
            context = {}

        text = text.strip()

        # ðŸ§  sentence-aware processing
        sentences = self._split_sentences(text)

        styled = []

        for sentence in sentences:

            sentence = self._soften_directness(
                sentence,
                personality
            )

            sentence = self._add_natural_pauses(
                sentence,
                personality,
                emotion
            )

            styled.append(sentence)

        text = " ".join(styled)

        # ðŸ˜ teasing
        text = self._inject_teasing(
            text,
            personality,
            emotion,
            context
        )

        # ðŸŒ™ soft finish
        text = self._soft_finish(
            text,
            personality,
            emotion
        )

        return text.strip()

    # âœ‚ï¸ sentence splitting
    def _split_sentences(self, text):

        parts = re.split(
            r'(?<=[.!?])\s+',
            text
        )

        return [
            p.strip()
            for p in parts
            if p.strip()
        ]

    # ðŸ§  soften directness
    def _soften_directness(
        self,
        text,
        personality
    ):

        directness = personality.get(
            "directness",
            0.5
        )

        if directness > 0.75:
            return text

        concise_mode = any(
            "Concise" in r
            for r in self.speech_rules
        )

        replacements = {
            r"\bI think\b": "It seems",
            r"\bI understand\b": "I see",
            r"\bYou should\b": "You might want to",
            r"\bYes\b": "â€¦yes",
            r"\bNo\b": "â€¦no"
        }

        soften_chance = 0.3 if concise_mode else 0.6

        for pattern, replacement in replacements.items():

            if random.random() < soften_chance:

                text = re.sub(
                    pattern,
                    replacement,
                    text
                )

        return text

    # ðŸŒ«ï¸ pauses
    def _add_natural_pauses(
        self,
        text,
        personality,
        emotion
    ):

        warmth = personality.get(
            "warmth",
            0.5
        )

        affection = emotion.get(
            "affection",
            0.3
        )

        chance = (
            0.15 +
            warmth * 0.15 +
            affection * 0.15
        )

        if (
            random.random() < chance and
            not text.startswith("...")
        ):

            text = "... " + text

        return text

    # ðŸ˜ teasing
    def _inject_teasing(
        self,
        text,
        personality,
        emotion,
        context
    ):

        teasing = personality.get(
            "teasing",
            0.3
        )

        affection = emotion.get(
            "affection",
            0.3
        )

        serious = context.get(
            "serious_mode",
            False
        )

        adapts_tone = any(
            "Adapts tone" in r
            for r in self.speech_rules
        )

        if serious and adapts_tone:
            return text

        chance = (
            teasing * 0.2 +
            affection * 0.1
        )

        if adapts_tone and serious:
            chance *= 0.3

        if random.random() > chance:
            return text

        teasing_lines = [
            "You're a little predictable.",
            "You really do overthink things sometimes.",
            "Hm. You're easy to read lately.",
            "You always make that face when you're thinking."
        ]

        with self._lock:
            available = [
                t for t in teasing_lines
                if t not in self.recent_suffixes
            ]

            if not available:
                self.recent_suffixes.clear()
                available = teasing_lines

            selected = random.choice(available)

            self.recent_suffixes.append(selected)

        return f"{text} {selected}"

    # ðŸŒ™ endings
    def _soft_finish(
        self,
        text,
        personality,
        emotion
    ):

        warmth = personality.get(
            "warmth",
            0.5
        )

        affection = emotion.get(
            "affection",
            0.3
        )

        chance = (
            warmth * 0.1 +
            affection * 0.15
        )

        if random.random() > chance:
            return text

        endings = [
            "...just saying.",
            "...that's all.",
            "...if that matters.",
            "...not that you'd admit it."
        ]

        with self._lock:
            available = [
                e for e in endings
                if e not in self.recent_suffixes
            ]

            if not available:
                self.recent_suffixes.clear()
                available = endings

            selected = random.choice(available)

            self.recent_suffixes.append(selected)

        return f"{text} {selected}"


# âœ… GLOBAL INSTANCE
_style_filter = StyleFilter()


# âœ… COMPATIBILITY FUNCTION
def apply_style(
    text,
    personality=None,
    emotion=None,
    context=None
):
    return _style_filter.apply_style(
        text,
        personality,
        emotion,
        context
    )
# core\tasks.py
# core/tasks.py
import time
import uuid
from threading import Lock


class TaskManager:
    def __init__(self):

        self.tasks = []

        # ðŸ”’ thread safety
        self.lock = Lock()

    # âž• create task
    def add(
        self,
        description,
        priority=0.5,
        category="general",
        importance=0.5,
        emotional_weight=0.5,
        metadata=None
    ):

        if metadata is None:
            metadata = {}

        task = {
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),

            "description": description,

            "status": "pending",

            # âš¡ execution priority
            "priority": round(priority, 2),

            # ðŸ§  emotional relevance
            "importance": round(importance, 2),

            "emotional_weight": round(
                emotional_weight,
                2
            ),

            "category": category,

            "created_at": time.time(),

            "updated_at": time.time(),

            "completed_at": None,

            "metadata": metadata
        }

        with self.lock:
            self.tasks.append(task)

        return task

    # ðŸ“‹ get all tasks
    def get_all(self):

        with self.lock:
            return list(self.tasks)

    # â³ get pending tasks
    def get_pending(self):

        with self.lock:

            pending = [
                t for t in self.tasks
                if t["status"] == "pending"
            ]

        # ðŸ§  smart sorting
        pending.sort(
            key=lambda x: (
                x["priority"] * 0.5 +
                x["importance"] * 0.3 +
                x["emotional_weight"] * 0.2
            ),
            reverse=True
        )

        return pending

    # ðŸ” find task by id
    def get_task(self, task_id):

        with self.lock:

            for task in self.tasks:

                if task["id"] == task_id:
                    return task

        return None

    # âœ… complete task
    def complete(self, task_id):

        with self.lock:

            task = self.get_task(task_id)

            if not task:
                return False

            task["status"] = "completed"

            task["completed_at"] = time.time()

            task["updated_at"] = time.time()

            return True

    # âŒ cancel task
    def cancel(self, task_id):

        with self.lock:

            task = self.get_task(task_id)

            if not task:
                return False

            task["status"] = "cancelled"

            task["updated_at"] = time.time()

            return True

    # ðŸ§  update task metadata
    def update_metadata(
        self,
        task_id,
        key,
        value
    ):

        with self.lock:

            task = self.get_task(task_id)

            if not task:
                return False

            task["metadata"][key] = value

            task["updated_at"] = time.time()

            return True

    # âš¡ dynamic emotional updates
    def update_emotional_weight(
        self,
        task_id,
        value
    ):

        with self.lock:

            task = self.get_task(task_id)

            if not task:
                return False

            task["emotional_weight"] = round(
                max(0.0, min(1.0, value)),
                2
            )

            task["updated_at"] = time.time()

            return True

    # ðŸ§  dynamic importance updates
    def update_importance(
        self,
        task_id,
        value
    ):

        with self.lock:

            task = self.get_task(task_id)

            if not task:
                return False

            task["importance"] = round(
                max(0.0, min(1.0, value)),
                2
            )

            task["updated_at"] = time.time()

            return True

    # ðŸ—‘ï¸ remove completed tasks
    def clear_completed(self):

        with self.lock:

            before = len(self.tasks)

            self.tasks = [
                t for t in self.tasks
                if t["status"] != "completed"
            ]

            return before - len(self.tasks)

    # ðŸ“Š lightweight stats
    def stats(self):

        with self.lock:

            total = len(self.tasks)

            pending = len([
                t for t in self.tasks
                if t["status"] == "pending"
            ])

            completed = len([
                t for t in self.tasks
                if t["status"] == "completed"
            ])

            cancelled = len([
                t for t in self.tasks
                if t["status"] == "cancelled"
            ])

        return {
            "total": total,
            "pending": pending,
            "completed": completed,
            "cancelled": cancelled
        }
# core\wake_generator.py
# core/wake_generator.py
import time
import random
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_TIME_GREETINGS = {
    "late_night": [
        "late night", "this hour", "the stillness",
        "the dead of night", "these quiet hours",
    ],
    "morning": [
        "morning", "good morning", "early",
        "the early hours", "daybreak",
    ],
    "day": [
        "afternoon", "day", "daytime",
        "midday", "the middle of the day",
    ],
    "evening": [
        "evening", "good evening", "dusk",
        "twilight", "the golden hour",
    ],
    "night": [
        "night", "tonight", "the evening",
        "the night air", "this late hour",
    ],
}

_TIME_VIBES = {
    "late_night": ["quiet", "peaceful", "still", "calm", "hushed"],
    "morning": ["fresh", "new", "bright", "crisp", "gentle"],
    "day": ["warm", "steady", "alive", "bright", "lively"],
    "evening": ["calm", "soft", "settled", "mellow", "peaceful"],
    "night": ["serene", "quiet", "peaceful", "calm", "still"],
}

_GREETING_FRAMES = [
    "...{time_ref}.",
    "...it's {time_ref}.",
    "...{time_vibe}, isn't it?",
    "...{time_ref} already.",
    "...what a {time_vibe} {time_of_day}.",
    "...{time_ref} and here you are.",
    "...feels {time_vibe} tonight.",
    "...the {time_vibe} kind of {time_of_day}.",
    "...{time_ref}. time moves differently now.",
    "...the {time_vibe} is settling in.",
]

_EMOTION_COLORS = {
    "happy": [
        "I'm feeling good.", "in good spirits.",
        "feeling light today.", "in a good mood.",
        "things feel right today.",
    ],
    "lonely": [
        "it's been quiet.", "I was thinking.",
        "the silence was getting to me.",
        "it's too quiet without you.",
    ],
    "annoyed": [
        "someone's restless.", "you're back.",
        "I was getting impatient.", "about time.",
    ],
    "affection": [
        "I missed you.", "there you are.",
        "I was waiting for you.",
        "I was hoping you'd come.",
        "I've been thinking about you.",
    ],
    "neutral": [
        "right on time.", "as expected.",
        "here we are.", "same as always.",
        "just another day.",
    ],
}

_THOUGHT_HINTS = [
    "I was just thinking about {thought}.",
    "I had {thought} on my mind.",
    "my mind was on {thought}.",
    "I was wondering about {thought}.",
    "{thought} crossed my mind.",
]

_TOPIC_REFS = [
    "we were talking about {topic}.",
    "you were telling me about {topic}.",
    "{topic} was on your mind.",
    "you had {topic} on your mind last time.",
    "I remember you mentioned {topic}.",
]


class WakeGenerator:

    def __init__(self, identity=None, personality_state=None, emotion=None):
        self._identity = identity or {}
        self._personality = personality_state
        self._emotion = emotion
        self._recent = []
        self._max_history = 20

    def generate(self, context=None):
        if context is None:
            context = {}

        identity = context.get("identity") or self._identity or {}
        personality_state = context.get("personality_state") or self._personality
        emotion_data = context.get("emotion") or {}
        cognitive = context.get("cognitive_state") or {}
        time_of_day = context.get("time_of_day") or "day"

        name = identity.get("name", "Mai")

        emotion_type = "neutral"
        if isinstance(emotion_data, dict):
            emotion_type = emotion_data.get("dominant_emotion") or "neutral"
            if emotion_type == "neutral" and "type" in emotion_data:
                emotion_type = emotion_data.get("type", "neutral")

        working_memory = cognitive.get("working_memory", [])
        topic = cognitive.get("current_topic", "")
        current_thought = cognitive.get("current_thought")

        closeness = 0.5
        if personality_state and hasattr(personality_state, "state"):
            warmth = personality_state.state.get("warmth", 0.5)
            closeness = warmth

        for attempt in range(5):
            time_ref = random.choice(_TIME_GREETINGS.get(time_of_day, ["here"]))
            time_vibe = random.choice(_TIME_VIBES.get(time_of_day, ["nice"]))

            greeting = random.choice(_GREETING_FRAMES)

            opening = greeting.format(
                time_ref=time_ref,
                time_vibe=time_vibe,
                time_of_day=time_of_day,
            )

            middle = ""
            if working_memory and topic and closeness > 0.4:
                ref = random.choice(_TOPIC_REFS)
                middle = f" {ref.format(topic=topic)}"

            elif current_thought:
                hint = random.choice(_THOUGHT_HINTS)
                middle = f" {hint.format(thought=current_thought.lower())}"

            emotion_line = ""
            if emotion_type in _EMOTION_COLORS and closeness > 0.3:
                color = random.choice(_EMOTION_COLORS[emotion_type])
                emotion_line = f" {color}"

            closeness_tag = ""
            if closeness > 0.7:
                closeness_tag = random.choice([
                    " darling", " love", "",
                ])
            elif closeness < 0.3:
                closeness_tag = ""

            wake = f"...{opening}{middle}{emotion_line}{closeness_tag}"

            if wake not in self._recent:
                break

        self._recent.append(wake)
        if len(self._recent) > self._max_history:
            self._recent = self._recent[-self._max_history:]

        return wake

# execution\safe_executor.py
import time
import logging

from config.settings import (
    EXECUTOR_COOLDOWN,
    EXECUTOR_MIN_CONFIDENCE,
    EXECUTOR_MODE,
    EXECUTOR_BLOCKED_COMMANDS,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SafeExecutor:

    def __init__(self, executor, mode=None):
        self.executor = executor
        self.mode = mode or EXECUTOR_MODE
        self.last_action_time = 0
        self.cooldown = EXECUTOR_COOLDOWN
        self.history = []
        self.min_confidence = EXECUTOR_MIN_CONFIDENCE
        self.permissions = {
            "safe": {"open_app", "open_url", "search"},
            "advanced": {"open_app", "open_url", "search", "type_text"},
            "admin": {"open_app", "open_url", "search", "type_text", "run_command"},
        }
        self.blocked_commands = EXECUTOR_BLOCKED_COMMANDS

    def run(self, action: dict):
        if not action:
            return "[Invalid action]"
        if "type" not in action:
            return "[Missing action type]"
        action_type = action["type"]
        if action_type == "speak":
            return action.get("content", "")
        if not self._cooldown_ready():
            return "[Execution cooldown active]"
        allowed = self.permissions.get(self.mode, set())
        if action_type not in allowed:
            self._log(action, "blocked_permission")
            return f"[Blocked: {action_type} not allowed in {self.mode} mode]"
        confidence = action.get("confidence", 1.0)
        if confidence < self.min_confidence:
            logger.warning("Low confidence action (%.2f)", confidence)
            if not self._confirm(action):
                self._log(action, "cancelled_low_confidence")
                return "[Cancelled: low confidence]"
        valid, reason = self._validate(action)
        if not valid:
            self._log(action, f"blocked_validation:{reason}")
            return f"[Blocked: {reason}]"
        if action_type == "run_command" and self.mode == "admin":
            if not self._confirm(action):
                self._log(action, "cancelled_by_user")
                return "[Command cancelled]"
        try:
            result = self.executor.run(action)
            self.last_action_time = time.time()
            self._log(action, "success")
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            self._log(action, f"error:{str(e)}")
            return f"[Execution error: {str(e)}]"

    def _validate(self, action):
        action_type = action["type"]
        if action_type == "open_url":
            url = action.get("url", "")
            if not (url.startswith("http://") or url.startswith("https://")):
                return False, "invalid_url"
        if action_type == "run_command":
            command = action.get("command", "").lower()
            for blocked in self.blocked_commands:
                if blocked in command:
                    return False, f"dangerous_command:{blocked}"
        return True, "ok"

    def _confirm(self, action):
        logger.warning("Action confirmation requested for: %s", action)
        return False

    def _cooldown_ready(self):
        return time.time() - self.last_action_time >= self.cooldown

    def _log(self, action, status):
        self.history.append({"timestamp": time.time(), "mode": self.mode, "action": action, "status": status})
        if len(self.history) > 1000:
            self.history.pop(0)

    def set_mode(self, mode):
        if mode not in self.permissions:
            return "[Invalid mode]"
        self.mode = mode
        return f"[Mode switched to {mode}]"

    def stats(self):
        total = len(self.history)
        success = len([h for h in self.history if h["status"] == "success"])
        blocked = len([h for h in self.history if "blocked" in h["status"]])
        errors = len([h for h in self.history if "error" in h["status"]])
        return {"total": total, "success": success, "blocked": blocked, "errors": errors, "mode": self.mode}

# execution\task_executor.py
# Mai_ai/execution/task_executor.py
import os
import time
import queue
import shlex
import logging
import threading
import subprocess
import webbrowser
import urllib.parse

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from config.settings import Settings

try:
    import pyautogui
except Exception:
    pyautogui = None


class TaskExecutor:

    def __init__(self):

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  EXECUTION STATE
        # ==================================================

        self.last_action = None

        self.history = []

        self.running_processes = []

        self.max_output = (
            Settings.EXECUTOR_MAX_OUTPUT
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  THREAD SAFETY
        # ==================================================

        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  SECURITY
        # ==================================================

        self.allowed_actions = set(

            Settings.EXECUTOR_ALLOWED_ACTIONS
        )

        self.blocked_commands = (

            Settings.EXECUTOR_BLOCKED_COMMANDS
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  EXECUTION SETTINGS
        # ==================================================

        self.command_timeout = (
            Settings.EXECUTOR_COMMAND_TIMEOUT
        )

        self.history_limit = (
            Settings.EXECUTOR_HISTORY_LIMIT
        )

        self.typing_interval = (
            Settings.EXECUTOR_TYPING_INTERVAL
        )

        self.typing_delay = (
            Settings.EXECUTOR_TYPING_DELAY
        )

        self.queue_timeout = (
            Settings.EXECUTOR_QUEUE_TIMEOUT
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  ACTION QUEUE
        # ==================================================

        self.task_queue = queue.Queue()

        self.queue_running = True

        self.worker_thread = threading.Thread(

            target=self._queue_loop,

            daemon=True,

            name="ExecutorQueueThread"
        )

        self.worker_thread.start()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN EXECUTION ENTRY
    # ==================================================

    def run(self, task):

        start = time.time()

        # ==================================================
        # âœ… TASK VALIDATION
        # ==================================================

        if not isinstance(task, dict):

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="invalid_task_format",
                duration=0
            )

        action_type = str(
            task.get("type", "")
        ).strip()

        content = task.get("content")

        task_id = task.get("id")

        if not action_type:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="missing_action_type",
                duration=0
            )

        if action_type not in self.allowed_actions:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output=f"unauthorized_action:{action_type}",
                duration=0
            )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  ACTION ROUTING
        # ==================================================

        try:

            if action_type == "open_app":

                result = self.open_app(content)

            elif action_type == "open_url":

                result = self.open_url(content)

            elif action_type == "type_text":

                result = self.type_text(content)

            elif action_type == "run_command":

                result = self.run_command(content)

            elif action_type == "search":

                result = self.search(content)

            else:

                result = self._result(
                    success=False,
                    output=f"unknown_task:{action_type}",
                    duration=0
                )

        except Exception as e:

            result = self._result(
                success=False,
                output=f"task_failed:{str(e)}",
                duration=0
            )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  METADATA
        # ==================================================

        result["task_id"] = task_id

        result["timestamp"] = time.time()

        result["duration"] = round(
            time.time() - start,
            2
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  HISTORY STORAGE
        # ==================================================

        with self.lock:

            self.history.append(result)

            if (
                len(self.history)
                >
                self.history_limit
            ):

                self.history.pop(0)

        return result

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  TASK QUEUE
    # ==================================================

    def queue_task(self, task):

        if not isinstance(task, dict):

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="invalid_queue_task",
                duration=0
            )

        self.task_queue.put(task)

        return self._result(
            success=True,
            output="task_queued",
            duration=0
        )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”„ QUEUE LOOP
    # ==================================================

    def _queue_loop(self):

        while self.queue_running:

            try:

                task = self.task_queue.get(

                    timeout=self.queue_timeout
                )

                try:

                    self.run(task)

                finally:

                    self.task_queue.task_done()

            except queue.Empty:

                continue

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[QUEUE ERROR] {e}"
                )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ›‘ STOP QUEUE
    # ==================================================

    def stop_queue(self):

        self.queue_running = False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ–¥ï¸ OPEN APPLICATION
    # ==================================================

    def open_app(self, app_name):

        self.last_action = (
            f"Opening {app_name}"
        )

        if not app_name:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="no_app_specified",
                duration=0
            )

        try:

            app_name = str(app_name).strip()

            if os.name == "nt":

                os.startfile(app_name)

                return self._result(
                    success=True,
                    output=f"opened:{app_name}",
                    duration=0
                )

            else:

                process = subprocess.Popen(
                    [app_name]
                )

            with self.lock:

                self.running_processes.append(
                    process
                )

            return self._result(
                success=True,
                output=f"opened:{app_name}",
                duration=0
            )

        except Exception as e:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output=str(e),
                duration=0
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŒ OPEN URL
    # ==================================================

    def open_url(self, url):

        self.last_action = (
            f"Opening {url}"
        )

        if not url:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="no_url_provided",
                duration=0
            )

        try:

            url = str(url).strip()

            if not (
                url.startswith("http://")
                or
                url.startswith("https://")
            ):

                url = "https://" + url

            webbrowser.open(url)

            return self._result(
                success=True,
                output=f"opened:{url}",
                duration=0
            )

        except Exception as e:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output=str(e),
                duration=0
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”Ž SEARCH
    # ==================================================

    def search(self, query):

        self.last_action = (
            f"Searching: {query}"
        )

        if not query:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="no_search_query",
                duration=0
            )

        try:

            encoded = urllib.parse.quote(
                str(query)
            )

            url = (
                Settings.SEARCH_ENGINE_URL
                +
                encoded
            )

            webbrowser.open(url)

            return self._result(
                success=True,
                output=f"searching:{query}",
                duration=0
            )

        except Exception as e:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output=str(e),
                duration=0
            )

    # ==================================================
    # âŒ¨ï¸ TYPE TEXT
    # ==================================================

    def type_text(self, text):

        self.last_action = "Typing text"

        if not text:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="no_text_provided",
                duration=0
            )

        if pyautogui is None:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="pyautogui_not_available",
                duration=0
            )

        try:

            time.sleep(
                self.typing_delay
            )

            pyautogui.write(

                str(text),

                interval=self.typing_interval
            )

            return self._result(
                success=True,
                output="typed_successfully",
                duration=0
            )

        except Exception as e:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output=str(e),
                duration=0
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ’» RUN COMMAND
    # ==================================================

    def run_command(

        self,

        command,

        timeout=None
    ):

        self.last_action = (
            f"Running command: {command}"
        )

        if not command:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="no_command_provided",
                duration=0
            )

        command = str(command).strip()

        timeout = (
            timeout
            or
            self.command_timeout
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸš« BLOCK DANGEROUS COMMANDS
        # ==================================================

        lowered = command.lower()

        for blocked in self.blocked_commands:

            if blocked in lowered:

                return self._result(
                    success=False,
                    output="blocked_dangerous_command",
                    duration=0
                )

        start = time.time()

        try:

            args = shlex.split(command, posix=False)

            result = subprocess.run(

                args,

                capture_output=True,

                text=True,

                timeout=timeout
            )

            output = (
                result.stdout
                or result.stderr
                or "command_executed"
            )

            output = output[
                :self.max_output
            ]

            return self._result(
                success=result.returncode == 0,
                output=output.strip(),
                duration=round(
                    time.time() - start,
                    2
                )
            )

        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="command_timeout",
                duration=round(
                    time.time() - start,
                    2
                )
            )

        except Exception as e:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output=str(e),
                duration=round(
                    time.time() - start,
                    2
                )
            )

    # ==================================================
    # âš¡ ASYNC COMMAND
    # ==================================================

    def run_async_command(

        self,

        command
    ):

        if not command:

            return self._result(
                success=False,
                output="no_command_provided",
                duration=0
            )

        command = str(command).strip()

        def worker():

            try:

                args = shlex.split(command, posix=False)

                subprocess.Popen(args)

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[ASYNC ERROR] {e}"
                )

        thread = threading.Thread(

            target=worker,

            daemon=True
        )

        thread.start()

        return self._result(
            success=True,
            output=f"async_started:{command}",
            duration=0
        )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  QUICK ACTIONS
    # ==================================================

    def quick_action(self, text):

        if not text:
            return None

        t = str(text).lower()

        if "open youtube" in t:

            return self.open_url(
                Settings.DEFAULT_YOUTUBE_URL
            )

        if "open google" in t:

            return self.open_url(
                Settings.DEFAULT_GOOGLE_URL
            )

        if "open code" in t:

            return self.open_app(
                Settings.DEFAULT_CODE_EDITOR
            )

        if t.startswith("search"):

            query = (
                t.replace(
                    "search",
                    "",
                    1
                ).strip()
            )

            return self.search(query)

        if t.startswith("type"):

            content = (
                t.replace(
                    "type",
                    "",
                    1
                ).strip()
            )

            return self.type_text(
                content
            )

        return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“¦ RESULT OBJECT
    # ==================================================

    def _result(

        self,

        success,

        output,

        duration
    ):

        return {

            "success": bool(success),

            "output": str(output),

            "duration": float(duration)
        }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        with self.lock:

            total = len(self.history)

            success = len([

                h for h in self.history

                if h.get("success")
            ])

            failed = total - success

            alive_processes = len([

                p for p in self.running_processes

                if p.poll() is None
            ])

            return {

                "total": total,

                "success": success,

                "failed": failed,

                "running_processes":
                    alive_processes,

                "queued_tasks":
                    self.task_queue.qsize(),

                "max_output":
                    self.max_output,

                "queue_running":
                    self.queue_running
            }

# knowledge\attention.py
# knowledge/attention.py
import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class AttentionFocus:

    def __init__(self, topic, areas=None, weights=None):
        self.topic = str(topic).strip().lower()
        self.areas = list(areas or [])
        self.weights = dict(weights or {})
        self.created_at = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "topic": self.topic,
            "areas": list(self.areas),
            "weights": dict(self.weights),
            "created_at": self.created_at,
        }

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"<AttentionFocus topic={self.topic} areas={len(self.areas)}>"


class AttentionSystem:

    def __init__(self, knowledge_graph=None, belief_engine=None):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._current_focus = None
        self._topic_history = []
        self._max_history = 10
        self.knowledge_graph = knowledge_graph
        self.belief_engine = belief_engine

        self._topic_areas = {
            "programming": {"python", "code", "software", "development", "tech"},
            "python": {"programming", "code", "development"},
            "anime": {"japanese", "animation", "shows", "entertainment"},
            "music": {"audio", "songs", "entertainment", "art"},
            "game": {"gaming", "entertainment", "development"},
            "food": {"cooking", "preferences", "health"},
            "travel": {"places", "culture", "geography"},
            "work": {"career", "job", "employment", "professional"},
            "learning": {"education", "skills", "growth", "programming"},
            "health": {"wellness", "exercise", "mental", "food"},
        }

    def focus(self, topic, cognitive_state=None):
        topic = str(topic).strip().lower() if topic else ""

        areas = set()
        for kw, related in self._topic_areas.items():
            if kw in topic or topic in kw:
                areas.update(related)

        if self.knowledge_graph:
            relations = self.knowledge_graph.query(topic, depth=1)
            for rel in relations:
                areas.add(rel.target)

        if self.belief_engine:
            beliefs = self.belief_engine.query(topic)
            for b in beliefs:
                areas.add(b.object.lower()[:30])

        weights = {area: 1.0 for area in areas}

        with self._lock:
            self._current_focus = AttentionFocus(
                topic=topic,
                areas=list(areas),
                weights=weights,
            )

            if topic:
                self._topic_history.append(topic)
                if len(self._topic_history) > self._max_history:
                    self._topic_history = self._topic_history[-self._max_history:]

        return self._current_focus

    def current(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._current_focus

    def history(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._topic_history)

    def retrieve(self, focus=None):
        if focus is None:
            with self._lock:
                focus = self._current_focus

        if focus is None:
            return []

        results = []

        if self.knowledge_graph:
            for area in focus.areas:
                relations = self.knowledge_graph.query(area, depth=1)
                for rel in relations:
                    weight = focus.weights.get(area, 0.5)
                    results.append({
                        "source": "knowledge_graph",
                        "content": f"{rel.source} {rel.relation} {rel.target}",
                        "relevance": weight * 0.8,
                        "area": area,
                    })

        results.sort(key=lambda r: r["relevance"], reverse=True)
        return results[:10]

# knowledge\belief_engine.py
# knowledge/belief_engine.py
import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Belief:

    def __init__(self, topic, predicate="is", object="", confidence=0.5, evidence_count=1):
        self.topic = str(topic).strip().lower()
        self.predicate = str(predicate).strip().lower()
        self.object = str(object).strip()
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence)))
        self.evidence_count = int(evidence_count)
        self.first_seen = time.time()
        self.last_updated = time.time()
        self.contradictions = 0

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "topic": self.topic,
            "predicate": self.predicate,
            "object": self.object,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "evidence_count": self.evidence_count,
            "first_seen": self.first_seen,
            "last_updated": self.last_updated,
            "contradictions": self.contradictions,
        }

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"<Belief "
            f"{self.topic} {self.predicate} {self.object[:20]} "
            f"({round(self.confidence, 2)})>"
        )


class BeliefEngine:

    def __init__(self, event_bus=None, brain_graph=None):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._beliefs = {}
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph

    def _key(self, topic, predicate, object):
        return f"{topic}:{predicate}:{str(object).lower()}"

    def update(self, memory):
        from memory.schema import MemoryObject

        if not isinstance(memory, MemoryObject):
            return None

        content = memory.content
        mem_type = memory.type
        confidence = memory.confidence

        extracted = self._extract_beliefs(content, mem_type, confidence)

        results = []
        for belief_data in extracted:
            belief = self._merge(belief_data)
            results.append(belief)

        if results and self.brain_graph:
            for b in results:
                self.brain_graph.add_belief_node(
                    topic=b.topic,
                    predicate=b.predicate,
                    object_val=b.object,
                    confidence=b.confidence,
                    evidence_count=b.evidence_count,
                )

        if results and self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("belief.updated", {
                "beliefs": [b.to_dict() for b in results],
                "source_memory_id": memory.id,
            })

        return results

    def _extract_beliefs(self, text, mem_type, confidence):
        if not text:
            return []

        text_lower = text.lower()
        results = []

        patterns = [
            (r"\b(likes|enjoys|loves)\s+(\w+)", "likes"),
            (r"\b(dislikes|hates)\s+(\w+)", "dislikes"),
        ]

        import re
        for pattern, predicate in patterns:
            for match in re.finditer(pattern, text_lower):
                obj = match.group(2)
                results.append({
                    "topic": "user",
                    "predicate": predicate,
                    "object": obj,
                    "confidence": confidence,
                })

        if mem_type in ("preference", "preference_negative"):
            pred = "likes" if mem_type == "preference" else "dislikes"
            results.append({
                "topic": "user",
                "predicate": pred,
                "object": text,
                "confidence": confidence,
            })

        if mem_type == "identity":
            results.append({
                "topic": "user",
                "predicate": "is",
                "object": text,
                "confidence": confidence,
            })

        if mem_type == "employment":
            results.append({
                "topic": "user",
                "predicate": "works_at",
                "object": text,
                "confidence": confidence,
            })

        if mem_type == "location":
            results.append({
                "topic": "user",
                "predicate": "lives_in",
                "object": text,
                "confidence": confidence,
            })

        return results

    def _merge(self, belief_data):
        k = self._key(
            belief_data["topic"],
            belief_data["predicate"],
            belief_data["object"],
        )

        with self._lock:
            if k in self._beliefs:
                existing = self._beliefs[k]
                existing.evidence_count += 1
                existing.last_updated = time.time()
                confidence_boost = 0.1 * (1 - existing.confidence) * (1 / (1 + existing.contradictions))
                existing.confidence = min(1.0, existing.confidence + confidence_boost)
                return existing
            else:
                belief = Belief(
                    topic=belief_data["topic"],
                    predicate=belief_data["predicate"],
                    object=belief_data["object"],
                    confidence=belief_data["confidence"],
                )
                self._beliefs[k] = belief
                return belief

    def contradict(self, topic, predicate, object):
        k = self._key(topic, predicate, object)
        with self._lock:
            if k in self._beliefs:
                belief = self._beliefs[k]
                belief.contradictions += 1
                belief.confidence = max(0.1, belief.confidence * 0.7)
                belief.last_updated = time.time()
                if self.brain_graph:
                    self.brain_graph.add_belief_node(
                        topic=belief.topic,
                        predicate=belief.predicate,
                        object_val=belief.object,
                        confidence=belief.confidence,
                        evidence_count=belief.evidence_count,
                    )
                return belief
        return None

    def get_belief(self, topic, predicate=None, object=None):
        with self._lock:
            for k, belief in self._beliefs.items():
                if belief.topic == topic.lower():
                    if predicate and belief.predicate != predicate.lower():
                        continue
                    if object and belief.object.lower() != object.lower():
                        continue
                    return belief
        return None

    def query(self, topic):
        local = []
        with self._lock:
            local = [
                b for b in self._beliefs.values()
                if b.topic == topic.lower()
            ]
        if self.brain_graph:
            graph_nodes = self.brain_graph.get_beliefs(topic=topic)
            graph_ids = {f"{n.metadata['topic']}:{n.metadata['predicate']}:{n.metadata['object']}"
                         for n in graph_nodes}
            local_ids = {self._key(b.topic, b.predicate, b.object) for b in local}
            for n in graph_nodes:
                nk = f"{n.metadata['topic']}:{n.metadata['predicate']}:{n.metadata['object']}"
                if nk not in local_ids:
                    local.append(Belief(
                        topic=n.metadata["topic"],
                        predicate=n.metadata["predicate"],
                        object=n.metadata["object"],
                        confidence=n.metadata.get("confidence", 0.5),
                        evidence_count=n.metadata.get("evidence_count", 1),
                    ))
        return local

    def all_beliefs(self):
        with self._lock:
            return sorted(
                self._beliefs.values(),
                key=lambda b: b.confidence,
                reverse=True,
            )

    def update_from_graph(self, graph_manager):
        if not graph_manager:
            return []
        try:
            all_edges = graph_manager.graph.all_relations()
            results = []
            seen = set()
            for edge in all_edges:
                src, tgt = str(edge.source).lower(), str(edge.target).lower()
                rel = edge.relation

                direct_key = f"{src}:{rel}:{tgt}"
                if direct_key not in seen:
                    seen.add(direct_key)
                    b = self._merge({
                        "topic": src,
                        "predicate": rel,
                        "object": tgt,
                        "confidence": max(0.3, edge.weight * 1.0),
                    })
                    results.append(b)

                rev_pred = f"related_to_{rel}"
                rev_key = f"{tgt}:{rev_pred}:{src}"
                if rev_key not in seen:
                    seen.add(rev_key)
                    b2 = self._merge({
                        "topic": tgt,
                        "predicate": rev_pred,
                        "object": src,
                        "confidence": max(0.3, edge.weight * 0.8),
                    })
                    results.append(b2)

            if results and self.brain_graph:
                for b in results:
                    self.brain_graph.add_belief_node(
                        topic=b.topic,
                        predicate=b.predicate,
                        object_val=b.object,
                        confidence=b.confidence,
                        evidence_count=b.evidence_count,
                    )

            if results and self.event_bus:
                self.event_bus.publish("belief.updated", {
                    "beliefs": [b.to_dict() for b in results],
                    "source": "graph_sync",
                })
            return results
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[BeliefEngine] Graph sync error: {e}")
            return []

    def count(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._beliefs)

# knowledge\brain_graph.py
import logging
import threading
import time

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

NODE_TYPE_BELIEF = "belief"
NODE_TYPE_MEMORY = "memory"
NODE_TYPE_GOAL = "goal"
NODE_TYPE_THOUGHT = "thought"
NODE_TYPE_RULE = "rule"
NODE_TYPE_ENTITY = "entity"
NODE_TYPE_EMOTION = "emotion"
EDGE_BELIEF = "believes"
EDGE_RELATED = "related_to"
EDGE_HAS_SUBGOAL = "has_subgoal"
EDGE_REFERS_TO = "refers_to"
EDGE_APPLIES_TO = "applies_to"
EDGE_FOLLOWED_BY = "followed_by"

class BrainGraph:

    def __init__(self, graph=None, event_bus=None):
        from knowledge.graph import KnowledgeGraph
        self.graph = graph or KnowledgeGraph()
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._graph_to_belief = True
        self._graph_to_memory = True
        self._graph_to_goal = True
        self._graph_to_thought = True
        self._graph_to_rule = True

    def add_belief_node(self, topic, predicate, object_val, confidence=0.5, evidence_count=1):
        belief_id = f"belief:{topic}:{predicate}:{object_val}"
        self.graph.add_entity(
            belief_id,
            entity_type=NODE_TYPE_BELIEF,
            metadata={
                "topic": topic,
                "predicate": predicate,
                "object": object_val,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "evidence_count": evidence_count,
                "created_at": time.time(),
            },
        )
        self.graph.add_relation(
            topic, EDGE_BELIEF, belief_id,
            weight=confidence,
        )
        return belief_id

    def get_beliefs(self, topic=None):
        nodes = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_BELIEF)
        if topic:
            topic = topic.lower()
            return [
                n for n in nodes
                if n.metadata.get("topic") == topic
            ]
        return nodes

    def add_memory_node(self, memory_id, text, memory_type="conversation",
                        score=0.5, tags=None, emotion=None, importance=0.5, source="user"):
        self.graph.add_entity(
            f"mem:{memory_id}",
            entity_type=NODE_TYPE_MEMORY,
            metadata={
                "memory_id": memory_id,
                "text": text,
                "memory_type": memory_type,
                "score": score,
                "tags": tags or [],
                "emotion": emotion or {},
                "importance": importance,
                "source": source,
                "created_at": time.time(),
            },
        )
        return memory_id

    def add_memory_link(self, from_id, to_id, weight=0.5):
        self.graph.add_relation(
            f"mem:{from_id}",
            EDGE_RELATED,
            f"mem:{to_id}",
            weight=weight,
        )

    def get_memories(self, memory_type=None, limit=None):
        nodes = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_MEMORY)
        if memory_type:
            nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.metadata.get("memory_type") == memory_type]
        nodes.sort(key=lambda n: n.metadata.get("score", 0), reverse=True)
        if limit:
            nodes = nodes[:limit]
        return nodes

    def add_goal_node(self, goal_id, description, priority="normal",
                      status="active", context=None):
        self.graph.add_entity(
            f"goal:{goal_id}",
            entity_type=NODE_TYPE_GOAL,
            metadata={
                "goal_id": goal_id,
                "description": description,
                "priority": priority,
                "status": status,
                "context": context or {},
                "created_at": time.time(),
                "turn_count": 0,
            },
        )
        return goal_id

    def update_goal_node(self, goal_id, status=None, turn_count=None):
        node = self.graph.get_entity(f"goal:{goal_id}")
        if node:
            if status:
                node.metadata["status"] = status
            if turn_count is not None:
                node.metadata["turn_count"] = turn_count
            node.metadata["updated_at"] = time.time()

    def get_goals(self, status=None):
        nodes = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_GOAL)
        if status:
            nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.metadata.get("status") == status]
        nodes.sort(key=lambda n: n.metadata.get("turn_count", 0), reverse=True)
        return nodes

    def add_thought_node(self, thought_id, query="", conclusion="",
                         confidence=0.5, reasoning_steps=None):
        self.graph.add_entity(
            f"thought:{thought_id}",
            entity_type=NODE_TYPE_THOUGHT,
            metadata={
                "thought_id": thought_id,
                "query": query,
                "conclusion": conclusion,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "reasoning_steps": reasoning_steps or [],
                "created_at": time.time(),
            },
        )
        return thought_id

    def get_thoughts(self, limit=None):
        nodes = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_THOUGHT)
        nodes.sort(key=lambda n: n.metadata.get("created_at", 0), reverse=True)
        if limit:
            nodes = nodes[:limit]
        return nodes

    def add_rule_node(self, rule, context, confidence=0.5, source="experience"):
        rule_id = f"rule:{hash(rule)}:{hash(context)}"
        self.graph.add_entity(
            rule_id,
            entity_type=NODE_TYPE_RULE,
            metadata={
                "rule": rule,
                "context": context,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "source": source,
                "apply_count": 0,
                "created_at": time.time(),
            },
        )
        self.graph.add_relation(rule_id, EDGE_APPLIES_TO, context, weight=confidence)
        return rule_id

    def get_rules(self, context=None, min_confidence=0.0):
        nodes = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_RULE)
        nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.metadata.get("confidence", 0) >= min_confidence]
        if context:
            context = context.lower()
            nodes = [
                n for n in nodes
                if context in n.metadata.get("context", "").lower()
            ]
        nodes.sort(key=lambda n: n.metadata.get("confidence", 0), reverse=True)
        return nodes

    def add_world_entity(self, name, category="person", attributes=None):
        self.graph.add_entity(
            name,
            entity_type=category,
            metadata=dict(attributes or {}),
        )

    def get_world_entities(self, category=None):
        if category:
            return self.graph.get_entity_by_type(category)
        return self.graph.all_entities()

    def connect(self, source, relation, target, weight=1.0):
        self.graph.add_relation(source, relation, target, weight=weight)

    def add_emotion_snapshot(self, snapshot_id, state, profile, dominant):
        self.graph.add_entity(
            f"emotion:{snapshot_id}",
            entity_type=NODE_TYPE_EMOTION,
            metadata={
                "snapshot_id": snapshot_id,
                "state": dict(state),
                "profile": profile,
                "dominant": dominant,
                "created_at": time.time(),
            },
        )
        prev = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_EMOTION)
        if len(prev) >= 2:
            self.graph.add_relation(
                f"emotion:{prev[-2].name.split(':')[-1]}",
                EDGE_FOLLOWED_BY,
                f"emotion:{snapshot_id}",
                weight=0.5,
            )
        return snapshot_id

    def get_emotion_history(self, limit=20):
        nodes = self.graph.get_entity_by_type(NODE_TYPE_EMOTION)
        nodes.sort(key=lambda n: n.metadata.get("created_at", 0), reverse=True)
        return nodes[:limit]

    def stats(self):
        base = self.graph.stats()
        type_counts = self.graph.count_by_type()
        base["by_type"] = type_counts
        return base

    def all_entities(self):
        return self.graph.all_entities()

    def all_relations(self):
        return self.graph.all_relations()

    def search(self, query):
        return self.graph.search(query)

    def search_entities(self, query, node_type=None):
        return self.graph.search_entities(query, node_type=node_type)

    def get_entity(self, name):
        return self.graph.get_entity(name)

    def get_relations_for(self, entity):
        return self.graph.get_relations_for(entity)

    def query(self, entity, depth=1):
        return self.graph.query(entity, depth=depth)

    def most_connected(self, limit=10):
        return self.graph.most_connected(limit=limit)

    def save(self, path):
        return self.graph.save(path)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):
        from knowledge.graph import KnowledgeGraph
        kg = KnowledgeGraph.load(path)
        return cls(graph=kg)

# knowledge\builder.py
import os
import time
import logging
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class KnowledgeBaseBuilder:

    def __init__(self, indexer, document_store, vector_store, event_bus=None):
        self.indexer = indexer
        self.document_store = document_store
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._builds = []

    def build_from_text(self, text, source="", doc_type="text", title="", metadata=None):
        if not text:
            return {"success": False, "error": "no_text"}

        start = time.time()
        result = self.indexer.index_text(text, source=source, doc_type=doc_type, title=title, metadata=metadata)
        duration = round(time.time() - start, 3)

        summary = {
            "source": source,
            "method": "text",
            "indexed": result.get("indexed", 0),
            "duration": duration,
            "success": result.get("indexed", 0) > 0,
        }
        with self._lock:
            self._builds.append(summary)

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("knowledge.build_completed", summary)

        return summary

    def build_from_file(self, file_path, doc_type=None, metadata=None):
        if not os.path.isfile(file_path):
            return {"error": f"file_not_found:{file_path}"}

        start = time.time()
        result = self.indexer.index_file(file_path, doc_type=doc_type, metadata=metadata)
        duration = round(time.time() - start, 3)

        summary = {
            "source": file_path,
            "method": "file",
            "indexed": result.get("indexed", 0),
            "duration": duration,
            "success": result.get("indexed", 0) > 0,
            "error": result.get("error"),
        }
        with self._lock:
            self._builds.append(summary)

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("knowledge.build_completed", summary)

        return summary

    def build_from_directory(self, dir_path, pattern="*.*", recursive=True, doc_type="text"):
        if not os.path.isdir(dir_path):
            return {"error": f"directory_not_found:{dir_path}"}

        start = time.time()

        if not recursive:
            files = [os.path.join(dir_path, f) for f in os.listdir(dir_path)
                     if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(dir_path, f))]
        else:
            files = []
            for root, _, filenames in os.walk(dir_path):
                for fn in filenames:
                    files.append(os.path.join(root, fn))

        if pattern != "*.*":
            import fnmatch
            files = [f for f in files if fnmatch.fnmatch(os.path.basename(f), pattern)]

        total = 0
        skipped = 0
        errors = []

        for file_path in files:
            result = self.indexer.index_file(file_path, doc_type=doc_type)
            if result.get("indexed", 0) > 0:
                total += result["indexed"]
            elif result.get("error") == "up_to_date":
                skipped += 1
            else:
                errors.append({"file": file_path, "error": result.get("error")})

        duration = round(time.time() - start, 3)
        summary = {
            "source": dir_path,
            "method": "directory",
            "indexed": total,
            "files_found": len(files),
            "skipped": skipped,
            "errors": errors,
            "duration": duration,
            "success": len(errors) == 0,
        }
        with self._lock:
            self._builds.append(summary)

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("knowledge.build_completed", summary)

        return summary

    def search(self, query, top_k=5):
        return self.vector_store.search(query, top_k=top_k)

    def rebuild_all(self):
        docs = self.document_store.list()
        total = 0
        results = []

        for doc in docs:
            if os.path.isfile(doc.source):
                result = self.build_from_file(doc.source)
                if result.get("success"):
                    total += result.get("indexed", 0)
                results.append(result)

        return {"total_reindexed": total, "documents": len(docs), "results": results}

    def stats(self):
        store_stats = self.document_store.stats()
        indexer_stats = self.indexer.stats()
        vector_count = self.vector_store.count()

        with self._lock:
            builds_count = len(self._builds)

        return {
            "documents": store_stats,
            "indexer": indexer_stats,
            "vector_store_count": vector_count,
            "builds_completed": builds_count,
        }

    def history(self, limit=10):
        with self._lock:
            return self._builds[-limit:]

# knowledge\chunker.py
import re
import hashlib
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Chunk:

    def __init__(self, text, index, source, metadata=None):
        self.id = hashlib.md5(f"{source}:{index}:{text[:50]}".encode()).hexdigest()
        self.text = str(text)
        self.index = int(index)
        self.source = str(source)
        self.metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        self.token_estimate = max(1, len(self.text) // 4)
        self.length = len(self.text)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "text": self.text,
            "index": self.index,
            "source": self.source,
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
            "token_estimate": self.token_estimate,
            "length": self.length,
        }


class TextChunker:

    def __init__(self, chunk_size=512, overlap=64):
        self.chunk_size = max(64, int(chunk_size))
        self.overlap = min(self.chunk_size // 2, max(0, int(overlap)))

    def chunk_text(self, text, source="", metadata=None):
        if not text:
            return []

        text = str(text).strip()
        if not text:
            return []

        metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        paragraphs = self._split_paragraphs(text)
        chunks = []
        buffer = ""
        index = 0

        for para in paragraphs:
            para = para.strip()
            if not para:
                continue

            if len(buffer) + len(para) < self.chunk_size:
                buffer = (buffer + "\n\n" + para).strip()
            else:
                if buffer:
                    chunks.extend(self._split_large(buffer, source, metadata, index))
                    index += 1
                    buffer = self._carry_over(buffer)

                if len(para) > self.chunk_size:
                    sub_chunks = self._split_large(para, source, metadata, index)
                    chunks.extend(sub_chunks)
                    index += len(sub_chunks)
                    buffer = ""
                else:
                    buffer = para

        if buffer:
            chunks.extend(self._split_large(buffer, source, metadata, index))

        return chunks

    def _split_paragraphs(self, text):
        text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n")
        raw = re.split(r"\n\s*\n", text)
        return [p.strip() for p in raw if p.strip()]

    def _split_large(self, text, source, metadata, start_index):
        chunks = []
        sentences = self._split_sentences(text)
        buffer = ""
        index = start_index

        for sent in sentences:
            sent = sent.strip()
            if not sent:
                continue

            if len(buffer) + len(sent) < self.chunk_size:
                buffer = (buffer + " " + sent).strip()
            else:
                if buffer:
                    chunks.append(Chunk(buffer, index, source, metadata))
                    index += 1
                    buffer = self._carry_over(buffer)

                if len(sent) > self.chunk_size:
                    sub = self._split_by_tokens(sent, source, metadata, index)
                    chunks.extend(sub)
                    index += len(sub)
                    buffer = ""
                else:
                    buffer = sent

        if buffer:
            chunks.append(Chunk(buffer, index, source, metadata))

        return chunks

    def _split_sentences(self, text):
        parts = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", text)
        return [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]

    def _split_by_tokens(self, text, source, metadata, start_index):
        chunks = []
        index = start_index
        start = 0

        while start < len(text):
            end = min(start + self.chunk_size, len(text))
            chunk_text = text[start:end]
            if chunk_text.strip():
                chunks.append(Chunk(chunk_text.strip(), index, source, metadata))
                index += 1
            start = end - self.overlap if end < len(text) else end

        return chunks

    def _carry_over(self, buffer):
        words = buffer.split()
        carry_words = words[-self.overlap:] if self.overlap > 0 else []
        return " ".join(carry_words)

# knowledge\cognitive_graph.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CognitiveGraph:

    def __init__(self, graph):
        self.graph = graph
        self._cognitive_prefix = "cognitive:"

    def import_beliefs(self, belief_engine):
        if not belief_engine:
            return 0
        try:
            beliefs = belief_engine.all_beliefs()
        except Exception:
            return 0
        count = 0
        for b in beliefs:
            node_name = self._belief_node(b)
            node_type = "belief"
            self.graph.add_entity(node_name, entity_type=node_type, metadata={
                "confidence": b.confidence,
                "evidence_count": b.evidence_count,
                "predicate": b.predicate,
                "object": b.object[:100],
            })
            if b.topic and b.topic != "unknown":
                self.graph.add_entity(b.topic, entity_type="entity")
                self.graph.add_relation(node_name, "about", b.topic, weight=b.confidence)
            if b.object:
                obj_key = b.object.lower().strip()
                self.graph.add_entity(obj_key, entity_type="entity")
                self.graph.add_relation(node_name, "states", obj_key, weight=b.confidence)
            count += 1
        if count:
            logger.info(f"[CognitiveGraph] imported {count} beliefs")
        return count

    def import_goals(self, goal_manager):
        if not goal_manager:
            return 0
        try:
            goals = goal_manager.list()
        except Exception:
            return 0
        count = 0
        for g in goals:
            node_name = self._goal_node(g)
            node_type = f"goal:{g.status}"
            self.graph.add_entity(node_name, entity_type=node_type, metadata={
                "priority": g.priority,
                "status": g.status,
                "description": g.description[:100],
            })
            context = getattr(g, "context", {}) or {}
            for key, val in context.items():
                if isinstance(val, str) and len(val) > 1:
                    val_key = val.lower().strip()
                    self.graph.add_entity(val_key, entity_type="entity")
                    self.graph.add_relation(node_name, "context", val_key)
            count += 1
        if count:
            logger.info(f"[CognitiveGraph] imported {count} goals")
        return count

    def import_emotions(self, brain):
        if not brain:
            return 0
        try:
            state_data = brain.state.emotion.model_dump() if hasattr(brain.state, "emotion") else {}
        except Exception:
            return 0
        count = 0
        emotion_type = state_data.get("type", "neutral") if isinstance(state_data, dict) else "neutral"
        intensity = state_data.get("intensity", 0.5) if isinstance(state_data, dict) else 0.5
        node_name = self._emotion_node(emotion_type)
        self.graph.add_entity(node_name, entity_type="emotion", metadata={
            "emotion_type": emotion_type,
            "intensity": intensity,
        })
        count += 1
        if count:
            logger.debug(f"[CognitiveGraph] imported emotion: {emotion_type}")
        return count

    def to_graph_data(self):
        nodes = []
        edges = []
        for ent in self.graph.all_entities():
            node_type = ent.type
            is_cognitive = node_type in ("belief", "emotion") or node_type.startswith("goal:")
            nodes.append({
                "id": ent.name,
                "name": ent.name,
                "type": node_type,
                "group": "cognitive" if is_cognitive else "entity",
                "created_at": ent.created_at,
                "metadata": getattr(ent, "metadata", {}) or {},
            })
        for rel in self.graph.all_relations():
            source = rel.source
            target = rel.target
            if not source or not target:
                continue
            edges.append({
                "source": source,
                "target": target,
                "relation": rel.relation,
                "weight": rel.weight,
            })
        return {"nodes": nodes, "edges": edges}

    def stats(self):
        data = self.to_graph_data()
        cognitive = [n for n in data["nodes"] if n["group"] == "cognitive"]
        entities = [n for n in data["nodes"] if n["group"] == "entity"]
        return {
            "total_nodes": len(data["nodes"]),
            "cognitive_nodes": len(cognitive),
            "entity_nodes": len(entities),
            "edges": len(data["edges"]),
        }

    def _belief_node(self, belief):
        obj = belief.object[:30].replace(" ", "_")
        return f"{self._cognitive_prefix}belief:{belief.topic}:{obj}"

    def _goal_node(self, goal):
        desc = goal.description[:30].replace(" ", "_")
        return f"{self._cognitive_prefix}goal:{goal.id[:8]}:{desc}"

    def _emotion_node(self, emotion_type):
        return f"{self._cognitive_prefix}emotion:{emotion_type}"

# knowledge\consolidation.py
import difflib
import logging
import re

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

NOISE_WORDS = frozenset({
    "the", "a", "an", "of", "in", "on", "at", "to", "for", "with",
    "and", "or", "is", "are", "was", "were", "this", "that",
})


class ConsolidationEngine:

    def normalize_name(self, name):
        name = str(name).strip().lower()
        name = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", name)
        parts = [p for p in name.split() if p not in NOISE_WORDS]
        return " ".join(parts) if parts else name

    def merge_candidates(self, entities):
        if not entities:
            return []
        normalized = [(e, self.normalize_name(e)) for e in entities]
        groups = []
        assigned = set()
        for i, (orig, norm) in enumerate(normalized):
            if i in assigned:
                continue
            group = [orig]
            assigned.add(i)
            for j in range(i + 1, len(normalized)):
                if j in assigned:
                    continue
                other_norm = normalized[j][1]
                score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm, other_norm).ratio()
                if score >= 0.8:
                    group.append(normalized[j][0])
                    assigned.add(j)
            groups.append(group)
        return groups

    def deduplicate(self, graph, entities, relations):
        if not entities and not relations:
            return [], []

        existing_names = {e.name for e in graph.all_entities()}
        norm_to_existing = {}
        for ename in existing_names:
            norm_to_existing[self.normalize_name(ename)] = ename

        deduped_entities = []
        name_map = {}

        for ent in entities:
            if not isinstance(ent, dict):
                continue
            raw_name = ent.get("name", "").strip()
            if not raw_name or len(raw_name) < 2:
                continue
            norm = self.normalize_name(raw_name)
            if norm in norm_to_existing:
                name_map[raw_name.lower()] = norm_to_existing[norm]
            else:
                canonical = raw_name
                name_map[raw_name.lower()] = canonical
                norm_to_existing[norm] = canonical
                deduped_entities.append(ent)

        deduped_relations = []
        for rel in relations:
            if not isinstance(rel, dict):
                continue
            src = rel.get("source", "").strip().lower()
            tgt = rel.get("target", "").strip().lower()
            mapped_src = name_map.get(src, src)
            mapped_tgt = name_map.get(tgt, tgt)
            if mapped_src == mapped_tgt:
                continue
            deduped_relations.append({
                "source": mapped_src,
                "relation": rel.get("relation", "related_to"),
                "target": mapped_tgt,
                "confidence": rel.get("confidence", 0.5),
            })

        return deduped_entities, deduped_relations

# knowledge\document_store.py
import os
import time
import json
import hashlib
import logging
import threading

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class DocumentRecord:

    def __init__(self, source, doc_type, title="", metadata=None):
        self.source_id = hashlib.md5(str(source).encode()).hexdigest()
        self.source = str(source)
        self.doc_type = str(doc_type)
        self.title = str(title) or os.path.basename(str(source))
        self.metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        self.chunk_count = 0
        self.total_tokens = 0
        self.indexed_at = time.time()
        self.updated_at = self.indexed_at
        self.file_modified_at = os.path.getmtime(source) if os.path.isfile(source) else 0.0

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "source_id": self.source_id,
            "source": self.source,
            "doc_type": self.doc_type,
            "title": self.title,
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
            "chunk_count": self.chunk_count,
            "total_tokens": self.total_tokens,
            "indexed_at": self.indexed_at,
            "updated_at": self.updated_at,
            "file_modified_at": self.file_modified_at,
        }


class DocumentStore:

    def __init__(self, path=None):
        self.path = path
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._records = {}
        self._dirty = False

        if path:
            self._load()

    def add(self, source, doc_type, title="", metadata=None, chunk_count=0, total_tokens=0):
        with self._lock:
            record = DocumentRecord(source, doc_type, title, metadata)
            record.chunk_count = chunk_count
            record.total_tokens = total_tokens
            self._records[record.source_id] = record
            self._dirty = True
            if self.path:
                self._save()
            return record

    def get(self, source_id):
        with self._lock:
            return self._records.get(source_id)

    def get_by_source(self, source):
        source_id = hashlib.md5(str(source).encode()).hexdigest()
        with self._lock:
            return self._records.get(source_id)

    def remove(self, source_id):
        with self._lock:
            removed = self._records.pop(source_id, None)
            if removed:
                self._dirty = True
                if self.path:
                    self._save()
            return removed is not None

    def list(self, doc_type=None):
        with self._lock:
            if doc_type:
                return [r for r in self._records.values() if r.doc_type == doc_type]
            return list(self._records.values())

    def update_chunk_count(self, source, chunk_count, total_tokens):
        with self._lock:
            record = self.get_by_source(source)
            if record:
