                record.chunk_count = chunk_count
                record.total_tokens = total_tokens
                record.updated_at = time.time()
                self._dirty = True
                if self.path:
                    self._save()
                return True
            return False

    def needs_reindex(self, source):
        with self._lock:
            record = self.get_by_source(source)
            if record is None:
                return True
            if os.path.isfile(source):
                current_mtime = os.path.getmtime(source)
                if current_mtime != record.file_modified_at:
                    return True
            return False

    def count(self, doc_type=None):
        with self._lock:
            if doc_type:
                return sum(1 for r in self._records.values() if r.doc_type == doc_type)
            return len(self._records)

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            records = self._records.values()
            return {
                "total_documents": len(records),
                "total_chunks": sum(r.chunk_count for r in records),
                "total_tokens": sum(r.total_tokens for r in records),
                "types": {t: sum(1 for r in records if r.doc_type == t) for t in {r.doc_type for r in records}},
            }

    def _load(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                if os.path.exists(self.path):
                    with open(self.path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        data = json.load(f)
                    for item in data:
                        record = DocumentRecord(
                            source=item.get("source", ""),
                            doc_type=item.get("doc_type", "unknown"),
                            title=item.get("title", ""),
                            metadata=item.get("metadata", {}),
                        )
                        record.source_id = item.get("source_id", record.source_id)
                        record.chunk_count = item.get("chunk_count", 0)
                        record.total_tokens = item.get("total_tokens", 0)
                        record.indexed_at = item.get("indexed_at", time.time())
                        record.updated_at = item.get("updated_at", time.time())
                        record.file_modified_at = item.get("file_modified_at", 0.0)
                        self._records[record.source_id] = record
                    logger.info(f"[DocumentStore] Loaded {len(data)} records from {self.path}")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[DocumentStore] Load error: {e}")

    def _save(self):
        try:
            data = [r.to_dict() for r in self._records.values()]
            with open(self.path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            self._dirty = False
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[DocumentStore] Save error: {e}")

# knowledge\extractor.py
import json
import logging
import re

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EXTRACTION_PROMPT = """Extract entities and relationships from the text below.

Return ONLY valid JSON with this exact structure:
{{"entities": [
    {{"name": "EntityName", "type": "person|project|technology|character|place|skill|organization"}}
  ],
  "relations": [
    {{"source": "EntityName", "relation": "uses|builds|likes|contains|works_on|created_by", "target": "EntityName"}}
  ]
}}

Rules:
- Entities are real-world nouns: people, projects, technologies, characters, places, skills, organizations
- Relations connect two entities that appear in the entities list
- Use the simplest canonical form for names (e.g. "OpenRouter" not "open router")
- Minimum 2 characters per entity name
- Skip generic words like "this", "that", "it", "thing"

Text:
{text}"""


class EntityExtractor:

    def __init__(self, llm=None):
        self.llm = llm

    def extract(self, text):
        if self.llm:
            return self._llm_extract(text)
        entities = self._fallback_extract_entities(text)
        relations = self._fallback_extract_relations(text, entities)
        return {"entities": entities, "relations": relations}

    def _llm_extract(self, text):
        prompt = EXTRACTION_PROMPT.format(text=text[:2000])
        try:
            raw = self.llm.generate(
                system="You are a knowledge graph extractor. Return ONLY valid JSON.",
                user=prompt,
            )
            if not raw:
                return self._fallback_extract(text)
            start = raw.find("{")
            end = raw.rfind("}") + 1
            if start == -1 or end <= start:
                return self._fallback_extract(text)
            data = json.loads(raw[start:end])
            entities = data.get("entities", [])
            relations = data.get("relations", [])
            if not isinstance(entities, list):
                entities = []
            if not isinstance(relations, list):
                relations = []
            validated_entities = [
                e for e in entities
                if isinstance(e, dict) and len(e.get("name", "")) >= 2
            ]
            enames = {e["name"].lower().strip() for e in validated_entities}
            enames.add("user")
            validated_relations = [
                r for r in relations
                if isinstance(r, dict)
                and r.get("source", "").lower().strip() in enames
                and r.get("target", "").lower().strip() in enames
            ]
            return {
                "entities": validated_entities,
                "relations": validated_relations,
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[EntityExtractor] LLM extraction failed, falling back: {e}")
            return self._fallback_extract(text)

    def _fallback_extract(self, text):
        entities = self._fallback_extract_entities(text)
        relations = self._fallback_extract_relations(text, entities)
        return {"entities": entities, "relations": relations}

    def _fallback_extract_entities(self, text):
        entities = []
        seen = set()
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            for match in re.finditer(r"[A-Z][a-zA-Z]*(?:\s+[A-Z][a-zA-Z]*)*", line):
                name = match.group(0).strip()
                if len(name) < 2:
                    continue
                key = name.lower()
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                entities.append({"name": name, "type": "unknown", "confidence": 0.3})
        return entities

    def _fallback_extract_relations(self, text, entities):
        relations = []
        entity_names = {e["name"].lower() for e in entities}
        text_lower = text.lower()
        verb_relations = {
            "builds": re.compile(r"\b(build|builds|built|making|made)\b"),
            "uses": re.compile(r"\b(use|uses|using|used)\b"),
            "likes": re.compile(r"\b(like|likes|loved|loves)\b"),
            "works_on": re.compile(r"\b(work\s+on|works\s+on|working\s+on)\b"),
            "switched_to": re.compile(r"\b(switch|switches|switched)\s+(?:from\s+\w+\s+)?to\b"),
        }
        for sentence in re.split(r"[.!?\n]", text_lower):
            entity_in_sentence = [n for n in entity_names if n in sentence]
            skip_words = {"i", "we", "you", "they"}
            targets = [e for e in entity_in_sentence if e not in skip_words]
            if not targets:
                continue
            for rel_type, vpat in verb_relations.items():
                if vpat.search(sentence):
                    for t in targets:
                        relations.append({
                            "source": "user",
                            "relation": "uses" if rel_type == "switched_to" else rel_type,
                            "target": t,
                            "confidence": 0.3,
                        })
        return relations

    def extract_from_conversation(self, user_message, ai_response):
        return self.extract(user_message)

# knowledge\graph.py
# knowledge/graph.py
import time
import threading
import json
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Entity:

    def __init__(self, name, entity_type="unknown", metadata=None):
        self.name = str(name).strip().lower()
        self.type = str(entity_type).strip().lower()
        self.metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        self.created_at = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "type": self.type,
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
            "created_at": self.created_at,
        }

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"<Entity {self.name} ({self.type})>"


class Relation:

    def __init__(self, source, relation, target, weight=1.0, metadata=None):
        self.source = str(source).strip().lower()
        self.relation = str(relation).strip().lower()
        self.target = str(target).strip().lower()
        self.weight = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(weight)))
        self.metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        self.created_at = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "source": self.source,
            "relation": self.relation,
            "target": self.target,
            "weight": self.weight,
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
            "created_at": self.created_at,
        }

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"<Relation "
            f"{self.source} --[{self.relation}]--> {self.target}>"
        )


class KnowledgeGraph:

    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._entities = {}
        self._relations = []

    def add_entity(self, name, entity_type="unknown", metadata=None):
        name = str(name).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            if name not in self._entities:
                self._entities[name] = Entity(
                    name=name,
                    entity_type=entity_type,
                    metadata=metadata,
                )
            else:
                if metadata:
                    self._entities[name].metadata.update(metadata)
            return self._entities[name]

    def get_entity(self, name):
        name = str(name).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            return self._entities.get(name)

    def add_relation(self, source, relation, target, weight=1.0, metadata=None):
        source = str(source).strip().lower()
        target = str(target).strip().lower()

        with self._lock:
            if source not in self._entities:
                self._entities[source] = Entity(name=source)
            if target not in self._entities:
                self._entities[target] = Entity(name=target)

            rel = Relation(
                source=source,
                relation=relation,
                target=target,
                weight=weight,
                metadata=metadata,
            )
            self._relations.append(rel)
            return rel

    def query(self, entity, depth=1):
        entity = str(entity).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            visited = {entity}
            results = []
            queue = [(entity, 0)]

            while queue:
                current, d = queue.pop(0)
                if d >= depth:
                    continue

                for rel in self._relations:
                    if rel.source == current and rel.target not in visited:
                        results.append(rel)
                        visited.add(rel.target)
                        queue.append((rel.target, d + 1))
                    elif rel.target == current and rel.source not in visited:
                        rev_rel = Relation(
                            source=rel.target,
                            relation=rel.relation,
                            target=rel.source,
                            weight=rel.weight,
                        )
                        results.append(rev_rel)
                        visited.add(rel.source)
                        queue.append((rel.source, d + 1))

            return results

    def get_relations_for(self, entity):
        entity = str(entity).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            return [
                rel for rel in self._relations
                if rel.source == entity or rel.target == entity
            ]

    def all_entities(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._entities.values())

    def all_relations(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._relations)

    def to_dict(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "entities": {
                    name: e.to_dict()
                    for name, e in self._entities.items()
                },
                "relations": [
                    r.to_dict() for r in self._relations
                ],
            }

    def save(self, path):
        with self._lock:
            data = {
                "entities": {
                    name: e.to_dict()
                    for name, e in self._entities.items()
                },
                "relations": [
                    r.to_dict() for r in self._relations
                ],
            }
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)

        kg = cls()
        for name, e_data in data.get("entities", {}).items():
            kg._entities[name] = Entity(
                name=e_data.get("name", name),
                entity_type=e_data.get("type", "unknown"),
                metadata=e_data.get("metadata", {}),
            )
            kg._entities[name].created_at = e_data.get("created_at", time.time())

        for r_data in data.get("relations", []):
            kg._relations.append(Relation(
                source=r_data["source"],
                relation=r_data["relation"],
                target=r_data["target"],
                weight=r_data.get("weight", 1.0),
                metadata=r_data.get("metadata", {}),
            ))

        return kg

    def entity_count(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._entities)

    def relation_count(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._relations)

    def get_entity_by_type(self, entity_type):
        entity_type = str(entity_type).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            return [
                e for e in self._entities.values()
                if e.type == entity_type
            ]

    def most_connected(self, limit=10):
        with self._lock:
            degree = {}
            for rel in self._relations:
                degree[rel.source] = degree.get(rel.source, 0) + 1
                degree[rel.target] = degree.get(rel.target, 0) + 1
            sorted_nodes = sorted(
                degree.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
            )
            return sorted_nodes[:limit]

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            total_nodes = len(self._entities)
            total_edges = len(self._relations)
            if total_nodes == 0:
                return {
                    "nodes": 0, "edges": 0, "avg_degree": 0.0,
                    "isolated": 0, "most_connected": [],
                }
            degree = {}
            for rel in self._relations:
                degree[rel.source] = degree.get(rel.source, 0) + 1
                degree[rel.target] = degree.get(rel.target, 0) + 1
            avg_degree = sum(degree.values()) / total_nodes if total_nodes else 0.0
            isolated = sum(1 for e in self._entities if degree.get(e, 0) == 0)
            sorted_nodes = sorted(
                degree.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
            )[:5]
            return {
                "nodes": total_nodes,
                "edges": total_edges,
                "avg_degree": round(avg_degree, 2),
                "isolated": isolated,
                "most_connected": sorted_nodes,
            }

    def remove_entity(self, name):
        name = str(name).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            if name not in self._entities:
                return False
            del self._entities[name]
            self._relations = [
                rel for rel in self._relations
                if rel.source != name and rel.target != name
            ]
            return True

    def remove_relation(self, source, relation, target):
        source = str(source).strip().lower()
        relation = str(relation).strip().lower()
        target = str(target).strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            before = len(self._relations)
            self._relations = [
                rel for rel in self._relations
                if not (rel.source == source and rel.relation == relation and rel.target == target)
            ]
            return len(self._relations) < before

    def search(self, query):
        query = str(query).strip().lower()
        if not query:
            return []
        with self._lock:
            return [
                e for name, e in self._entities.items()
                if query in name
            ]

    def count_by_type(self):
        with self._lock:
            counts = {}
            for e in self._entities.values():
                t = e.type or "unknown"
                counts[t] = counts.get(t, 0) + 1
            return counts

    def stats_by_type(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "total_nodes": len(self._entities),
                "total_edges": len(self._relations),
                "by_type": self.count_by_type(),
            }

    def search_entities(self, query, node_type=None):
        query = str(query).strip().lower()
        if not query:
            return []
        with self._lock:
            results = []
            for name, e in self._entities.items():
                if node_type and e.type != node_type:
                    continue
                if query in name or any(query in str(v).lower() for v in e.metadata.values()):
                    results.append(e)
            return results

    def import_from_memory_graph(self, memory_graph):
        for node in getattr(memory_graph, "nodes", []):
            text = getattr(node, "text", "") or getattr(node, "content", "")
            mem_type = getattr(node, "memory_type", "") or getattr(node, "type", "")
            tags = getattr(node, "tags", [])

            if tags:
                for tag in tags:
                    self.add_entity(tag, entity_type="topic")
                    self.add_relation("memory", "tagged", tag)

            if mem_type in ("identity", "employment", "location"):
                self.add_entity("user", entity_type="person")
                self.add_relation(
                    "user",
                    mem_type,
                    text[:100],
                )

# knowledge\graph_manager.py
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GraphManager:

    def __init__(self, graph=None, extractor=None, consolidation=None, event_bus=None):
        from knowledge.graph import KnowledgeGraph
        from knowledge.extractor import EntityExtractor
        from knowledge.consolidation import ConsolidationEngine
        self.graph = graph or KnowledgeGraph()
        self.extractor = extractor or EntityExtractor()
        self.consolidation = consolidation or ConsolidationEngine()
        self.event_bus = event_bus

    def process_conversation(self, user_message, ai_response):
        extracted = self.extractor.extract_from_conversation(
            user_message, ai_response
        )
        raw_entities = extracted.get("entities", [])
        raw_relations = extracted.get("relations", [])

        raw_names = [e.get("name", "") for e in raw_entities if isinstance(e, dict)]
        deduped_entities, deduped_relations = self.consolidation.deduplicate(
            self.graph, raw_entities, raw_relations
        )

        added_count = 0
        for ent in deduped_entities:
            name = ent.get("name", "").strip()
            etype = ent.get("type", "unknown")
            confidence = ent.get("confidence", 0.5)
            self.graph.add_entity(
                name,
                entity_type=etype,
                metadata={"confidence": confidence, "source": "extractor"},
            )
            added_count += 1

        relation_added = 0
        for rel in deduped_relations:
            src = rel.get("source", "").strip()
            rel_type = rel.get("relation", "related_to")
            tgt = rel.get("target", "").strip()
            confidence = rel.get("confidence", 0.5)
            if src and tgt:
                self.graph.add_relation(
                    src, rel_type, tgt,
                    weight=confidence,
                    metadata={"source": "extractor"},
                )
                relation_added += 1

        duplicates_skipped = len(raw_names) - added_count

        summary = {
            "entities_added": added_count,
            "relations_added": relation_added,
            "duplicates_skipped": max(0, duplicates_skipped),
        }

        if self.event_bus:
            try:
                self.event_bus.publish("graph.updated", summary)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[GraphManager] Event publish failed: {e}")

        return summary

    def search(self, query):
        return self.graph.search(query)

    def query(self, entity, depth=1):
        return self.graph.query(entity, depth=depth)

    def stats(self):
        return self.graph.stats()

    def get_entity(self, name):
        return self.graph.get_entity(name)

    def add_entity(self, name, entity_type="unknown", metadata=None):
        return self.graph.add_entity(name, entity_type=entity_type, metadata=metadata)

    def add_relation(self, source, relation, target, weight=1.0, metadata=None):
        return self.graph.add_relation(source, relation, target, weight=weight, metadata=metadata)

    def entity_count(self):
        return self.graph.entity_count()

    def relation_count(self):
        return self.graph.relation_count()

    def get_entity_by_type(self, entity_type):
        return self.graph.get_entity_by_type(entity_type)

    def most_connected(self, limit=10):
        return self.graph.most_connected(limit=limit)

    def all_entities(self):
        return self.graph.all_entities()

    def get_relations_for(self, entity):
        return self.graph.get_relations_for(entity)

    def remove_entity(self, name):
        return self.graph.remove_entity(name)

    def remove_relation(self, source, relation, target):
        return self.graph.remove_relation(source, relation, target)

# knowledge\graph_reflection.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GraphReflection:

    def __init__(self, graph):
        self.graph = graph

    def identify_clusters(self):
        all_ents = self.graph.all_entities()
        all_rels = self.graph.all_relations()
        if not all_ents:
            return []
        adj = {e.name: set() for e in all_ents}
        for rel in all_rels:
            adj.setdefault(rel.source, set()).add(rel.target)
            adj.setdefault(rel.target, set()).add(rel.source)
        visited = set()
        clusters = []
        for node in adj:
            if node in visited:
                continue
            cluster = []
            queue = [node]
            while queue:
                current = queue.pop(0)
                if current in visited:
                    continue
                visited.add(current)
                cluster.append(current)
                for nbr in adj.get(current, set()):
                    if nbr not in visited:
                        queue.append(nbr)
            clusters.append(cluster)
        clusters.sort(key=len, reverse=True)
        return clusters

    def isolated_entities(self):
        all_rels = self.graph.all_relations()
        connected = set()
        for rel in all_rels:
            connected.add(rel.source)
            connected.add(rel.target)
        return [e for e in self.graph.all_entities() if e.name not in connected]

    def suggest_new_relations(self, max_suggestions=5):
        clusters = self.identify_clusters()
        if len(clusters) < 2:
            return []
        suggestions = []
        for i in range(len(clusters)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(clusters)):
                for src_name in clusters[i][:3]:
                    for tgt_name in clusters[j][:3]:
                        if len(suggestions) >= max_suggestions:
                            return suggestions
                        existing = self.graph.get_relations_for(src_name)
                        already = any(
                            r.source == tgt_name or r.target == tgt_name
                            for r in existing
                        )
                        if not already:
                            suggestions.append({
                                "source": src_name,
                                "target": tgt_name,
                                "suggested_relation": "related_to",
                                "reason": "bridges two clusters",
                            })
        return suggestions

    def summary(self):
        clusters = self.identify_clusters()
        isolated = self.isolated_entities()
        suggestions = self.suggest_new_relations(max_suggestions=3)
        total = self.graph.entity_count()
        rels = self.graph.relation_count()
        return {
            "total_entities": total,
            "total_relations": rels,
            "clusters": len(clusters),
            "largest_cluster_size": len(clusters[0]) if clusters else 0,
            "isolated_count": len(isolated),
            "suggested_new_relations": len(suggestions),
            "suggestions": suggestions,
        }

    def recent_activity(self, minutes=60):
        cutoff = time.time() - (minutes * 60)
        recent_ents = [
            e for e in self.graph.all_entities()
            if getattr(e, "created_at", 0) >= cutoff
        ]
        recent_rels = [
            r for r in self.graph.all_relations()
            if getattr(r, "created_at", 0) >= cutoff
        ]
        return {
            "recent_entities": len(recent_ents),
            "recent_relations": len(recent_rels),
            "entities": [e.name for e in recent_ents],
            "relations": [
                f"{r.source} --{r.relation}--> {r.target}"
                for r in recent_rels
            ],
        }

# knowledge\indexer.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Indexer:

    def __init__(self, vector_store, chunker, document_store=None, event_bus=None):
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.chunker = chunker
        self.document_store = document_store
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._indexed_count = 0

    def index_text(self, text, source="", doc_type="text", title="", metadata=None):
        if not text:
            return {"indexed": 0, "error": "no_text"}

        metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        chunks = self.chunker.chunk_text(text, source=source, metadata=metadata)

        return self._index_chunks(chunks, source, doc_type, title, metadata)

    def index_file(self, file_path, doc_type=None, metadata=None):
        import os

        if not os.path.isfile(file_path):
            return {"indexed": 0, "error": f"file_not_found:{file_path}"}

        if self.document_store and not self.document_store.needs_reindex(file_path):
            return {"indexed": 0, "error": "up_to_date"}

        try:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
                text = f.read()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[Indexer] Read error {file_path}: {e}")
            return {"indexed": 0, "error": f"read_failed:{e}"}

        detected_type = doc_type or self._detect_type(file_path)
        title = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(file_path))[0]
        meta = dict(metadata or {})
        meta["file_path"] = file_path
        meta["file_size"] = os.path.getsize(file_path)

        return self.index_text(text, source=file_path, doc_type=detected_type, title=title, metadata=meta)

    def index_directory(self, dir_path, pattern="*.txt", recursive=True, doc_type="text"):
        import glob as glob_mod
        import os

        if not os.path.isdir(dir_path):
            return {"indexed": 0, "error": f"directory_not_found:{dir_path}"}

        total_indexed = 0
        total_files = 0
        skipped = 0
        errors = []

        if recursive:
            search = os.path.join(dir_path, "**", pattern)
        else:
            search = os.path.join(dir_path, pattern)

        for file_path in glob_mod.glob(search, recursive=recursive):
            if os.path.isfile(file_path):
                total_files += 1
                result = self.index_file(file_path, doc_type=doc_type)
                if result.get("indexed", 0) > 0:
                    total_indexed += result["indexed"]
                elif result.get("error") == "up_to_date":
                    skipped += 1
                else:
                    errors.append({"file": file_path, "error": result.get("error")})

        summary = {
            "indexed": total_indexed,
            "files_processed": total_files,
            "skipped": skipped,
            "errors": errors,
        }

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("indexer.completed", summary)

        return summary

    def _index_chunks(self, chunks, source, doc_type, title, metadata):
        count = 0
        total_tokens = 0
        errors = 0

        for chunk in chunks:
            try:
                chunk_meta = dict(chunk.metadata)
                chunk_meta["chunk_index"] = chunk.index
                chunk_meta["chunk_source"] = chunk.source
                chunk_meta["chunk_id"] = chunk.id
                chunk_meta["doc_type"] = doc_type
                chunk_meta["title"] = title
                chunk_meta["source"] = source

                self.vector_store.add(chunk.text, metadata=chunk_meta)
                count += 1
                total_tokens += chunk.token_estimate
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[Indexer] Chunk index error: {e}")
                errors += 1

        if self.document_store:
            existing = self.document_store.get_by_source(source)
            if existing:
                self.document_store.update_chunk_count(source, count, total_tokens)
            else:
                self.document_store.add(source, doc_type, title, metadata, count, total_tokens)

        self._indexed_count += count

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("indexer.chunks_indexed", {
                "source": source,
                "chunks": count,
                "errors": errors,
                "total_tokens": total_tokens,
            })

        return {"indexed": count, "chunks": len(chunks), "errors": errors, "total_tokens": total_tokens}

    def _detect_type(self, file_path):
        ext = file_path.lower()
        if ext.endswith(".md"):
            return "markdown"
        if ext.endswith((".py", ".js", ".ts", ".java", ".cpp", ".go", ".rs")):
            return "code"
        if ext.endswith((".txt", ".rst")):
            return "text"
        if ext.endswith((".json", ".yaml", ".yml", ".toml", ".ini", ".cfg")):
            return "config"
        if ext.endswith((".html", ".htm", ".xml")):
            return "markup"
        if ext.endswith(".csv"):
            return "data"
        return "text"

    def stats(self):
        return {"total_indexed_chunks": self._indexed_count}

# knowledge\obsidian_reader.py
import os
import time
import hashlib
import logging
import threading
from pathlib import Path

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ObsidianReader:

    def __init__(self, vault_path=None, entity_subdir="Entities"):
        from config.paths import OBSIDIAN_VAULT
        vault = Path(vault_path or OBSIDIAN_VAULT)
        self.vault_root = vault
        self.entity_dir = vault / entity_subdir if entity_subdir else vault
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.cache = {}
        self.file_hashes = {}
        self.last_reload = 0
        self.reload_interval = 5

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“– SAFE FILE READ
    # ==================================================

    def _read(self, folder, file):
        try:
            path = self.vault_root / folder / file
            if not path.exists():
                return ""
            current_hash = self._file_hash(path)
            with self._lock:
                cached_hash = self.file_hashes.get(str(path))
                if str(path) in self.cache and cached_hash == current_hash:
                    return self.cache[str(path)]
            content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            with self._lock:
                self.cache[str(path)] = content
                self.file_hashes[str(path)] = current_hash
            return content
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[ObsidianReader] read error: {e}")
            return ""

    def _file_hash(self, path):
        try:
            return hashlib.md5(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        except Exception:
            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  STRUCTURED MEMORY EXPORT (legacy compat)
    # ==================================================

    def get_all(self):
        return {
            "identity": self._parse_markdown(self._read("identity", "user.md")),
            "preferences": self._parse_markdown(self._read("preferences", "preferences.md")),
            "relationship": self._parse_markdown(self._read("relationships", "state.md")),
            "reflections": self._parse_markdown(self._read("reflections", "reflections.md")),
            "system": self._parse_markdown(self._read("system", "notes.md")),
        }

    def load_memories(self):
        memories = []
        folders = [
            "conversations", "important", "identity",
            "preferences", "relationships", "reflections", "archive",
        ]
        try:
            for folder in folders:
                folder_path = self.vault_root / folder
                if not folder_path.exists():
                    continue
                for filename in os.listdir(str(folder_path)):
                    if not filename.endswith(".md"):
                        continue
                    content = self._read(folder, filename)
                    parsed = self._extract_entries(content)
                    memories.extend(parsed)
            return memories
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[ObsidianReader] load_memories error: {e}")
            return []

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  ENTITY NOTE READING
    # ==================================================

    def read_entity(self, name):
        sanitized = name.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
        sanitized = "".join(c for c in sanitized if c.isalnum() or c in ("_", "-"))
        filepath = self.entity_dir / f"{sanitized}.md"
        if not filepath.exists():
            return None
        content = filepath.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        return {"name": name, "content": content, "path": str(filepath)}

    def list_entities(self):
        if not self.entity_dir.exists():
            return []
        entities = []
        for fpath in self.entity_dir.iterdir():
            if fpath.suffix != ".md":
                continue
            entities.append(fpath.stem)
        return sorted(entities)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  ENTRY PARSING (legacy compat)
    # ==================================================

    def _extract_entries(self, content):
        if not content:
            return []
        entries = []
        chunks = content.split("\n---")
        for chunk in chunks:
            chunk = chunk.strip()
            if not chunk:
                continue
            try:
                memory = {
                    "id": self._extract_field(chunk, "id"),
                    "timestamp": self._extract_field(chunk, "timestamp"),
                    "importance": self._safe_float(self._extract_field(chunk, "importance"), 0.5),
                    "emotion": self._extract_field(chunk, "emotion"),
                    "intensity": self._safe_float(self._extract_field(chunk, "intensity"), 0.5),
                    "user": self._extract_section(chunk, "### ðŸ‘¤ User"),
                    "ai": self._extract_section(chunk, "### ðŸŒ¸ Mai"),
                    "tags": self._extract_tags(chunk),
                }
                if not memory["user"]:
                    continue
                entries.append(memory)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[ObsidianReader] entry parse: {e}")
        return entries

    def _extract_field(self, text, field):
        try:
            for line in text.splitlines():
                line = line.strip()
                if line.startswith(f"{field}:"):
                    return line.split(":", 1)[1].strip()
            return ""
        except Exception:
            return ""

    def _extract_section(self, text, section):
        try:
            lines = text.splitlines()
            capture = False
            collected = []
            for line in lines:
                if line.strip() == section:
                    capture = True
                    continue
                if capture and line.startswith("### "):
                    break
                if capture:
                    collected.append(line)
            return "\n".join(collected).strip()
        except Exception:
            return ""

    def _extract_tags(self, text):
        tags = []
        try:
            for line in text.splitlines():
                if line.startswith("tags:"):
                    raw = line.split(":", 1)[1].strip()
                    raw = raw.replace("[", "").replace("]", "")
                    for tag in raw.split(","):
                        cleaned = tag.strip().replace("'", "").replace('"', "")
                        if cleaned:
                            tags.append(cleaned)
            return list(dict.fromkeys(tags))
        except Exception:
            return []

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  UTILITY
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_float(self, value, default=0.0):
        try:
            return float(value)
        except Exception:
            return default

    def _parse_markdown(self, content):
        if not content:
            return {}
        result = {}
        try:
            for line in content.splitlines():
                line = line.strip()
                if ": " in line and not line.startswith("#"):
                    key, _, value = line.partition(": ")
                    key = key.strip().lower()
                    value = value.strip()
                    if value.lower() == "true":
                        value = True
                    elif value.lower() == "false":
                        value = False
                    else:
                        try:
                            value = float(value)
                        except ValueError:
                            pass
                    result[key] = value
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[ObsidianReader] markdown parse: {e}")
            return {}

    def clear_cache(self):
        with self._lock:
            self.cache.clear()
            self.file_hashes.clear()

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "vault": str(self.vault_root),
                "cached_files": len(self.cache),
                "tracked_hashes": len(self.file_hashes),
            }

# knowledge\obsidian_writer.py
import os
import time
import uuid
import hashlib
import logging
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ObsidianWriter:

    def __init__(self, graph_manager=None, vault_path=None, subdir="Entities"):
        from config.paths import OBSIDIAN_VAULT
        self.graph = graph_manager
        vault = Path(vault_path or OBSIDIAN_VAULT)
        self.vault_root = vault
        self.entity_dir = (vault / subdir) if subdir else vault
        self.entity_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._write_count = 0
        self._delete_count = 0
        self._mem_write_count = 0
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.hash_cache = set()
        self.memory_folders = [
            "conversations", "identity", "preferences",
            "relationships", "important", "reflections", "archive",
        ]
        for folder in self.memory_folders:
            (vault / folder).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  KNOWLEDGE GRAPH SYNC
    # ==================================================

    def sync(self):
        if not self.graph:
            return {"written": 0, "deleted": 0, "total": 0, "path": str(self.entity_dir)}
        with self._lock:
            self._write_count = 0
            self._delete_count = 0
            all_entities = self.graph.all_entities()
            current = set()
            for entity in all_entities:
                self.write_entity(entity)
                current.add(self._sanitize(entity.name))
            self._delete_stale(current)
            stats = {
            "written": self._write_count,
            "deleted": self._delete_count,
            "total": len(all_entities),
            "path": str(self.entity_dir),
        }
            logger.info(f"[ObsidianWriter] graph sync: {stats}")
            return stats

    def write_entity(self, entity):
        content = self._build_content(entity)
        filename = self._sanitize(entity.name) + ".md"
        filepath = self.entity_dir / filename
        filepath.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
        self._write_count += 1

    def _build_content(self, entity):
        lines = []
        lines.append("---")
        lines.append(f'type: "{entity.type}"')
        created = getattr(entity, "created_at", time.time())
        lines.append(f"created_at: {created:.0f}")
        metadata = getattr(entity, "metadata", {}) or {}
        for k, v in metadata.items():
            if isinstance(v, str):
                lines.append(f'{k}: "{v}"')
            else:
                lines.append(f"{k}: {v}")
        lines.append("---")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"# {entity.name}")
        lines.append("")

        related = self.graph.get_relations_for(entity.name) if self.graph and hasattr(self.graph, "get_relations_for") else []
        if related:
            lines.append("## Relations")
            lines.append("")
            for rel in related:
                if rel.source == entity.name:
                    lines.append(f"- {rel.relation} â†’ [[{rel.target}]]")
                else:
                    lines.append(f"- [[{rel.source}]] â†’ {rel.relation}")
            lines.append("")

        backlinks = self._find_backlinks(entity.name)
        if backlinks:
            lines.append("## Referenced By")
            lines.append("")
            for name in sorted(backlinks):
                lines.append(f"- [[{name}]]")
            lines.append("")

        return "\n".join(lines)

    def _find_backlinks(self, entity_name):
        if not self.graph:
            return set()
        backlinks = set()
        all_entities = self.graph.all_entities()
        for ent in all_entities:
            if ent.name == entity_name:
                continue
            rels = self.graph.get_relations_for(ent.name) if hasattr(self.graph, "get_relations_for") else []
            for rel in rels:
                if rel.target == entity_name or rel.source == entity_name:
                    backlinks.add(ent.name)
        return backlinks

    def _delete_stale(self, current_names):
        for fpath in self.entity_dir.iterdir():
            if fpath.suffix != ".md":
                continue
            stem = fpath.stem.lower()
            if stem not in current_names:
                try:
                    fpath.unlink()
                    with self._lock:
                        self._delete_count += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"[ObsidianWriter] failed to delete {fpath}: {e}")

    def _sanitize(self, name):
        safe = name.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
        safe = "".join(c for c in safe if c.isalnum() or c in ("_", "-"))
        return safe or "unnamed"

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MEMORY WRITE (legacy conversation persistence)
    # ==================================================
    def write(self, folder, user, ai, tags, emotion, score, metadata=None):
        try:
            if not isinstance(folder, str) or folder not in self.memory_folders:
                folder = "conversations"
            timestamp = datetime.now()
            filename = timestamp.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") + ".md"
            path = self.vault_root / folder / filename
            user = str(user or "").strip()
            ai = str(ai or "").strip()
            signature = hashlib.md5(f"{folder}:{user}{ai}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
            with self._lock:
                if signature in self.hash_cache:
                    return False
                self.hash_cache.add(signature)
            metadata = metadata or {}
            tags = tags or []
            emotion = emotion or {"type": "neutral", "intensity": 0.5}
            memory_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
            wiki_tags = " ".join([f"[[{t}]]" for t in tags])
            md_tags = " ".join([f"#{t}" for t in tags])
            entry = f"""

---

id: {memory_id}
timestamp: {timestamp.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}
importance: {round(float(score), 2)}
emotion: {emotion.get("type")}
intensity: {emotion.get("intensity", 0.5)}
tags: {tags}
---

## ðŸ•’ {timestamp.strftime("%H:%M:%S")}

### ðŸ‘¤ User
{user}

### ðŸŒ¸ Mai
{ai}

### ðŸ§  Emotional Context
Mood: {emotion.get("type")}
Intensity: {emotion.get("intensity", 0.5)}

### ðŸ·ï¸ Tags
{md_tags}

### ðŸ”— Memory Links
{wiki_tags}

### ðŸ“Š Metadata
{metadata}

---

"""
            with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(entry)
            with self._lock:
                self._mem_write_count += 1
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[ObsidianWriter] memory write error: {e}")
            return False

    def write_memory(self, routes, user, ai, tags, emotion, score, metadata=None):
        if not routes:
            routes = ["conversations"]
        unique_routes = list(dict.fromkeys(routes))
        results = {}
        for folder in unique_routes:
            if folder not in self.memory_folders:
                logger.debug(f"[ObsidianWriter] unknown folder: {folder}")
                continue
            results[folder] = self.write(
                folder=folder, user=user, ai=ai, tags=tags,
                emotion=emotion, score=score, metadata=metadata,
            )
        return results

    def write_important(self, text, tags=None, emotion=None, score=0.9):
        return self.write(
            folder="important", user=text, ai="",
            tags=tags or [], emotion=emotion or {"type": "important", "intensity": 1.0},
            score=score,
        )

    def write_reflection(self, reflection):
        return self.write(
            folder="reflections", user=reflection, ai="",
            tags=["reflection"], emotion={"type": "thoughtful", "intensity": 0.7},
            score=0.8,
        )

    def archive(self, text):
        return self.write(
            folder="archive", user=text, ai="",
            tags=["archive"], emotion={"type": "neutral", "intensity": 0.3},
            score=0.2,
        )

    def clear_cache(self):
        with self._lock:
            self.hash_cache.clear()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "vault": str(self.vault_root),
                "entity_dir": str(self.entity_dir),
                "memory_folders": self.memory_folders,
                "cached_hashes": len(self.hash_cache),
                "memory_writes": self._mem_write_count,
            }

# knowledge\reasoning.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GraphReasoning:

    def __init__(self, graph_manager=None):
        self.graph_manager = graph_manager

    def neighbors(self, entity, depth=1):
        if not self.graph_manager:
            return []
        try:
            relations = self.graph_manager.query(entity, depth=depth)
            seen = set()
            results = []
            for rel in relations:
                for name in (rel.source, rel.target):
                    if name != entity.lower() and name not in seen:
                        seen.add(name)
                        ent = self.graph_manager.get_entity(name)
                        results.append({
                            "name": name,
                            "type": ent.type if ent else "unknown",
                            "relation": rel.relation,
                            "weight": rel.weight,
                        })
            return results
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[GraphReasoning] neighbors error: {e}")
            return []

    def shortest_path(self, source, target):
        if not self.graph_manager:
            return []
        source = source.lower().strip()
        target = target.lower().strip()
        if source == target:
            return [source]
        try:
            visited = {source}
            queue = [[source]]
            while queue:
                path = queue.pop(0)
                current = path[-1]
                relations = self.graph_manager.query(current, depth=1)
                for rel in relations:
                    neighbor = rel.target if rel.source == current else rel.source
                    if neighbor == target:
                        return path + [neighbor]
                    if neighbor not in visited:
                        visited.add(neighbor)
                        queue.append(path + [neighbor])
            return []
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[GraphReasoning] shortest_path error: {e}")
            return []

    def query_by_type(self, entity_type):
        if not self.graph_manager:
            return []
        try:
            entities = self.graph_manager.get_entity_by_type(entity_type)
            return [{"name": e.name, "type": e.type} for e in entities]
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[GraphReasoning] query_by_type error: {e}")
            return []

    def recent_entities(self, minutes=60):
        if not self.graph_manager:
            return []
        try:
            cutoff = time.time() - (minutes * 60)
            all_ents = self.graph_manager.graph.all_entities() if hasattr(self.graph_manager, 'graph') else []
            recent = []
            for e in all_ents:
                created = getattr(e, 'created_at', 0)
                if created >= cutoff:
                    recent.append({"name": e.name, "type": e.type, "created_at": created})
            return sorted(recent, key=lambda x: x["created_at"], reverse=True)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[GraphReasoning] recent_entities error: {e}")
            return []

    def what_connects(self, source, target):
        if not self.graph_manager:
            return []
        path = self.shortest_path(source, target)
        if not path:
            return []
        edges = []
        for i in range(len(path) - 1):
            relations = self.graph_manager.query(path[i], depth=1)
            for rel in relations:
                r_src = rel.source.lower()
                r_tgt = rel.target.lower()
                if r_src == path[i].lower() and r_tgt == path[i + 1].lower():
                    edges.append(f"{rel.source} --[{rel.relation}]--> {rel.target}")
                    break
                elif r_tgt == path[i].lower() and r_src == path[i + 1].lower():
                    edges.append(f"{rel.target} --[{rel.relation}]--> {rel.source}")
                    break
        return edges

    def answer(self, query):
        q = query.lower().strip()
        if not q:
            return "Please provide a query."

        if q.startswith("what ") and "connect" in q:
            parts = q.replace("what connects", "").replace("?", "").strip()
            tokens = [t.strip() for t in parts.split("and") if t.strip()]
            if len(tokens) >= 2:
                edges = self.what_connects(tokens[0], tokens[-1])
                if edges:
                    return "Connection: " + " â†’ ".join(edges)
                return f"No connection found between '{tokens[0]}' and '{tokens[-1]}'."

        if q.startswith("what ") and ("related" in q or "neighbor" in q):
            target = q.replace("what", "").replace("is related to", "").replace("are the neighbors of", "").replace("?", "").strip()
            if target:
                nbrs = self.neighbors(target, depth=1)
                if nbrs:
                    lines = [f"{n['name']} ({n['type']}) â€” via {n['relation']}" for n in nbrs]
                    return f"Entities related to '{target}':\n" + "\n".join(lines)
                return f"No related entities found for '{target}'."

        if q.startswith("what ") and "type" in q:
            etype = q.replace("what", "").replace("type", "").replace("are the", "").replace("is", "").replace("?", "").strip()
            if etype:
                ents = self.query_by_type(etype)
                if ents:
                    names = [e["name"] for e in ents]
                    return f"Entities of type '{etype}': {', '.join(names)}"
                return f"No entities of type '{etype}' found."

        if q.startswith("path ") or q.startswith("shortest path "):
            parts = q.replace("shortest path", "").replace("path", "").replace("from", "").replace("to", "").replace("?", "").strip()
            tokens = [t.strip() for t in parts.split() if t.strip()]
            if len(tokens) >= 2:
                path = self.shortest_path(tokens[0], tokens[-1])
                if path:
                    return f"Path: {' â†’ '.join(path)}"
                return f"No path between '{tokens[0]}' and '{tokens[-1]}'."

        return "Try: 'what connects X and Y', 'what is related to X', 'path from X to Y'."

    def format_context(self, query, max_items=5):
        if not self.graph_manager:
            return ""
        try:
            nbrs = self.neighbors(query, depth=1)
            if not nbrs:
                return ""
            lines = [f"[graph] {n['name']} ({n['type']}) â€” {n['relation']}" for n in nbrs[:max_items]]
            return "\n".join(lines)
        except Exception:
            return ""

# knowledge\retriever.py
# knowledge/retriever.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class AdvancedRetriever:

    def __init__(self, vector_store=None, knowledge_graph=None,
                 belief_engine=None, attention=None, event_bus=None,
                 graph_reasoning=None, emotion=None):
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.knowledge_graph = knowledge_graph
        self.belief_engine = belief_engine
        self.attention = attention
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.graph_reasoning = graph_reasoning
        self.emotion = emotion

    def retrieve(self, query, attention_focus=None, top_k=5, speaker=None):
        if not query:
            return []

        results = []
        seen_content = set()

        if self.vector_store:
            try:
                vector_results = self.vector_store.search(query, top_k=top_k)
                for item in vector_results:
                    text = ""
                    score = 0.5
                    if isinstance(item, dict):
                        text = item.get("text", item.get("content", ""))
                        score = item.get("score", item.get("relevance", 0.5))
                    else:
                        text = str(item)

                    # Filter by speaker if specified
                    if speaker and isinstance(item, dict):
                        meta = item.get("metadata", {}) or {}
                        item_speaker = meta.get("speaker", "")
                        if item_speaker and item_speaker != speaker:
                            continue

                    content = str(text).strip()
                    if content and content not in seen_content:
                        seen_content.add(content)
                        results.append({
                            "content": content,
                            "score": float(score),
                            "source": "vector",
                        })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[Retriever] Vector search error: {e}")

        if self.belief_engine:
            try:
                beliefs = self.belief_engine.query("user")
                for b in beliefs:
                    content = f"{b.topic} {b.predicate} {b.object}"
                    if content not in seen_content:
                        seen_content.add(content)
                        results.append({
                            "content": content,
                            "score": b.confidence * 0.9,
                            "source": "belief",
                        })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[Retriever] Belief query error: {e}")

        if self.knowledge_graph:
            try:
                enriched = self._enriched_graph_retrieval(
                    query, seen_content
                )
                for item in enriched:
                    seen_content.add(item["content"])
                    results.append(item)

                # Fallback: direct entity-term matching for remaining terms
                if not enriched:
                    candidates = set()
                    terms = [t.lower().strip() for t in query.split() if len(t) > 2]
                    for term in terms:
                        for entity in self.knowledge_graph.all_entities():
                            if term in entity.name.lower():
                                candidates.add(entity.name)

                    seen_rels = set()
                    for cand in candidates:
                        rels = self.knowledge_graph.query(cand, depth=2)
                        for rel in rels:
                            content = f"{rel.source} -- {rel.relation} --> {rel.target}"
                            if content not in seen_content and content not in seen_rels:
                                seen_rels.add(content)
                                seen_content.add(content)
                                results.append({
                                    "content": content,
                                    "score": rel.weight * 0.7,
                                    "source": "knowledge_graph",
                                })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[Retriever] Knowledge graph error: {e}")

        if attention_focus and self.attention:
            try:
                focus_results = self.attention.retrieve(attention_focus)
                for item in focus_results:
                    content = item.get("content", "")
                    if content and content not in seen_content:
                        seen_content.add(content)
                        results.append({
                            "content": content,
                            "score": item.get("relevance", 0.5),
                            "source": "attention",
                        })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[Retriever] Attention error: {e}")

        if self.emotion:
            try:
                retrieval_bias = self.emotion.influence("retrieval")
                for r in results:
                    r["score"] = r["score"] * retrieval_bias
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[Retriever] Emotion influence: {e}")

        results.sort(key=lambda r: r["score"], reverse=True)
        results = self._diversify(results, top_k)

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("retriever.results", {
                "query": query,
                "count": len(results),
                "emotion_bias": retrieval_bias if self.emotion else None,
            })

        return results[:top_k]

    def _enriched_graph_retrieval(self, query, seen_content):
        if not self.graph_reasoning or not self.knowledge_graph:
            return []

        items = []
        seen_graph = set()
        q = query.lower().strip()

        try:
            # 1. Type-based â€” e.g. "what technologies does Mai use"
            type_keywords = ["type", "kind", "sort", "category", "class"]
            if any(k in q for k in type_keywords):
                type_candidates = [w for w in q.split() if len(w) > 2
                                   and w not in type_keywords]
                for tc in type_candidates:
                    ents = self.graph_reasoning.query_by_type(tc)
                    for e in ents:
                        content = f"[graph] {e['name']} ({e['type']})"
                        if content not in seen_content and content not in seen_graph:
                            seen_graph.add(content)
                            items.append({
                                "content": content,
                                "score": 0.85,
                                "source": "knowledge_graph",
                            })

            # 2. Neighbor expansion â€” entities related to query terms
            for term in q.split():
                if len(term) <= 2:
                    continue
                nbrs = self.graph_reasoning.neighbors(term, depth=1)
                for n in nbrs:
                    content = f"[graph] {n['name']} ({n['type']}) â€” {n['relation']}"
                    if content not in seen_content and content not in seen_graph:
                        seen_graph.add(content)
                        items.append({
                            "content": content,
                            "score": n.get("weight", 0.7) * 0.8,
                            "source": "knowledge_graph",
                        })

            # 3. Connection path â€” e.g. "what connects X and Y"
            if q.startswith("what connects") or "connect" in q:
                parts = q.replace("what connects", "").replace("?", "").strip()
                tokens = [t.strip() for t in parts.split("and") if t.strip()]
                if len(tokens) >= 2:
                    edges = self.graph_reasoning.what_connects(
                        tokens[0], tokens[-1]
                    )
                    for edge in edges:
                        if edge not in seen_graph:
                            seen_graph.add(edge)
                            items.append({
                                "content": f"[graph path] {edge}",
                                "score": 0.9,
                                "source": "knowledge_graph",
                            })

        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[Retriever] Enriched graph retrieval: {e}")

        return items

    def _diversify(self, results, top_k):
        if len(results) <= 1:
            return results

        diversified = [results[0]]
        sources = {results[0]["source"]}
        seen_texts = {results[0]["content"]}

        for r in results[1:]:
            if len(diversified) >= top_k:
                break
            if r["source"] not in sources or r["content"] not in seen_texts:
                diversified.append(r)
                sources.add(r["source"])
                seen_texts.add(r["content"])

        if len(diversified) < top_k:
            for r in results[1:]:
                if len(diversified) >= top_k:
                    break
                if r["content"] not in seen_texts:
                    diversified.append(r)
                    seen_texts.add(r["content"])

        return diversified

# knowledge\watcher.py
import os
import time
import logging
import threading
import fnmatch

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class DirectoryWatcher:

    def __init__(self, builder, directories=None, interval=60.0, patterns=("*.md", "*.txt", "*.py", "*.json", "*.yaml", "*.csv")):
        self.builder = builder
        self.directories = list(directories or [])
        self.interval = interval
        self.patterns = patterns
        self._running = False
        self._thread = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._known_files = {}

    def add_directory(self, path):
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            with self._lock:
                if path not in self.directories:
                    self.directories.append(path)
                    logger.info("[Watcher] Added directory: %s", path)
            return True
        logger.warning("[Watcher] Not a directory: %s", path)
        return False

    def start(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._running:
                return
            self._running = True
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()
        logger.info("[Watcher] Started (interval=%ss)", self.interval)

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._running = False

    def _run(self):
        while True:
            with self._lock:
                if not self._running:
                    return
            try:
                with self._lock:
                    dirs = list(self.directories)
                for d in dirs:
                    if not os.path.isdir(d):
                        continue
                    for root, _, files in os.walk(d):
                        for fn in files:
                            if not any(fnmatch.fnmatch(fn, p) for p in self.patterns):
                                continue
                            path = os.path.join(root, fn)
                            mtime = os.path.getmtime(path)
                            last = self._known_files.get(path)
                            if last and mtime <= last:
                                continue
                            self._known_files[path] = mtime
                            try:
                                self.builder.build_from_file(path)
                            except Exception as e:
                                logger.debug("[Watcher] Index error %s: %s", path, e)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("[Watcher] Scan error: %s", e)
            for _ in range(int(self.interval * 10)):
                with self._lock:
                    if not self._running:
                        return
                time.sleep(0.1)

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "running": self._running,
                "directories": list(self.directories),
                "interval": self.interval,
                "known_files": len(self._known_files),
            }

# llm\manager.py
# llm/manager.py

import logging
from typing import Iterator, Optional

from llm.providers.ollama import OllamaProvider
from llm.providers.openrouter import OpenRouterProvider
from config.settings import OPENROUTER_API_KEY

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.LLMManager")


class LLMManager:

    def __init__(self) -> None:

        self.ollama = OllamaProvider()

        self.openrouter = OpenRouterProvider(
            api_key=OPENROUTER_API_KEY
        )

    def is_available(self) -> bool:

        if self.openrouter.is_alive():
            return True

        if self.ollama.is_alive():
            return True

        return False

    def generate(
        self,
        system: str = "",
        user: str = "",
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> str:

        logger.info("Attempting request via OpenRouter...")

        if self.openrouter.is_alive():

            try:

                return self.openrouter.generate(
                    system, user, history
                )

            except Exception as e:

                logger.warning(
                    f"OpenRouter failed: {e}. "
                    f"Switching to offline failover (Ollama)."
                )

        else:

            logger.info(
                "OpenRouter unreachable. "
                "Switching to offline failover (Ollama)."
            )

        if self.ollama.is_alive():

            return self.ollama.generate(
                system, user, history
            )

        raise ConnectionError(

            "Both OpenRouter and Ollama are currently unreachable."
        )

    def generate_with_tools(
        self,
        system: str,
        user_input: str,
    ) -> str:

        return self.generate(
            system=system,
            user=user_input,
        )

    def stream(
        self,
        system: str = "",
        user: str = "",
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> Iterator[str]:

        if (
            hasattr(
                self.openrouter,
                "stream"
            )
            and
            self.openrouter.is_alive()
        ):

            try:

                return self.openrouter.stream(
                    system, user, history
                )

            except Exception as e:

                logger.warning(
                    f"OpenRouter stream failed: {e}. "
                    f"Switching to Ollama."
                )

        if (
            hasattr(
                self.ollama,
                "stream"
            )
            and
            self.ollama.is_alive()
        ):

            return self.ollama.stream(
                system, user, history
            )

        response: str = self.generate(
            system=system,
            user=user,
        )

        return iter([response])

# llm\providers\ollama.py
# llm/providers/ollama.py

import json
import logging
import re
import time
from typing import Iterator, Optional

import requests

from config.settings import (
    LLM_MODEL,
    LLM_TEMPERATURE,
    LLM_MAX_CONTEXT,
    LLM_TIMEOUT,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.OllamaProvider")


class OllamaProvider:

    IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = re.compile(
        r'\b\w+\.(png|jpg|jpeg|gif|bmp|webp|svg|tiff|ico)\b',
        re.IGNORECASE
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: str = LLM_MODEL,
        base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434",
        temperature: Optional[float] = None,
        context_window: Optional[int] = None,
        timeout: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:

        self.model = model
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")

        self.temperature = (
            temperature if temperature is not None
            else LLM_TEMPERATURE
        )

        self.context_window = (
            context_window or LLM_MAX_CONTEXT
        )

        self.timeout = timeout or LLM_TIMEOUT

        self.max_tokens = 8192

        self.cancel_requested = False

    def is_alive(self) -> bool:

        try:

            response = requests.get(

                f"{self.base_url}/api/tags",

                timeout=2
            )

            return (
                response.status_code == 200
            )

        except Exception:

            return False

    def _has_image_reference(self, text: str) -> bool:
        return bool(self.IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.search(text))

    def _options(self) -> dict:

        return {
            "temperature": self.temperature,
            "num_ctx":     self.context_window,
            "num_predict": self.max_tokens,
        }

    def _build_messages(
        self,
        system: str,
        user: str,
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> list:

        messages = []

        if system:
            messages.append({
                "role": "system",
                "content": system
            })

        if history:
            messages.extend(history)

        if user:
            messages.append({
                "role": "user",
                "content": user
            })

        return messages

    def _chat_payload(
        self,
        messages: list,
        stream: bool = False,
    ) -> dict:

        return {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "stream": stream,
            "options": self._options(),
        }

    def _check_image_error(self, content: str) -> Optional[str]:

        if "does not support image" in content.lower():
            return "I can't read or analyze images â€” I'm a text-only assistant."

        return None

    def generate(
        self,
        system: str,
        user: str,
        history: Optional[list] = None,
        retries: int = 2,
    ) -> str:

        if self._has_image_reference(system + user):
            logger.info("Detected image reference in prompt")
            return "I can't read or analyze images â€” I'm a text-only assistant."

        messages = self._build_messages(system, user, history)

        for attempt in range(retries):

            try:
                start = time.time()

                response = requests.post(

                    f"{self.base_url}/api/chat",

                    json=self._chat_payload(messages),

                    timeout=self.timeout,
                )

                response.raise_for_status()

                data = response.json()

                content: str = data["message"]["content"].strip()

                latency = round(time.time() - start, 2)
                logger.info(f"Response in {latency}s")

                image_msg = self._check_image_error(content)
                if image_msg:
                    return image_msg

                return content

            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Attempt {attempt + 1}/{retries} failed: {e}")

                if attempt < retries - 1:
                    time.sleep(1)

        return "â€¦something went wrong."

    def stream(
        self,
        system: str,
        user: str,
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> Iterator[str]:

        self.cancel_requested = False

        if self._has_image_reference(system + user):
            yield "I can't read or analyze images â€” I'm a text-only assistant."
            return

        messages = self._build_messages(system, user, history)

        try:
            start = time.time()

            response = requests.post(

                f"{self.base_url}/api/chat",

                json=self._chat_payload(messages, stream=True),

                timeout=self.timeout,

                stream=True,
            )

            response.raise_for_status()

            buffer = ""

            for line in response.iter_lines(decode_unicode=True):

                if self.cancel_requested:
                    logger.info("Stream cancelled")
                    return

                if not line:
                    continue

                try:
                    chunk = json.loads(line)

                    content = chunk.get("message", {}).get("content", "")

                    if not content:
                        continue

                    buffer += content

                    while self._has_sentence(buffer):

                        sentence, buffer = (
                            self._extract_sentence(buffer)
                        )

                        if sentence:
                            yield sentence

                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue

                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Stream chunk error: {e}")

            if buffer.strip():
                yield buffer.strip()

            latency = round(time.time() - start, 2)
            logger.info(f"Stream finished in {latency}s")

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Stream error: {e}")
            yield "â€¦something went wrong."

    def cancel_stream(self) -> None:
        self.cancel_requested = True

    def generate_with_tools(
        self,
        system: str,
        user: str,
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> str:

        try:
            original = self.temperature
            self.temperature = 0.2
            result = self.generate(system, user, history)
            self.temperature = original
            return result

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Tools error: {e}")
            return "{}"

    def generate_json(
        self,
        system: str,
        user: str,
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> str:

        prompt = system + "\n\nReturn ONLY valid JSON."

        return self.generate(prompt, user, history)

    def _has_sentence(self, text: str) -> bool:

        endings = [". ", "? ", "! ", "\n"]

        return any(
            ending in text
            for ending in endings
        )

    def _extract_sentence(self, text: str):

        endings = [". ", "? ", "! ", "\n"]

        indexes = [
            text.find(e)
            for e in endings
            if text.find(e) != -1
        ]

        if not indexes:
            return None, text

        index = min(indexes)

        sentence = text[:index + 1].strip()

        remaining = text[index + 1:].strip()

        return sentence, remaining

# llm\providers\openrouter.py
# llm/providers/openrouter.py

import logging
import re
import requests
import time
from typing import Iterator, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class OpenRouterProvider:

    IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = re.compile(
        r'\b\w+\.(png|jpg|jpeg|gif|bmp|webp|svg|tiff|ico)\b',
        re.IGNORECASE
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str,
        models: Optional[list[str]] = None
    ) -> None:

        self.api_key = api_key

        self.models = models or [

            "google/gemma-4-31b-it:free",

            "deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash",

            "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free"
        ]

        self.base_url = (
            "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
        )

    def is_alive(self) -> bool:

        headers = {
            "Authorization":
                f"Bearer {self.api_key}"
        }

        try:

            response = requests.get(

                f"{self.base_url}/models",

                headers=headers,

                timeout=5
            )

            return (
                response.status_code == 200
            )

        except Exception:
