            return False

    def _has_image_reference(self, text: str) -> bool:
        return bool(self.IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.search(text))

    def _build_messages(
        self,
        system: str = "",
        user: str = "",
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> list:

        messages = []

        if system:
            messages.append({
                "role": "system",
                "content": system
            })

        if history:
            messages.extend(history)

        if user:
            messages.append({
                "role": "user",
                "content": user
            })
        elif system and not user and not history:
            # backward compat: if only one string arg was passed, treat as user
            messages.append({
                "role": "user",
                "content": system
            })

        return messages

    def generate(
        self,
        system: str = "",
        user: str = "",
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> str:

        headers = {

            "Authorization":
                f"Bearer {self.api_key}",

            "Content-Type":
                "application/json"
        }

        last_error: Optional[Exception] = None

        combined = system + user
        if self._has_image_reference(combined):
            logger.info("[OpenRouter] Detected image reference in prompt")
            return "I can't read or analyze images â€” I'm a text-only assistant."

        messages = self._build_messages(system, user, history)

        for model in self.models:

            logger.info(
                "[OpenRouter] Using model: %s", model
            )

            payload = {

                "model": model,

                "messages": messages,
            }

            for attempt in range(3):

                try:

                    response = requests.post(

                        f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions",

                        json=payload,

                        headers=headers,

                        timeout=60
                    )

                    response.raise_for_status()

                    data = response.json()

                    content: str = data[
                        "choices"
                    ][0][
                        "message"
                    ][
                        "content"
                    ]

                    if "does not support image" in content.lower():
                        logger.warning("Model returned image support error: %s", model)
                        raise ValueError(content)

                    return content

                except Exception as e:

                    last_error = e

                    logger.warning("%s attempt %d/3 failed: %s", model, attempt + 1, e)

                    if attempt < 2:
                        time.sleep(1)

            logger.info("Failing over from %s", model)

        if last_error is None:
            last_error = RuntimeError("All OpenRouter models failed without raising an exception")

        error_msg = str(last_error).lower()
        if "image" in error_msg or "does not support" in error_msg:
            return "I can't read or analyze images â€” I'm a text-only assistant."

        raise last_error

    def stream(
        self,
        system: str = "",
        user: str = "",
        history: Optional[list] = None,
    ) -> Iterator[str]:

        response: str = self.generate(
            system, user, history,
        )

        return iter([response])

# memory\consolidation.py
# memory/consolidation.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_SIMILARITY_TYPE_GROUPS = {
    "preference": {"preference", "preference_negative"},
    "fact": {"identity", "employment", "location", "fact"},
    "emotional": {"emotional_state"},
    "goal": {"goal"},
    "conversation": {"conversation"},
}


def _type_group(memory_type):
    for group, types in _SIMILARITY_TYPE_GROUPS.items():
        if memory_type in types:
            return group
    return "other"


def _text_overlap_similarity(a, b):
    words_a = set(str(a).lower().split())
    words_b = set(str(b).lower().split())
    if not words_a or not words_b:
        return 0.0
    intersection = words_a & words_b
    union = words_a | words_b
    return len(intersection) / len(union)


def consolidate(nodes):
    if not nodes:
        return [], nodes

    groups = {}
    for node in nodes:
        if getattr(node, "consolidated", False) or getattr(node, "archived", False):
            continue
        group = _type_group(getattr(node, "memory_type", "conversation"))
        if group not in groups:
            groups[group] = []
        groups[group].append(node)

    consolidated_entries = []
    remaining = list(nodes)

    for group_name, group_nodes in groups.items():
        if len(group_nodes) < 2:
            continue

        merged = set()
        for i, a in enumerate(group_nodes):
            if id(a) in merged:
                continue
            cluster = [a]
            merged.add(id(a))
            for j, b in enumerate(group_nodes):
                if i == j or id(b) in merged:
                    continue
                a_text = getattr(a, "text", "") or getattr(a, "content", "")
                b_text = getattr(b, "text", "") or getattr(b, "content", "")
                if _text_overlap_similarity(a_text, b_text) > 0.4:
                    cluster.append(b)
                    merged.add(id(b))

            if len(cluster) >= 2:
                texts = [
                    getattr(n, "text", "") or getattr(n, "content", "")
                    for n in cluster
                ]
                avg_score = sum(
                    getattr(n, "score", 0.5) or getattr(n, "importance", 0.5)
                    for n in cluster
                ) / len(cluster)
                avg_confidence = sum(
                    getattr(n, "confidence", 0.5) for n in cluster
                ) / len(cluster)

                consolidated_entries.append({
                    "cluster": cluster,
                    "summary": texts[0],
                    "score": min(1.0, avg_score + 0.1 * (len(cluster) - 1)),
                    "confidence": avg_confidence,
                    "type": group_name,
                    "source_ids": [getattr(n, "id", "") for n in cluster],
                    "timestamp": time.time(),
                })

                for n in cluster:
                    n.consolidated = True

    new_nodes = []
    for entry in consolidated_entries:
        from memory.schema import MemoryObject

        c_node = MemoryObject(
            content=entry["summary"],
            memory_type="semantic",
            confidence=entry["confidence"],
            importance=entry["score"],
            metadata={
                "consolidated_from": entry["type"],
                "source_ids": entry["source_ids"],
                "cluster_size": len(entry["cluster"]),
            },
        )
        new_nodes.append(c_node)

    return new_nodes, remaining

# memory\decay.py
# Mai_ai/memory/decay.py
import time
import threading
import logging
from collections import defaultdict

from config.settings import Settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryDecay:

    def __init__(self):

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  CONFIG
        # ==================================================

        self.min_score = (
            Settings.MEMORY_MIN_SCORE
        )

        self.archive_score = (
            Settings.MEMORY_ARCHIVE_SCORE
        )

        self.max_consolidation = (
            Settings.MEMORY_MAX_CONSOLIDATION
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ“¦ STORAGE
        # ==================================================

        self.archived = set()

        self.consolidated = []

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  THREAD SAFETY
        # ==================================================

        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  DECAY CURVES
        # ==================================================

        self.type_decay = {

            "conversation":
                Settings.DECAY_CONVERSATION,

            "emotion":
                Settings.DECAY_EMOTION,

            "relationship":
                Settings.DECAY_RELATIONSHIP,

            "identity":
                Settings.DECAY_IDENTITY,

            "reflection":
                Settings.DECAY_REFLECTION,

            "important":
                Settings.DECAY_IMPORTANT,

            "general":
                Settings.DECAY_GENERAL
        }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN DECAY SYSTEM
    # ==================================================

    def apply(self, nodes):

        if not isinstance(nodes, list):
            return []

        active = []

        now = time.time()

        with self.lock:

            for node in nodes:

                try:

                    if node is None:
                        continue

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  SAFE ATTRIBUTES
                    # ==================================================

                    memory_type = getattr(
                        node,
                        "memory_type",
                        "general"
                    )

                    score = self._safe_float(
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "score",
                            0.5
                        ),
                        0.5
                    )

                    importance = self._safe_float(
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "importance",
                            0.5
                        ),
                        0.5
                    )

                    emotional_weight = self._safe_float(
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "emotional_weight",
                            0.5
                        ),
                        0.5
                    )

                    reinforcement = int(
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "reinforcement",
                            1
                        )
                    )

                    last_accessed = getattr(
                        node,
                        "last_accessed",
                        now
                    )

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ•’ AGE FACTOR
                    # ==================================================

                    age_hours = max(
                        1.0,
                        (now - last_accessed) / 3600
                    )

                    age_penalty = min(
                        Settings.MEMORY_MAX_AGE_PENALTY,
                        (
                            age_hours
                            *
                            Settings.MEMORY_AGE_DECAY_RATE
                        )
                    )

                    recency_factor = (
                        1.0 - age_penalty
                    )

                    recency_factor = max(
                        Settings.MEMORY_MIN_RECENCY_FACTOR,
                        recency_factor
                    )

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  BASE DECAY
                    # ==================================================

                    decay = self.type_decay.get(
                        memory_type,
                        Settings.DECAY_GENERAL
                    )

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ›¡ï¸ PROTECTIONS
                    # ==================================================

                    emotional_protection = (
                        emotional_weight
                        *
                        Settings.MEMORY_EMOTIONAL_PROTECTION
                    )

                    importance_protection = (
                        importance
                        *
                        Settings.MEMORY_IMPORTANCE_PROTECTION
                    )

                    reinforcement_protection = min(
                        Settings.MEMORY_MAX_REINFORCEMENT_PROTECTION,
                        (
                            reinforcement
                            *
                            Settings.MEMORY_REINFORCEMENT_PROTECTION
                        )
                    )

                    final_decay = (

                        decay

                        +

                        emotional_protection

                        +

                        importance_protection

                        +

                        reinforcement_protection
                    )

                    final_decay = min(
                        Settings.MEMORY_MAX_DECAY,
                        max(
                            Settings.MEMORY_MIN_DECAY,
                            final_decay
                        )
                    )

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ“‰ APPLY DECAY
                    # ==================================================

                    new_score = (
                        score
                        *
                        final_decay
                        *
                        recency_factor
                    )

                    node.score = round(
                        max(0.0, new_score),
                        4
                    )

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ“¦ ARCHIVE LOGIC
                    # ==================================================

                    if node.score < self.archive_score:

                        if node not in self.archived:

                            node.archived = True

                            self.archived.add(
                                node
                            )

                        continue

                    active.append(node)

                except Exception as e:

                    logger.error(
                        f"[DECAY APPLY ERROR] {e}"
                    )

                    active.append(node)

        return active

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ” REINFORCE MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def reinforce(
        self,
        node,
        amount=None
    ):

        if node is None:
            return None

        with self.lock:

            try:

                if amount is None:

                    amount = (
                        Settings.MEMORY_REINFORCE_AMOUNT
                    )

                amount = max(
                    0.0,
                    float(amount)
                )

                current = self._safe_float(
                    getattr(
                        node,
                        "score",
                        0.5
                    ),
                    0.5
                )

                node.score = min(
                    1.0,
                    current + amount
                )

                current_reinforcement = int(
                    getattr(
                        node,
                        "reinforcement",
                        1
                    )
                )

                node.reinforcement = (
                    current_reinforcement + 1
                )

                node.last_accessed = (
                    time.time()
                )

                node.archived = False

                return node

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[REINFORCE ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return node

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  REVIVE MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def revive(self, node):

        if node is None:
            return False

        with self.lock:

            try:

                if node in self.archived:

                    self.archived.discard(node)

                    node.archived = False

                    node.score = max(
                        Settings.MEMORY_REVIVE_SCORE,
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "score",
                            0.0
                        )
                    )

                    node.last_accessed = (
                        time.time()
                    )

                    return True

                return False

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[REVIVE ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  CONSOLIDATE MEMORIES
    # ==================================================

    def consolidate(self, nodes):

        if not isinstance(nodes, list):
            return []

        grouped = defaultdict(list)

        with self.lock:

            try:

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  GROUP MEMORIES
                # ==================================================

                for node in nodes:

                    if node is None:
                        continue

                    memory_type = getattr(
                        node,
                        "memory_type",
                        "general"
                    )

                    grouped[
                        memory_type
                    ].append(node)

                consolidated_nodes = []

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  BUILD CONSOLIDATED MEMORIES
                # ==================================================

                for memory_type, group in grouped.items():

                    if len(group) < (
                        Settings.MEMORY_MIN_CONSOLIDATION
                    ):
                        continue

                    # highest score first
                    group.sort(

                        key=lambda n:
                        getattr(
                            n,
                            "score",
                            0.0
                        ),

                        reverse=True
                    )

                    group = group[
                        :self.max_consolidation
                    ]

                    texts = []

                    total_score = 0.0

                    total_importance = 0.0

                    total_emotion = 0.0

                    total_reinforcement = 0

                    tags = set()

                    for node in group:

                        texts.append(
                            getattr(
                                node,
                                "text",
                                ""
                            )
                        )

                        total_score += getattr(
                            node,
                            "score",
                            0.5
                        )

                        total_importance += getattr(
                            node,
                            "importance",
                            0.5
                        )

                        total_emotion += getattr(
                            node,
                            "emotional_weight",
                            0.5
                        )

                        total_reinforcement += getattr(
                            node,
                            "reinforcement",
                            1
                        )

                        node_tags = getattr(
                            node,
                            "tags",
                            []
                        )

                        if isinstance(
                            node_tags,
                            list
                        ):
                            tags.update(
                                node_tags
                            )

                    count = len(group)

                    consolidated_memory = {

                        "memory_type":
                            memory_type,

                        "summary":
                            " | ".join(
                                texts[
                                    :Settings.MEMORY_SUMMARY_LIMIT
                                ]
                            ),

                        "source_count":
                            count,

                        "score":
                            round(
                                total_score / count,
                                3
                            ),

                        "importance":
                            round(
                                total_importance / count,
                                3
                            ),

                        "emotional_weight":
                            round(
                                total_emotion / count,
                                3
                            ),

                        "reinforcement":
                            total_reinforcement,

                        "tags":
                            list(tags),

                        "created_at":
                            time.time(),

                        "last_accessed":
                            time.time()
                    }

                    consolidated_nodes.append(
                        consolidated_memory
                    )

                self.consolidated.extend(
                    consolidated_nodes
                )

                return consolidated_nodes

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[CONSOLIDATION ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return []

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self, active_nodes=None):

        if active_nodes is None:
            active_nodes = []

        return {

            "active_memories":
                len(active_nodes),

            "archived_memories":
                len(self.archived),

            "consolidated_memories":
                len(self.consolidated),

            "min_score":
                self.min_score,

            "archive_score":
                self.archive_score
        }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§¹ CLEAR ARCHIVE
    # ==================================================

    def clear_archive(self):

        with self.lock:

            self.archived.clear()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§¹ CLEAR CONSOLIDATED
    # ==================================================

    def clear_consolidated(self):

        with self.lock:

            self.consolidated.clear()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ›¡ï¸ SAFE FLOAT
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_float(
        self,
        value,
        default=0.0
    ):

        try:

            return float(value)

        except Exception:

            return default
# memory\emotion_analyzer.py
# Mai_ai/memory/emotion_analyzer.py
import re


class EmotionAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self):

        # ðŸ§  emotional vocabulary
        self.emotions = {

            "affection": [
                "love",
                "adore",
                "care",
                "miss",
                "darling",
                "cherish"
            ],

            "joy": [
                "happy",
                "excited",
                "great",
                "amazing",
                "fun",
                "wonderful"
            ],

            "sadness": [
                "sad",
                "hurt",
                "cry",
                "lonely",
                "depressed",
                "upset"
            ],

            "anger": [
                "hate",
                "angry",
                "annoyed",
                "furious",
                "mad",
                "irritated"
            ],

            "fear": [
                "afraid",
                "scared",
                "worried",
                "anxious",
                "nervous"
            ],

            "stress": [
                "tired",
                "exhausted",
                "overwhelmed",
                "burned out",
                "stressed"
            ]
        }

        # âš¡ intensity modifiers
        self.intensity_words = {

            "very": 0.15,

            "really": 0.15,

            "extremely": 0.25,

            "so": 0.10,

            "a lot": 0.10
        }

    # ðŸ§  analyze emotional state
    def analyze(self, text):

        if not text:

            return self._neutral()

        text = text.lower()

        scores = {}

        # ðŸ” detect emotions
        for emotion, keywords in self.emotions.items():

            score = 0

            for keyword in keywords:

                matches = len(
                    re.findall(
                        rf"\b{re.escape(keyword)}\b",
                        text
                    )
                )

                score += matches

            if score > 0:
                scores[emotion] = score

        # ðŸ˜ no emotion detected
        if not scores:

            return self._neutral()

        # âš¡ intensity calculation
        intensity = 0.5

        for word, boost in self.intensity_words.items():

            if word in text:
                intensity += boost

        intensity = min(1.0, intensity)

        # ðŸ§  strongest emotion
        dominant = max(
            scores,
            key=lambda k: scores[k],
            default="neutral"
        )

        # ðŸŽ­ mixed emotions
        mixed = [
            emotion
            for emotion, value in scores.items()
            if value >= 1 and emotion != dominant
        ]

        # ðŸ“Š confidence
        total_score = sum(scores.values())

        confidence = min(
            1.0,
            0.5 + total_score * 0.1
        )

        return {

            "type": dominant,

            "intensity": round(
                intensity,
                2
            ),

            "confidence": round(
                confidence,
                2
            ),

            "secondary": mixed,

            "raw_scores": scores
        }

    # ðŸ˜ fallback neutral state
    def _neutral(self):

        return {

            "type": "neutral",

            "intensity": 0.3,

            "confidence": 1.0,

            "secondary": [],

            "raw_scores": {}
        }
# memory\extractor.py
# memory/extractor.py
import re
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_EXTRACTION_PATTERNS = [
    ("preference", re.compile(
        r"\bmy favorite\s+\w+\s+is\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("preference", re.compile(
        r"\bi (?:like|enjoy|love)\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("preference_negative", re.compile(
        r"\bi (?:don't like|hate|dislike)\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("identity", re.compile(
        r"\bmy name is\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("identity", re.compile(
        r"\bi'?m\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("employment", re.compile(
        r"\bi work (?:at|as|for)\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("location", re.compile(
        r"\bi live (?:in|at|near)\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("goal", re.compile(
        r"\bi (?:want to|wanna|'?m going to)\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("goal", re.compile(
        r"\bi'?m learning\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("goal", re.compile(
        r"\bi'?ve (?:always )?dreamed of\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
    ("emotional_state", re.compile(
        r"\bi feel\s+(.+?)(?:[\.!]|$)",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )),
]


class ExtractedKnowledge:

    def __init__(self, fact_type, value, confidence=0.6, source="fast"):
        self.type = fact_type
        self.value = str(value).strip()
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence)))
        self.source = source
        self.timestamp = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "type": self.type,
            "value": self.value,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "source": self.source,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
        }

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"<ExtractedKnowledge "
            f"type={self.type} "
            f"value={self.value[:30]}...>"
        )


class FastExtractor:

    def extract(self, text):
        if not text:
            return []

        text = str(text).strip()
        results = []
        seen = set()

        for fact_type, pattern in _EXTRACTION_PATTERNS:
            matches = pattern.findall(text)
            for match in matches:
                value = match.strip().rstrip(".")
                if not value:
                    continue
                key = (fact_type, value.lower())
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key)
                    confidence = 0.6
                    if fact_type in ("identity", "employment", "location"):
                        confidence = 0.8
                    results.append(
                        ExtractedKnowledge(
                            fact_type=fact_type,
                            value=value,
                            confidence=confidence,
                            source="fast",
                        )
                    )

        return results

# memory\graph.py
# Mai_ai/memory/graph.py
import json
import logging
import threading
import time
from collections import defaultdict

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from memory.node import MemoryNode


class MemoryGraph:

    def __init__(self, brain_graph=None):

        # ðŸ§  memory storage
        self.nodes = []

        # ðŸ”— adjacency graph
        self.edges = defaultdict(set)

        # ðŸ§  lookup cache
        self.node_index = {}

        # ðŸ§  similarity threshold
        self.link_threshold = 0.35

        # ðŸ§  max links per node
        self.max_links = 15

        # ðŸ§  thread safety
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # ðŸ§  runtime stats
        self.total_links = 0

        # ðŸ§  brain graph bridge
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  ADD NODE
    # ==================================================
    def add(self, node):

        if node is None:
            return None

        with self.lock:

            node_id = self._node_id(node)

            # âœ… duplicate prevention
            if node_id in self.node_index:

                existing = self.node_index[node_id]

                # ðŸ” reinforce existing memory
                existing.score = min(
                    1.0,
                    getattr(existing, "score", 0.5) + 0.05
                )

                existing.reinforcement_count = (
                    getattr(
                        existing,
                        "reinforcement_count",
                        0
                    ) + 1
                )

                existing.last_accessed = time.time()

                return existing

            # ðŸ§  safe defaults
            if not hasattr(node, "reinforcement_count"):
                node.reinforcement_count = 0

            if not hasattr(node, "connections"):
                node.connections = []

            self.nodes.append(node)

            self.node_index[node_id] = node

        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.add_memory_node(
                memory_id=node.id,
                text=getattr(node, "text", ""),
                memory_type=getattr(node, "memory_type", "conversation"),
                score=getattr(node, "score", 0.5),
                tags=getattr(node, "tags", []),
                emotion=getattr(node, "emotion", {}),
                importance=getattr(node, "importance", 0.5),
                source=getattr(node, "source", "user"),
            )

        return node

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”— LINK NODES
    # ==================================================
    def link_nodes(self):

        with self.lock:

            total = len(self.nodes)

            if total < 2:
                return

            # âœ… only compare newest node
            newest = self.nodes[-1]

            newest_id = self._node_id(newest)

            linked = 0

            # compare against recent memories only
            recent_nodes = self.nodes[-100:-1]

            for other in recent_nodes:

                other_id = self._node_id(other)

                if newest is other:
                    continue

                similarity = self._similarity(
                    newest,
                    other
                )

                if similarity < self.link_threshold:
                    continue

                # ðŸ”— bidirectional edges
                self.edges[newest_id].add(other_id)

                self.edges[other_id].add(newest_id)

                linked += 1

                self.total_links += 1

                # ðŸ§  optional node-level connections
                if hasattr(newest, "connections"):

                    if other_id not in newest.connections:
                        newest.connections.append(
                            other_id
                        )

                if hasattr(other, "connections"):

                    if newest_id not in other.connections:
                        other.connections.append(
                            newest_id
                        )

                if self.brain_graph and hasattr(newest, "id") and hasattr(other, "id"):
                    self.brain_graph.add_memory_link(
                        newest.id, other.id, weight=similarity
                    )

                # âœ… prevent graph explosion
                if linked >= self.max_links:
                    break

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SIMILARITY ENGINE
    # ==================================================
    def _similarity(self, a, b):

        try:

            score = 0.0

            # ðŸ·ï¸ TAG OVERLAP
            tags_a = set(
                getattr(a, "tags", [])
            )

            tags_b = set(
                getattr(b, "tags", [])
            )

            if tags_a and tags_b:

                overlap = len(
                    tags_a.intersection(tags_b)
                )

                union = len(
                    tags_a.union(tags_b)
                )

                if union > 0:
                    score += (
                        overlap / union
                    ) * 0.5

            # ðŸ§  MEMORY TYPE
            type_a = getattr(
                a,
                "memory_type",
                ""
            )

            type_b = getattr(
                b,
                "memory_type",
                ""
            )

            if (
                type_a
                and
                type_a == type_b
            ):
                score += 0.2

            # â¤ï¸ EMOTION TYPE
            emo_a = getattr(
                a,
                "emotion",
                {}
            )

            emo_b = getattr(
                b,
                "emotion",
                {}
            )

            if (
                isinstance(emo_a, dict)
                and
                isinstance(emo_b, dict)
            ):

                if (
                    emo_a.get("type")
                    ==
                    emo_b.get("type")
                ):
                    score += 0.2

            # ðŸ“ TEXT OVERLAP
            text_a = set(
                str(
                    getattr(a, "text", "")
                ).lower().split()
            )

            text_b = set(
                str(
                    getattr(b, "text", "")
                ).lower().split()
            )

            if text_a and text_b:

                overlap = len(
                    text_a.intersection(text_b)
                )

                union = len(
                    text_a.union(text_b)
                )

                if union > 0:
                    score += (
                        overlap / union
                    ) * 0.3

            return round(
                min(score, 1.0),
                3
            )

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Graph similarity error: %s", e)
            return 0.0

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  GET CONNECTED MEMORIES
    # ==================================================
    def connected(self, node):

        with self.lock:

            node_id = self._node_id(node)

            connections = self.edges.get(
                node_id,
                set()
            )

            result = []

            for conn_id in connections:

                memory = self.node_index.get(
                    conn_id
                )

                if memory:
                    result.append(memory)

            return result

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  REMOVE NODE
    # ==================================================
    def remove(self, node):

        with self.lock:

            node_id = self._node_id(node)

            if node in self.nodes:
                self.nodes.remove(node)

            if node_id in self.node_index:
                del self.node_index[node_id]

            # ðŸ”— remove graph edges
            if node_id in self.edges:

                connected = self.edges[node_id]

                for other_id in connected:

                    if other_id in self.edges:

                        self.edges[other_id].discard(
                            node_id
                        )

                del self.edges[node_id]

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MEMORY SEARCH
    # ==================================================
    def search_by_tag(self, tag):

        with self.lock:

            results = []

            for node in self.nodes:

                tags = getattr(
                    node,
                    "tags",
                    []
                )

                if tag in tags:
                    results.append(node)

            return results

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SAFE NODE ID
    # ==================================================
    def _node_id(self, node):

        node_uuid = getattr(
            node,
            "id",
            None
        )

        if node_uuid:
            return str(node_uuid)

        return str(id(node))

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================
    def stats(self):

        with self.lock:

            edge_count = sum(
                len(v)
                for v in self.edges.values()
            )

            return {

                "nodes":
                    len(self.nodes),

                "edges":
                    edge_count,

                "indexed_nodes":
                    len(self.node_index),

                "total_links":
                    self.total_links,

                "link_threshold":
                    self.link_threshold,
            }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ’¾ SERIALIZATION
    # ==================================================

    def save(self, path):

        with self.lock:

            data = {
                "nodes": [
                    n.to_dict() for n in self.nodes
                ],
                "edges": {
                    k: list(v)
                    for k, v in self.edges.items()
                },
                "link_threshold":
                    self.link_threshold,
                "max_links":
                    self.max_links,
                "total_links":
                    self.total_links,
            }

            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):

        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)

        graph = cls()
        graph.link_threshold = data.get(
            "link_threshold", 0.35
        )
        graph.max_links = data.get(
            "max_links", 15
        )
        graph.total_links = data.get(
            "total_links", 0
        )

        for node_data in data.get("nodes", []):
            node = MemoryNode.from_dict(node_data)
            graph.nodes.append(node)
            graph.node_index[
                graph._node_id(node)
            ] = node

        for src_id, targets in data.get(
            "edges", {}
        ).items():
            graph.edges[src_id] = set(targets)

        return graph
# memory\hierarchy.py
import time
import uuid
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np
from enum import Enum

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryType(Enum):
    SEMANTIC = "semantic"
    EPISODIC = "episodic"
    PROCEDURAL = "procedural"
    AUTOBIOGRAPHICAL = "autobiographical"


class MemoryCode:
    def __init__(self, memory_type, content, confidence=0.5, importance=0.5,
                 timestamp=None, metadata=None, tags=None, source=None):
        self.id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.type = MemoryType(memory_type) if isinstance(memory_type, str) else memory_type
        self.content = content
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, confidence))
        self.importance = max(0.0, min(1.0, importance))
        self.timestamp = timestamp or time.time()
        self.metadata = metadata or {}
        self.tags = tags or []
        self.source = source or "conversation"
        self.access_count = 0
        self.last_accessed = self.timestamp
        self.decay_factor = self._default_decay()
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def _default_decay(self):
        decay_map = {
            MemoryType.SEMANTIC: 0.95,
            MemoryType.EPISODIC: 0.85,
            MemoryType.PROCEDURAL: 0.90,
            MemoryType.AUTOBIOGRAPHICAL: 0.98,
        }
        return decay_map.get(self.type, 0.9)

    def access(self):
        with self._lock:
            self.access_count += 1
            self.last_accessed = time.time()

    def apply_decay(self, elapsed_hours):
        with self._lock:
            decay = self.decay_factor ** elapsed_hours
            self.confidence *= decay
            self.importance *= decay

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "type": self.type.value,
            "content": self.content,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "importance": self.importance,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
            "metadata": self.metadata,
            "tags": self.tags,
            "source": self.source,
            "access_count": self.access_count,
            "last_accessed": self.last_accessed,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        obj = cls(
            memory_type=data["type"],
            content=data["content"],
            confidence=data.get("confidence", 0.5),
            importance=data.get("importance", 0.5),
            timestamp=data.get("timestamp", time.time()),
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {}),
            tags=data.get("tags", []),
            source=data.get("source", "conversation"),
        )
        obj.id = data.get("id", obj.id)
        obj.access_count = data.get("access_count", 0)
        obj.last_accessed = data.get("last_accessed", obj.timestamp)
        return obj

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"<MemoryCode type={self.type.value} conf={self.confidence:.2f}>"


class SemanticMemoryStore:
    def __init__(self):
        self._facts = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def add(self, fact_key, content, confidence=0.5, tags=None, source=None):
        with self._lock:
            if fact_key in self._facts:
                existing = self._facts[fact_key]
                existing.confidence = max(existing.confidence, confidence)
                existing.access_count += 1
                existing.last_accessed = time.time()
                return existing
            code = MemoryCode(MemoryType.SEMANTIC, content,
                              confidence=confidence, tags=tags, source=source)
            self._facts[fact_key] = code
            return code

    def get(self, fact_key):
        with self._lock:
            code = self._facts.get(fact_key)
            if code:
                code.access()
            return code

    def query(self, text, limit=5):
        with self._lock:
            results = []
            text_lower = text.lower()
            for key, code in self._facts.items():
                score = 0.0
                if text_lower in key.lower():
                    score += 0.5
                if any(t.lower() in text_lower for t in code.tags):
                    score += 0.3
                score *= code.confidence * code.importance
                if score > 0:
                    results.append((code, score))
            results.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            return [r[0] for r in results[:limit]]

    def all(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._facts.values())


class EpisodicMemoryStore:
    def __init__(self):
        self._episodes = []
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def add(self, content, confidence=0.5, importance=0.5, tags=None, source=None, metadata=None):
        with self._lock:
            code = MemoryCode(MemoryType.EPISODIC, content,
                              confidence=confidence, importance=importance,
                              tags=tags, source=source, metadata=metadata)
            self._episodes.append(code)
            return code

    def recall(self, query=None, limit=5):
        with self._lock:
            if not query:
                results = sorted(self._episodes, key=lambda x: x.timestamp, reverse=True)[:limit]
                for r in results:
                    r.access()
                return results
            text_lower = query.lower()
            scored = []
            for code in self._episodes:
                score = 0.0
                if text_lower in code.content.lower():
                    score += 0.4
                if any(t.lower() in text_lower for t in code.tags):
                    score += 0.3
                recency = 1.0 / (1.0 + (time.time() - code.timestamp) / 3600.0)
                score += 0.3 * recency
                score *= code.confidence * code.importance
                if score > 0:
                    scored.append((code, score))
            scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            results = [r[0] for r in scored[:limit]]
            for r in results:
                r.access()
            return results

    def all(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._episodes)


class ProceduralMemoryStore:
    def __init__(self):
        self._rules = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def add(self, trigger, action, confidence=0.5, context=None):
        with self._lock:
            rule_key = f"{trigger}:{action}"
            if rule_key in self._rules:
                existing = self._rules[rule_key]
                existing.confidence = min(1.0, existing.confidence + 0.1)
                existing.access_count += 1
                return existing
            code = MemoryCode(MemoryType.PROCEDURAL,
                              f"When {trigger}, then {action}",
                              confidence=confidence, importance=0.6,
                              tags=[trigger], source="experience",
                              metadata={"trigger": trigger, "action": action, "context": context or {}})
            self._rules[rule_key] = code
            return code

    def find(self, trigger, context=None):
        with self._lock:
            results = []
            text_lower = trigger.lower()
            for key, code in self._rules.items():
                score = 0.0
                trig = code.metadata.get("trigger", "")
                if text_lower in trig.lower():
                    score += 0.5
                if context and code.metadata.get("context"):
                    ctx = code.metadata["context"]
                    score += sum(1 for k, v in context.items()
                                 if ctx.get(k) == v) * 0.1
                score *= code.confidence
                if score > 0:
                    results.append((code, score))
            results.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            for r, _ in results:
                r.access()
            return [r[0] for r in results[:3]]

    def all(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._rules.values())


class AutobiographicalMemoryStore:
    def __init__(self):
        self._traits = {}
        self._history = []
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def add(self, trait_key, content, confidence=0.5, importance=0.8):
        with self._lock:
            if trait_key in self._traits:
                existing = self._traits[trait_key]
                existing.confidence = max(existing.confidence, confidence)
                existing.last_accessed = time.time()
                return existing
            code = MemoryCode(MemoryType.AUTOBIOGRAPHICAL, content,
                              confidence=confidence, importance=importance,
                              tags=[trait_key], source="self")
            self._traits[trait_key] = code
            self._history.append({"key": trait_key, "content": content, "timestamp": time.time()})
            return code

    def get(self, trait_key):
        with self._lock:
            code = self._traits.get(trait_key)
            if code:
                code.access()
            return code

    def query(self, topic, limit=3):
        with self._lock:
            results = []
            text_lower = topic.lower()
            for key, code in self._traits.items():
                score = 0.0
                if text_lower in key.lower() or text_lower in code.content.lower():
                    score += 0.5
                score *= code.importance
                if score > 0:
                    results.append((code, score))
            results.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            return [r[0] for r in results[:limit]]

    def history(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._history)

    def all(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._traits.values())


class MemoryHierarchy:
    def __init__(self, brain_graph=None, event_bus=None):
        self.semantic = SemanticMemoryStore()
        self.episodic = EpisodicMemoryStore()
        self.procedural = ProceduralMemoryStore()
        self.autobiographical = AutobiographicalMemoryStore()
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph
        self.event_bus = event_bus

    def store_semantic(self, fact_key, content, confidence=0.5, tags=None, source=None, emotion=None):
        code = self.semantic.add(fact_key, content, confidence, tags, source)
        self._sync_to_brain_graph(code, emotion=emotion)
        return code

    def store_episodic(self, content, confidence=0.5, importance=0.5,
                       tags=None, source=None, metadata=None, emotion=None):
        code = self.episodic.add(content, confidence, importance, tags, source, metadata)
        self._sync_to_brain_graph(code, emotion=emotion)
        return code

    def store_procedural(self, trigger, action, confidence=0.5, context=None):
        code = self.procedural.add(trigger, action, confidence, context)
        self._sync_to_brain_graph(code)
        return code

    def store_autobiographical(self, trait_key, content, confidence=0.5, importance=0.8):
        code = self.autobiographical.add(trait_key, content, confidence, importance)
        self._sync_to_brain_graph(code)
        return code

    def store_from_memory_object(self, memory_obj, source="conversation"):
        content = memory_obj.content
        tags = memory_obj.metadata.get("tags", [])
        emotion = memory_obj.metadata.get("emotion", {})
        confidence = memory_obj.confidence
        importance = memory_obj.importance
        text_lower = content.lower()

        is_factual = any(word in text_lower for word in [
            "is", "are", "was", "were", "called", "known as",
            "located", "capital", "means",
        ])
        is_identity = any(word in text_lower for word in [
            "i am", "my name", "i'm", "i was", "i have",
        ])
        is_event = any(word in text_lower for word in [
            "today", "yesterday", "just", "happened", "went",
            "did", "saw", "heard", "felt",
        ])
        is_rule = any(word in text_lower for word in [
            "always", "never", "should", "must", "when",
            "if", "then",
        ])

        results = []
        if is_factual and not is_identity:
            fact_key = content[:60].strip().lower()
            code = self.store_semantic(fact_key, content, confidence, tags, source, emotion=emotion)
            results.append(code)

        if is_event or not (is_factual or is_identity or is_rule):
            code = self.store_episodic(content, confidence, importance, tags, source,
                                       metadata={"emotion": emotion}, emotion=emotion)
            results.append(code)

        if is_rule:
            parts = content.split(",")
            trigger = parts[0].replace("if", "").replace("when", "").strip() if parts else content
            action = parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else content
            code = self.store_procedural(trigger, action, confidence)
            results.append(code)

        if is_identity:
            trait_key = content[:40].strip().lower()
            code = self.store_autobiographical(trait_key, content, confidence)
            results.append(code)

        return results

    def query(self, text, memory_types=None, limit=5):
        types = [MemoryType(t) if isinstance(t, str) else t
                 for t in (memory_types or list(MemoryType))]
        results = []
        if MemoryType.SEMANTIC in types:
            results.extend(self.semantic.query(text, limit))
        if MemoryType.EPISODIC in types:
            results.extend(self.episodic.recall(text, limit))
        if MemoryType.PROCEDURAL in types:
            results.extend(self.procedural.find(text))
        if MemoryType.AUTOBIOGRAPHICAL in types:
            results.extend(self.autobiographical.query(text, limit))
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.importance * x.confidence, reverse=True)
        seen = set()
        deduped = []
        for r in results:
            if r.id not in seen:
                seen.add(r.id)
                deduped.append(r)
        return deduped[:limit]

    def apply_decay(self, elapsed_hours=1.0):
        for store in [self.semantic, self.episodic, self.procedural, self.autobiographical]:
            for code in store.all():
                code.apply_decay(elapsed_hours)

    def stats(self):
        return {
            "semantic": len(self.semantic.all()),
            "episodic": len(self.episodic.all()),
            "procedural": len(self.procedural.all()),
            "autobiographical": len(self.autobiographical.all()),
            "total": sum(len(s.all()) for s in [
                self.semantic, self.episodic, self.procedural, self.autobiographical
            ]),
        }

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "semantic": [c.to_dict() for c in self.semantic.all()],
            "episodic": [c.to_dict() for c in self.episodic.all()],
            "procedural": [c.to_dict() for c in self.procedural.all()],
            "autobiographical": [c.to_dict() for c in self.autobiographical.all()],
        }

    def _sync_to_brain_graph(self, code, emotion=None):
        if not self.brain_graph:
            return
        try:
            self.brain_graph.add_memory_node(
                memory_id=code.id,
                text=code.content,
                memory_type=code.type.value,
                score=code.confidence,
                tags=code.tags,
                importance=code.importance,
                source=code.source,
                emotion=emotion,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[MemoryHierarchy] BrainGraph sync error: %s", e)

# memory\intelligence.py
# Mai_ai/memory/intelligence.py
import time
import threading
import json
import re
import logging

from memory.scoring import MemoryScorer

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
from memory.node import MemoryNode
from memory.graph import MemoryGraph
from memory.recall import RecallEngine
from memory.router import MemoryRouter
from knowledge.obsidian_writer import ObsidianWriter
from memory.reflection import MemoryReflectionEngine
from memory.decay import MemoryDecay


class MemoryIntelligence:

    def __init__(
        self,
        tagger,
        emotion,
        scorer=None,
        graph=None,
        recall_engine=None,
        router=None,
        writer=None,
        reflector=None,
        decay=None,
    ):

        self.tagger = tagger
        self.emotion = emotion

        self.scorer = scorer if scorer is not None else MemoryScorer()
        self.graph = graph if graph is not None else MemoryGraph()
        self.recall_engine = recall_engine if recall_engine is not None else RecallEngine()
        self.router = router if router is not None else MemoryRouter()
        self.writer = writer if writer is not None else ObsidianWriter()
        self.reflector = reflector if reflector is not None else MemoryReflectionEngine()
        self.decay = decay if decay is not None else MemoryDecay()

        # ðŸ§  thread safety
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # âš¡ runtime state
        self.working_memory = []  # ðŸ§  Volatile Short-Term Context Buffer
        self.last_reflection = time.time()

        # ðŸ§  reflection interval
        self.reflection_interval = 900

        # ðŸ§  minimum memory threshold
        self.memory_threshold = 0.25


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN MEMORY PIPELINE
    # ==================================================
    def process(self, user, ai):

        if not user:
            return None

        user = str(user).strip()
        ai = str(ai or "").strip()

        if not user:
            return None

        with self.lock:

            try:

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ·ï¸ TAG EXTRACTION
                # ==================================================

                tags = self.tagger.extract_tags(user)

                if not isinstance(tags, list):
                    tags = []


                # ==================================================
                # â¤ï¸ EMOTION ANALYSIS
                # ==================================================

                emo = self.emotion.analyze(user)

                if not isinstance(emo, dict):

                    emo = {
                        "type": "neutral",
                        "intensity": 0.5
                    }

                intensity = float(
                    emo.get("intensity", 0.5)
                )

                intensity = max(
                    0.0,
                    min(1.0, intensity)
                )


                # ==================================================
                # â­ MEMORY SCORING
                # ==================================================

                score = self.scorer.score(
                    text=user,
                    emotion=emo
                )

                try:
                    score = float(score)

                except Exception:
                    score = 0.5

                # ðŸ§  emotional weighting
                score += intensity * 0.15

                score = round(
                    max(0.0, min(score, 1.0)),
                    3
                )


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ›ï¸ ATKINSON-SHIFFRIN WORKING MEMORY BUFFER
                # ==================================================

                # Every interaction is tracked here dynamically 
                # so short-term conversation context is never dropped
                self.working_memory.append({
                    "user": user,
                    "ai": ai,
                    "tags": tags,
                    "emotion": emo,
                    "score": score,
                    "timestamp": time.time()
                })

                if len(self.working_memory) > 10:
                    self.working_memory.pop(0)


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸš« FILTER LOW VALUE MEMORIES FOR LONG-TERM STORAGE
                # ==================================================

                if score < self.memory_threshold:
                    return None


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  MEMORY TYPE DETECTION
                # ==================================================

                memory_type = self._detect_memory_type(
                    tags,
                    emo,
                    user
                )

                # Fact-update: archive old identity statements
                if memory_type == "identity":
                    stale = [
                        n for n in self.graph.nodes
                        if getattr(n, "memory_type", "") == "identity"
                    ]
                    for old in stale:
                        self.graph.remove(old)
                        old.archived = True


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  CREATE MEMORY NODE
                # ==================================================

                now = time.time()

                node = MemoryNode(
                    text=user,
                    score=score,
                    tags=tags,
                    emotion=emo,
                    memory_type=memory_type,
                    created_at=now,
                    last_accessed=now,
                    emotional_weight=intensity,
                    importance=score,
                )


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ”— INSERT INTO GRAPH
                # ==================================================

                existing = self.graph.add(node)

                # ðŸ§  only link brand new memories
                if existing == node:

                    try:
                        self.graph.link_nodes()

                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(
                            f"[GRAPH LINK ERROR] {e}"
                        )


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  CONSOLIDATION
                # ==================================================

                if (
                    len(self.graph.nodes) > 0
                    and
                    len(self.graph.nodes) % 25 == 0
                ):

                    try:

                        consolidated = (
                            self.decay.consolidate(
                                self.graph.nodes
                            )
                        )

                        if consolidated:

                            logger.info(
                                f"[MEMORY] "
                                f"Consolidated "
                                f"{len(consolidated)} memories"
                            )

                            for entry in consolidated:
                                try:
                                    c_node = MemoryNode(
                                        text=entry.get("summary", ""),
                                        score=entry.get("score", 0.5),
                                        tags=entry.get("tags", []),
                                        memory_type="semantic",
                                        emotional_weight=entry.get(
                                            "emotional_weight", 0.5
                                        ),
                                        importance=entry.get(
                                            "importance", 0.5
                                        ),
                                        source="system",
                                    )
                                    self.graph.add(c_node)
                                except Exception as e:
                                    logger.error(
                                        f"[CONSOLIDATED NODE ERROR] {e}"
                                    )
                            self.graph.link_nodes()

                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(
                            f"[CONSOLIDATION ERROR] {e}"
                        )


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§­ MEMORY ROUTING
                # ==================================================

                try:

                    routing = self.router.route(
                        user,
                        score,
                        tags,
                        emo
                    )

                    # âœ… FIXED: router returns dict
                    folder = routing.get(
                        "primary",
                        "conversations"
                    )

                except Exception as e:

                    logger.error(
                        f"[ROUTER ERROR] {e}"
                    )

                    folder = "conversations"


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ’¾ OBSIDIAN WRITE
                # ==================================================

                try:

                    self.writer.write(
                        folder=folder,
                        user=user,
                        ai=ai,
                        tags=tags,
                        emotion=emo,
                        score=score,
                        metadata={
                            "memory_type":
                                memory_type,
                            "reinforcement_count":
                                getattr(
                                    node,
                                    "reinforcement_count",
                                    0
                                )
                        }
                    )

                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(
                        f"[OBSIDIAN WRITE ERROR] {e}"
                    )


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  REFLECTION SYSTEM
                # ==================================================

                self._maybe_reflect(
                    tags,
                    emo,
                    score
                )

                return node

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[MEMORY PIPELINE ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MEMORY RECALL
    # ==================================================
    def recall(self, query):

        if not query:
            return None

        query = str(query).strip()

        if not query:
            return None

        with self.lock:

            try:

                tags = self.tagger.extract_tags(
                    query
                )

                if not isinstance(tags, list):
                    tags = []

                recalled = self.recall_engine.recall(
                    self.graph.nodes,
                    tags
                )


                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ” MEMORY REINFORCEMENT
                # ==================================================

                if (
                    recalled
                    and
                    isinstance(recalled, dict)
                ):

                    recalled_text = recalled.get(
                        "memory",
                        ""
                    )

                    if recalled_text:

                        for node in self.graph.nodes:

                            node_text = getattr(
                                node,
                                "text",
                                ""
                            )

                            if node_text == recalled_text:

                                try:
                                    self.decay.reinforce(
                                        node
                                    )
                                except Exception as e:
                                    logger.error(
                                        f"[REINFORCE ERROR] {e}"
                                    )

                                break

                return recalled

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[RECALL ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return None


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  REFLECTION SYSTEM
    # ==================================================
    def _maybe_reflect(
        self,
        tags,
        emo,
        score
    ):

        now = time.time()

        if (
            now - self.last_reflection
            < self.reflection_interval
        ):
            return

        try:

            reflection = self.reflector.generate(
                self.graph.nodes
            )

            if not reflection:
                return

            # âœ… FIXED: reflection engine returns dict
            reflection_text = reflection.get(
                "reflection",
                ""
            )

            if not reflection_text:
                return

            reflection_text = str(
                reflection_text
            ).strip()

            if not reflection_text:
                return

            self.writer.write(
                folder="reflections",
                user=reflection_text,
                ai="",
                tags=tags + ["reflection"],
                emotion=emo,
                score=score,
                metadata={
                    "confidence":
                        reflection.get(
                            "confidence",
                            0.5
                        ),
                    "total_patterns":
                        reflection.get(
                            "total_patterns",
                            0
                        )
                }
            )

            self.last_reflection = now

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(
                f"[REFLECTION ERROR] {e}"
            )


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MEMORY TYPE DETECTION
    # ==================================================
    def _detect_memory_type(
        self,
        tags,
        emo,
        text
    ):

        t = str(text).lower()

        if any(
            w in t for w in [
                "my name",
                "i am",
                "about me",
            ]
        ):
            return "identity"

        if any(
            w in t for w in [
                "you",
                "us",
                "together",
                "miss you",
            ]
        ):
            return "relationship"

        if emo.get(
            "intensity",
            0
        ) > 0.7:
            return "emotion"

        if "task" in tags:
            return "task"

        if "personal" in tags:
            return "reflection"

        return "conversation"


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MANUAL MEMORY DECAY
    # ==================================================
    def decay_memories(self):

        with self.lock:

            try:

                self.graph.nodes = (
                    self.decay.apply(
                        self.graph.nodes
                    )
                )

                return True

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[MEMORY DECAY ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return False


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MANUAL CONSOLIDATION
    # ==================================================
    def consolidate(self):

        with self.lock:

            try:

                return self.decay.consolidate(
                    self.graph.nodes
                )

            except Exception as e:

                logger.error(
                    f"[MANUAL CONSOLIDATION ERROR] {e}"
                )

                return []

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================
    def stats(self):

        return {
            "nodes":
                len(self.graph.nodes),
            "graph":
                self.graph.stats(),
            "decay":
                self.decay.stats(
                    self.graph.nodes
                ),
            "reflection_interval":
                self.reflection_interval,
            "memory_threshold":
                self.memory_threshold,
            "last_reflection":
                self.last_reflection,
            "working_memory_depth":
                len(self.working_memory)
        }
# memory\lifecycle.py
# memory/lifecycle.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryLifecycle:

    def __init__(self):
        self.short_term_hours = 48
        self.archive_days = 90
        self.importance_archive_threshold = 0.3
        self.importance_delete_threshold = 0.1

    def classify(self, node):
        if getattr(node, "archived", False):
            return "archived"

        age_seconds = time.time() - getattr(
            node, "timestamp", getattr(node, "created_at", time.time())
        )
        age_hours = age_seconds / 3600

        importance = (
            getattr(node, "importance", 0.5)
            or getattr(node, "score", 0.5)
            or 0.5
        )

        if age_hours < 1:
            return "working"
        elif age_hours < self.short_term_hours:
            return "short_term"
        else:
            if importance < self.importance_delete_threshold:
                return "delete"
            elif importance < self.importance_archive_threshold:
                return "archive"
            else:
                return "long_term"

    def should_archive(self, node):
        return self.classify(node) in ("archive", "archived")

    def should_delete(self, node):
        state = self.classify(node)

        if state == "delete":
            return True

        if state == "archived":
            age_seconds = time.time() - getattr(
                node, "timestamp", getattr(node, "created_at", time.time())
            )
            age_days = age_seconds / 86400
            return age_days > self.archive_days

        return False

    def apply_lifecycle(self, nodes):
        if not nodes:
            return [], [], []

        active = []
        to_archive = []
        to_delete = []

        for node in nodes:
            state = self.classify(node)
            if state == "delete":
                to_delete.append(node)
            elif state == "archive":
                node.archived = True
                to_archive.append(node)
            else:
                active.append(node)

        return active, to_archive, to_delete

# memory\manager.py
"""
MemoryManager â€” the single gate through which all memories pass.

Architecture direction: MemoryManager delegates to MemoryPipeline (the new,
cleaner path) for all storage operations. The old Memory class is kept as a
backward-compatible fallback.

Migration status (Phase 3 of 3):
- Phase 1: âœ… MemoryPipeline created with extract/validate/consolidation/lifecycle
- Phase 2: âœ… MemoryPipeline.store() added â€” full store path via optional plugins
- Phase 3: âž¡ MemoryManager.pipeline.store() is now the primary write path;
           old Memory.store() is the fallback when no pipeline is configured.
"""
import time
import logging
import threading

from core.event_bus import EventBus

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryManager:

    def __init__(self, memory, pipeline=None, event_bus=None,
                 tagger=None, emotion_analyzer=None, vector_store=None,
                 obsidian_writer=None, router=None, intelligence=None,
                 graph=None, relationship_updater=None):
        self.memory = memory
        self.pipeline = pipeline
        self.event_bus = event_bus or EventBus()
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        if self.pipeline is not None:
            self.pipeline.tagger = tagger or self.pipeline.tagger
            self.pipeline.emotion_analyzer = emotion_analyzer or self.pipeline.emotion_analyzer
            self.pipeline.vector_store = vector_store or self.pipeline.vector_store
            self.pipeline.obsidian_writer = obsidian_writer or self.pipeline.obsidian_writer
            self.pipeline.router = router or self.pipeline.router
            self.pipeline.intelligence = intelligence or self.pipeline.intelligence
            self.pipeline.graph = graph or self.pipeline.graph
            self.pipeline.relationship_updater = relationship_updater or self.pipeline.relationship_updater

    def process(self, user_input, response="", speaker="User"):
        if not user_input:
            return None

        with self._lock:
            extracted = []
            if self.pipeline:
                extracted = self.pipeline.extract(user_input)
                node = self.pipeline.store(user_input, response, speaker=speaker)
            else:
