                node = None

            if node is None and self.memory and hasattr(self.memory, "store"):
                node = self.memory.store(user_input, response, speaker=speaker)

            self.event_bus.publish("memory.extracted", {
                "user_input": user_input,
                "response": response,
                "extracted": [e.to_dict() for e in extracted],
                "node_id": getattr(node, "id", None) if node else None,
                "timestamp": time.time(),
            })

        return node

    def store(self, user, ai, speaker="User"):
        with self._lock:
            node = None
            if self.pipeline and hasattr(self.pipeline, "store"):
                node = self.pipeline.store(user, ai, speaker=speaker)
            if node is None and self.memory and hasattr(self.memory, "store"):
                node = self.memory.store(user, ai, speaker=speaker)
        return node

    def get_context(self, limit=5):
        if self.memory and hasattr(self.memory, "get_context"):
            return self.memory.get_context(limit)
        return []

    def get_structured(self):
        if self.memory and hasattr(self.memory, "get_structured"):
            return self.memory.get_structured()
        return {}

    def recall(self, query, limit=5):
        if self.memory and hasattr(self.memory, "recall"):
            return self.memory.recall(query, limit)
        return ""

    def extract(self, text):
        if self.pipeline:
            return self.pipeline.extract(text)
        return []

    def validate(self, text):
        if self.pipeline:
            return self.pipeline.validate(text)
        return True

    def run_consolidation(self, nodes=None):
        if self.pipeline:
            return self.pipeline.run_consolidation(nodes or [])
        return [], nodes or []

    def run_lifecycle(self, nodes=None):
        if self.pipeline:
            return self.pipeline.run_lifecycle(nodes or [])
        return (nodes or []), [], []

    @classmethod
    def from_memory(cls, memory, pipeline=None, event_bus=None,
                    vector_store=None, **kwargs):
        mgr = cls(memory=memory, pipeline=pipeline, event_bus=event_bus, **kwargs)
        if hasattr(memory, "tagger"):
            mgr.configure_pipeline(tagger=memory.tagger)
        if hasattr(memory, "emotion"):
            mgr.configure_pipeline(emotion_analyzer=memory.emotion)
        if hasattr(memory, "intel"):
            mgr.configure_pipeline(intelligence=memory.intel)
        if hasattr(memory, "writer"):
            mgr.configure_pipeline(obsidian_writer=memory.writer)
        if hasattr(memory, "router"):
            mgr.configure_pipeline(router=memory.router)
        if hasattr(memory, "rel_updater"):
            mgr.configure_pipeline(relationship_updater=memory.rel_updater)
        if vector_store is not None:
            mgr.configure_pipeline(vector_store=vector_store)
        return mgr

    def configure_pipeline(self, tagger=None, emotion_analyzer=None,
                            vector_store=None, obsidian_writer=None,
                            router=None, intelligence=None,
                            graph=None, relationship_updater=None):
        with self._lock:
            if self.pipeline is None:
                return
            if tagger is not None:
                self.pipeline.tagger = tagger
            if emotion_analyzer is not None:
                self.pipeline.emotion_analyzer = emotion_analyzer
            if vector_store is not None:
                self.pipeline.vector_store = vector_store
            if obsidian_writer is not None:
                self.pipeline.obsidian_writer = obsidian_writer
            if router is not None:
                self.pipeline.router = router
            if intelligence is not None:
                self.pipeline.intelligence = intelligence
            if graph is not None:
                self.pipeline.graph = graph
            if relationship_updater is not None:
                self.pipeline.relationship_updater = relationship_updater

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        if name.startswith("_"):
            raise AttributeError(name)
        if self.memory and hasattr(self.memory, name):
            return getattr(self.memory, name)
        raise AttributeError(
            f"'MemoryManager' has no attribute '{name}'"
        )

# memory\memory.py
# Mai_ai/memory/memory.py
# >>> UPDATED VERSION â€” includes MemoryRouter integration <<<
import logging
import os
import time
import threading
from collections import deque

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from config.settings import OBSIDIAN_VAULT

from knowledge.obsidian_reader import ObsidianReader
from knowledge.obsidian_writer import ObsidianWriter
from memory.relationship_updater import RelationshipUpdater
from memory.intelligence import MemoryIntelligence
from memory.tagger import Tagger
from memory.emotion_analyzer import EmotionAnalyzer
from memory.vector_store import VectorStore
from memory.router import MemoryRouter
from core.event_bus import EventBus


class Memory:

    def __init__(
        self,
        vault_path=None,
        event_bus=None,
    ):

        # ðŸ§  default vault path
        vault_path = vault_path or str(OBSIDIAN_VAULT)

        # ðŸ§  thread safety
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

        # ðŸ§  event bus
        self.event_bus = event_bus or EventBus()

        # ðŸ§  short-term memory
        self.short = deque(maxlen=20)

        # ðŸ§  obsidian systems
        self.reader = ObsidianReader(vault_path)

        self.writer = ObsidianWriter(vault_path)

        # ðŸ§  routing system
        self.router = MemoryRouter()

        # ðŸ§  relationship system
        self.rel_updater = RelationshipUpdater(
            os.path.join(
                vault_path,
                "relationships",
                "state.md"
            )
        )

        # ðŸ§  analyzers
        self.tagger = Tagger()

        self.emotion = EmotionAnalyzer()

        # ðŸ§  intelligence system
        self.intel = MemoryIntelligence(
            self.tagger,
            self.emotion
        )

        # ðŸ§  vector semantic memory
        self.vector = VectorStore()

        # ðŸ§  runtime state
        self.current_topic = None

        self.last_user_message = None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  STORE MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def store(self, user, ai, speaker="User"):

        if not user:
            return None

        try:

            # ðŸ·ï¸ extract tags safely
            tags = self.tagger.extract_tags(user) or []

            # â¤ï¸ analyze emotion safely
            emo = self.emotion.analyze(user) or {
                "type": "neutral",
                "intensity": 0.5
            }

            # ðŸ§  short-term memory object
            memory_object = {
                "user": user,
                "ai": ai,
                "tags": tags,
                "emotion": emo,
                "timestamp": time.time(),
                "speaker": speaker,
            }

            # ðŸ§  working memory (thread-safe)
            with self._lock:
                self.short.append(memory_object)

            # â¤ï¸ relationship learning
            self.rel_updater.update(user)

            # ðŸ§  intelligence pipeline
            node = self.intel.process(user, ai)

            # ðŸ§  semantic vector memory
            combined = f"""
USER:
{user}

AI:
{ai}
"""

            vector_id = self.vector.add(
                text=combined,
                metadata={
                    "tags": tags,
                    "emotion": emo,
                    "timestamp": time.time(),
                    "importance": getattr(
                        node,
                        "score",
                        0.5
                    ),
                    "speaker": speaker,
                }
            )

            if node and vector_id:
                node.vector_id = vector_id

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  ROUTE MEMORY
            # ==================================================

            route = self.router.route(
                text=user,
                score=getattr(
                    node,
                    "score",
                    0.5
                ),
                tags=tags,
                emotion=emo
            )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  WRITE TO OBSIDIAN
            # ==================================================

            self.writer.write_memory(
                routes=route["routes"],
                user=user,
                ai=ai,
                tags=tags,
                emotion=emo,
                score=getattr(
                    node,
                    "score",
                    0.5
                ),
                metadata={
                    "topic": self.current_topic
                }
            )

            # ðŸ§  runtime updates (thread-safe)
            with self._lock:
                self.last_user_message = user
                if tags:
                    self.current_topic = tags[0]

            # ðŸ§  publish conversation.finished
            self.event_bus.publish("conversation.finished", {
                "user_input": user,
                "response": ai,
                "emotion": emo,
                "tags": tags,
                "node_id": getattr(node, "id", None),
                "speaker": speaker,
            })

            return node

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("[MEMORY STORE ERROR] %s", e)
            return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SHORT-TERM CONTEXT
    # ==================================================

    def get_context(self, limit=5):

        recent = list(self.short)[-limit:]

        formatted = []

        for mem in recent:

            formatted.append(
                f"\nUser: {mem['user']}\nMai: {mem['ai']}\n"
            )

        return formatted

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  HYBRID MEMORY RECALL
    # ==================================================

    def recall(self, query, limit=5):

        if not query:
            return ""

        try:

            # ðŸ·ï¸ tags
            tags = self.tagger.extract_tags(query) or []

            # ðŸ” vector semantic search
            vector_memories = self.vector.search(
                query,
                top_k=limit
            )

            # ðŸ§  intelligence graph recall
            graph_memories = self.intel.recall(query)

            merged = []

            seen = set()

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  VECTOR RESULTS
            # ==================================================

            if vector_memories:

                for mem in vector_memories:

                    if isinstance(mem, dict):

                        text = mem.get("text", "")

                    else:

                        text = str(mem)

                    if text and text not in seen:

                        merged.append(text)

                        seen.add(text)

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  GRAPH RESULTS
            # ==================================================

            if graph_memories:

                if isinstance(graph_memories, dict):

                    text = graph_memories.get(
                        "memory",
                        ""
                    )

                    if text and text not in seen:

                        merged.append(text)

                        seen.add(text)

                    for related in graph_memories.get(
                        "related",
                        []
                    ):

                        if related and related not in seen:

                            merged.append(related)

                            seen.add(related)

                elif isinstance(graph_memories, list):

                    for mem in graph_memories:

                        text = getattr(
                            mem,
                            "text",
                            str(mem)
                        )

                        if text and text not in seen:

                            merged.append(text)

                            seen.add(text)

            # ==================================================
            # â¤ï¸ EMOTIONAL RECALL
            # ==================================================

            emotional = self._emotionally_related(
                query
            )

            for mem in emotional:

                if mem not in seen:

                    merged.append(mem)

                    seen.add(mem)

            # ðŸ›¡ï¸ safe return
            if not merged:
                return ""

            merged = merged[:limit]

            return "\n\n".join(merged)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("[MEMORY RECALL ERROR] %s", e)
            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # â¤ï¸ EMOTIONAL RECALL
    # ==================================================

    def _emotionally_related(self, query):

        emotional = []

        query_emo = self.emotion.analyze(query) or {
            "type": "neutral"
        }

        target_type = query_emo.get(
            "type",
            "neutral"
        )

        for mem in self.short:

            mem_type = mem["emotion"].get(
                "type",
                "neutral"
            )

            if mem_type == target_type:

                emotional.append(
                    f"\nUser: {mem['user']}\nMai: {mem['ai']}\n"
                )

        return emotional[:2]

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  STRUCTURED OBSIDIAN MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def get_structured(self):

        try:

            data = self.reader.get_all()

            if not isinstance(data, dict):
                logger.warning("[MEMORY WARNING] Structured memory was not a dict. Resetting.")

                return {
                    "relationship": {},
                    "preferences": {},
                    "reflections": {},
                    "system": ""
                }

            data.setdefault(
                "relationship",
                {}
            )

            data.setdefault(
                "preferences",
                {}
            )

            data.setdefault(
                "reflections",
                {}
            )

            data.setdefault(
                "system",
            ""
            )

            if not isinstance(
                data["relationship"],
                dict
            ):
                data["relationship"] = {}

            if not isinstance(
                data["preferences"],
                dict
            ):
                data["preferences"] = {}

            if not isinstance(
                data["reflections"],
                dict
            ):
                data["reflections"] = {}

            if not isinstance(
                data["system"],
                str
            ):
                data["system"] = str(
                    data["system"]
                )

            return data

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("[STRUCTURED MEMORY ERROR] %s", e)

            return {
                "relationship": {},
                "preferences": {},
                "reflections": {},
                "system": ""
            }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SESSION STATE
    # ==================================================

    def session_state(self):

        return {
            "short_term_memories": len(self.short),
            "current_topic": self.current_topic,
            "last_user_message": self.last_user_message,
        }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š MEMORY STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        return {
            "working_memory": len(self.short),
            "graph_nodes": len(
                self.intel.graph.nodes
            ),
            "current_topic": self.current_topic,
        }

# memory\node.py
# Mai_ai/memory/node.py
import time
import uuid
import threading

from config.settings import Settings
from memory.schema import MemoryObject


class MemoryNode(MemoryObject):

    def __init__(
        self,
        text="",
        score=None,
        tags=None,
        emotion=None,
        memory_type="conversation",
        emotional_weight=None,
        importance=None,
        confidence=None,
        source="user",
        created_at=None,
        last_accessed=None,
    ):

        super().__init__(
            content=text,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            confidence=(
                confidence
                if confidence is not None
                else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_CONFIDENCE
            ),
            importance=(
                importance
                if importance is not None
                else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_IMPORTANCE
            ),
            timestamp=created_at,
        )

        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        self.id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.vector_id = None
        self.text = str(text or "").strip()
        self.score = self._clamp(
            score
            if score is not None
            else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_SCORE
        )
        self.tags = self._clean_tags(tags or [])
        self.emotion = self._clean_emotion(emotion)
        self.memory_type = str(memory_type or "conversation").lower()
        self.emotional_weight = self._clamp(
            emotional_weight
            if emotional_weight is not None
            else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_EMOTIONAL_WEIGHT
        )
        self.importance = self._clamp(
            importance
            if importance is not None
            else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_IMPORTANCE
        )
        self.confidence = self._clamp(
            confidence
            if confidence is not None
            else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_CONFIDENCE
        )

        self.links = []
        self.link_strengths = {}
        self.reinforcement = Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_REINFORCEMENT
        self.reinforcement_count = Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_REINFORCEMENT_COUNT
        self.access_count = Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_ACCESS_COUNT

        now = time.time()
        self.created_at = created_at if created_at is not None else now
        self.last_accessed = last_accessed if last_accessed is not None else now
        self.source = str(source or "user")

        self.archived = False
        self.consolidated = False

        self.content = self.text
        self.type = self.memory_type
        self.timestamp = self.created_at

    def _clamp(self, value):
        try:
            value = float(value)
        except Exception:
            value = Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_SCORE
        return max(
            Settings.MEMORY_NODE_MIN_VALUE,
            min(Settings.MEMORY_NODE_MAX_VALUE, value)
        )

    def _clean_tags(self, tags):
        if not isinstance(tags, list):
            return []
        cleaned = []
        for tag in tags:
            if not tag:
                continue
            tag = str(tag).strip().lower()
            if tag and tag not in cleaned:
                cleaned.append(tag)
        return cleaned

    def _clean_emotion(self, emotion):
        if not isinstance(emotion, dict):
            emotion = {}
        return {
            "type": str(
                emotion.get("type", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_EMOTION_TYPE)
            ),
            "intensity": self._clamp(
                emotion.get("intensity", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_EMOTION_INTENSITY)
            ),
        }

    def reinforce(self, amount=None):
        with self.lock:
            amount = (
                amount
                if amount is not None
                else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_REINFORCE_AMOUNT
            )
            amount = max(0.0, float(amount))
            self.reinforcement += 1
            self.reinforcement_count += 1
            self.access_count += 1
            self.last_accessed = time.time()

            boost = amount * (
                1 / (1 + (self.reinforcement * Settings.MEMORY_NODE_REINFORCEMENT_DECAY))
            )
            boost *= (1 + (self.emotional_weight * Settings.MEMORY_NODE_EMOTIONAL_BOOST))

            self.score = self._clamp(self.score + boost)
            self.importance = self._clamp(
                self.importance + (boost * Settings.MEMORY_NODE_IMPORTANCE_BOOST)
            )
            return self.score

    def add_link(self, node, strength=None):
        with self.lock:
            if not node:
                return
            if not hasattr(node, "id"):
                return
            if node.id == self.id:
                return

            strength = self._clamp(
                strength
                if strength is not None
                else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_LINK_STRENGTH
            )

            if node not in self.links:
                self.links.append(node)
                self.link_strengths[node.id] = strength
            else:
                current = self.link_strengths.get(
                    node.id, Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_LINK_STRENGTH
                )
                self.link_strengths[node.id] = self._clamp(
                    current + Settings.MEMORY_NODE_LINK_INCREMENT
                )

    def decay(self, rate=None):
        with self.lock:
            rate = max(
                Settings.MEMORY_NODE_MIN_DECAY,
                float(rate if rate is not None else Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_DECAY_RATE)
            )
            resistance = self.reinforcement * Settings.MEMORY_NODE_REINFORCEMENT_RESISTANCE
            emotional_resistance = self.emotional_weight * Settings.MEMORY_NODE_EMOTIONAL_RESISTANCE
            final_decay = max(
                Settings.MEMORY_NODE_MIN_DECAY,
                rate - resistance - emotional_resistance
            )
            self.score = max(Settings.MEMORY_NODE_MIN_VALUE, self.score - final_decay)
            return self.score

    def access(self):
        with self.lock:
            self.access_count += 1
            self.last_accessed = time.time()
            self.reinforce(Settings.MEMORY_NODE_ACCESS_REINFORCE)
            return self.score

    def age(self):
        return max(0, time.time() - self.created_at)

    def to_dict(self):
        with self.lock:
            data = {
                "id": self.id,
                "vector_id": self.vector_id,
                "text": self.text,
                "score": self.score,
                "tags": self.tags,
                "emotion": self.emotion,
                "memory_type": self.memory_type,
                "emotional_weight": self.emotional_weight,
                "importance": self.importance,
                "confidence": self.confidence,
                "reinforcement": self.reinforcement,
                "reinforcement_count": self.reinforcement_count,
                "access_count": self.access_count,
                "created_at": self.created_at,
                "last_accessed": self.last_accessed,
                "source": self.source,
                "archived": self.archived,
                "consolidated": self.consolidated,
                "version": self.version,
                "links": [
                    n.id for n in self.links if hasattr(n, "id")
                ],
            }
            return data

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            data = {}

        node = cls(
            text=data.get("text", ""),
            score=data.get("score", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_SCORE),
            tags=data.get("tags", []),
            emotion=data.get("emotion", {}),
            memory_type=data.get("memory_type", "conversation"),
            emotional_weight=data.get("emotional_weight", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_EMOTIONAL_WEIGHT),
            importance=data.get("importance", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_IMPORTANCE),
            confidence=data.get("confidence", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_CONFIDENCE),
            source=data.get("source", "user"),
            created_at=data.get("created_at"),
            last_accessed=data.get("last_accessed"),
        )

        node.id = data.get("id", node.id)
        node.vector_id = data.get("vector_id")
        node.reinforcement = int(data.get("reinforcement", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_REINFORCEMENT))
        node.reinforcement_count = int(data.get("reinforcement_count", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_REINFORCEMENT_COUNT))
        node.access_count = int(data.get("access_count", Settings.MEMORY_NODE_DEFAULT_ACCESS_COUNT))
        node.archived = bool(data.get("archived", False))
        node.consolidated = bool(data.get("consolidated", False))
        node.version = int(data.get("version", 1))

        node.content = node.text
        node.type = node.memory_type
        node.timestamp = node.created_at

        return node

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"<MemoryNode "
            f"type={self.memory_type} "
            f"score={round(self.score, 2)} "
            f"tags={self.tags}>"
        )

# memory\pipeline.py
import time
import logging
import threading
from collections import deque

from memory.extractor import FastExtractor
from memory.consolidation import consolidate
from memory.lifecycle import MemoryLifecycle
from memory.schema import MemoryObject

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryPipeline:

    def __init__(self, fast_extractor=None, lifecycle=None, event_bus=None,
                 tagger=None, emotion_analyzer=None, vector_store=None,
                 obsidian_writer=None, router=None, intelligence=None,
                 graph=None, relationship_updater=None,
                 memory_hierarchy=None):
        self.fast_extractor = fast_extractor or FastExtractor()
        self.lifecycle = lifecycle or MemoryLifecycle()
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.tagger = tagger
        self.emotion_analyzer = emotion_analyzer
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.obsidian_writer = obsidian_writer
        self.router = router
        self.intelligence = intelligence
        self.graph = graph
        self.relationship_updater = relationship_updater
        self.memory_hierarchy = memory_hierarchy
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        self.short = deque(maxlen=20)
        self.current_topic = None
        self.last_user_message = None

    def extract(self, text):
        if not text:
            return []
        text = str(text).strip()
        return self.fast_extractor.extract(text)

    def validate(self, text):
        if not text:
            return False
        text = str(text).strip()
        if len(text) < 2:
            return False
        if text.lower() in ("ok", "okay", "yes", "no", "hi", "hello", "hey"):
            return False
        return True

    def store(self, user, ai, speaker="User"):
        if not self.validate(user):
            return None

        tags = self.tagger.extract_tags(user) if self.tagger else []
        emo = (self.emotion_analyzer.analyze(user) or
               {"type": "neutral", "intensity": 0.5}) if self.emotion_analyzer else {}

        extracted = self.extract(user)
        obj = MemoryObject(content=user, memory_type="conversation")
        if tags:
            obj.metadata["tags"] = tags
        if emo:
            obj.metadata["emotion"] = emo
        obj.metadata["speaker"] = speaker

        with self._lock:
            self.short.append({"user": user, "ai": ai, "tags": tags, "emotion": emo,
                               "timestamp": time.time(), "speaker": speaker})

        if self.relationship_updater:
            self.relationship_updater.update(user)

        node = None
        if self.intelligence:
            node = self.intelligence.process(user, ai)
        elif self.vector_store:
            node = obj

        if self.vector_store:
            combined = f"\nUSER:\n{user}\n\nAI:\n{ai}\n"
            vector_id = self.vector_store.add(
                text=combined,
                metadata={
                    "tags": tags,
                    "emotion": emo,
                    "timestamp": time.time(),
                    "importance": getattr(node, "confidence", 0.5),
                    "speaker": speaker,
                }
            )
            if node and vector_id:
                node.vector_id = vector_id

        route = {}
        if self.router:
            route = self.router.route(
                text=user,
                score=getattr(node, "confidence", 0.5),
                tags=tags,
                emotion=emo,
            )

        if self.obsidian_writer:
            self.obsidian_writer.write_memory(
                routes=route.get("routes") if route else [],
                user=user,
                ai=ai,
                tags=tags,
                emotion=emo,
                score=getattr(node, "confidence", 0.5),
                metadata={"topic": self.current_topic},
            )

        if self.graph:
            try:
                self.graph.process_conversation(user, ai)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[GRAPH PROCESS ERROR] {e}")

        if self.memory_hierarchy:
            try:
                self.memory_hierarchy.store_from_memory_object(obj)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[MEMORY HIERARCHY ERROR] {e}")

        with self._lock:
            self.current_topic = tags[0] if tags else self.current_topic
            self.last_user_message = user

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("conversation.finished", {
                "user_input": user,
                "response": ai,
                "emotion": emo,
                "tags": tags,
                "node_id": getattr(node, "id", None) if node else None,
            })

        return node

    def process(self, user_input, response=None, existing_pipeline=None):
        if not self.validate(user_input):
            return None

        extracted = self.extract(user_input)

        event_data = {
            "user_input": user_input,
            "response": response or "",
            "extracted": [e.to_dict() for e in extracted],
            "timestamp": time.time(),
        }

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("memory.extracted", event_data)

        if existing_pipeline is not None:
            try:
                node = existing_pipeline(user_input, response or "")
                if node:
                    event_data["node_id"] = getattr(node, "id", None)
                    if self.event_bus:
                        self.event_bus.publish("memory.stored", event_data)
                return node
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[MemoryPipeline] Pipeline error: {e}")
                return None

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("memory.stored", event_data)

        return event_data

    def run_consolidation(self, nodes):
        if not nodes:
            return [], nodes
        new_nodes, remaining = consolidate(nodes)
        if new_nodes and self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("memory.consolidated", {
                "count": len(new_nodes),
                "source_count": len(nodes),
            })
        return new_nodes, remaining

    def run_lifecycle(self, nodes):
        if not nodes:
            return [], [], []
        active, to_archive, to_delete = self.lifecycle.apply_lifecycle(nodes)
        if to_archive and self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("memory.lifecycle", {
                "archived": len(to_archive),
                "deleted": len(to_delete),
            })
        return active, to_archive, to_delete

# memory\recall.py
# Mai_ai/memory/recall.py
import threading
import time
import logging
from collections import Counter

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class RecallEngine:

    def __init__(self):

        # ðŸ§  recall tuning
        self.tag_weight = 0.45

        self.text_weight = 0.30

        self.score_weight = 0.15

        self.recency_weight = 0.10

        # ðŸ§  recall limits
        self.max_results = 5

        self.min_relevance = 0.30

        # ðŸ§  thread safety
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN RECALL
    # ==================================================
    def recall(
        self,
        nodes,
        query_tags=None,
        query_text=""
    ):

        with self.lock:

            if not nodes:
                return None

            query_tags = query_tags or []

            query_text = str(
                query_text or ""
            ).lower()

            ranked = []

            now = time.time()

            for node in nodes:

                try:

                    relevance = self._relevance(

                        node=node,

                        query_tags=query_tags,

                        query_text=query_text,

                        now=now
                    )

                    if relevance < self.min_relevance:
                        continue

                    ranked.append({

                        "node": node,

                        "relevance": round(
                            relevance,
                            4
                        )
                    })

                except Exception as e:

                    logger.error(
                        f"[RECALL NODE ERROR] {e}"
                    )

            # ðŸš« nothing found
            if not ranked:
                return None

            # ðŸ§  best first
            ranked.sort(

                key=lambda x: x["relevance"],

                reverse=True
            )

            top = ranked[:self.max_results]

            # ðŸ§  reinforce recalled memories
            for item in top:

                node = item["node"]

                node.last_accessed = now

                node.reinforcement_count = (
                    getattr(
                        node,
                        "reinforcement_count",
                        0
                    ) + 1
                )

            # ðŸ§  structured return
            results = []

            for item in top:

                node = item["node"]

                results.append({

                    "memory":
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "text",
                            ""
                        ),

                    "score":
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "score",
                            0.5
                        ),

                    "relevance":
                        item["relevance"],

                    "tags":
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "tags",
                            []
                        ),

                    "memory_type":
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "memory_type",
                            "general"
                        ),

                    "emotion":
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "emotion",
                            {}
                        ),

                    "created_at":
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "created_at",
                            0
                        ),
                })

            # âœ… single best memory shortcut
            if len(results) == 1:
                return results[0]

            return results

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  RELEVANCE SCORING
    # ==================================================
    def _relevance(
        self,
        node,
        query_tags,
        query_text,
        now
    ):

        relevance = 0.0

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ·ï¸ TAG MATCHING
        # ==================================================

        node_tags = set(
            getattr(
                node,
                "tags",
                []
            )
        )

        query_tags = set(query_tags)

        if node_tags and query_tags:

            overlap = len(
                node_tags.intersection(
                    query_tags
                )
            )

            union = len(
                node_tags.union(
                    query_tags
                )
            )

            if union > 0:

                tag_score = overlap / union

                relevance += (
                    tag_score
                    *
                    self.tag_weight
                )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ“ TEXT MATCHING
        # ==================================================

        if query_text:

            node_text = str(
                getattr(
                    node,
                    "text",
                    ""
                )
            ).lower()

            query_words = set(
                query_text.split()
            )

            node_words = set(
                node_text.split()
            )

            if query_words and node_words:

                overlap = len(
                    query_words.intersection(
                        node_words
                    )
                )

                union = len(
                    query_words.union(
                        node_words
                    )
                )

                if union > 0:

                    text_score = overlap / union

                    relevance += (
                        text_score
                        *
                        self.text_weight
                    )

        # ==================================================
        # â­ MEMORY IMPORTANCE
        # ==================================================

        memory_score = float(
            getattr(
                node,
                "score",
                0.5
            )
        )

        relevance += (
            memory_score
            *
            self.score_weight
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ•’ RECENCY BOOST
        # ==================================================

        last_accessed = getattr(
            node,
            "last_accessed",
            now
        )

        age_hours = max(

            1,

            (now - last_accessed)
            / 3600
        )

        recency = min(

            1.0,

            1 / (
                age_hours * 0.08
            )
        )

        relevance += (
            recency
            *
            self.recency_weight
        )

        # ==================================================
        # â¤ï¸ EMOTIONAL BOOST
        # ==================================================

        emotion = getattr(
            node,
            "emotion",
            {}
        )

        if isinstance(emotion, dict):

            intensity = float(
                emotion.get(
                    "intensity",
                    0.5
                )
            )

            relevance += (
                intensity * 0.05
            )

        return round(
            min(relevance, 1.0),
            4
        )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SEARCH BY MEMORY TYPE
    # ==================================================
    def search_type(
        self,
        nodes,
        memory_type
    ):

        with self.lock:

            results = []

            for node in nodes:

                if getattr(
                    node,
                    "memory_type",
                    ""
                ) == memory_type:

                    results.append(node)

            return results

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MOST COMMON TAGS
    # ==================================================
    def common_tags(
        self,
        nodes,
        limit=10
    ):

        with self.lock:

            counter = Counter()

            for node in nodes:

                tags = getattr(
                    node,
                    "tags",
                    []
                )

                counter.update(tags)

            return counter.most_common(
                limit
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================
    def stats(self):

        return {

            "tag_weight":
                self.tag_weight,

            "text_weight":
                self.text_weight,

            "score_weight":
                self.score_weight,

            "recency_weight":
                self.recency_weight,

            "max_results":
                self.max_results,

            "min_relevance":
                self.min_relevance,
        }
# memory\reflection.py
# Mai_ai/memory/reflection.py
import logging
import random
import threading
from collections import Counter

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from config.settings import Settings


class MemoryReflectionEngine:

    def __init__(self):

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  THREAD SAFETY
        # ==================================================

        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  CONFIG
        # ==================================================

        self.history_limit = (
            Settings.REFLECTION_HISTORY_LIMIT
        )

        self.max_ranked_nodes = (
            Settings.REFLECTION_MAX_RANKED_NODES
        )

        self.max_top_reflections = (
            Settings.REFLECTION_MAX_TOP_RESULTS
        )

        # ==================================================
        # ðŸ§  RUNTIME MEMORY
        # ==================================================

        self.previous_reflections = []

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN REFLECTION SYSTEM
    # ==================================================

    def generate(
        self,
        nodes
    ):

        if not nodes:
            return None

        if not isinstance(nodes, list):
            return None

        with self.lock:

            try:

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  PRIORITIZE IMPORTANT MEMORIES
                # ==================================================

                ranked_nodes = sorted(

                    nodes,

                    key=lambda n: (

                        getattr(
                            n,
                            "score",
                            Settings.REFLECTION_DEFAULT_SCORE
                        )

                        *

                        max(
                            1,
                            getattr(
                                n,
                                "reinforcement_count",
                                0
                            )
                        )
                    ),

                    reverse=True

                )[:self.max_ranked_nodes]

                reflections = []

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  PATTERN TRACKING
                # ==================================================

                tag_counter = Counter()

                emotion_counter = Counter()

                memory_type_counter = Counter()

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  ANALYZE MEMORIES
                # ==================================================

                for node in ranked_nodes:

                    tags = getattr(
                        node,
                        "tags",
                        []
                    )

                    if not isinstance(tags, list):
                        tags = []

                    emotion = getattr(
                        node,
                        "emotion",
                        {}
                    )

                    if not isinstance(emotion, dict):
                        emotion = {}

                    memory_type = getattr(
                        node,
                        "memory_type",
                        "conversation"
                    )

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  COUNT PATTERNS
                    # ==================================================

                    for tag in tags:

                        tag_counter[
                            str(tag).lower()
                        ] += 1

                    emotion_type = str(
                        emotion.get(
                            "type",
                            "neutral"
                        )
                    ).lower()

                    emotion_counter[
                        emotion_type
                    ] += 1

                    memory_type_counter[
                        memory_type
                    ] += 1

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  CODING INTEREST
                    # ==================================================

                    if any(
                        tag in tags
                        for tag in [
                            "coding",
                            "programming"
                        ]
                    ):

                        reflections.append({

                            "text":
                                "User consistently shows strong interest in coding and technical topics.",

                            "confidence":
                                Settings.REFLECTION_CODING_CONFIDENCE,

                            "type":
                                "interest"
                        })

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  AI INTEREST
                    # ==================================================

                    node_text = str(
                        getattr(
                            node,
                            "text",
                            ""
                        )
                    ).lower()

                    if any(
                        keyword in node_text
                        for keyword in
                        Settings.REFLECTION_AI_KEYWORDS
                    ):

                        reflections.append({

                            "text":
                                "User frequently explores AI and intelligence-related ideas.",

                            "confidence":
                                Settings.REFLECTION_AI_CONFIDENCE,

                            "type":
                                "interest"
                        })

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  EMOTIONAL LEARNING
                    # ==================================================

                    if emotion_type in (
                        Settings.REFLECTION_POSITIVE_EMOTIONS
                    ):

                        reflections.append({

                            "text":
                                "The current conversational style appears emotionally positive for the user.",

                            "confidence":
                                Settings.REFLECTION_EMOTIONAL_CONFIDENCE,

                            "type":
                                "emotional"
                        })

                    if emotion_type in (
                        Settings.REFLECTION_NEGATIVE_EMOTIONS
                    ):

                        reflections.append({

                            "text":
                                "Certain conversational patterns may create emotional friction for the user.",

                            "confidence":
                                Settings.REFLECTION_EMOTIONAL_CONFIDENCE,

                            "type":
                                "emotional"
                        })

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  PERSONAL DISCLOSURE
                    # ==================================================

                    if (
                        "personal" in tags
                        or
                        memory_type == "identity"
                    ):

                        reflections.append({

                            "text":
                                "User increasingly shares personal thoughts and identity-related information.",

                            "confidence":
                                Settings.REFLECTION_RELATIONSHIP_CONFIDENCE,

                            "type":
                                "relationship"
                        })

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  LONG-TERM PATTERNS
                # ==================================================

                if tag_counter.get(
                    "coding",
                    0
                ) >= Settings.REFLECTION_CODING_THRESHOLD:

                    reflections.append({

                        "text":
                            "Coding appears to be a stable long-term interest.",

                        "confidence":
                            Settings.REFLECTION_PATTERN_CONFIDENCE,

                        "type":
                            "pattern"
                    })

                if tag_counter.get(
                    "personal",
                    0
                ) >= Settings.REFLECTION_PERSONAL_THRESHOLD:

                    reflections.append({

                        "text":
                            "User is becoming increasingly comfortable with personal disclosure.",

                        "confidence":
                            Settings.REFLECTION_PERSONAL_CONFIDENCE,

                        "type":
                            "relationship"
                    })

                if (
                    emotion_counter.get(
                        "positive",
                        0
                    )
                    >
                    emotion_counter.get(
                        "negative",
                        0
                    )
                ):

                    reflections.append({

                        "text":
                            "Recent interactions have generally carried a positive emotional tone.",

                        "confidence":
                            Settings.REFLECTION_POSITIVE_TONE_CONFIDENCE,

                        "type":
                            "emotional"
                    })

                if memory_type_counter.get(
                    "relationship",
                    0
                ) >= Settings.REFLECTION_RELATIONSHIP_THRESHOLD:

                    reflections.append({

                        "text":
                            "Relationship-oriented interactions are becoming increasingly significant.",

                        "confidence":
                            Settings.REFLECTION_RELATIONSHIP_PATTERN_CONFIDENCE,

                        "type":
                            "relationship"
                    })

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸš« NO REFLECTIONS
                # ==================================================

                if not reflections:
                    return None

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸš« REMOVE REPETITIONS
                # ==================================================

                available = [

                    reflection

                    for reflection in reflections

                    if reflection["text"]
                    not in self.previous_reflections
                ]

                if not available:
                    available = reflections

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  PRIORITIZE CONFIDENT REFLECTIONS
                # ==================================================

                available = sorted(

                    available,

                    key=lambda r: r.get(
                        "confidence",
                        Settings.REFLECTION_DEFAULT_CONFIDENCE
                    ),

                    reverse=True
                )

                top_reflections = available[
                    :self.max_top_reflections
                ]

                selected = random.choice(
                    top_reflections
                )

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  STORE HISTORY
                # ==================================================

                self._remember(
                    selected["text"]
                )

                return {

                    "reflection":
                        selected["text"],

                    "confidence":
                        round(
                            selected.get(
                                "confidence",
                                Settings.REFLECTION_DEFAULT_CONFIDENCE
                            ),
                            3
                        ),

                    "type":
                        selected.get(
                            "type",
                            "general"
                        ),

                    "total_patterns":
                        len(reflections),

                    "dominant_tags":
                        dict(
                            tag_counter.most_common(
                                Settings.REFLECTION_TOP_TAGS
                            )
                        ),

                    "dominant_emotions":
                        dict(
                            emotion_counter.most_common(
                                Settings.REFLECTION_TOP_EMOTIONS
                            )
                        )
                }

            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("[REFLECTION ERROR] %s", e)
                return None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  STORE REFLECTION HISTORY
    # ==================================================

    def _remember(
        self,
        reflection
    ):

        if not reflection:
            return

        with self.lock:

            self.previous_reflections.append(
                reflection
            )

            # ðŸ§  limit memory growth
            if (
                len(self.previous_reflections)
                >
                self.history_limit
            ):

                self.previous_reflections.pop(0)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§¹ CLEAR HISTORY
    # ==================================================

    def clear_history(self):

        with self.lock:

            self.previous_reflections.clear()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG STATS
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        with self.lock:

            return {

                "stored_reflections":
                    len(
                        self.previous_reflections
                    ),

                "history_limit":
                    self.history_limit,

                "max_ranked_nodes":
                    self.max_ranked_nodes
            }
# memory\relationship_updater.py
# Mai_ai/memory/relationship_updater.py
import os
import re
import time
import threading  # Added for thread-safe operations

class RelationshipUpdater:
    def __init__(self, path):

        self.path = path

        # ðŸ§  relationship decay settings
        self.decay_rate = 0.002

        # ðŸ§  emotional amplification
        self.strong_emotion_bonus = 0.05

        # ðŸ”’ Thread safety lock to prevent race conditions during file updates
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

    # ðŸ§  MAIN UPDATE
    def update(
        self,
        text,
        emotion=None
    ):
        # Acquire lock to ensure only one thread updates our relationship file at a time
        with self.lock:
            # ðŸ›¡ï¸ create file if missing
            self._ensure_file()

            try:

                with open(
                    self.path,
                    "r",
                    encoding="utf-8"
                ) as f:

                    content = f.read()

            except Exception:

                content = ""

            closeness = self._get(
                content,
                "Closeness"
            )

            trust = self._get(
                content,
                "Trust"
            )

            interaction_count = self._get(
                content,
                "Interactions"
            )

            last_interaction = self._get_timestamp(
                content
            )

            t = text.lower()

            # ðŸ§  TIME DECAY
            closeness, trust = self._apply_decay(
                closeness,
                trust,
                last_interaction
            )

            # ðŸ§  POSITIVE SOCIAL SIGNALS
            if any(
                word in t
                for word in [
                    "thank",
                    "thanks",
                    "appreciate"
                ]
            ):

                closeness += 0.02

            # ðŸ§  TRUST SIGNALS
            if any(
                word in t
                for word in [
                    "trust",
                    "help",
                    "rely",
                    "need you"
                ]
            ):

                trust += 0.02

            # ðŸ§  EMOTIONAL OPENNESS
            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in [
                    "miss you",
                    "sad",
                    "lonely",
                    "worried",
                    "i feel"
                ]
            ):

                closeness += 0.03

            # ðŸ§  NEGATIVE SIGNALS
            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in [
                    "shut up",
                    "annoying",
                    "leave me alone",
                    "hate you"
                ]
            ):

                closeness -= 0.04
                trust -= 0.03

            # ðŸ§  STRONG EMOTION BONUS
            if (
                emotion
                and
                isinstance(emotion, dict)
            ):

                intensity = emotion.get(
                    "intensity",
                    0.5
                )

                if intensity > 0.8:

                    closeness += (
                        self.strong_emotion_bonus
                        * 0.5
                    )

            # ðŸ§  INTERACTION MOMENTUM
            interaction_count += 1

            if interaction_count > 50:
                closeness += 0.01

            # ðŸ§  CLAMP VALUES
            closeness = max(
                0.0,
                min(closeness, 1.0)
            )

            trust = max(
                0.0,
                min(trust, 1.0)
            )

            # ðŸ§  RELATIONSHIP STAGE
            stage = self._relationship_stage(
                closeness,
                trust
            )

            # ðŸ§  WRITE UPDATED STATE
            new_content = f"""
Closeness: {closeness:.2f}
Trust: {trust:.2f}
Interactions: {interaction_count}
Stage: {stage}
LastInteraction: {int(time.time())}
"""

            with open(
                self.path,
                "w",
                encoding="utf-8"
            ) as f:

                f.write(
                    new_content.strip()
                )

    # ðŸ§  EXTRACT FLOAT VALUE
    def _get(
        self,
        text,
        key
    ):

        match = re.search(
            rf"{key}:\s*([0-9.]+)",
            text
        )

        if match:
            return float(match.group(1))

        return 0.5

    # ðŸ§  EXTRACT TIMESTAMP
    def _get_timestamp(
        self,
        text
    ):

        match = re.search(
            r"LastInteraction:\s*(\d+)",
            text
        )

        if match:
            return int(match.group(1))

        return int(time.time())

    # ðŸ§  RELATIONSHIP DECAY
    def _apply_decay(
        self,
        closeness,
        trust,
        last_interaction
    ):

        now = time.time()

        days = (
            now - last_interaction
        ) / 86400

        closeness -= days * self.decay_rate
        trust -= days * (
            self.decay_rate * 0.5
        )

        return closeness, trust

    # ðŸ§  RELATIONSHIP STAGES
    def _relationship_stage(
        self,
        closeness,
        trust
    ):

        avg = (
            closeness + trust
        ) / 2

        if avg < 0.3:
            return "distant"

        if avg < 0.5:
            return "familiar"

        if avg < 0.7:
            return "trusted"

        if avg < 0.9:
            return "close"

        return "deeply_attached"

    # ðŸ›¡ï¸ CREATE DEFAULT FILE
    def _ensure_file(self):

        os.makedirs(
            os.path.dirname(
                self.path
            ),
            exist_ok=True
        )

        if not os.path.exists(
            self.path
        ):

            with open(
                self.path,
                "w",
                encoding="utf-8"
            ) as f:

                f.write(
                    """
Closeness: 0.50
Trust: 0.50
Interactions: 0
Stage: familiar
LastInteraction: 0
                    """.strip()
                )

    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    def stats(self):
        with self.lock:
            with open(
                self.path,
                "r",
                encoding="utf-8"
            ) as f:

                return f.read()
# memory\router.py
# Mai_ai/memory/router.py
import json
import logging
import re

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryRouter:

    def __init__(self):

        # ðŸ§  routing thresholds
        self.importance_threshold = 0.75

        self.emotional_threshold = 0.7

        # ðŸ“ valid obsidian folders
        self.valid_routes = {
            "conversations",
            "identity",
            "preferences",
            "relationships",
            "important",
            "reflections",
            "archive",
        }


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN ROUTER
    # ==================================================
    def route(
        self,
        text,
        score,
        tags,
        emotion
    ):

        if not text:

            return {
                "routes": ["conversations"],
                "primary": "conversations",
                "confidence": {
                    "conversations": 0.5
                }
            }

        try:

            t = text.lower().strip()

            routes = []

            confidence = {}

            tags = tags or []

            emotion = emotion or {}


            # ==================================================
            # â­ IMPORTANT MEMORY
            # ==================================================
            if score >= self.importance_threshold:

                routes.append("important")

                confidence["important"] = 0.95


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ‘¤ IDENTITY MEMORY
            # ==================================================
            identity_patterns = [
                "i am",
                "i'm",
                "my name",
                "i was",
                "i work as",
                "i live",
                "my age",
                "i study",
                "i like to be called",
            ]

            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in identity_patterns
            ):

                routes.append("identity")

                confidence["identity"] = 0.85


            # ==================================================
            # â¤ï¸ PREFERENCES
            # ==================================================
            preference_patterns = [
                "i like",
                "i love",
                "i hate",
                "i prefer",
                "favorite",
                "i enjoy",
            ]

            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in preference_patterns
            ):

                routes.append("preferences")

                confidence["preferences"] = 0.80


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ¤ RELATIONSHIP SIGNALS
            # ==================================================
            relationship_patterns = [
                "trust you",
                "miss you",
                "feel safe",
                "you matter",
                "need you",
                "with you",
                "care about you",
                "love you",
            ]

            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in relationship_patterns
            ):

                routes.append("relationships")

                confidence["relationships"] = 0.90


            # ==================================================
            # â¤ï¸ EMOTIONAL MEMORIES
            # ==================================================
            if (
                isinstance(emotion, dict)
                and
                emotion.get(
                    "intensity",
                    0.5
                ) >= self.emotional_threshold
            ):

                # âš ï¸ FIX: Route emotional memories into safely available folders
                if "relationships" not in routes:

                    routes.append("relationships")

                confidence["relationships"] = max(
                    confidence.get(
                        "relationships",
                        0.0
                    ),
                    0.90
                )


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸŽ¯ GOALS / TASKS
            # ==================================================
            goal_patterns = [
                "i want to",
                "my goal",
                "i plan to",
                "i need to",
                "i will",
                "trying to",
            ]

            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in goal_patterns
            ):

                # âš ï¸ FIX: Fallback safely until dedicated folder exists
                routes.append("conversations")

                confidence["conversations"] = max(
                    confidence.get(
                        "conversations",
                        0.0
                    ),
                    0.75
                )


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ” HABITS / ROUTINES
            # ==================================================
            habit_patterns = [
                "every day",
                "usually",
                "always",
                "often",
                "routine",
            ]

            if any(
                phrase in t
                for phrase in habit_patterns
            ):

                routes.append("preferences")

                confidence["preferences"] = max(
                    confidence.get(
                        "preferences",
                        0.0
                    ),
                    0.70
                )


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  TAG-BASED ROUTING
            # ==================================================
            lowered_tags = [
                str(t).lower()
                for t in tags
            ]

            if "reflection" in lowered_tags:

                routes.append("reflections")

                confidence["reflections"] = 0.85


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ“ FILTER INVALID ROUTES
            # ==================================================
            routes = [
                r for r in routes
                if r in self.valid_routes
            ]


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  FALLBACK
            # ==================================================
            if not routes:

                routes = ["conversations"]

                confidence["conversations"] = 0.5


            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  REMOVE DUPLICATES
            # ==================================================
            routes = list(
                dict.fromkeys(routes)
            )

            return {
                "routes": routes,
                "primary": routes[0],
                "confidence": confidence,
            }

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("[MEMORY ROUTER ERROR] %s", e)

            return {
                "routes": ["conversations"],
                "primary": "conversations",
                "confidence": {
                    "conversations": 0.5
                }
            }


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ› ï¸ INTERNAL UTILITIES
    # ==================================================
    def _extract_json(self, text: str) -> dict:
        """Safely clean markdown artifacts and parse raw JSON text fields from LLM."""
        if not text:
            return {}
        try:
            return json.loads(text.strip())
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            # Fallback wrapper cleaner if the LLM slips up and returns codeblocks
            match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
            if match:
                try:
                    return json.loads(match.group(0).strip())
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
            return {}


    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG ROUTING
    # ==================================================
    def stats(self):

        return {
            "importance_threshold":
                self.importance_threshold,
            "emotional_threshold":
                self.emotional_threshold,
            "valid_routes":
                list(self.valid_routes),
        }
# memory\schema.py
# memory/schema.py
import time
import uuid
import threading


class MemoryObject:

    def __init__(
        self,
        content="",
        memory_type="conversation",
        confidence=0.5,
        importance=0.5,
        timestamp=None,
        metadata=None,
        embeddings=None,
        links=None,
    ):
        self.lock = threading.RLock()

        self.id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.type = str(memory_type or "conversation").lower()
        self.content = str(content or "").strip()
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence)))
        self.importance = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(importance)))
        self.timestamp = timestamp if timestamp is not None else time.time()
        self.metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        self.embeddings = embeddings if embeddings is not None else None
        self.links = list(links or [])
        self.version = 1
        self.archived = False
        self.consolidated = False

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "type": self.type,
            "content": self.content,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "importance": self.importance,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
            "embeddings": self.embeddings,
            "links": list(self.links),
            "version": self.version,
            "archived": self.archived,
            "consolidated": self.consolidated,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            data = {}
        obj = cls(
            content=data.get("content", ""),
            memory_type=data.get("type", "conversation"),
            confidence=data.get("confidence", 0.5),
            importance=data.get("importance", 0.5),
            timestamp=data.get("timestamp"),
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {}),
            embeddings=data.get("embeddings"),
            links=data.get("links", []),
        )
        obj.id = data.get("id", obj.id)
        obj.version = int(data.get("version", 1))
        obj.archived = bool(data.get("archived", False))
        obj.consolidated = bool(data.get("consolidated", False))
        return obj

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"<MemoryObject "
            f"type={self.type} "
            f"confidence={round(self.confidence, 2)}>"
        )

# memory\scoring.py
# Mai_ai/memory/scoring.py
import re


class MemoryScorer:

    def __init__(self):

        self.base_score = 0.2

        self.strong_emotions = {

            "love": 0.3,
            "hate": 0.3,
            "miss": 0.25,
            "afraid": 0.35,
            "sad": 0.25,
            "happy": 0.2,
            "angry": 0.25,
            "lonely": 0.35,
            "trust": 0.25,
            "important": 0.4,
            "never forget": 0.5
        }

        self.identity_phrases = [

            "i am",
            "my name",
            "i work",
            "i live",
            "i was born",
            "my goal",
            "i dream"
        ]

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MAIN SCORE
    # ==================================================

    def score(
        self,
        text,
        emotion=None,
        reinforcement=1,
        novelty=1.0
    ):

        if not text:
            return 0.0

        t = str(text).lower()

        score = self.base_score

        # ðŸ§  explicit memory signals
        if any(
            phrase in t
            for phrase in [
                "remember",
                "don't forget",
                "important"
            ]
        ):

            score += 0.4

        # â¤ï¸ emotional salience
        for word, value in self.strong_emotions.items():

            pattern = (
                r"\b"
                + re.escape(word)
                + r"\b"
            )

            if re.search(pattern, t):

                score += value

        # ðŸ§  identity importance
        if any(
            phrase in t
            for phrase in self.identity_phrases
        ):

            score += 0.25

        word_count = len(t.split())

        if word_count > 25:
            score += 0.05

        if word_count > 60:
            score += 0.05

        # ðŸ˜Š emotion intensity
        if (
            emotion
            and
            isinstance(emotion, dict)
        ):

            intensity = float(
                emotion.get(
                    "intensity",
                    0.5
                )
            )

            score += intensity * 0.2

        # ðŸ” reinforcement
        score += min(
            reinforcement * 0.03,
            0.15
        )

        # âœ¨ novelty
        score *= float(novelty)

        # â“ questions
        if "?" in t:
            score += 0.03

        # ðŸ§  disclosure
        if any(
            phrase in t
            for phrase in [
                "i feel",
                "i think",
                "i want",
                "i need"
            ]
        ):

            score += 0.08

        score = max(
            0.0,
            min(score, 1.0)
        )

        return round(score, 2)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG
    # ==================================================

    def explain(self, text):

        return {

            "text": text,

            "score":
                self.score(text)
        }
# memory\tagger.py
# Mai_ai/memory/tagger.py
import re

from core.identity.preferences import PREFERENCES


class Tagger:

    def __init__(self):

        self.tag_map = {

            "coding": [

                "code",
                "coding",
                "programming",
                "developer",
                "python",
                "javascript",
                "software",
                "debug",
                "ai",
                "backend",
                "frontend"
            ],

            "gaming": [

                "game",
                "gaming",
                "minecraft",
                "valorant",
                "fps",
                "rpg",
                "steam"
            ],

            "emotion": [

                "sad",
                "happy",
                "angry",
                "lonely",
                "afraid",
                "love",
                "hate",
                "miss"
            ],

            "relationship": [

                "trust",
                "close",
                "miss you",
                "care",
                "love you",
                "feel safe"
            ],

            "goals": [

                "goal",
                "dream",
                "plan",
                "future",
                "build",
                "create",
                "want to"
            ],

            "learning": [

                "learn",
                "study",
                "research",
                "understand",
                "improve"
            ],

            "productivity": [

                "work",
                "task",
                "schedule",
                "focus",
                "project"
            ],

            # From identity/preferences.py â€” topics Mai likes
            "likes": [

                "book",
                "reading",
                "astronomy",
                "star",
                "space",
                "telescope",
                "music",
                "song",
                "melody",
                "technology",
                "tech",
                "deep conversation",
                "philosophy",
                "meaning",
                "quiet",
                "calm",
                "learning",
                "curious"
            ],
