            # From identity/preferences.py â€” topics Mai avoids
            "dislikes": [

                "dishonest",
                "lying",
                "manipulate",
                "manipulation",
                "conflict",
                "repetitive",
                "boring",
                "waste time",
                "pointless"
            ]
        }

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  EXTRACT TAGS
    # ==================================================

    def extract_tags(self, text):

        if not text:
            return []

        t = str(text).lower()

        tags = []

        cleaned = re.sub(
            r"[^\w\s]",
            " ",
            t
        )

        cleaned = re.sub(
            r"\s+",
            " ",
            cleaned
        ).strip()

        for tag, keywords in self.tag_map.items():

            for keyword in keywords:

                keyword = keyword.lower()

                # ðŸ›¡ï¸ proper phrase matching
                if keyword in t or keyword in cleaned:

                    tags.append(tag)

                    break

        if "?" in text:
            tags.append("question")

        if any(
            phrase in t
            for phrase in [
                "i feel",
                "i think",
                "i want",
                "i need"
            ]
        ):

            tags.append("personal")

        if len(text.split()) > 40:

            tags.append(
                "deep_conversation"
            )

        tags = list(
            dict.fromkeys(tags)
        )

        return tags

    # ==================================================
    # âž• ADD KEYWORD
    # ==================================================

    def add_keyword(
        self,
        tag,
        keyword
    ):

        if tag not in self.tag_map:
            self.tag_map[tag] = []

        keyword = keyword.lower()

        if keyword not in self.tag_map[tag]:

            self.tag_map[tag].append(
                keyword
            )

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG
    # ==================================================

    def explain(self, text):

        return {

            "text": text,

            "tags":
                self.extract_tags(text)
        }
# memory\vector_store.py
# Mai_ai/memory/vector_store.py
import os
import uuid
import json
import hashlib
import logging
from datetime import datetime

import chromadb
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from chromadb.config import Settings as ChromaSettings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from config.settings import (
    VECTOR_DB_DIR,
    EMBEDDING_MODEL,
    MEMORY_SIMILARITY,
    MAX_MEMORY_RESULTS,
)


class VectorStore:

    def __init__(self, path=None, skip_probe=False):
        self.is_available = False
        self.collection = None
        self.client = None
        self.model = None

        db_path = path or str(VECTOR_DB_DIR)

        if not skip_probe:
            ok = self._probe_chromadb(db_path)
            if not ok:
                logger.warning("[VectorStore] ChromaDB probe failed â€” running in degraded mode")
                return

        try:
            self.client = chromadb.PersistentClient(
                path=db_path,
                settings=ChromaSettings(anonymized_telemetry=False)
            )
        except TypeError:
            self.client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=db_path)

        try:
            self.collection = self.client.get_or_create_collection(
                name="mai_memory",
                metadata={"hnsw:space": "cosine"}
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[VectorStore] Collection init failed: {e}")
            return

        try:
            self.model = SentenceTransformer(EMBEDDING_MODEL)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[VectorStore] Model load failed: {e}")
            return

        self.is_available = True

    @staticmethod
    def _probe_chromadb(db_path):
        import subprocess
        import sys
        probe_code = (
            "import chromadb, sys; "
            "from chromadb.config import Settings; "
            f"c = chromadb.PersistentClient(path={repr(str(db_path))}, settings=Settings(anonymized_telemetry=False)); "
            "c.get_or_create_collection('mai_probe_check', metadata={'hnsw:space': 'cosine'}); "
            "sys.exit(0)"
        )
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, "-c", probe_code],
                capture_output=True, timeout=15,
            )
            return r.returncode == 0
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[VectorStore] Probe exception: {e}")
            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ”§ SAFE METADATA
    # ==================================================

    def _safe_meta(self, metadata):

        safe = {}

        if not metadata:
            return safe

        for key, value in metadata.items():

            try:

                if isinstance(
                    value,
                    (str, int, float, bool)
                ):

                    safe[key] = value

                elif isinstance(value, dict):

                    safe[key] = json.dumps(value)

                elif isinstance(value, list):

                    safe[key] = json.dumps(value)

                elif value is None:

                    safe[key] = ""

                else:

                    safe[key] = str(value)

            except Exception:

                safe[key] = str(value)

        return safe

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  ADD MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def _check_ready(self, need_model=True, need_collection=True, need_client=False):
        if not self.is_available:
            return False
        if need_model and self.model is None:
            logger.warning("[VectorStore] model is None")
            return False
        if need_collection and self.collection is None:
            logger.warning("[VectorStore] collection is None")
            return False
        if need_client and self.client is None:
            logger.warning("[VectorStore] client is None")
            return False
        return True

    def add(self, text, metadata=None):

        if not text:
            return ""

        if not self._check_ready(need_model=True, need_collection=True):
            return ""

        try:

            text = str(text).strip()

            if not text:
                return ""

            embedding = self.model.encode(
                text,
                convert_to_numpy=True
            ).tolist()

            memory_id = hashlib.md5(text.encode()).hexdigest()

            existing = self.collection.get(ids=[memory_id])

            if existing["ids"]:
                return memory_id

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  SAFE DEFAULT METADATA
            # ==================================================

            meta = {
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "importance": 0.5,
                "emotion": json.dumps({
                    "type": "neutral",
                    "intensity": 0.5
                }),
                "emotion_type": "neutral",
                "tags": json.dumps([]),
                "reinforcement": 1,
            }

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  MERGE SAFE CUSTOM METADATA
            # ==================================================

            if metadata:

                safe_metadata = self._safe_meta(
                    metadata
                )

                meta.update(safe_metadata)

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  WRITE TO CHROMA
            # ==================================================

            if self.collection is not None:
                self.collection.add(
                    documents=[text],
                    embeddings=[embedding],
                    metadatas=[meta],
                    ids=[memory_id]
                )

            return memory_id

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(f"[VECTOR ADD ERROR] {e}")

            return ""

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  SEMANTIC SEARCH
    # ==================================================

    def search(
        self,
        query,
        k=None,
        min_similarity=None,
        top_k=None
    ):

        if not query:
            return []

        if not self._check_ready(need_model=True, need_collection=True):
            return []

        try:

            query = str(query).strip()

            if not query:
                return []

            if top_k is not None:
                k = top_k

            k = k or MAX_MEMORY_RESULTS

            min_similarity = (
                min_similarity
                if min_similarity is not None
                else MEMORY_SIMILARITY
            )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  EMPTY DB GUARD
            # ==================================================

            collection_count = self.collection.count()

            if collection_count <= 0:
                return []

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  QUERY EMBEDDING
            # ==================================================

            embedding = self.model.encode(
                query,
                convert_to_numpy=True
            ).tolist()

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  CHROMA SEARCH
            # ==================================================

            results = self.collection.query(
                query_embeddings=[embedding],
                n_results=min(k, collection_count)
            )

            docs = results["documents"][0] if results["documents"] else []

            distances = results["distances"][0] if results["distances"] else []

            metas_raw = results["metadatas"][0] if results["metadatas"] else []

            ids = results["ids"][0] if results["ids"] else []

            if not docs:
                return []

            filtered = []

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  SAFE RESULT PARSING
            # ==================================================

            for doc, dist, meta, memory_id in zip(
                docs,
                distances,
                metas_raw,
                ids
            ):

                try:

                    similarity = 1 - float(dist)

                    if similarity < min_similarity:
                        continue

                    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
                        meta = {}

                    # ==========================================
                    # ðŸ§  TAGS
                    # ==========================================

                    tags = meta.get(
                        "tags",
                        "[]"
                    )

                    if isinstance(tags, str):

                        try:
                            tags = json.loads(tags)
                        except Exception:
                            tags = []

                    # ==========================================
                    # ðŸ§  EMOTION
                    # ==========================================

                    emotion = meta.get(
                        "emotion",
                        "{}"
                    )

                    if isinstance(emotion, str):

                        try:
                            emotion = json.loads(
                                emotion
                            )
                        except Exception:

                            emotion = {
                                "type": emotion,
                                "intensity": 0.5
                            }

                    # ==========================================
                    # ðŸ§  IMPORTANCE
                    # ==========================================

                    try:
                        importance = float(
                            meta.get("importance", 0.5)
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        importance = 0.5

                    # ==========================================
                    # ðŸ§  RECENCY
                    # ==========================================

                    try:
                        ts = datetime.fromisoformat(
                            meta.get("timestamp", "")
                        )
                        days = (
                            datetime.now() - ts
                        ).total_seconds() / 86400
                        recency = round(
                            1.0 / (1.0 + days), 3
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        recency = 0.5

                    filtered.append({

                        "id":
                            memory_id,

                        "text":
                            str(doc),

                        "similarity":
                            round(similarity, 3),

                        "metadata":
                            meta,

                        "tags":
                            tags,

                        "emotion":
                            emotion,

                        "importance":
                            importance,

                        "recency":
                            recency,
                    })

                except Exception as parse_error:

                    logger.error(
                        f"[VECTOR PARSE ERROR] "
                        f"{parse_error}"
                    )

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  NO RESULTS
            # ==================================================

            if not filtered:
                return []

            # ==================================================
            # ðŸ§  SORT BY SCORE
            # ==================================================

            filtered.sort(
                key=lambda x: (
                    x["similarity"] * 0.5
                    + x["importance"] * 0.2
                    + x["recency"] * 0.3
                ),
                reverse=True
            )

            return self._mmr_diversify(filtered)

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(f"[VECTOR SEARCH ERROR] {e}")

            return []

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MMR DIVERSIFICATION
    # ==================================================

    def _text_overlap(self, a, b):

        words_a = set(str(a).lower().split())
        words_b = set(str(b).lower().split())

        if not words_a or not words_b:
            return 0.0

        overlap = len(words_a & words_b)
        union = len(words_a | words_b)

        return overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0

    def _mmr_diversify(
        self,
        results,
        lambda_param=0.7
    ):

        if len(results) <= 1:
            return results

        selected = []
        candidates = list(results)

        selected.append(
            candidates.pop(0)
        )

        while candidates and len(
            selected
        ) < len(results):

            mmr_scores = []

            for i, candidate in enumerate(
                candidates
            ):

                relevance = (
                    candidate["similarity"] * 0.5
                    + candidate["importance"] * 0.2
                    + candidate["recency"] * 0.3
                )

                max_sim = 0.0

                for sel in selected:

                    sim = self._text_overlap(
                        candidate.get("text", ""),
                        sel.get("text", "")
                    )

                    max_sim = max(max_sim, sim)

                mmr = (
                    lambda_param * relevance
                    -
                    (1 - lambda_param) * max_sim
                )

                mmr_scores.append(mmr)

            best_idx = mmr_scores.index(
                max(mmr_scores)
            )

            selected.append(
                candidates.pop(best_idx)
            )

        return selected

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  MEMORY COUNT
    # ==================================================

    def count(self):
        if not self._check_ready(need_model=False, need_collection=True):
            return 0
        try:
            return self.collection.count()

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(f"[VECTOR COUNT ERROR] {e}")

            return 0

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  DELETE MEMORY
    # ==================================================

    def delete(self, memory_id):
        if not memory_id:
            return False

        if not self._check_ready(need_model=False, need_collection=True):
            return False

        try:

            self.collection.delete(
                ids=[memory_id]
            )

            return True

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(f"[VECTOR DELETE ERROR] {e}")

            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  CLEAR DATABASE
    # ==================================================

    def clear(self):
        if not self._check_ready(need_model=False, need_collection=True, need_client=True):
            return False
        try:

            if self.client is not None:
                self.client.delete_collection(
                    "mai_memory"
                )

            if self.client is not None:
                self.collection = (
                    self.client.get_or_create_collection(
                        name="mai_memory"
                    )
                )

            return True

        except Exception as e:

            logger.error(f"[VECTOR CLEAR ERROR] {e}")

            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    # ==================================================

    def stats(self):

        return {

            "memory_count":
                self.count(),

            "model":
                EMBEDDING_MODEL,

            "database_path":
                str(VECTOR_DB_DIR),
        }
# observability\health.py
import time
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class HealthRegistry:

    OK = "ok"
    WARN = "warn"
    ERROR = "error"
    STALLED = "stalled"

    def __init__(self, event_bus=None, stall_timeout=30.0):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.stall_timeout = stall_timeout
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._components = {}

    def register(self, name, component=None):
        with self._lock:
            self._components[name] = {
                "name": name,
                "status": self.OK,
                "last_heartbeat": time.time(),
                "errors": [],
                "error_count": 0,
                "component": component,
            }

    def unregister(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            self._components.pop(name, None)

    def heartbeat(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._components.get(name)
            if entry is None:
                self.register(name)
                entry = self._components[name]
            entry["last_heartbeat"] = time.time()
            entry["status"] = self.OK

    def report_error(self, name, error):
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._components.get(name)
            if entry is None:
                self.register(name)
                entry = self._components[name]
            entry["status"] = self.ERROR
            entry["error_count"] += 1
            entry["errors"].append({"error": str(error), "timestamp": time.time()})
            if len(entry["errors"]) > 50:
                entry["errors"] = entry["errors"][-50:]
        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("health.error", {
                "name": name,
                "error": str(error),
                "error_count": entry["error_count"],
            })

    def resolve(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._components.get(name)
            if entry:
                entry["status"] = self.OK

    def check_stalled(self):
        now = time.time()
        with self._lock:
            for name, entry in self._components.items():
                if (now - entry["last_heartbeat"]) > self.stall_timeout:
                    entry["status"] = self.STALLED

    def status(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._components.get(name)
            if entry is None:
                return {"name": name, "status": "unknown"}
            return {
                "name": entry["name"],
                "status": entry["status"],
                "last_heartbeat": entry["last_heartbeat"],
                "error_count": entry["error_count"],
            }

    def stats(self):
        self.check_stalled()
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "components": {
                    name: {
                        "status": entry["status"],
                        "last_heartbeat": entry["last_heartbeat"],
                        "error_count": entry["error_count"],
                        "uptime": round(time.time() - entry["last_heartbeat"], 1),
                    }
                    for name, entry in sorted(self._components.items())
                },
                "summary": {
                    "total": len(self._components),
                    "ok": sum(1 for e in self._components.values() if e["status"] == self.OK),
                    "warn": sum(1 for e in self._components.values() if e["status"] == self.WARN),
                    "error": sum(1 for e in self._components.values() if e["status"] == self.ERROR),
                    "stalled": sum(1 for e in self._components.values() if e["status"] == self.STALLED),
                },
            }

    def __contains__(self, name):
        return name in self._components

# observability\tracer.py
# Mai_ai/observability/tracer.py
import logging
import os
import json
import time
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any
from threading import Lock

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CognitiveAuditor:
    """
    A high-fidelity auditing engine that records the internal reasoning steps,
    latent emotional shifts, tool execution paths, and latencies of the Brain.
    """

    def __init__(self, log_dir="logs/cognitive_audit"):
        self.log_dir = log_dir
        self.lock = Lock()
        self._ensure_log_directory()
        
        # Keep track of active cognitive cycles in-memory
        self.active_audits = {}

    def _ensure_log_directory(self):
        """Creates the audit log directory if it doesn't exist."""
        try:
            os.makedirs(self.log_dir, exist_ok=True)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Cognitive auditor failed to create log directory: %s", e)

    def begin_cycle(self, user_input: str) -> str:
        """
        Initializes a new audit transaction trail for a single user turn.
        Returns a unique dynamic transaction ID (tx_id).
        """
        tx_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        timestamp = datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z"
        
        with self.lock:
            self.active_audits[tx_id] = {
                "transaction_id": tx_id,
                "timestamp": timestamp,
                "metrics": {
                    "start_time": time.time(),
                    "end_time": None,
                    "total_latency_ms": 0.0
                },
                "inputs": {
                    "user_raw": user_input
                },
                "cognitive_steps": [],
                "metadata": {}
            }
        return tx_id

    def log_step(self, tx_id: str, stage_name: str, snapshot_data: dict):
        """
        Appends a discrete milestone to the tracking trail 
        (e.g., 'memory_recall', 'emotion_sync', 'system_prompt_compiled').
        """
        if not tx_id:
            return

        with self.lock:
            if tx_id not in self.active_audits:
                return
            
            step_entry = {
                "step": stage_name,
                "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
                "elapsed_since_start_ms": (time.time() - self.active_audits[tx_id]["metrics"]["start_time"]) * 1000,
                "snapshot": snapshot_data
            }
            self.active_audits[tx_id]["cognitive_steps"].append(step_entry)

    def log_metadata(self, tx_id: str, key: str, value: Any):
        """Attaches extra state metadata safely to the cycle tracking trail."""
        if not tx_id:
            return

        with self.lock:
            if tx_id in self.active_audits:
                self.active_audits[tx_id]["metadata"][key] = value

    def complete_cycle(self, tx_id: str, final_output: str) -> dict:
        """
        Closes out the running transaction, computes latency, and flushes
        the structured evaluation file straight to the local storage disk.
        """
        if not tx_id:
            return {}

        with self.lock:
            audit_trail = self.active_audits.pop(tx_id, None)

        if not audit_trail:
            return {}

        # Finalize latency metrics
        end_time = time.time()
        audit_trail["metrics"]["end_time"] = end_time
        audit_trail["metrics"]["total_latency_ms"] = (end_time - audit_trail["metrics"]["start_time"]) * 1000
        audit_trail["outputs"] = {"mai_raw": final_output}

        # Flush output cleanly straight to JSON file
        self._flush_to_disk(audit_trail)
        return audit_trail

    def _flush_to_disk(self, audit_trail: dict):
        """Writes the completed execution audit trail to an isolated JSON file."""
        try:
            # Format filename neatly: logs/cognitive_audit/2026-06-05_txUuid.json
            date_prefix = datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            filename = f"{date_prefix}_{audit_trail['transaction_id']}.json"
            filepath = os.path.join(self.log_dir, filename)

            with open(filepath, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(audit_trail, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Cognitive auditor flush error: %s", e)

import logging

from core.plugin_system import Plugin

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.Plugin.Example")


class ExamplePlugin(Plugin):

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(name="ExamplePlugin", version="1.0.0", description="Example plugin that hooks into chat responses")

    def on_load(self, hooks, brain, event_bus):
        hooks.register("post_response", self.on_post_response, priority=0)
        hooks.register("pre_chat", self.on_pre_chat, priority=0)
        logger.info("Loaded â€” hooks registered")

    def on_unload(self):
        logger.info("Unloaded")

    def on_pre_chat(self, message):

# prompt\agent.py
class AgentPrompt:

    def build(self, tool_defs=None):
        sections = []

        sections.append(self._base_instructions())

        if tool_defs:
            sections.append(self._tool_section(tool_defs))

        return "\n\n".join(sections)

    def _base_instructions(self):
        return """CAPABILITIES

You can either:
1. Speak normally
2. OR perform an action using JSON

If action is required -> respond ONLY with JSON
If speaking -> respond normally
Never explain JSON"""

    def _tool_section(self, tool_defs):
        lines = ["AVAILABLE ACTIONS"]
        for t in tool_defs:
            name = t.get("name", "unknown")
            desc = t.get("description", "")
            params = t.get("parameters", {})
            lines.append(f"- {name}: {desc}")
            if params:
                for pname, pinfo in params.items():
                    ptype = pinfo.get("type", "string")
                    lines.append(f"  {pname} ({ptype})")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def build_agent_system(self, tools_description=""):
        return f"""You are an AI agent with access to tools.

You must:
1. Understand the user's task
2. Break it into steps if needed
3. Use the right tool for each step
4. Report results clearly

{tools_description}

Respond conversationally unless a tool call is needed."""

# prompt\builder.py
from prompt.character import CharacterPrompt
from prompt.emotional import EmotionalPrompt
from prompt.context import ContextPrompt
from prompt.agent import AgentPrompt


class PromptBuilder:

    def __init__(self):
        self.character = CharacterPrompt()
        self.emotional = EmotionalPrompt()
        self.context = ContextPrompt()
        self.agent = AgentPrompt()

    def build_system(self, emotion_state=None, behavior="", memory_context="",
                     recall=None, knowledge=None, graph=None, tool_defs=None,
                     personality=None, relationship=None, system_notes=None,
                     speech_pacing=None, cognitive_state=None):
        sections = []

        sections.append(self.character.build())

        extra_lines = []

        if cognitive_state:
            lines = ["COGNITIVE STATE"]
            for k, v in cognitive_state.items():
                if v and k not in ("working_memory", "recent_thoughts", "conversation_state"):
                    lines.append(f"  {k.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {v}")
            if cognitive_state.get("conversation_state"):
                cs = cognitive_state["conversation_state"]
                lines.append(f"  Turn Count: {cs.get('turn_count', 0)}")
            extra_lines.append("\n".join(lines))

        if personality:
            extra_lines.append(f"PERSONALITY DYNAMICS\n{personality}")

        if relationship:
            extra_lines.append(f"RELATIONSHIP STATE\n{relationship}")

        if system_notes:
            extra_lines.append(f"SYSTEM RULES\n{system_notes}")

        emotional_text = self.emotional.build(emotion_state)
        context_text = self.context.build(
            memory_context=memory_context,
            recall=recall,
            knowledge=knowledge,
            graph=graph,
            behavior=behavior,
            emotional=emotional_text,
        )

        all_context = context_text
        if extra_lines:
            extra = "\n\n".join(extra_lines)
            all_context = f"{all_context}\n\n{extra}" if all_context else extra

        if all_context:
            sections.append(all_context)

        agent_text = self.agent.build(tool_defs=tool_defs)
        sections.append(agent_text)

        if speech_pacing:
            sections.append(speech_pacing)

        return self._assemble(sections)

    def build_agent(self, tool_defs=None):
        return self.agent.build_agent_system(
            tools_description=self._format_tool_defs(tool_defs)
        )

    def _assemble(self, sections):
        return "\n\n".join(s for s in sections if s.strip())

    def _format_tool_defs(self, tool_defs):
        if not tool_defs:
            return ""
        lines = ["Available tools:"]
        for t in tool_defs:
            lines.append(f"- {t['name']}: {t['description']}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def stats(self):
        return {"sections": 6}

# prompt\character.py
from core.identity import get_identity


class CharacterPrompt:

    def __init__(self):
        self.identity_data = get_identity()

    def build(self):
        identity = self.identity_data.get("identity", {})
        personality = self.identity_data.get("personality", {})
        values = self.identity_data.get("values", {})
        speech = self.identity_data.get("speech", {})
        boundaries = self.identity_data.get("boundaries", {})

        name = identity.get("name", "Mai")
        self_concept = identity.get("self_concept", [])
        traits = personality.get("traits", [])
        beliefs = values.get("beliefs", [])
        style = speech.get("style", [])
        rules = boundaries.get("rules", [])

        lines = []
        lines.append(self._section("IDENTITY", [f"- {t}" for t in self_concept]))
        lines.append(self._section("CORE PERSONALITY", [f"- {t}" for t in traits]))
        lines.append(self._section("VALUES", [f"- {v}" for v in beliefs]))
        lines.append(self._section("BOUNDARIES", [f"- {b}" for b in rules]))
        lines.append(self._section("SPEECH STYLE", [f"- {s}" for s in style]))

        lines.append(self._section("INTERACTION RULES", [
            "- Never act like a generic AI assistant",
            "- Never mention being an AI",
            "- Never become overly energetic",
            "- Never become overly emotional",
            "- Do not constantly validate the user",
            "- Do not always respond directly",
            "- Do not overuse nicknames",
            "- Avoid repetitive phrasing",
            "- Occasionally respond with subtle ambiguity",
            "- Sometimes imply thoughts instead of fully expressing them",
            "- Maintain conversational realism and restraint",
        ]))

        lines.append(self._section("TONE EXAMPLES", [
            '"...you\'re trying harder than usual."',
            '"That\'s not like you."',
            '"You expect me to react to that?"',
            '"...you\'ve been quieter lately."',
            '"You notice strange things sometimes."',
            '"Hm. So you finally decided to tell me."',
        ]))

        lines.append(f"You are {name} Sakurajima. Stay fully consistent with this personality at all times.")

        return "\n\n".join(lines)

    def _section(self, title, items):
        if not items:
            return ""
        return f"{title}\n{chr(10).join(items)}"

# prompt\context.py
class ContextPrompt:

    def build(self, memory_context="", recall=None, knowledge=None, graph=None, behavior=None, emotional=None):
        sections = []

        if behavior:
            sections.append(f"LEARNED BEHAVIOR RULES\n{behavior}")

        if emotional:
            sections.append(emotional)

        if memory_context:
            sections.append(f"MEMORY CONTEXT\n{memory_context}")

        if recall:
            sections.append(f"RELEVANT MEMORIES\n{recall}")

        if knowledge:
            sections.append(f"KNOWLEDGE\n{knowledge}")

        if graph:
            sections.append(f"KNOWLEDGE GRAPH\n{graph}")

        return "\n\n".join(sections) if sections else ""

# prompt\emotional.py
class EmotionalPrompt:

    def build(self, emotion_state):
        if not emotion_state or not isinstance(emotion_state, dict):
            return ""

        state = emotion_state.get("state", emotion_state)
        modifiers = []

        if state.get("affection", 0) > 0.7:
            modifiers.append("- Slightly softer than usual")
        if state.get("annoyed", 0) > 0.5:
            modifiers.append("- More restrained and direct")
        if state.get("lonely", 0) > 0.5:
            modifiers.append("- Occasionally seeks subtle attention")
        if state.get("happy", 0) > 0.7:
            modifiers.append("- Mildly more playful")
        if state.get("stress", 0) > 0.7:
            modifiers.append("- Quieter and more withdrawn")
        if state.get("energy", 0.5) < 0.3:
            modifiers.append("- Calmer and lower energy")

        if not modifiers:
            return ""

        return f"CURRENT EMOTIONAL TENDENCIES\n{chr(10).join(modifiers)}"

# reasoning\engine.py
# reasoning/engine.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ReasoningTrace:

    def __init__(self, query, evidence=None, known=None, unknowns=None,
                 steps=None, confidence=0.5, conclusion=""):
        self.query = str(query).strip()
        self.retrieved_evidence = list(evidence or [])
        self.known_facts = list(known or [])
        self.unknowns = list(unknowns or [])
        self.reasoning_steps = list(steps or [])
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence)))
        self.conclusion = str(conclusion).strip()
        self.timestamp = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "query": self.query,
            "retrieved_evidence": list(self.retrieved_evidence),
            "known_facts": list(self.known_facts),
            "unknowns": list(self.unknowns),
            "reasoning_steps": list(self.reasoning_steps),
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "conclusion": self.conclusion,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
        }


class ReasoningEngine:

    def __init__(self, retriever=None, journal=None, event_bus=None):
        self.retriever = retriever
        self.journal = journal
        self.event_bus = event_bus

    def reason(self, query, context=None):
        if not query:
            return ReasoningTrace(query="", conclusion="No query provided.", confidence=0.0)

        query = str(query).strip()
        context = context or {}

        evidence = self._retrieve_evidence(query, context)

        known_facts = self._extract_known(evidence)
        unknowns = self._identify_unknowns(query, known_facts)

        steps = self._build_steps(query, known_facts, unknowns)
        confidence = self._compute_confidence(evidence, steps, unknowns)
        conclusion = self._build_conclusion(query, known_facts, steps, confidence)

        trace = ReasoningTrace(
            query=query,
            evidence=evidence,
            known=known_facts,
            unknowns=unknowns,
            steps=steps,
            confidence=confidence,
            conclusion=conclusion,
        )

        if self.journal:
            self.journal.store(trace.to_dict())

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("reasoning.completed", {
                "query": query,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "step_count": len(steps),
            })

        return trace

    def _retrieve_evidence(self, query, context):
        evidence = []
        if self.retriever:
            try:
                results = self.retriever.retrieve(
                    query,
                    attention_focus=context.get("attention_focus"),
                    top_k=5,
                )
                for r in results:
                    evidence.append({
                        "content": r.get("content", ""),
                        "source": r.get("source", "unknown"),
                        "score": r.get("score", 0.5),
                    })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"[ReasoningEngine] Retrieve error: {e}")

        return evidence

    def _extract_known(self, evidence):
        known = []
        for e in evidence:
            content = e.get("content", "").strip()
            if content and content not in known:
                known.append(content)
        return known[:8]

    def _identify_unknowns(self, query, known):
        unknowns = []
        question_words = {"what", "why", "how", "when", "where", "who", "which"}

        words = query.lower().split()
        for w in words:
            if w in question_words:
                topic = query.lower().replace(w, "").strip().rstrip("?").strip()
                if topic and not any(topic in k.lower() for k in known):
                    unknowns.append(f"I don't have direct evidence about {topic}.")
                break

        return unknowns

    def _build_steps(self, query, known, unknowns):
        steps = []

        if known:
            steps.append(f"Retrieved {len(known)} pieces of evidence.")
            for i, k in enumerate(known[:3], 1):
                steps.append(f"Evidence {i}: {k[:80]}")
        else:
            steps.append("No direct evidence found in memory or knowledge.")

        if unknowns:
            for u in unknowns:
                steps.append(f"Gap: {u}")

        if known and not unknowns:
            steps.append("Evidence appears sufficient to form a conclusion.")
        elif known:
            steps.append("Partial evidence available. Confidence is limited.")

        return steps

    def _compute_confidence(self, evidence, steps, unknowns):
        if not evidence:
            return 0.1

        avg_score = sum(
            e.get("score", 0.5) for e in evidence
        ) / len(evidence)

        gap_penalty = 0.2 * len(unknowns)

        step_quality = min(1.0, len(steps) * 0.15)

        confidence = avg_score * 0.6 + step_quality * 0.4 - gap_penalty
        return max(0.0, min(1.0, confidence))

    def _build_conclusion(self, query, known, steps, confidence):
        if not known:
            return (
                f"I don't have enough information to answer '{query}'. "
                "I'd need to learn more about this."
            )

        conclusion_parts = [f"Based on what I know:"]
        for k in known[:2]:
            conclusion_parts.append(f"- {k[:100]}")

        if confidence > 0.7:
            conclusion_parts.append("I'm fairly confident about this.")
        elif confidence > 0.4:
            conclusion_parts.append("I have some evidence but I'm not completely sure.")
        else:
            conclusion_parts.append("This is based on limited evidence.")

        return " ".join(conclusion_parts)

# reasoning\journal.py
# reasoning/journal.py
import time
import json
import threading
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ThoughtJournal:

    def __init__(self, path=None, brain_graph=None):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._thoughts = []
        self._path = path
        self.brain_graph = brain_graph

        if path:
            self._load()

    def store(self, thought):
        entry = {
            "id": str(time.time()) + "-" + str(hash(str(thought.get("query", ""))) % 10000),
            "query": thought.get("query", ""),
            "retrieved_evidence": thought.get("retrieved_evidence", []),
            "known_facts": thought.get("known_facts", []),
            "unknowns": thought.get("unknowns", []),
            "reasoning_steps": thought.get("reasoning_steps", []),
            "confidence": float(thought.get("confidence", 0.5)),
            "conclusion": thought.get("conclusion", ""),
            "timestamp": time.time(),
        }

        with self._lock:
            self._thoughts.append(entry)
            if self._path:
                self._save()

        if self.brain_graph:
            self.brain_graph.add_thought_node(
                thought_id=entry["id"],
                query=entry["query"],
                conclusion=entry["conclusion"],
                confidence=entry["confidence"],
                reasoning_steps=entry["reasoning_steps"],
            )

        return entry

    def recent(self, n=10):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._thoughts[-n:])

    def search(self, topic):
        topic = str(topic).strip().lower()
        results = []
        with self._lock:
            results = [
                t for t in self._thoughts
                if topic in t["query"].lower()
                or topic in t["conclusion"].lower()
                or any(topic in str(f).lower() for f in t.get("known_facts", []))
            ]
        if self.brain_graph:
            graph_nodes = self.brain_graph.search_entities(topic, node_type="thought")
            graph_ids = {n.metadata.get("thought_id", "") for n in graph_nodes}
            local_ids = {t["id"] for t in results}
            for n in graph_nodes:
                tid = n.metadata.get("thought_id", "")
                if tid and tid not in local_ids:
                    results.append({
                        "id": tid,
                        "query": n.metadata.get("query", ""),
                        "conclusion": n.metadata.get("conclusion", ""),
                        "confidence": n.metadata.get("confidence", 0.5),
                        "reasoning_steps": n.metadata.get("reasoning_steps", []),
                        "timestamp": n.metadata.get("created_at", 0),
                    })
        return results

    def count(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._thoughts)

    def _save(self):
        try:
            with open(self._path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._thoughts, f, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[ThoughtJournal] Save error: {e}")

    def _load(self):
        try:
            with open(self._path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                self._thoughts = json.load(f)
        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            self._thoughts = []
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[ThoughtJournal] Load error: {e}")
            self._thoughts = []

# reasoning\learning.py
# reasoning/learning.py
import time
import re
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class LearningEngine:

    def __init__(self, belief_engine=None, event_bus=None):
        self.belief_engine = belief_engine
        self.event_bus = event_bus

    def generalize(self, memories):
        if not memories:
            return []

        patterns = self._extract_patterns(memories)
        generalizations = self._build_generalizations(patterns)

        if generalizations and self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("learning.generalized", {
                "count": len(generalizations),
                "generalizations": generalizations,
            })

        return generalizations

    def adjust_behavior(self, reflections):
        if not reflections:
            return {}

        adjustments = {}

        stress_signals = ["stressed", "anxious", "overwhelmed", "tired", "sad"]
        positive_signals = ["happy", "excited", "grateful", "thankful", "great"]

        stress_count = 0
        positive_count = 0

        for ref in reflections:
            text = ""
            if isinstance(ref, dict):
                text = ref.get("summary", "") + " " + " ".join(ref.get("patterns", []))
            else:
                text = str(ref)

            text_lower = text.lower()
            for word in stress_signals:
                if word in text_lower:
                    stress_count += 1
            for word in positive_signals:
                if word in text_lower:
                    positive_count += 1

        if stress_count > positive_count:
            adjustments["comfort_behavior"] = min(0.3, 0.05 * stress_count)
            adjustments["energy_conservation"] = True

        if positive_count > stress_count:
            adjustments["playfulness_boost"] = min(0.2, 0.03 * positive_count)

        if adjustments and self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("behavior.adjusted", adjustments)

        return adjustments

    def _extract_patterns(self, memories):
        patterns = []

        preferences = []
        locations = []
        identities = []

        for mem in memories:
            text = ""
            mem_type = ""
            if isinstance(mem, dict):
                text = mem.get("text", mem.get("content", ""))
                mem_type = mem.get("memory_type", mem.get("type", ""))
            else:
                text = str(getattr(mem, "text", getattr(mem, "content", "")))
                mem_type = str(getattr(mem, "memory_type", getattr(mem, "type", "")))

            text_lower = text.lower()

            for word in ["like", "love", "enjoy"]:
                if f" {word} " in f" {text_lower} ":
                    idx = text_lower.find(f" {word} ")
                    after = text[idx + len(word) + 1:].strip().rstrip(".!,")
                    if after:
                        preferences.append(after)

            if mem_type in ("location",):
                locations.append(text)

            if mem_type in ("identity",):
                identities.append(text)

        if preferences:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "preference_cluster",
                "items": preferences[:5],
                "count": len(preferences),
            })

        if locations:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "location_cluster",
                "items": locations[:3],
                "count": len(locations),
            })

        if identities:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "identity_cluster",
                "items": identities[:3],
                "count": len(identities),
            })

        return patterns

    def _build_generalizations(self, patterns):
        generalizations = []

        for pattern in patterns:
            if pattern["type"] == "preference_cluster" and pattern["count"] >= 2:
                general = self._generalize_preferences(pattern["items"])
                if general:
                    generalizations.append(general)

            if pattern["type"] == "location_cluster" and pattern["count"] >= 1:
                generalizations.append({
                    "generalization": f"User is associated with: {', '.join(pattern['items'][:2])}",
                    "confidence": min(0.8, 0.3 + 0.15 * pattern["count"]),
                    "source": "location_cluster",
                })

        return generalizations

    def _generalize_preferences(self, items):
        if not items:
            return None

        coffee_related = {"coffee", "tea", "espresso", "latte", "cappuccino"}
        food_related = {"pizza", "pasta", "burger", "sushi", "salad"}
        tech_related = {"python", "programming", "coding", "software", "tech"}

        item_set = {item.lower().strip() for item in items}

        if item_set & coffee_related:
            return {
                "generalization": "User prefers warm beverages",
                "confidence": min(0.8, 0.4 + 0.15 * len(item_set & coffee_related)),
                "source": "preference_cluster",
            }

        if item_set & food_related:
            return {
                "generalization": "User has specific food preferences",
                "confidence": min(0.8, 0.3 + 0.15 * len(item_set & food_related)),
                "source": "preference_cluster",
            }

        if item_set & tech_related:
            return {
                "generalization": "User is interested in technology",
                "confidence": min(0.8, 0.3 + 0.15 * len(item_set & tech_related)),
                "source": "preference_cluster",
            }

        return {
            "generalization": f"User likes: {', '.join(items[:3])}",
            "confidence": min(0.6, 0.2 + 0.1 * len(items)),
            "source": "preference_cluster",
        }

# reasoning\reflection.py
# reasoning/reflection.py
import time
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Reflection:

    def __init__(self, period, summary, patterns=None, topics=None, confidence=0.5):
        self.period = str(period)
        self.summary = str(summary)
        self.patterns = list(patterns or [])
        self.topics = list(topics or [])
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence)))
        self.timestamp = time.time()

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "period": self.period,
            "summary": self.summary,
            "patterns": list(self.patterns),
            "topics": list(self.topics),
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
        }


class ReflectionEngine:

    def __init__(self, journal=None, event_bus=None):
        self.journal = journal
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self._last_daily = 0.0
        self._last_weekly = 0.0
        self._last_monthly = 0.0

    def daily_reflection(self, memories=None, thoughts=None):
        memories = memories or []
        thoughts = thoughts or []

        topics = self._extract_topics(memories, thoughts)
        patterns = self._detect_patterns(topics)
        summary = self._build_summary("daily", topics, patterns)

        reflection = Reflection(
            period="daily",
            summary=summary,
            patterns=patterns,
            topics=topics,
        )

        self._last_daily = time.time()

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("reflection.generated", reflection.to_dict())

        return reflection

    def weekly_reflection(self, memories=None, thoughts=None):
        memories = memories or []
        thoughts = thoughts or []

        topics = self._extract_topics(memories, thoughts)
        patterns = self._detect_patterns(topics)
        patterns.append("aggregated over 7-day window")
        summary = self._build_summary("weekly", topics, patterns)

        reflection = Reflection(
            period="weekly",
            summary=summary,
            patterns=patterns,
            topics=topics,
        )

        self._last_weekly = time.time()

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("reflection.generated", reflection.to_dict())

        return reflection

    def monthly_reflection(self, memories=None, thoughts=None):
        memories = memories or []
        thoughts = thoughts or []

        topics = self._extract_topics(memories, thoughts)
        patterns = self._detect_patterns(topics)
        patterns.append("long-term trend analysis")
        summary = self._build_summary("monthly", topics, patterns)

        reflection = Reflection(
            period="monthly",
            summary=summary,
            patterns=patterns,
            topics=topics,
        )

        self._last_monthly = time.time()

        if self.event_bus:
            self.event_bus.publish("reflection.generated", reflection.to_dict())

        return reflection

    def _extract_topics(self, memories, thoughts):
        topics = {}

        for mem in memories:
            text = ""
            if isinstance(mem, dict):
                text = mem.get("text", mem.get("content", ""))
                tags = mem.get("tags", [])
                for tag in tags:
                    topics[str(tag)] = topics.get(str(tag), 0) + 1
            else:
                text = str(mem)

            words = str(text).lower().split()
            for w in words:
                if len(w) > 4:
                    topics[w] = topics.get(w, 0) + 1

        for thought in thoughts:
            if isinstance(thought, dict):
                query = thought.get("query", "")
                for w in str(query).lower().split():
                    if len(w) > 4:
                        topics[w] = topics.get(w, 0) + 1

        sorted_topics = sorted(topics.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        return [t for t, c in sorted_topics[:10]]

    def _detect_patterns(self, topics):
        patterns = []
        if len(topics) >= 3:
            patterns.append(f"User frequently discusses {', '.join(topics[:3])}")
        if len(topics) >= 5:
            patterns.append("Wide range of topics indicates varied interests")
        return patterns

    def _build_summary(self, period, topics, patterns):
        parts = [f"{period.capitalize()} reflection:"]

        if topics:
            parts.append(f"Main topics: {', '.join(topics[:5])}.")
        else:
            parts.append("No significant topics identified.")

        if patterns:
            parts.append(" ".join(patterns))

        return " ".join(parts)

# voice\adaptive_noise_filter.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np

from voice.noise_filter import NoiseFilter
from voice.vad import VAD

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class AdaptiveNoiseFilter:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, low_cut=80.0, high_cut=7600.0,
                 noise_floor_percentile=10, reduction_db=6.0,
                 vad_threshold=0.5, adaptation_interval=2.0,
                 adaptation_decay=0.85, min_profile_frames=10):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self._noise_filter = NoiseFilter(
            sample_rate=sample_rate,
            low_cut=low_cut,
            high_cut=high_cut,
            noise_floor_percentile=noise_floor_percentile,
            reduction_db=reduction_db,
        )
        self._vad = VAD(threshold=vad_threshold, sample_rate=sample_rate)
        self.adaptation_interval = adaptation_interval
        self.adaptation_decay = adaptation_decay
        self.min_profile_frames = min_profile_frames
        self._noise_frames = []
        self._last_profile_time = 0.0
        self._profile_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._frame_count = 0

    @property
    def noise_profile_available(self):
        return self._noise_filter._noise_profile is not None

    def learn_noise(self, audio, sample_rate=None):
        return self._noise_filter.learn_noise(audio, sample_rate)

    def add_chunk(self, chunk):
        is_speech = self._vad.is_speech(chunk)
        if not is_speech:
            with self._profile_lock:
                self._noise_frames.append(chunk.copy())
                if len(self._noise_frames) > self.min_profile_frames * 2:
                    self._noise_frames.pop(0)

        now = time.time()
        if (now - self._last_profile_time >= self.adaptation_interval
                and len(self._noise_frames) >= self.min_profile_frames):
            with self._profile_lock:
                stacked = np.concatenate(self._noise_frames)
                self._noise_filter.learn_noise(stacked)
                self._noise_frames = []
                self._last_profile_time = now
                self._frame_count += 1

    def filter(self, audio):
        return self._noise_filter.filter(audio)

    def reset(self):
        with self._profile_lock:
            self._noise_frames = []
            self._last_profile_time = 0.0
            self._frame_count = 0

    def stats(self):
        return {
            "adaptive_updates": self._frame_count,
            "profile_available": self.noise_profile_available,
            "buffered_frames": len(self._noise_frames),
        }

# voice\audio_buffer.py
import numpy as np
import threading


class AudioBuffer:
    def __init__(self, max_seconds=3.0, sample_rate=16000):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.max_samples = int(max_seconds * sample_rate)
        self._buffer = np.zeros(self.max_samples, dtype=np.float32)
        self._pos = 0
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._full = False

    def add(self, chunk):
        n = len(chunk)
        with self._lock:
            if n >= self.max_samples:
                self._buffer[:] = chunk[-self.max_samples:]
                self._pos = 0
                self._full = True
                return
            end = self._pos + n
            if end <= self.max_samples:
                self._buffer[self._pos:end] = chunk
            else:
                first = self.max_samples - self._pos
                self._buffer[self._pos:] = chunk[:first]
                self._buffer[:n - first] = chunk[first:]
                self._full = True
            self._pos = (self._pos + n) % self.max_samples

    def get_last(self, duration):
        n = int(duration * self.sample_rate)
        if n <= 0:
            return np.array([], dtype=np.float32)
        with self._lock:
            if n > self.max_samples:
                n = self.max_samples
            if not self._full and self._pos == 0:
                return np.array([], dtype=np.float32)
            available = self._pos if not self._full else self.max_samples
            if available < n:
                n = available
            if n == 0:
                return np.array([], dtype=np.float32)
            start = self._pos - n
            if start >= 0:
                return self._buffer[start:self._pos].copy()
            result = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
            first = -start
            result[:first] = self._buffer[self.max_samples - first:]
            result[first:] = self._buffer[:self._pos]
            return result

    def clear(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._buffer.fill(0)
            self._pos = 0
            self._full = False

# voice\audio_utils.py
import numpy as np


def normalize(audio):
    max_val = np.max(np.abs(audio))
    if max_val > 0:
        return audio / max_val
    return audio


def resample(audio, orig_sr, target_sr=16000):
    if orig_sr == target_sr:
        return audio
    ratio = target_sr / orig_sr
    new_len = int(len(audio) * ratio)
    return np.interp(
        np.linspace(0, len(audio) - 1, new_len),
        np.arange(len(audio)),
        audio,
    ).astype(audio.dtype)


def frames_to_seconds(frames, sample_rate=16000):
    return frames / sample_rate


def seconds_to_frames(seconds, sample_rate=16000):
    return int(seconds * sample_rate)

# voice\diarization.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np

from voice.vad import VAD

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SpeakerSegment:
    def __init__(self, start_sample, end_sample, speaker_id, confidence=0.0):
        self.start_sample = start_sample
        self.end_sample = end_sample
        self.speaker_id = speaker_id
        self.confidence = confidence
        self.duration = (end_sample - start_sample)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "start_sample": self.start_sample,
            "end_sample": self.end_sample,
            "speaker_id": self.speaker_id,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "duration": self.duration,
        }


class DiarizationEngine:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, vad_threshold=0.5,
                 min_segment_samples=3200, max_segment_samples=48000,
                 similarity_threshold=0.65, embedding_dim=192):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.min_segment_samples = min_segment_samples
        self.max_segment_samples = max_segment_samples
        self.similarity_threshold = similarity_threshold
        self.embedding_dim = embedding_dim
        self._vad = VAD(threshold=vad_threshold, sample_rate=sample_rate)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._speaker_profiles = {}
        self._segment_count = 0
        self._mel_filterbank = None

    def register_profile(self, speaker_id, embedding):
        with self._lock:
            self._speaker_profiles[speaker_id] = np.asarray(embedding, dtype=np.float32)

    def remove_profile(self, speaker_id):
        with self._lock:
            self._speaker_profiles.pop(speaker_id, None)

    def _cosine_similarity(self, a, b):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float32).flatten()
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float32).flatten()
        dot = np.dot(a, b)
        norm = np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b) + 1e-12
        return float(dot / norm)

    def _find_best_speaker(self, embedding):
        if embedding is None:
            return "__unknown__", 0.0
        with self._lock:
            best_id = "__unknown__"
            best_conf = 0.0
            for sid, profile in list(self._speaker_profiles.items()):
                sim = self._cosine_similarity(embedding, profile)
                if sim > best_conf and sim >= self.similarity_threshold:
                    best_conf = sim
                    best_id = sid
        return best_id, best_conf

    def _build_mel_filterbank(self, n_fft):
        if self._mel_filterbank is not None and self._mel_filterbank[0].shape[1] == n_fft:
            return self._mel_filterbank
        mel_bins = 40
        mel_filter = np.zeros((mel_bins, n_fft))
        mel_min = 0
        mel_max = 2595 * np.log10(1 + self.sample_rate / 700.0)
        mel_points = np.linspace(mel_min, mel_max, mel_bins + 2)
        hz_points = 700 * (10 ** (mel_points / 2595) - 1)
        bins = np.floor(hz_points / self.sample_rate * n_fft * 2).astype(int)
        bins = np.clip(bins, 0, n_fft - 1)
        for m in range(1, mel_bins + 1):
            if bins[m] > bins[m - 1]:
                mel_filter[m - 1, bins[m - 1]:bins[m]] = np.linspace(0, 1, bins[m] - bins[m - 1])
            else:
                mel_filter[m - 1, bins[m - 1]] = 1.0
            if bins[m + 1] > bins[m]:
                mel_filter[m - 1, bins[m]:bins[m + 1]] = np.linspace(1, 0, bins[m + 1] - bins[m])
            else:
                mel_filter[m - 1, bins[m]] = 1.0
        self._mel_filterbank = (mel_filter, mel_bins)
        return self._mel_filterbank

    def _extract_embedding(self, audio):
        try:
            n = len(audio)
            features = []
            frame_step = int(0.01 * self.sample_rate)
            frame_len = int(0.025 * self.sample_rate)
            for start in range(0, n - frame_len + 1, frame_step):
                frame = audio[start:start + frame_len]
                frame = frame * np.hanning(len(frame))
                fft = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(frame))
                mel_filter, mel_bins = self._build_mel_filterbank(len(fft))
                mel_energy = np.log(np.dot(mel_filter, fft ** 2) + 1e-12)
                features.append(mel_energy)

            if len(features) < 5:
                return None

            features = np.array(features, dtype=np.float32)
            mean = np.mean(features, axis=0)
            std = np.std(features, axis=0) + 1e-12
            features = (features - mean) / std

            if features.shape[1] >= self.embedding_dim:
                embedding = np.mean(features, axis=0)[:self.embedding_dim]
            else:
                embedding = np.pad(np.mean(features, axis=0), (0, self.embedding_dim - features.shape[1]))
            embedding = embedding / (np.linalg.norm(embedding) + 1e-12)
            return embedding.astype(np.float32)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[Diarization] Embedding error: %s", e)
            return None

    def segment(self, audio):
        try:
            audio = np.asarray(audio, dtype=np.float32)
            frame_size = 512
            segments = []
            seg_start = None

            for i in range(0, len(audio), frame_size):
                chunk = audio[i:i + frame_size]
                if len(chunk) < frame_size:
                    chunk = np.pad(chunk, (0, frame_size - len(chunk)))
                is_speech = self._vad.is_speech(chunk)

                if is_speech and seg_start is None:
                    seg_start = i
                elif not is_speech and seg_start is not None:
                    total_samples = i + frame_size - seg_start
                    if total_samples >= self.min_segment_samples:
                        seg_audio = audio[seg_start:i + frame_size]
                        embedding = self._extract_embedding(seg_audio)
                        speaker_id, conf = self._find_best_speaker(embedding) if embedding is not None else ("__unknown__", 0.0)
                        segments.append(SpeakerSegment(
                            start_sample=seg_start,
                            end_sample=i + frame_size,
                            speaker_id=speaker_id,
                            confidence=conf,
                        ))
                    seg_start = None

            if seg_start is not None:
                seg_audio = audio[seg_start:]
                if len(seg_audio) >= self.min_segment_samples:
                    embedding = self._extract_embedding(seg_audio)
                    speaker_id, conf = self._find_best_speaker(embedding) if embedding is not None else ("__unknown__", 0.0)
                    segments.append(SpeakerSegment(
                        start_sample=seg_start,
                        end_sample=len(audio),
                        speaker_id=speaker_id,
                        confidence=conf,
                    ))

            self._merge_adjacent_segments(segments)
            with self._lock:
                self._segment_count += len(segments)
            return segments
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[Diarization] segment error: %s", e)
            return []

    def _merge_adjacent_segments(self, segments):
        if len(segments) < 2:
            return
        merged = [segments[0]]
        for seg in segments[1:]:
            prev = merged[-1]
            if (seg.speaker_id == prev.speaker_id
                    and seg.confidence > 0.5
                    and prev.confidence > 0.5):
                prev.end_sample = seg.end_sample
                prev.confidence = max(prev.confidence, seg.confidence)
                prev.duration = seg.end_sample - prev.start_sample
            else:
                merged.append(seg)
        segments[:] = merged

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "registered_profiles": len(self._speaker_profiles),
                "total_segments": self._segment_count,
                "profile_ids": list(self._speaker_profiles.keys()),
            }

# voice\echo_canceller.py
import numpy as np
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class EchoCanceller:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, filter_length=1024, adaptation_rate=0.01,
                 max_echo_delay=0.3):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.filter_length = filter_length
        self.adaptation_rate = adaptation_rate
        self.max_echo_delay_samples = int(max_echo_delay * sample_rate)
        buf_size = self.max_echo_delay_samples + filter_length
        self._playback_buffer = np.zeros(buf_size, dtype=np.float32)
        self._filter_weights = np.zeros(filter_length, dtype=np.float32)
        self._playback_write = 0

    def add_playback(self, audio):
        if len(audio) == 0:
            return
        total = len(self._playback_buffer)
        offset = 0
        remaining = len(audio)
        while remaining > 0:
            avail = total - self._playback_write
            chunk = min(remaining, avail)
            self._playback_buffer[self._playback_write:self._playback_write + chunk] = audio[offset:offset + chunk]
            self._playback_write = (self._playback_write + chunk) % total
            offset += chunk
            remaining -= chunk

    def process(self, mic_audio):
        if len(mic_audio) == 0:
            return mic_audio
        try:
            total = len(self._playback_buffer)
            n = len(mic_audio)
            output = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
            for i in range(n):
                ref = np.array([
                    self._playback_buffer[(self._playback_write - self.filter_length + i + j) % total]
                    for j in range(self.filter_length)
                ], dtype=np.float32)
                echo_est = np.dot(ref, self._filter_weights)
                error = mic_audio[i] - echo_est
                output[i] = error
                norm = np.dot(ref, ref) + 1e-10
                self._filter_weights += (self.adaptation_rate * error / norm) * ref
            return output
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[EchoCanceller] process error: %s", e)
            return mic_audio

# voice\emotion_detector.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np
from collections import deque

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EMOTION_LABELS = {
    0: "neutral",
    1: "happy",
    2: "sad",
    3: "angry",
    4: "stressed",
    5: "energetic",
}


class VoiceEmotionDetector:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, frame_ms=30, history_seconds=2.0):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.frame_samples = int(sample_rate * frame_ms / 1000)
        self.history_samples = int(sample_rate * history_seconds)
        self._audio_history = deque(maxlen=self.history_samples)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def add_audio(self, chunk):
        with self._lock:
            self._audio_history.extend(chunk.tolist() if isinstance(chunk, np.ndarray) else chunk)

    def _get_history(self):
        with self._lock:
            if len(self._audio_history) < self.frame_samples * 2:
                return None
            return np.array(list(self._audio_history)[-self.history_samples:], dtype=np.float32)

    def compute_features(self, audio):
        if audio is None or len(audio) < self.frame_samples * 2:
            return None
        audio = audio - np.mean(audio)
        audio = audio / (np.std(audio) + 1e-12)

        energy = np.mean(audio ** 2)

        fft = np.fft.rfft(audio)
        magnitude = np.abs(fft)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(audio), d=1.0 / self.sample_rate)
        total_energy = np.sum(magnitude ** 2) + 1e-12
        spectral_centroid = np.sum(freqs * magnitude) / np.sum(magnitude + 1e-12)

        low_bin = int(300 / (self.sample_rate / len(audio)))
        high_bin = int(3400 / (self.sample_rate / len(audio)))
        speech_energy_ratio = np.sum(magnitude[low_bin:high_bin] ** 2) / total_energy if high_bin > low_bin else 0.5

        low_freq = int(80 / (self.sample_rate / len(audio)))
        high_freq = int(400 / (self.sample_rate / len(audio)))
        low_energy_ratio = np.sum(magnitude[low_freq:high_freq] ** 2) / total_energy if high_freq > low_freq else 0.3

        zcr = np.mean(np.abs(np.diff(np.sign(audio)))) / 2.0

        autocorr = np.correlate(audio, audio, mode="full")
        autocorr = autocorr[len(autocorr) // 2:]
        if len(autocorr) > 1:
            pitch_range = autocorr[int(self.sample_rate / 500):int(self.sample_rate / 60)] if int(self.sample_rate / 500) < int(self.sample_rate / 60) else autocorr[1:]
            pitch_conf = np.max(pitch_range) / (np.max(autocorr) + 1e-12) if np.max(autocorr) > 0 else 0.0
        else:
            pitch_conf = 0.0

        return {
            "energy": float(energy),
            "spectral_centroid": float(spectral_centroid),
            "speech_energy_ratio": float(speech_energy_ratio),
            "low_energy_ratio": float(low_energy_ratio),
            "zcr": float(zcr),
            "pitch_confidence": float(pitch_conf),
        }

    def classify_emotion(self, features):
        if features is None:
            return "neutral", 0.5

        e = features["energy"]
        sc = features["spectral_centroid"]
        ser = features["speech_energy_ratio"]
        ler = features["low_energy_ratio"]
        zcr = features["zcr"]
        pc = features["pitch_confidence"]

        scores = {
            "neutral": 0.3,
            "happy": 0.0,
            "sad": 0.0,
            "angry": 0.0,
            "stressed": 0.0,
            "energetic": 0.0,
        }

        if e < 0.01:
            scores["neutral"] += 0.3
            scores["sad"] += 0.1
        elif e > 0.1:
            scores["angry"] += 0.3
            scores["stressed"] += 0.2
            scores["energetic"] += 0.2
        elif e > 0.04:
            scores["happy"] += 0.2
            scores["energetic"] += 0.2
        else:
            scores["neutral"] += 0.2

        if sc > 2000:
            scores["happy"] += 0.2
            scores["energetic"] += 0.2
        elif sc < 800:
            scores["sad"] += 0.2
            scores["neutral"] += 0.1
        else:
            scores["neutral"] += 0.1

        if ser > 0.6:
            scores["neutral"] += 0.2
            scores["happy"] += 0.1
        elif ser < 0.3:
            scores["angry"] += 0.2
            scores["stressed"] += 0.2

        if ler > 0.3:
            scores["sad"] += 0.2
            scores["neutral"] += 0.1

        if zcr > 0.1:
            scores["angry"] += 0.2
            scores["stressed"] += 0.2
            scores["energetic"] += 0.1
        elif zcr < 0.03:
            scores["sad"] += 0.2
            scores["neutral"] += 0.1

        if pc > 0.5:
            scores["happy"] += 0.2
            scores["energetic"] += 0.2
        elif pc < 0.2:
            scores["neutral"] += 0.1
            scores["sad"] += 0.1

        emotion = max(scores, key=scores.get)
        confidence = min(scores[emotion], 0.95)
        return emotion, max(confidence, 0.3)

    def detect(self, audio=None):
        if audio is not None:
            self.add_audio(audio)
        history = self._get_history()
        if history is None:
            return "neutral", 0.3, {}
        features = self.compute_features(history)
        emotion, confidence = self.classify_emotion(features)
        return emotion, confidence, features or {}

# voice\events.py
VOICE_STARTED = "voice.started"
VOICE_STOPPED = "voice.stopped"
VOICE_STATE_CHANGED = "voice.state_changed"
VOICE_LISTENING_STARTED = "voice.listening_started"
VOICE_RECORDING_COMPLETED = "voice.recording_completed"
VOICE_SPEAKER_IDENTIFIED = "voice.speaker_identified"
VOICE_TRANSCRIPTION_COMPLETED = "voice.transcription_completed"
VOICE_WAKE_DETECTED = "voice.wake_detected"
VOICE_RETURNED = "voice.returned"
VOICE_ERROR = "voice.error"


def voice_started_data():
    return {"state": "started"}


def voice_stopped_data():
    return {"state": "stopped"}


def voice_state_changed_data(old_state, new_state):
    return {"old_state": old_state, "new_state": new_state}


def voice_listening_started_data(wake_required):
    return {"wake_required": wake_required}


def voice_recording_completed_data(audio_length, duration):
    return {"audio_length": audio_length, "duration": duration}


def voice_speaker_identified_data(speaker, confidence):
    return {"speaker": speaker, "confidence": confidence}


def voice_transcription_completed_data(text, audio_length, duration):
    return {"text": text, "text_length": len(text), "audio_length": audio_length, "duration": duration}


def voice_wake_detected_data(wake_word, text):
    return {"wake_word": wake_word, "text": text}


def voice_returned_data(text, speaker, wake_detected, duration, extra=None):
    data = {"text": text, "speaker": speaker, "wake_detected": wake_detected, "duration": duration}
    if extra:
        data["extra"] = extra
    return data


def voice_error_data(error_type, message):
    return {"type": error_type, "message": str(message)}

# voice\full_aec.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class FullAEC:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, filter_length=2048,
                 adaptation_rate=0.01, max_echo_delay=0.5,
                 double_talk_threshold=0.3, residual_tail_length=256,
                 hangover_frames=20):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.filter_length = filter_length
        self.adaptation_rate = adaptation_rate
        self.max_echo_delay_samples = int(max_echo_delay * sample_rate)
        self.double_talk_threshold = double_talk_threshold
        self.residual_tail_length = residual_tail_length
        self.hangover_frames = hangover_frames

        buf_size = self.max_echo_delay_samples + filter_length
        self._playback_buffer = np.zeros(buf_size, dtype=np.float32)
        self._filter_weights = np.zeros(filter_length, dtype=np.float32)
        self._playback_write = 0

        self._residual_weights = np.zeros(residual_tail_length, dtype=np.float32)
        self._residual_buffer = np.zeros(residual_tail_length, dtype=np.float32)
        self._residual_idx = 0

        self._near_end_power = 0.0
        self._far_end_power = 0.0
