        self._double_talk = False
        self._hangover = 0
        self._clip_counter = 0
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def add_playback(self, audio):
        if len(audio) == 0:
            return
        with self._lock:
            total = len(self._playback_buffer)
            offset = 0
            remaining = len(audio)
            while remaining > 0:
                avail = total - self._playback_write
                chunk = min(remaining, avail)
                self._playback_buffer[self._playback_write:self._playback_write + chunk] = audio[offset:offset + chunk]
                self._playback_write = (self._playback_write + chunk) % total
                offset += chunk
                remaining -= chunk

    def process(self, mic_audio):
        if len(mic_audio) == 0:
            return mic_audio
        try:
            with self._lock:
                total = len(self._playback_buffer)
                n = len(mic_audio)
                output = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)

                near_power = np.mean(mic_audio ** 2) + 1e-12
                self._near_end_power = 0.95 * self._near_end_power + 0.05 * near_power

                far_end_samples = min(self.filter_length, total)
                if self._playback_write >= far_end_samples:
                    far_slice = self._playback_buffer[
                        self._playback_write - far_end_samples:self._playback_write
                    ]
                else:
                    part1 = self._playback_buffer[total - (far_end_samples - self._playback_write):]
                    part2 = self._playback_buffer[:self._playback_write]
                    far_slice = np.concatenate([part1, part2])
                far_slice = far_slice[:far_end_samples]
                far_power = np.mean(far_slice ** 2) + 1e-12
                self._far_end_power = 0.95 * self._far_end_power + 0.05 * far_power

                if self._far_end_power > 1e-6:
                    ratio = near_power / self._far_end_power
                    in_double_talk = ratio > self.double_talk_threshold
                else:
                    in_double_talk = False

                if in_double_talk:
                    self._hangover = self.hangover_frames
                    self._double_talk = True
                elif self._hangover > 0:
                    self._hangover -= 1
                else:
                    self._double_talk = False

                mu = self.adaptation_rate
                if self._double_talk:
                    mu *= 0.05

                for i in range(n):
                    ref = np.array([
                        self._playback_buffer[(self._playback_write - self.filter_length + i + j) % total]
                        for j in range(self.filter_length)
                    ], dtype=np.float32)

                    echo_est = np.dot(ref, self._filter_weights)
                    error = mic_audio[i] - echo_est

                    self._residual_buffer[self._residual_idx] = error
                    self._residual_idx = (self._residual_idx + 1) % self.residual_tail_length
                    residual_echo = np.dot(
                        np.concatenate([
                            self._residual_buffer[self._residual_idx:],
                            self._residual_buffer[:self._residual_idx]
                        ]),
                        self._residual_weights
                    )
                    error -= residual_echo

                    if abs(error) > 3.0 * np.sqrt(self._near_end_power + 1e-12):
                        error *= 0.5

                    output[i] = error
                    norm = np.dot(ref, ref) + 1e-10
                    if not self._double_talk:
                        self._filter_weights += (mu * error / norm) * ref
                        res_norm = np.dot(self._residual_buffer, self._residual_buffer) + 1e-10
                        self._residual_weights += (mu * 0.3 * error / res_norm) * self._residual_buffer

                return output
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[FullAEC] process error: %s", e)
            return mic_audio

    def is_double_talk(self):
        return self._double_talk

    def reset(self):
        with self._lock:
            buf_size = self.max_echo_delay_samples + self.filter_length
            self._playback_buffer = np.zeros(buf_size, dtype=np.float32)
            self._filter_weights = np.zeros(self.filter_length, dtype=np.float32)
            self._playback_write = 0
            self._residual_weights = np.zeros(self.residual_tail_length, dtype=np.float32)
            self._residual_buffer = np.zeros(self.residual_tail_length, dtype=np.float32)
            self._residual_idx = 0
            self._near_end_power = 0.0
            self._far_end_power = 0.0
            self._double_talk = False
            self._hangover = 0

    def stats(self):
        return {
            "filter_length": self.filter_length,
            "double_talk": self._double_talk,
            "near_end_power": float(f"{self._near_end_power:.6f}"),
            "far_end_power": float(f"{self._far_end_power:.6f}"),
        }

# voice\interrupt.py
# Mai_ai/voice/interrupt.py
import threading
import time


class InterruptManager:

    def __init__(self):

        # ðŸ§  thread-safe interrupt signal
        self._event = threading.Event()

        # ðŸ§  metadata
        self.reason = None

        self.source = None

        self.priority = 0

        self.timestamp = None

        self.history = []

        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    # ðŸ›‘ TRIGGER INTERRUPT
    def trigger(

        self,

        reason="unknown",

        source="system",

        priority=1
    ):

        with self.lock:

            self._event.set()

            self.reason = reason

            self.source = source

            self.priority = priority

            self.timestamp = time.time()

            # ðŸ§  history log
            self.history.append({

                "reason":
                    reason,

                "source":
                    source,

                "priority":
                    priority,

                "timestamp":
                    self.timestamp
            })

            # ðŸ§  prevent infinite history growth
            self.history = self.history[-50:]

    # ðŸ”„ RESET INTERRUPT
    def reset(self):

        with self.lock:

            self._event.clear()

            self.reason = None

            self.source = None

            self.priority = 0

            self.timestamp = None

    # â“ CHECK INTERRUPT STATE
    def is_interrupted(self):

        return self._event.is_set()

    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    def get_state(self):

        return {

            "interrupted":
                self.is_interrupted(),

            "reason":
                self.reason,

            "source":
                self.source,

            "priority":
                self.priority,

            "timestamp":
                self.timestamp
        }

    # ðŸ“œ RECENT INTERRUPTS
    def recent_history(
        self,
        limit=10
    ):

        return self.history[-limit:]

    # â³ TIME SINCE INTERRUPT
    def seconds_since_interrupt(self):

        if not self.timestamp:
            return None

        return round(
            time.time() - self.timestamp,
            2
        )
# voice\kokoro_tts.py
# Mai_ai/voice/kokoro_tts.py
import logging
import re
import threading

import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf

from config.settings import (
    KOKORO_VOICE,
    KOKORO_LANG_CODE,
    VOICE_SAMPLE_RATE,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.TTS")

# ðŸ§  lazy import â€” kokoro loads slow
KPipeline: type = None  # type: ignore[assignment]


def _load_kokoro():
    global KPipeline
    if KPipeline is None:
        from kokoro import KPipeline as _KPipeline
        KPipeline = _KPipeline


class KokoroTTS:

    # ðŸ§  shared singleton pipeline
    _shared_pipeline = None

    _model_lock = threading.Lock()

    def __init__(
        self,
        language="en",
        base_speed=0.92,
        voice=None,
    ):
        self.language  = language
        self.base_speed = base_speed

        # voice from settings if not passed
        self.voice = voice or KOKORO_VOICE

        self.last_emotion = "neutral"

        self.sample_rate = VOICE_SAMPLE_RATE

        # ðŸ§  load pipeline once globally
        with self._model_lock:

            if KokoroTTS._shared_pipeline is None:

                logger.info("Loading Kokoro...")

                _load_kokoro()

                KokoroTTS._shared_pipeline = KPipeline(
                    lang_code=KOKORO_LANG_CODE,
                    repo_id='hexgrad/Kokoro-82M',
                )

                logger.info("Kokoro ready")

        self.pipeline = KokoroTTS._shared_pipeline

    # ðŸ—£ï¸ MAIN GENERATION
    def generate(
        self,
        text,
        output_path,
        emotion="neutral"
    ):

        if not text:
            return

        self.last_emotion = emotion

        enhanced = self._enhance_text(text, emotion)

        speed = self._emotion_speed(emotion)

        try:

            audio_chunks = []

            for _, _, audio in self.pipeline(
                enhanced,
                voice=self.voice,
                speed=speed,
            ):
                if audio is not None:
                    audio_chunks.append(audio)

            if audio_chunks:

                full_audio = np.concatenate(
                    audio_chunks
                )

                sf.write(
                    output_path,
                    full_audio,
                    self.sample_rate
                )

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[TTS ERROR] {e}")

    # ðŸŽ­ ENHANCE SPEECH RHYTHM
    def _enhance_text(self, text, emotion="neutral"):

        text = text.strip()

        text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)

        text = text.replace("...", "â€¦ ")

        text = re.sub(r",\s*", ", ", text)

        text = re.sub(r"\.\s*", ". ", text)

        if emotion == "soft":
            text = text.replace(".", "... ")

        elif emotion == "annoyed":
            text = text.replace("...", ".")

        elif emotion == "teasing":
            text = text.replace("?", "? â€¦")

        if len(text) > 40:
            text = text.replace(" and ", "â€¦ and ")

        return text.strip()

    # âš¡ EMOTION â†’ SPEED
    def _emotion_speed(self, emotion):

        speeds = {
            "soft":    0.88,
            "neutral": 0.92,
            "teasing": 0.90,
            "annoyed": 0.98,
            "sad":     0.85,
            "calm":    0.90,
        }

        return speeds.get(emotion, self.base_speed)

    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    def stats(self):
        return {
            "engine":       "kokoro",
            "voice":        self.voice,
            "language":     self.language,
            "last_emotion": self.last_emotion,
            "base_speed":   self.base_speed,
        }
# voice\metrics.py
import threading


class VoiceMetrics:
    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.recordings = 0
        self.total_duration_seconds = 0.0
        self.total_audio_bytes = 0
        self.successful_transcriptions = 0
        self.failed_transcriptions = 0
        self.wake_detections = 0
        self.speaker_identifications = 0
        self.errors = 0
        self._transcription_confidence_sum = 0.0

    def record_recording(self, duration, audio_length):
        with self._lock:
            self.recordings += 1
            self.total_duration_seconds += duration
            self.total_audio_bytes += audio_length

    def record_transcription(self, success):
        with self._lock:
            if success:
                self.successful_transcriptions += 1
            else:
                self.failed_transcriptions += 1

    def record_wake(self):
        with self._lock:
            self.wake_detections += 1

    def record_speaker_id(self):
        with self._lock:
            self.speaker_identifications += 1

    def record_error(self):
        with self._lock:
            self.errors += 1

    def avg_duration(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self.recordings == 0:
                return 0.0
            return self.total_duration_seconds / self.recordings

    def transcription_success_rate(self):
        with self._lock:
            total = self.successful_transcriptions + self.failed_transcriptions
            if total == 0:
                return 1.0
            return self.successful_transcriptions / total

    def stats(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "recordings": self.recordings,
                "total_duration_seconds": round(self.total_duration_seconds, 2),
                "total_audio_bytes": self.total_audio_bytes,
                "avg_duration_seconds": round(self.avg_duration(), 3),
                "successful_transcriptions": self.successful_transcriptions,
                "failed_transcriptions": self.failed_transcriptions,
                "transcription_success_rate": round(self.transcription_success_rate(), 3),
                "wake_detections": self.wake_detections,
                "speaker_identifications": self.speaker_identifications,
                "errors": self.errors,
            }

# voice\microphone.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Microphone:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, channels=1, blocksize=512, device=None):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.channels = channels
        self.blocksize = blocksize
        self.device = device
        self._stream = None
        self._running = False
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._callbacks = []
        self._error_count = 0
        self._max_errors = 5

    def start(self, callback=None):
        import sounddevice as sd

        if callback:
            self._callbacks.append(callback)

        with self._lock:
            if self._running:
                return

            def audio_callback(indata, frames, time_info, status):
                if status:
                    logger.debug("[Mic] Status: %s", status)
                chunk = indata[:, 0].copy().astype(np.float32)
                with self._lock:
                    for cb in self._callbacks:
                        try:
                            cb(chunk)
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug("[Mic] Callback error: %s", e)

            try:
                self._stream = sd.InputStream(
                    samplerate=self.sample_rate,
                    channels=self.channels,
                    blocksize=self.blocksize,
                    device=self.device,
                    dtype="float32",
                    callback=audio_callback,
                )
                self._stream.start()
                self._running = True
                self._error_count = 0
                logger.info("[Mic] Started (sr=%d, blocksize=%d)", self.sample_rate, self.blocksize)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[Mic] Start failed: %s", e)
                self._running = False

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._stream:
                try:
                    self._stream.stop()
                    self._stream.close()
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("[Mic] Stop error: %s", e)
                self._stream = None
            self._running = False

    @property
    def is_running(self):
        return self._running

    def add_callback(self, callback):
        with self._lock:
            self._callbacks.append(callback)

    def remove_callback(self, callback):
        with self._lock:
            if callback in self._callbacks:
                self._callbacks.remove(callback)

# voice\noise_filter.py
import numpy as np
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class NoiseFilter:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, low_cut=80.0, high_cut=7600.0,
                 noise_floor_percentile=10, reduction_db=6.0):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.low_cut = low_cut
        self.high_cut = high_cut
        self.noise_floor_percentile = noise_floor_percentile
        self.reduction_db = reduction_db
        self._noise_profile = None

    def learn_noise(self, audio, sample_rate=None):
        sr = sample_rate or self.sample_rate
        try:
            freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(audio), d=1.0 / sr)
            spectrum = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(audio))
            noise_floor = np.percentile(spectrum, self.noise_floor_percentile)
            self._noise_profile = np.maximum(spectrum, noise_floor)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[NoiseFilter] learn_noise error: %s", e)
            return False

    def filter(self, audio):
        if len(audio) < 2:
            return audio
        try:
            filtered = self._bandpass(audio)
            if self._noise_profile is not None:
                filtered = self._spectral_subtract(filtered)
            return filtered
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[NoiseFilter] filter error: %s", e)
            return audio

    def _bandpass(self, audio):
        nyquist = self.sample_rate / 2.0
        low = self.low_cut / nyquist
        high = self.high_cut / nyquist
        if low <= 0 or high <= 0 or low >= 1 or high >= 1:
            return audio
        fft = np.fft.rfft(audio)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(audio), d=1.0 / self.sample_rate)
        mask = (freqs >= self.low_cut) & (freqs <= self.high_cut)
        fft = fft * mask
        return np.fft.irfft(fft, n=len(audio)).astype(audio.dtype)

    def _spectral_subtract(self, audio):
        fft = np.fft.rfft(audio)
        magnitude = np.abs(fft)
        phase = np.angle(fft)
        reduction_linear = 10 ** (-self.reduction_db / 20.0)
        noise_est = np.mean(self._noise_profile) * reduction_linear
        cleaned = np.maximum(magnitude - noise_est, 0)
        new_fft = cleaned * np.exp(1j * phase)
        return np.fft.irfft(new_fft, n=len(audio)).astype(audio.dtype)

# voice\realtime.py
# Mai_ai/voice/realtime.py
import logging
import tempfile
import threading
import time

from voice.stream_player import StreamPlayer
from voice.interrupt import InterruptManager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class RealtimeVoice:

    def __init__(
        self,
        tts_engine=None,
        max_queue=10,
        emotion=None,
    ):

        self.tts = tts_engine

        self.player = StreamPlayer()

        self.interrupt = InterruptManager()

        self.max_queue = max_queue

        self.is_speaking = False

        self.is_generating = False

        self.current_text = None

        self.voice_lock = threading.RLock()

        self.emotion = emotion

        # ðŸ§  state tracking
        self.last_activity = time.time()

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ—£ï¸ NORMAL SPEECH
    # ==================================================
    def speak(self, text):

        if not text:
            return

        text = str(text).strip()

        if not text:
            return

        with self.voice_lock:

            try:

                self.interrupt.reset()

                if self.emotion:
                    try:
                        speed = self.emotion.influence("speech_speed")
                        self.player.set_speed(speed)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                self.player.play_async()

                self.is_speaking = True

                self.current_text = text

                self.last_activity = time.time()

                path = self._temp_path()

                if self.tts:

                    self.is_generating = True

                    emotion_label = "neutral"
                    if self.emotion:
                        try:
                            word_bias = self.emotion.influence("word_choice")
                            if word_bias == "positive":
                                emotion_label = "happy"
                            elif word_bias == "warm":
                                emotion_label = "soft"
                            elif word_bias == "cautious":
                                emotion_label = "neutral"
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    kwargs = {}
                    if hasattr(self.tts, 'generate'):
                        import inspect
                        sig = inspect.signature(self.tts.generate)
                        if 'emotion' in sig.parameters:
                            kwargs['emotion'] = emotion_label
                    self.tts.generate(text, path, **kwargs)

                    self.is_generating = False

                    # âœ… don't enqueue empty/broken files
                    if self._valid_audio(path):

                        self.player.enqueue(path)

                    else:
                        logger.warning("Invalid audio generated")

                else:
                    logger.info("Mai: %s", text)

            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("Voice error: %s", e)

            finally:

                self.is_generating = False

                # âœ… wait for playback to finish
                self.wait_until_done()

                self.is_speaking = False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸŽ™ï¸ STREAMED SPEECH
    # ==================================================
    def speak_stream(self, text_chunks):

        with self.voice_lock:

            try:

                self.interrupt.reset()

                self.player.play_async()

                self.is_speaking = True

                buffer = ""

                last_emit = time.time()

                for chunk in text_chunks:

                    if self.interrupt.is_interrupted():

                        logger.info("[Voice Interrupted]")

                        break

                    if not chunk:
                        continue

                    chunk = str(chunk).strip()

                    if not chunk:
                        continue

                    # ðŸ§  queue protection
                    while (
                        self.player.audio_queue.qsize()
                        >= self.max_queue
                    ):

                        if self.interrupt.is_interrupted():
                            break

                        time.sleep(0.05)

                    buffer += " " + chunk

                    buffer = buffer.strip()

                    # ==================================================
                    # ðŸ§  SMART CHUNKING
                    # ==================================================

                    ends_sentence = any(
                        buffer.rstrip().endswith(p)
                        for p in [
                            ".",
                            "?",
                            "!",
                            "â€¦"
                        ]
                    )

                    should_emit = (

                        (ends_sentence and len(buffer) >= 50)

                        or

                        len(buffer) >= 180

                        or

                        (
                            time.time() - last_emit
                        ) > 3.5
                    )

                    if not should_emit:
                        continue

                    try:

                        self._generate_and_queue(buffer)

                        buffer = ""

                        last_emit = time.time()

                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.exception("Stream voice error: %s", e)

                # ==================================================
                # ðŸ§  FINAL BUFFER FLUSH
                # ==================================================

                if (
                    buffer.strip()
                    and not self.interrupt.is_interrupted()
                ):

                    try:
                        self._generate_and_queue(buffer.strip())
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.exception("Final chunk error: %s", e)

            finally:

                self.is_generating = False

                # âœ… wait until ALL playback ends
                self.wait_until_done()

                self.is_speaking = False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  GENERATE + QUEUE AUDIO
    # ==================================================
    def _generate_and_queue(self, text):

        if not text:
            return

        text = str(text).strip()

        if not text:
            return

        self.current_text = text

        self.last_activity = time.time()

        path = self._temp_path()

        if self.tts:

            try:

                self.is_generating = True

                self.tts.generate(text, path)

            finally:

                self.is_generating = False

            # âœ… validate before enqueue
            if self._valid_audio(path):

                self.player.enqueue(path)

            else:
                logger.warning("Skipping invalid audio")

        else:
            logger.info("Mai: %s", text)

    # ==================================================
    # âœ… WAIT FOR AUDIO COMPLETION
    # ==================================================
    def wait_until_done(self):

        timeout = 60

        start = time.time()

        while True:

            # âœ… fully idle
            if (
                not self.is_generating
                and self.player.audio_queue.empty()
                and self.player.state != "playing"
            ):
                break

            # ðŸ›‘ deadlock protection
            if (
                time.time() - start
            ) > timeout:

                logger.warning("wait_until_done timeout")

                break

            time.sleep(0.05)

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ›‘ STOP SPEECH
    # ==================================================
    def stop(self):

        with self.voice_lock:

            self.interrupt.trigger()

            try:

                self.player.stop()

            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("Voice stop error: %s", e)

            self.is_speaking = False

            self.is_generating = False

            self.current_text = None

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  VALID AUDIO CHECK
    # ==================================================
    def _valid_audio(self, path):

        try:

            return (
                path
                and path.endswith(".wav")
            )

        except Exception:
            return False

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ§  TEMP FILE CREATION
    # ==================================================
    def _temp_path(self):

        temp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
            suffix=".wav",
            delete=False
        )

        path = temp.name

        temp.close()

        return path

    # ==================================================
    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    # ==================================================
    def stats(self):

        return {

            "is_speaking":
                self.is_speaking,

            "is_generating":
                self.is_generating,

            "current_text":
                self.current_text,

            "queue_size":
                self.player.audio_queue.qsize(),

            "player_state":
                self.player.state,

            "last_activity":
                self.last_activity,
        }
# voice\recorder.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

STATE_IDLE = "idle"
STATE_STARTING = "starting"
STATE_RECORDING = "recording"
STATE_FINALIZING = "finalizing"

_ADAPTIVE_RATE = 0.01
_MIN_FLOOR = 1e-6


class Recorder:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, silence_timeout=0.7,
                 min_recording=0.5, max_recording=30.0, pre_roll=0.5,
                 vad=None):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.silence_timeout = silence_timeout
        self.min_recording = min_recording
        self.max_recording = max_recording
        self.pre_roll = pre_roll
        self._vad = vad
        self._state = STATE_IDLE
        self._audio = []
        self._silence_start = None
        self._recording_start = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._event = threading.Event()
        self._result = None
        self._noise_floor = _MIN_FLOOR
        self._settled = False

    @property
    def state(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._state

    def start_recording(self, pre_roll_audio=None):
        with self._lock:
            self._state = STATE_STARTING
            self._audio = []
            self._silence_start = None
            self._result = None
            self._event.clear()
            pre_roll_len = 0
            if pre_roll_audio is not None and len(pre_roll_audio) > 0:
                self._audio.append(np.copy(pre_roll_audio))
                pre_roll_len = len(pre_roll_audio)
            self._recording_start = time.time()
            self._state = STATE_RECORDING
            logger.info("[Recorder] Started (silence_timeout=%.1fs, max=%.1fs, pre_roll=%d samples)",
                         self.silence_timeout, self.max_recording, pre_roll_len)

    def _is_speech(self, chunk):
        if self._vad is not None:
            try:
                return self._vad.is_speech(chunk)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return self._volume_above_noise_floor(chunk)

    def _volume_above_noise_floor(self, chunk):
        volume = np.abs(chunk).mean()
        threshold = max(self._noise_floor * 3.0, _MIN_FLOOR)
        return volume > threshold

    def _update_noise_floor(self, chunk):
        volume = np.abs(chunk).mean()
        if not self._settled:
            self._noise_floor = self._noise_floor * (1 - _ADAPTIVE_RATE) + volume * _ADAPTIVE_RATE
            if self._noise_floor > 0:
                self._settled = True
        elif volume < self._noise_floor * 2.0:
            self._noise_floor = self._noise_floor * (1 - _ADAPTIVE_RATE * 0.1) + volume * (_ADAPTIVE_RATE * 0.1)

    def add_chunk(self, chunk):
        with self._lock:
            if self._state != STATE_RECORDING:
                return None

            self._update_noise_floor(chunk)
            self._audio.append(np.copy(chunk))

            speech = self._is_speech(chunk)
            chunk_count = len(self._audio)

            if speech:
                if self._silence_start is not None:
                    logger.info("[Recorder] Speech resumed after %.2fs silence (chunk %d)",
                                time.time() - self._silence_start, chunk_count)
                self._silence_start = None
            else:
                if self._silence_start is None:
                    self._silence_start = time.time()
                    logger.info("[Recorder] Silence started (chunk %d)", chunk_count)
                silence_duration = time.time() - self._silence_start
                if silence_duration >= self.silence_timeout:
                    elapsed = time.time() - self._recording_start
                    logger.info("[Recorder] Silence timeout (%.2fs), elapsed=%.2fs, chunks=%d",
                                silence_duration, elapsed, chunk_count)
                    self._state = STATE_FINALIZING
                    result = self._build_result(reason="silence")
                    self._result = result
                    self._event.set()
                    return result

            elapsed = time.time() - self._recording_start
            if elapsed >= self.max_recording:
                logger.info("[Recorder] Max recording time reached (%.1fs, chunks=%d)", elapsed, chunk_count)
                self._state = STATE_FINALIZING
                result = self._build_result(reason="max_duration")
                self._result = result
                self._event.set()
                return result

        return None

    def wait(self, timeout=None):
        logger.info("[Recorder] Waiting for audio (timeout=%s)...", timeout)
        self._event.wait(timeout=timeout)
        with self._lock:
            result = self._result
            audio_ok = result is not None
            logger.info("[Recorder] Wait returned: audio=%s, samples=%s",
                        audio_ok, len(result) if audio_ok else "N/A")
            self._reset()
            return result

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._state == STATE_RECORDING:
                result = self._build_result(reason="stopped")
                self._result = result
                self._event.set()
                self._reset()
                return result
            self._reset()
            return None

    def _build_result(self, reason=""):
        if not self._audio:
            logger.info("[Recorder] No audio collected â€” returning None")
            return None
        audio = np.concatenate(self._audio)
        elapsed = time.time() - self._recording_start if self._recording_start else 0
        if elapsed < self.min_recording:
            logger.info("[Recorder] Recording too short (%.3fs < %.3fs min) â€” returning None",
                        elapsed, self.min_recording)
            return None
        max_val = np.max(np.abs(audio))
        logger.info("[Recorder] Done: reason=%s elapsed=%.2fs samples=%d max_amplitude=%.4f",
                     reason, elapsed, len(audio), max_val)
        if max_val > 0:
            audio = audio / max_val
        return audio.astype(np.float32)

    def _reset(self):
        self._state = STATE_IDLE
        self._audio = []
        self._silence_start = None
        self._recording_start = None
        self._result = None
        self._event.clear()

# voice\speaker\__init__.py
from voice.speaker.speaker_profile import SpeakerProfile
from voice.speaker.speaker_encoder import SpeakerEncoder

    "SpeakerProfile",
    "SpeakerEncoder",

    "SpeakerManager",
]

# voice\speaker\speaker_database.py
import json
import logging
import math
import os
import threading
from pathlib import Path

from voice.speaker.speaker_profile import SpeakerProfile

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.SpeakerDatabase")


def _cosine_similarity(a: list[float], b: list[float]) -> float:
    if not a or not b:
        return 0.0
    dot = sum(ai * bi for ai, bi in zip(a, b))
    norm_a = math.sqrt(sum(ai * ai for ai in a))
    norm_b = math.sqrt(sum(bi * bi for bi in b))
    if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0:
        return 0.0
    return dot / (norm_a * norm_b)


class SpeakerDatabase:

    def __init__(self, profiles_dir: str | Path):
        self.profiles_dir = Path(profiles_dir)
        self.profiles_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def _path(self, name: str) -> Path:
        safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in " _-" else "_" for c in name)
        return self.profiles_dir / f"{safe}.json"

    def save(self, profile: SpeakerProfile) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            try:
                path = self._path(profile.name)
                path.write_text(json.dumps(profile.to_dict(), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                return True
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("save(%s) failed: %s", profile.name, exc)
                return False

    def load(self, name: str) -> SpeakerProfile | None:
        with self._lock:
            try:
                path = self._path(name)
                if not path.exists():
                    return None
                data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                return SpeakerProfile.from_dict(data)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("load(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
                return None

    def delete(self, name: str) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            try:
                path = self._path(name)
                if path.exists():
                    path.unlink()
                    return True
                return False
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("delete(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
                return False

    def list_all(self) -> list[SpeakerProfile]:
        with self._lock:
            results = []
            for fpath in self.profiles_dir.glob("*.json"):
                try:
                    data = json.loads(fpath.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                    results.append(SpeakerProfile.from_dict(data))
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug("list_all skipped %s: %s", fpath.name, exc)
            return results

    def search(
        self, query_embedding: list[float], threshold: float = 0.5
    ) -> tuple[SpeakerProfile | None, float]:
        best_profile = None
        best_score = 0.0
        for profile in self.list_all():
            if not profile.embedding:
                continue
            score = _cosine_similarity(query_embedding, profile.embedding)
            if score > best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_profile = profile

        if best_score >= threshold:
            return best_profile, best_score
        return None, best_score

    def count(self) -> int:
        return len(list(self.profiles_dir.glob("*.json")))

# voice\speaker\speaker_encoder.py
import logging
import warnings
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
import soundfile as sf

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.SpeakerEncoder")

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MODEL_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "models" / "speaker"

_REQUIRED_MODEL_FILES = (
    "hyperparams.yaml",
    "embedding_model.ckpt",
    "classifier.ckpt",
    "mean_var_norm_emb.ckpt",
)


class SpeakerEncoder:

    def __init__(self):
        self._model = None
        self._device = None
        self.ready = False
        self._load_model()

    def _validate_model_files(self) -> bool:
        missing = [fname for fname in _REQUIRED_MODEL_FILES if not (MODEL_DIR / fname).exists()]
        if missing:
            logger.error("Incomplete SpeechBrain model at %s.", MODEL_DIR)
            for fname in missing:
                logger.error("  Missing: %s", fname)
            return False
        return True

    def _load_model(self):
        try:
            self._device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
            logger.info("Using device: %s", self._device)

            if not MODEL_DIR.exists():
                logger.error(
                    "SpeechBrain model directory not found at %s. "
                    "Download the model and place it there, or run setup.",
                    MODEL_DIR,
                )
                self.ready = False
                return

            if not self._validate_model_files():
                self.ready = False
                return

            logger.info("Loading local SpeechBrain speaker model from %s ...", MODEL_DIR)

            with warnings.catch_warnings():
                warnings.simplefilter("ignore")

                from speechbrain.inference.speaker import SpeakerRecognition

            self._model = SpeakerRecognition.from_hparams(
                source=str(MODEL_DIR),
                savedir=str(MODEL_DIR),
                run_opts={"device": self._device},
            )
            self.ready = True
            logger.info("SpeakerEncoder initialized successfully on %s.", self._device)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(
                "SpeakerEncoder unavailable. Reason: %s",
                exc,
            )
            self.ready = False

    def is_ready(self) -> bool:
        return self.ready

    def _signal_to_tensor(self, signal: np.ndarray) -> torch.Tensor:
        if signal.ndim == 1:
            signal = signal.reshape(1, -1)
        else:
            signal = signal.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True).T
        return torch.tensor(signal, dtype=torch.float32).to(self._device)

    def embed(self, audio_path: str) -> list[float] | None:
        if not self.ready or self._model is None:
            logger.debug("embed() skipped â€” model not loaded")
            return None

        try:
            signal, _sr = sf.read(audio_path)
            return self._embed_signal(signal)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("embed() failed for %s: %s", audio_path, exc)
            return None

    def embed_raw(self, audio: np.ndarray, sample_rate: int = 16000) -> list[float] | None:
        if not self.ready or self._model is None:
            logger.debug("embed_raw() skipped â€” model not loaded")
            return None

        if sample_rate != 16000:
            logger.warning(
                "Expected 16000 Hz audio but received %d Hz.",
                sample_rate,
            )

        try:
            return self._embed_signal(audio)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("embed_raw() failed: %s", exc)
            return None

    def _embed_signal(self, signal: np.ndarray) -> list[float] | None:
        try:
            signal = signal.astype(np.float32)

            peak = np.max(np.abs(signal))
            if peak > 0:
                signal /= peak

            signal_tensor = self._signal_to_tensor(signal)
            with torch.no_grad():
                embedding = self._model.encode_batch(signal_tensor)
            return embedding.squeeze().cpu().numpy().tolist()

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("_embed_signal() failed: %s", exc)
            return None

# voice\speaker\speaker_manager.py
import logging
import threading
import time

import numpy as np

from voice.speaker.speaker_profile import SpeakerProfile
from voice.speaker.speaker_encoder import SpeakerEncoder
from voice.speaker.speaker_database import SpeakerDatabase

logger = logging.getLogger("Mai.SpeakerManager")


class SpeakerManager:

    def __init__(
        self,
        profiles_dir: str,
    ):
        self.encoder = SpeakerEncoder()
        self.db = SpeakerDatabase(profiles_dir)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._threshold_high = 0.7
        self._threshold_low = 0.3

    @property
    def ready(self) -> bool:
        return self.encoder.ready

    def _match(self, embedding: list[float]) -> tuple[SpeakerProfile | None, float]:
        with self._lock:
            profile, score = self.db.search(embedding, threshold=self._threshold_low)
            if profile is not None and score >= self._threshold_high:
                profile.last_seen = time.time()
                profile.confidence = score
                self.db.save(profile)
        return profile, score

    def identify(self, audio_path: str) -> tuple[SpeakerProfile | None, float]:
        try:
            embedding = self.encoder.embed(audio_path)
            if embedding is None:
                return None, 0.0
            return self._match(embedding)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("identify() failed: %s", exc)
            return None, 0.0

    def identify_raw(
        self, audio: np.ndarray, sample_rate: int = 16000
    ) -> tuple[SpeakerProfile | None, float]:
        try:
            embedding = self.encoder.embed_raw(audio, sample_rate)
            if embedding is None:
                return None, 0.0
            return self._match(embedding)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("identify_raw() failed: %s", exc)
            return None, 0.0

    def register(
        self, name: str, audio_path: str
    ) -> SpeakerProfile | None:
        try:
            embedding = self.encoder.embed(audio_path)
            return self._register_with_embedding(name, embedding)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("register(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
            return None

    def register_raw(
        self, name: str, audio: np.ndarray, sample_rate: int = 16000
    ) -> SpeakerProfile | None:
        try:
            embedding = self.encoder.embed_raw(audio, sample_rate)
            return self._register_with_embedding(name, embedding)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("register_raw(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
            return None

    def _register_with_embedding(
        self, name: str, embedding: list[float] | None
    ) -> SpeakerProfile | None:
        if embedding is None:
            return None

        with self._lock:
            existing = self.db.load(name)
            if existing is not None:
                existing.embedding = embedding
                existing.sample_count += 1
                existing.last_seen = time.time()
                existing.confidence = 1.0
                self.db.save(existing)
                return existing

            profile = SpeakerProfile(
                name=name,
                embedding=embedding,
                confidence=1.0,
                sample_count=1,
            )
            self.db.save(profile)
        return profile

    def update_embedding(self, name: str, audio_path: str) -> SpeakerProfile | None:
        try:
            with self._lock:
                profile = self.db.load(name)
                if profile is None:
                    logger.debug("update_embedding(%s): not found", name)
                    return None

                embedding = self.encoder.embed(audio_path)
                if embedding is None:
                    return None

                profile.embedding = embedding
                profile.sample_count += 1
                profile.last_seen = time.time()
                self.db.save(profile)
            return profile

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("update_embedding(%s) failed: %s", name, exc)
            return None

    def delete(self, name: str) -> bool:
        return self.db.delete(name)

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        profiles = self.db.list_all()
        return {
            "total_speakers": len(profiles),
            "ready": self.ready,
            "speakers": [
                {
                    "name": p.name,
                    "confidence": round(p.confidence, 3),
                    "sample_count": p.sample_count,
                    "last_seen": p.last_seen,
                }
                for p in profiles
            ],
        }

# voice\speaker\speaker_profile.py
import uuid
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict


@dataclass
class SpeakerProfile:
    name: str
    embedding: list[float] | None = None
    confidence: float = 0.0
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    created: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    last_seen: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    sample_count: int = 0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict) -> "SpeakerProfile":
        return cls(**data)

# voice\speaker_identifier.py
import time
import logging
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SpeakerIdentifier:
    def __init__(self, speaker_manager=None, sample_rate=16000):
        self.speaker_manager = speaker_manager
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate

    def identify(self, audio):
        if self.speaker_manager is None or not self.speaker_manager.ready:
            return "", 0.0
        try:
            profile, confidence = self.speaker_manager.identify_raw(
                audio, sample_rate=self.sample_rate
            )
            if profile and confidence >= 0.3:
                return profile.name, round(confidence, 3)
            return "", 0.0
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[SpeakerIdentifier] Error: %s", e)
            return "", 0.0

    def register(self, name, audio):
        if self.speaker_manager is None:
            return None
        try:
            profile = self.speaker_manager.register_raw(
                name, audio, sample_rate=self.sample_rate
            )
            return profile
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[SpeakerIdentifier] Register error: %s", e)
            return None

# voice\stream_player.py
# Mai_ai/voice/stream_player.py
import threading
import queue
import time
import os
import logging

import sounddevice as sd
import soundfile as sf
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class StreamPlayer:

    def __init__(
        self,
        volume=1.0,
        output_device=None,
        speed=1.0,
    ):

        self.audio_queue = queue.Queue()

        self.stop_flag = threading.Event()

        self.pause_flag = threading.Event()

        self.thread = None

        self.volume = volume

        self.speed = max(0.5, min(2.0, speed))

        self.output_device = output_device

        self.current_audio = None

        self.state = "idle"

        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    # â–¶ï¸ START PLAYER
    def play_async(self):

        # prevent duplicate threads
        if self.thread and self.thread.is_alive():
            return

        self.stop_flag.clear()

        self.thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._worker,
            daemon=True
        )

        self.thread.start()

        logger.info("[StreamPlayer] Started")

    # ðŸŽ§ AUDIO WORKER
    def _worker(self):

        while not self.stop_flag.is_set():

            try:

                # paused state
                if self.pause_flag.is_set():

                    time.sleep(0.1)
                    continue

                self.state = "idle"

                path = self.audio_queue.get(
                    timeout=1
                )

                if not path:
                    continue

                if not os.path.exists(path):
                    logger.warning("Missing file: %s", path)
                    continue

                self.current_audio = path

                self.state = "playing"

                # load audio
                data, sr = sf.read(path)

                # mono fix
                if len(data.shape) > 1:
                    data = np.mean(data, axis=1)

                # normalize safely
                peak = np.max(np.abs(data))

                if peak > 0:
                    data = data / peak

                # apply volume
                data = data * self.volume

                # apply speed (resample)
                if abs(self.speed - 1.0) > 0.01:
                    new_len = int(len(data) / self.speed)
                    data = np.interp(
                        np.linspace(0, len(data) - 1, new_len),
                        np.arange(len(data)),
                        data,
                    ).astype(data.dtype)
                    sr = int(sr / self.speed)

                logger.info("Playing: %s", path)

                # use chosen device
                if self.output_device is not None:

                    sd.play(
                        data,
                        sr,
                        device=self.output_device,
                        blocking=True
                    )

                else:

                    sd.play(
                        data,
                        sr,
                        blocking=True
                    )

                self.current_audio = None

                try:
                    os.remove(path)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            except queue.Empty:

                continue

            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("StreamPlayer error: %s", e)
                time.sleep(0.5)

    # âž• ENQUEUE AUDIO
    def enqueue(
        self,
        audio_path
    ):

        if not audio_path:
            return

        self.audio_queue.put(audio_path)

    # â¸ï¸ PAUSE
    def pause(self):

        self.pause_flag.set()

        sd.stop()

        self.state = "paused"

    # â–¶ï¸ RESUME
    def resume(self):

        self.pause_flag.clear()

        self.state = "playing"

    # â›” STOP EVERYTHING
    def stop(self):

        self.stop_flag.set()

        self.pause_flag.clear()

        sd.stop()

        self.state = "stopped"

        # clear queue
        while not self.audio_queue.empty():

            try:
                self.audio_queue.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break

        # wait for thread shutdown
        if self.thread:

            self.thread.join(timeout=2)

        self.current_audio = None

        logger.info("[StreamPlayer] Stopped")

    # ðŸ”Š VOLUME
    def set_volume(
        self,
        volume
    ):

        self.volume = max(
            0.0,
            min(volume, 2.0)
        )

    # ðŸŽ§ SET AUDIO DEVICE
    def set_output_device(
        self,
        device_index
    ):

        self.output_device = device_index

    # ðŸŽµ SET PLAYBACK SPEED
    def set_speed(
        self,
        speed
    ):

        self.speed = max(
            0.5,
            min(2.0, speed)
        )

    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    def stats(self):

        return {

            "state":
                self.state,

            "queue_size":
                self.audio_queue.qsize(),

            "volume":
                self.volume,

            "speed":
                self.speed,

            "current_audio":
                self.current_audio,

            "device":
                self.output_device
        }

    # ðŸ”Ž LIST AUDIO DEVICES
    @staticmethod
    def list_devices():

        logger.info("%s", sd.query_devices())
# voice\stt_whisper.py
import logging
import numpy as np
import sounddevice as sd
import whisper
import torch
import time

from config.settings import Settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class TranscriptionUnavailable(Exception):
    """Raised when Whisper model cannot be initialized for transcription."""
    pass


class WhisperSTT:

    def __init__(
        self,
        model_size=None,
        sample_rate=None,
        max_record_seconds=None,
        silence_threshold=None,
        silence_duration=None,
        input_device=None
    ):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate or Settings.VOICE_SAMPLE_RATE
        self.max_record_seconds = max_record_seconds or Settings.MAX_RECORDING_SECONDS
        self.silence_threshold = silence_threshold or Settings.SILENCE_THRESHOLD
        self.silence_duration = silence_duration or Settings.SILENCE_DURATION
        self._model_size = model_size or Settings.WHISPER_MODEL

        self.device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"

        devices = sd.query_devices()
        for i, device in enumerate(devices):
            logger.debug("Audio device %d: %s | inputs=%d", i, device["name"], device["max_input_channels"])

        self.input_device = input_device
        if self.input_device is not None:
            logger.info("Using manual mic ID: %s", self.input_device)
        else:
            preferred_keywords = ["mic", "microphone", "realtek", "jack"]
            for i, device in enumerate(devices):
                name = device["name"].lower()
                if any(k in name for k in preferred_keywords) and device["max_input_channels"] > 0:
                    self.input_device = i
                    logger.info("Using microphone: %s (ID: %d)", device["name"], i)
                    break

        if self.input_device is None:
            self.input_device = sd.default.device[0]
            logger.info("Using default mic (ID: %s)", self.input_device)

        self.model = None

    def _ensure_model(self):
        if self.model is not None:
            return True
        try:
            logger.info("Loading model '%s' on %s...", self._model_size, self.device)
            self.model = whisper.load_model(self._model_size, device=self.device)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Failed to load Whisper model: %s", e)
            return False

    def transcribe(self, audio, language="en"):
        if not self._ensure_model():
            raise TranscriptionUnavailable(
                "Whisper unavailable: model failed to load"
            )
        result = self.model.transcribe(audio, fp16=torch.cuda.is_available(), language=language)
        return result.get("text", "").strip()

    def _record_until_silence(self):
        logger.info("Listening...")
        audio_chunks = []
        silence_start = None
        voice_detected = False
        chunk_duration = 0.3
        chunk_size = int(self.sample_rate * chunk_duration)
        start_time = time.time()

        try:
            with sd.InputStream(
                samplerate=self.sample_rate,
                channels=1,
                dtype="float32",
                blocksize=chunk_size,
                device=self.input_device,
            ) as stream:
                while True:
                    chunk, overflowed = stream.read(chunk_size)
                    chunk = chunk.flatten()
                    gain = 2.5
                    chunk = np.tanh(chunk * gain)
                    volume = np.abs(chunk).mean()

                    if Settings.SHOW_WHISPER_VOLUME:
                        logger.debug("Volume: %.6f", volume)

                    if not voice_detected:
                        if volume > self.silence_threshold:
                            voice_detected = True
                            logger.info("Voice detected")
                        else:
                            continue

                    audio_chunks.append(chunk)

                    if volume < self.silence_threshold:
                        if silence_start is None:
                            silence_start = time.time()
                        elif time.time() - silence_start >= self.silence_duration:
                            logger.info("Silence detected")
                            break
                    else:
                        silence_start = None

                    if time.time() - start_time >= self.max_record_seconds:
                        logger.info("Max recording time reached")
                        break

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Stream error: %s", e)
            return None

        if not audio_chunks:
            return None

        audio = np.concatenate(audio_chunks)
        max_val = np.max(np.abs(audio))
        if max_val > 0:
            audio = audio / max_val
        return audio.astype(np.float32)

    def listen(self):
        try:
            audio = self._record_until_silence()
            if audio is None:
                return ""
            logger.info("Transcribing...")
            text = self.transcribe(audio)
            logger.info("Transcribed: %s", text)

            blocked = ["you called me darling", "thank you", "thanks for watching"]
            lower = text.lower()
            if any(b in lower for b in blocked):
                return ""
            if len(text) < 2:
                return ""
            logger.info("User: %s", text)
            return text
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Whisper error: %s", e)
            return ""

    def set_model(self, model_size):
        try:
            logger.info("Switching to %s...", model_size)
            self.model = whisper.load_model(model_size, device=self.device)
            self._model_size = model_size
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Model switch error: %s", e)
            return False

    def stats(self):
        return {
            "device": self.device,
            "input_device": self.input_device,
            "sample_rate": self.sample_rate,
            "max_record_seconds": self.max_record_seconds,
            "silence_threshold": self.silence_threshold,
            "silence_duration": self.silence_duration,
        }

# voice\utterance_segmenter.py
import numpy as np
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class UtteranceSegmenter:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, silence_threshold=0.01,
                 min_silence_duration=0.6, min_utterance_duration=0.3,
                 max_utterance_duration=30.0):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.silence_threshold = silence_threshold
        self.min_silence_frames = int(min_silence_duration * sample_rate)
        self.min_utterance_frames = int(min_utterance_duration * sample_rate)
        self.max_utterance_frames = int(max_utterance_duration * sample_rate)

    def segment(self, audio):
        if len(audio) == 0:
            return []

        try:
            energy = np.abs(audio)
            is_speech = energy > self.silence_threshold
            if not np.any(is_speech):
                return []

            segments = []
            start = None
            silence_run = 0
            utt_start = None

            for i in range(len(audio)):
                if is_speech[i]:
                    if start is None:
                        start = i
                    silence_run = 0
                else:
                    if start is not None:
                        silence_run += 1
                        if silence_run >= self.min_silence_frames:
                            end = i - silence_run + 1
                            if end - start >= self.min_utterance_frames:
                                chunk = audio[start:end]
                                if len(chunk) <= self.max_utterance_frames:
                                    segments.append(chunk)
                                else:
                                    segments.extend(self._split_long(chunk))
                            start = None

            if start is not None:
                remaining = audio[start:]
                if len(remaining) >= self.min_utterance_frames:
                    if len(remaining) <= self.max_utterance_frames:
                        segments.append(remaining)
                    else:
                        segments.extend(self._split_long(remaining))

            for s in segments:
                pass
            return segments
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("[UtteranceSegmenter] segment error: %s", e)
            return [audio] if len(audio) > 0 else []

    def _split_long(self, audio):
        chunks = []
        for i in range(0, len(audio), self.max_utterance_frames):
            chunk = audio[i:i + self.max_utterance_frames]
            if len(chunk) >= self.min_utterance_frames:
                chunks.append(chunk)
        return chunks

# voice\vad.py
import os
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np
import torch

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class VAD:
    _shared_model = None
    _shared_lock = threading.Lock()

    def __init__(self, threshold=0.5, sample_rate=16000, frame_size=512):
        self.threshold = threshold
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.frame_size = frame_size
        self._model = None
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    def _load_model(self):
        if VAD._shared_model is not None:
            self._model = VAD._shared_model
            return
        if self._model is not None:
            return
        with VAD._shared_lock:
            if VAD._shared_model is not None:
                self._model = VAD._shared_model
                return
            if self._model is not None:
                return
            try:
                model_path = os.path.join(
                    os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cache", "silero_vad", "silero_vad.jit"
                )
                if not os.path.exists(model_path):
                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(model_path), exist_ok=True)
                    import urllib.request
                    url = "https://github.com/snakers4/silero-vad/raw/master/src/silero_vad/data/silero_vad.jit"
                    urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, model_path)
                self._model = torch.jit.load(model_path, map_location="cpu")
                self._model.eval()
                VAD._shared_model = self._model
                logger.info("[VAD] Model loaded")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[VAD] Load failed: %s", e)
                self._model = None

    def is_speech(self, chunk):
        if len(chunk) != self.frame_size:
            chunk = self._resample_frame(chunk)
        with self._lock:
            self._load_model()
            if self._model is None:
                return False
            try:
                tensor = torch.from_numpy(chunk)
                sr = torch.tensor(self.sample_rate)
                prob = self._model(tensor, sr).item()
                return prob > self.threshold
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("[VAD] Inference error: %s", e)
                return False

    def speech_prob(self, chunk):
        if len(chunk) != self.frame_size:
            chunk = self._resample_frame(chunk)
        with self._lock:
            self._load_model()
            if self._model is None:
                return 0.0
            try:
                tensor = torch.from_numpy(chunk)
                sr = torch.tensor(self.sample_rate)
                return self._model(tensor, sr).item()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("[VAD] Inference error: %s", e)
                return 0.0

    def _resample_frame(self, chunk):
        if len(chunk) > self.frame_size:
            return chunk[:self.frame_size]
        result = np.zeros(self.frame_size, dtype=np.float32)
        result[:len(chunk)] = chunk
        return result
