# voice\voice_manager.py
import time
import logging
import threading
import numpy as np

from voice.stt_whisper import TranscriptionUnavailable

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class VoiceManager:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=16000, blocksize=512,
                 vad_threshold=0.5, silence_timeout=1.5,
                 pre_roll=0.5, min_recording=0.5, max_recording=30.0,
                 stt=None, speaker_manager=None, wake_detector=None,
                 speaker_identifier=None, microphone=None,
                 audio_buffer=None, vad=None, recorder=None,
                 noise_filter=None, echo_canceller=None,
                 utterance_segmenter=None, event_bus=None,
                 metrics=None,
                 adaptive_noise_filter=None, full_aec=None,
                 emotion_detector=None, diarization_engine=None):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.blocksize = blocksize
        self.vad_threshold = vad_threshold
        self.silence_timeout = silence_timeout
        self.pre_roll = pre_roll
        self.min_recording = min_recording
        self.max_recording = max_recording
        self.stt = stt
        self.speaker_manager = speaker_manager
        self.wake_detector = wake_detector
        self.speaker_identifier = speaker_identifier
        self.noise_filter = noise_filter
        self.echo_canceller = echo_canceller
        self.utterance_segmenter = utterance_segmenter
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.adaptive_noise_filter = adaptive_noise_filter
        self.full_aec = full_aec
        self.emotion_detector = emotion_detector
        self.diarization_engine = diarization_engine
        self._microphone = microphone
        self._audio_buffer = audio_buffer
        self._vad = vad
        self._recorder = recorder
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._running = False
        self._state = "off"
        self._speaking = False
        # Eagerly init VAD so health check passes even before start()
        if self._vad is None:
            try:
                from voice.vad import VAD
                self._vad = VAD(threshold=self.vad_threshold, sample_rate=self.sample_rate)
                self._vad._load_model()
                logger.info("[VoiceManager] VAD model pre-loaded")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("[VoiceManager] VAD pre-load failed: %s", e)
        self._interrupt_manager = None
        self._interrupt_energy_threshold = 0.015
        if metrics is not None:
            self._metrics = metrics
        else:
            from voice.metrics import VoiceMetrics
            self._metrics = VoiceMetrics()

    def set_speaking(self, speaking, interrupt_manager=None):
        self._speaking = speaking
        if interrupt_manager:
            self._interrupt_manager = interrupt_manager

    def is_speaking(self):
        return self._speaking

    @property
    def state(self):
        return self._state

    @property
    def metrics(self):
        return self._metrics

    def _set_state(self, new_state):
        old = self._state
        self._state = new_state
        logger.debug("[VoiceManager] State: %s", new_state)
        if self.event_bus and old != new_state:
            from voice.events import VOICE_STATE_CHANGED, voice_state_changed_data
            self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_STATE_CHANGED, voice_state_changed_data(old, new_state))

    def _ensure_components(self):
        from voice.microphone import Microphone
        from voice.audio_buffer import AudioBuffer
        from voice.vad import VAD
        from voice.recorder import Recorder
        if self._vad is None:
            self._vad = VAD(threshold=self.vad_threshold, sample_rate=self.sample_rate)
            self._vad._load_model()
        if self._audio_buffer is None:
            self._audio_buffer = AudioBuffer(max_seconds=3.0, sample_rate=self.sample_rate)
        if self._recorder is None:
            self._recorder = Recorder(
                sample_rate=self.sample_rate,
                silence_timeout=self.silence_timeout,
                min_recording=self.min_recording,
                max_recording=self.max_recording,
                pre_roll=self.pre_roll,
                vad=self._vad,
            )
        if self._microphone is None:
            self._microphone = Microphone(
                sample_rate=self.sample_rate,
                blocksize=self.blocksize,
            )
        if self.speaker_identifier is None and self.speaker_manager:
            from voice.speaker_identifier import SpeakerIdentifier
            self.speaker_identifier = SpeakerIdentifier(
                speaker_manager=self.speaker_manager,
                sample_rate=self.sample_rate,
            )
        if self.wake_detector is None:
            from voice.wake_detector import WakeDetector
            self.wake_detector = WakeDetector(stt=self.stt)
        if self.noise_filter is None and self.adaptive_noise_filter is None:
            from voice.adaptive_noise_filter import AdaptiveNoiseFilter
            self.adaptive_noise_filter = AdaptiveNoiseFilter(sample_rate=self.sample_rate)
        if self.utterance_segmenter is None:
            from voice.utterance_segmenter import UtteranceSegmenter
            self.utterance_segmenter = UtteranceSegmenter(sample_rate=self.sample_rate)
        if self.emotion_detector is None:
            from voice.emotion_detector import VoiceEmotionDetector
            self.emotion_detector = VoiceEmotionDetector(sample_rate=self.sample_rate)

        # Component health report
        stt_ok = self.stt is not None and (not hasattr(self.stt, 'model') or self.stt.model is not None)
        vad_ok = self._vad is not None
        mic_ok = self._microphone is not None
        logger.info(
            "[VoiceManager] Components: stt=%s vad=%s mic=%s recorder=%s noise=%s aec=%s emotion=%s diar=%s",
            "ok" if stt_ok else "MISSING",
            "ok" if vad_ok else "MISSING",
            "ok" if mic_ok else "MISSING",
            "ok" if self._recorder is not None else "MISSING",
            "ok" if (self.adaptive_noise_filter or self.noise_filter) else "none",
            "ok" if (self.full_aec or self.echo_canceller) else "none",
            "ok" if self.emotion_detector else "none",
            "ok" if self.diarization_engine else "none",
        )

    def start(self):
        self._ensure_components()
        with self._lock:
            if self._running:
                return
            self._running = True

        def mic_callback(chunk):
            if self._speaking and self._interrupt_manager:
                try:
                    rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(chunk.astype(np.float64)))) if chunk.size > 0 else 0.0
                    if rms > self._interrupt_energy_threshold:
                        self._interrupt_manager.trigger(reason="mic_energy", source="voice_manager", priority=2)
                        self._speaking = False
                except Exception:
                    pass
            processed = chunk

            # Heavy processing (VAD, FFT, AEC, features) only during active recording.
            # During idle the callback must stay <32ms to avoid starving sounddevice.
            is_recording = self._recorder is not None and self._recorder.state == "recording"
            if is_recording:
                noise_source = self.adaptive_noise_filter or self.noise_filter
                if noise_source:
                    if hasattr(noise_source, 'add_chunk'):
                        try:
                            noise_source.add_chunk(chunk)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    processed = noise_source.filter(processed)

                echo_source = self.full_aec or self.echo_canceller
                if echo_source:
                    processed = echo_source.process(processed)

                if self.emotion_detector:
                    try:
                        self.emotion_detector.add_audio(processed)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            self._audio_buffer.add(processed)
            if self._recorder:
                result = self._recorder.add_chunk(processed)
                if result is not None:
                    self._handle_recording(result)

        self._microphone.start(callback=mic_callback)
        self._set_state("idle")
        if self.event_bus:
            from voice.events import VOICE_STARTED, voice_started_data
            self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_STARTED, voice_started_data())
        logger.info("[VoiceManager] Started")

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._running = False
            if self._microphone:
                self._microphone.stop()
            if self._recorder:
                self._recorder.stop()
        self._set_state("off")
        if self.event_bus:
            from voice.events import VOICE_STOPPED, voice_stopped_data
            self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_STOPPED, voice_stopped_data())
        logger.info("[VoiceManager] Stopped")

    def listen(self, wake_required=True, timeout=None):
        logger.info("[VoiceManager] listen() entered (wake_required=%s)", wake_required)
        self._ensure_components()
        self._set_state("listening")
        if self.event_bus:
            from voice.events import VOICE_LISTENING_STARTED, voice_listening_started_data
            self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_LISTENING_STARTED, voice_listening_started_data(wake_required))
        try:
            pre_roll = self._audio_buffer.get_last(self._recorder.pre_roll)
            pre_roll_len = len(pre_roll) if pre_roll is not None else 0
            logger.info("[VoiceManager] Pre-roll: %d samples from audio buffer", pre_roll_len)
            self._recorder.start_recording(pre_roll_audio=pre_roll)
            audio = self._recorder.wait(timeout=timeout)
            if audio is None:
                logger.info("[VoiceManager] Recorder returned None â€” no speech captured")
                self._set_state("idle")
                return None

            logger.info("[VoiceManager] Audio received: duration=%.2fs samples=%d max=%.4f",
                        len(audio) / self.sample_rate, len(audio),
                        float(np.max(np.abs(audio))) if len(audio) > 0 else 0.0)

            self._set_state("processing")
            if self.event_bus:
                from voice.events import VOICE_RECORDING_COMPLETED, voice_recording_completed_data
                self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_RECORDING_COMPLETED, voice_recording_completed_data(len(audio), len(audio) / self.sample_rate))

            segments = [audio]
            if self.utterance_segmenter:
                segmented = self.utterance_segmenter.segment(audio)
                if segmented:
                    segments = segmented

            emotion = None
            if self.emotion_detector:
                try:
                    emotion_label, emotion_conf, emotion_feats = self.emotion_detector.detect(audio)
                    emotion = {"label": emotion_label, "confidence": emotion_conf, "features": emotion_feats}
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("[VoiceManager] Emotion detection error: %s", e)

            speaker_segments = None
            if self.diarization_engine:
                try:
                    speaker_segments = self.diarization_engine.segment(audio)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("[VoiceManager] Diarization error: %s", e)

            events = []
            if speaker_segments:
                for seg in speaker_segments:
                    seg_audio = audio[seg.start_sample:seg.end_sample]
                    if len(seg_audio) < 160:
                        continue
                    speaker, speaker_conf = seg.speaker_id, seg.confidence
                    event = self._transcribe(seg_audio, speaker, speaker_conf, emotion)
                    events.append(event)
            else:
                for seg in segments:
                    speaker, speaker_conf = self._identify_speaker(seg)
                    event = self._transcribe(seg, speaker, speaker_conf, emotion)
                    events.append(event)

            combined = self._merge_events(events)
            if combined:
                self._metrics.record_recording(combined.duration, combined.audio_length)
                if combined.text:
                    self._metrics.record_transcription(True)
                else:
                    self._metrics.record_transcription(False)
                if combined.wake_detected:
                    self._metrics.record_wake()
                if combined.speaker:
                    self._metrics.record_speaker_id()

            if wake_required and combined and not combined.wake_detected:
                self._set_state("idle")
                return None

            if combined and self.event_bus:
                from voice.events import VOICE_RETURNED, voice_returned_data
                extra = {}
                if emotion:
                    extra["emotion"] = emotion
                self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_RETURNED, voice_returned_data(
                    combined.text, combined.speaker, combined.wake_detected, combined.duration, extra
                ))

            delay = self._response_timing_delay(emotion)
            if delay > 0:
                time.sleep(delay)
            self._set_state("return")
            logger.info("[VoiceManager] listen() returning: text='%s', speaker='%s', wake=%s",
                        combined.text if combined else "N/A",
                        combined.speaker if combined else "N/A",
                        combined.wake_detected if combined else "N/A")
            return combined
        except TranscriptionUnavailable:
            logger.warning("[VoiceManager] Whisper unavailable â€” aborting listen")
            self._set_state("idle")
            raise
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[VoiceManager] listen error: %s", e)
            self._metrics.record_error()
            if self.event_bus:
                from voice.events import VOICE_ERROR, voice_error_data
                self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_ERROR, voice_error_data("listen_error", e))
            self._set_state("idle")
            return None

    def _response_timing_delay(self, emotion=None):
        if not emotion:
            return 0.0
        label = emotion.get("label", "neutral") if isinstance(emotion, dict) else "neutral"
        delays = {"neutral": 0.3, "happy": 0.1, "sad": 0.8, "angry": 0.2, "stressed": 0.7, "energetic": 0.1}
        return delays.get(label, 0.3)

    def listen_raw(self, timeout=None):
        self._ensure_components()
        try:
            pre_roll = self._audio_buffer.get_last(self._recorder.pre_roll)
            self._recorder.start_recording(pre_roll_audio=pre_roll)
            audio = self._recorder.wait(timeout=timeout)
            if audio is None:
                return None
            emotion = None
            if self.emotion_detector:
                try:
                    emotion_label, emotion_conf, _ = self.emotion_detector.detect(audio)
                    emotion = {"label": emotion_label, "confidence": emotion_conf}
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("[VoiceManager] listen_raw emotion error: %s", e)
            return {"audio": audio, "emotion": emotion}
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[VoiceManager] listen_raw error: %s", e)
            self._metrics.record_error()
            if self.event_bus:
                from voice.events import VOICE_ERROR, voice_error_data
                self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_ERROR, voice_error_data("listen_raw_error", e))
            return None

    def _identify_speaker(self, audio):
        if self.speaker_identifier:
            try:
                name, conf = self.speaker_identifier.identify(audio)
                if self.event_bus:
                    from voice.events import VOICE_SPEAKER_IDENTIFIED, voice_speaker_identified_data
                    self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_SPEAKER_IDENTIFIED, voice_speaker_identified_data(name, conf))
                return name, conf
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("[VoiceManager] Speaker ID error: %s", e)
        return "", 0.0

    def _transcribe(self, audio, speaker="", speaker_conf=0.0, emotion=None):
        from voice.voice_event import VoiceEvent
        text = ""
        wake_detected = False
        if self.stt:
            try:
                norm_audio = audio.astype(np.float32)
                max_val = np.max(np.abs(norm_audio))
                if max_val > 0:
                    norm_audio = norm_audio / max_val
                logger.info("[VoiceManager] Starting Whisper transcription (audio=%d samples, max=%.4f)",
                            len(norm_audio), max_val)
                text = self.stt.transcribe(norm_audio)
                logger.info("[VoiceManager] Whisper result: text=%s", repr(text) if text else "(empty)")
            except TranscriptionUnavailable:
                raise
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("[VoiceManager] STT error: %s", e)

        if self.wake_detector and text:
            wake_detected = self.wake_detector.detect_in_text(text)

        duration = len(audio) / self.sample_rate

        if self.event_bus:
            from voice.events import (VOICE_TRANSCRIPTION_COMPLETED, VOICE_WAKE_DETECTED,
                                      voice_transcription_completed_data, voice_wake_detected_data)
            self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_TRANSCRIPTION_COMPLETED, voice_transcription_completed_data(text, len(audio), duration))
            if wake_detected and text:
                self.event_bus.publish(VOICE_WAKE_DETECTED, voice_wake_detected_data("mai", text))

        return VoiceEvent(
            text=text,
            speaker=speaker,
            speaker_confidence=speaker_conf,
            audio_length=len(audio),
            duration=duration,
            timestamp=time.time(),
            wake_detected=wake_detected,
            emotion=emotion,
        )

    def _merge_events(self, events):
        if not events:
            return None
        if len(events) == 1:
            return events[0]
        from voice.voice_event import VoiceEvent
        texts = [e.text for e in events if e.text]
        best_speaker = max(
            ((e.speaker, e.speaker_confidence) for e in events if e.speaker),
            key=lambda x: x[1],
            default=("", 0.0),
        )
        best_emotion = max(
            (e.emotion for e in events if e.emotion and e.emotion.get("confidence", 0) > 0.3),
            key=lambda e: e.get("confidence", 0),
            default=None,
        )
        return VoiceEvent(
            text=" ".join(filter(None, texts)),
            speaker=best_speaker[0],
            speaker_confidence=best_speaker[1],
            audio_length=sum(e.audio_length for e in events),
            duration=sum(e.duration for e in events),
            timestamp=events[0].timestamp,
            wake_detected=any(e.wake_detected for e in events),
            emotion=best_emotion,
        )

    def send_playback_audio(self, audio):
        echo_source = self.full_aec or self.echo_canceller
        if echo_source:
            try:
                echo_source.add_playback(audio)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("[VoiceManager] send_playback_audio error: %s", e)

    def _handle_recording(self, audio):
        pass

# voice\voice_state.py
from enum import Enum


class VoiceState(str, Enum):
    OFF = "off"
    IDLE = "idle"
    LISTENING = "listening"
    SPEECH = "speech"
    RECORDING = "recording"
    PROCESSING = "processing"
    TRANSCRIBING = "transcribing"
    RETURN = "return"

# voice\wake_detector.py
import time
import logging
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class WakeDetector:
    def __init__(self, wake_words=None, stt=None, cooldown=2.0):
        from config.settings import WAKE_WORDS as DEFAULT_WAKE_WORDS
        self.wake_words = [w.lower().strip() for w in (wake_words or DEFAULT_WAKE_WORDS)]
        self.stt = stt
        self.cooldown = cooldown
        self._last_trigger = 0.0

    def is_wake(self, audio, transcribed_text=""):
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._last_trigger < self.cooldown:
            return False, ""

        text = transcribed_text
        if not text and self.stt and audio is not None:
            try:
                text = self.stt.transcribe(audio)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("[WakeDetector] STT error: %s", e)
                return False, ""

        text_lower = text.lower()
        for wake in self.wake_words:
            if wake in text_lower:
                self._last_trigger = now
                logger.info("[WakeDetector] Triggered: %s", wake)
                return True, text

        return False, text

    def detect_in_text(self, text):
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._last_trigger < self.cooldown:
            return False
        text_lower = text.lower()
        for wake in self.wake_words:
            if wake in text_lower:
                self._last_trigger = now
                logger.info("[WakeDetector] Triggered (text): %s", wake)
                return True
        return False

    def reset(self):
        self._last_trigger = 0.0

# voice\wake_word.py
# Mai_ai/voice/wake_word.py
import time
import logging
import speech_recognition as sr

from config.settings import (
    WAKE_WORDS,
    VOICE_COOLDOWN,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class WakeWord:

    def __init__(
        self,
        wake_words=None,
        timeout=3,
        phrase_time_limit=4,
        cooldown=None
    ):

        self.r = sr.Recognizer()

        self.mic = sr.Microphone()

        self.timeout = timeout

        self.phrase_time_limit = phrase_time_limit

        # cooldown from settings
        self.cooldown = cooldown or VOICE_COOLDOWN

        self.last_trigger = 0

        # wake words from settings
        self.wake_words = wake_words or WAKE_WORDS

        # ðŸ§  recognizer tuning
        self.r.energy_threshold = 300
        self.r.pause_threshold = 0.8
        self.r.dynamic_energy_threshold = True

        # ðŸ§  ambient calibration
        self._calibrate()

    # ðŸŽ™ï¸ AMBIENT NOISE CALIBRATION
    def _calibrate(self):
        logger.info("[WakeWord] Calibrating microphone...")

        try:

            with self.mic as source:

                self.r.adjust_for_ambient_noise(
                    source, duration=1
                )

            logger.info(
                "[WakeWord] Calibrated (ambient noise: %.1f)",
                self.r.energy_threshold
            )

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[WakeWord Calibration Error] {e}")

    # ðŸŽ§ LISTEN FOR WAKE WORD
    def listen(self):
        # âš ï¸ recognize_google sends audio to Google's cloud servers.
        # This is a privacy consideration â€” audio data is processed
        # externally and subject to Google's privacy policy.

        # ðŸ§  cooldown protection
        if (
            time.time() - self.last_trigger
            < self.cooldown
        ):
            return False

        logger.debug("[WakeWord] Listening...")

        try:

            with self.mic as source:
                audio = self.r.listen(
                    source,
                    timeout=self.timeout,
                    phrase_time_limit=self.phrase_time_limit
                )

            text = self.r.recognize_google(
                audio
            ).lower()

            logger.info(f"[Wake Heard] {text}")

            # ðŸ§  wake-word matching
            for wake in self.wake_words:

                if wake in text:

                    self.last_trigger = time.time()

                    logger.info(f"[Wake Triggered] {wake}")

                    return True

            return False

        except OSError as e:
            logger.error(f"[WakeWord Mic Error] {e} â€” retrying on next cycle")
            # Give sounddevice a chance to release; sleep is handled by caller
            return False

        except sr.WaitTimeoutError:
            return False

        except sr.UnknownValueError:
            return False

        except sr.RequestError as e:
            logger.error(f"[Speech API Error] {e}")
            return False

        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"[WakeWord Error] {e}")
            return False

    # ðŸ§  ADD NEW WAKE WORD
    def add_wake_word(self, word):

        word = word.lower().strip()

        if word not in self.wake_words:
            self.wake_words.append(word)

    # ðŸ“Š DEBUG INFO
    def stats(self):
        return {
            "wake_words":       self.wake_words,
            "energy_threshold": self.r.energy_threshold,
            "cooldown":         self.cooldown,
        }
